# 📖 Session Notes

Ky’tha is also referred to as Ky  
Ainslie is also referred to as Ains  
Aurelius is also referred to as Aury

# **01 \- Dock Troubles**

**Session Date**:  March 3, 2025  
**In-game Date**:  14th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  The Dyed Cane in Emberstran

## **The Dyed Cane \- 14th of Talil**

### **Emberstran (in Baltwood Territory)** 

City known for wines. Created from volcanic rock and ash.  The port is busy as well.  The city is foggy around the port; north of the city rises above fog.

**Lord Garris Dusklite** oversees the town.  He hires help to keep watch over the town with his three captains; no army to speak of.  Mercenaries are well paid to help.  There is a **Council of Advisors** (“the Council”).  Citizens are confident in his abilities to run the town.

**Dyed Cane** \- we gather at the “usual” table.  The board of odd jobs is all but empty…but a lady posts another job. Barkeep stands at the bar \- **Bordi** (portly barkeep with a mustache).  Ky recognizes the lady and thinks she is associated with a vineyard guild. Og goes to pull the notice off the board. Bordi tries to tap a new barrel and it is dry.  He calls Aury over.  Og reads (paraphrased):  Vintage is tainted…need to find out what is wrong in the cellars.  Bordi and Aury pry off the lid and find a tablet set in the shavings of barkentea (comes from trees in the Baltwood).  Aury tries to see if he recognizes the symbol on the tablet…he thinks they are elvish markings on the back (and an unknown language on the back).  Aury asks to see it with Hap looking on. The cellar job is 50 gp (Emlee Tryndel signed the posting).  Ky reads it to the group (dwarf) \- 

| “when ember rose and sea drank the trees,  the vault was sealed beneath twin roots and flame,  to find the heart that guides the way,  seek the 4 pieces where vine, bone, sea and heart remain.”   |
| :---: |

Hap feels like the words were changed during the awakening…but the tablet is much too old (Pre-Fracturing).  

Group talks about doing the paid quest…and keeping the tablet in mind.  Ainslie asks Og about the other post (calls him out).  Other posting is about a vanished crew at the docks…60gp (from yesterday).  Discussion on which to do.  Decide to do the docks quest and that we put the cellar quest on the board (with find dog note on the back).  

## **The Docks**

Quest (60 gold, 25 extra):  Crew vanished.  Cargo untouched.  No sign of struggle.  Ship remains moored.  Need to get answers before there is a panic.  **Berg Stugmyer** (dockmaster).  

As the group leaves the Dyed Cane, Ky stops at the **Headless Hydra**…the rest of the group continues toward the docks.  Fleur (Hap’s owl) flies around to see if anything is amiss.  \[Ky then secretly goes to find a contact at **Charum’s Oils**…she finds her and they step in a private room…Tessa is no help on the cellar or dock threats, but might have a clue to help with the tablet mystery.  She will talk to the leader of her group and see if she can get more information for Ky).

Meanwhile, Ainslie finds an old friend in town (**Darn**) \- he’s fishing.  He messages him about ships moored at the docks.  Fisherman says he knows about the ship…and Berg might be mad.  The two continue to talk via message while the others look on.  The guy then points to a ship across the way…and then says out loud “but you may not make it off.”  They discuss possibilities that happened on the ship.  The guy asks him for some ale in exchange for keeping this conversation a secret from Berg.  He keeps saying “no one came off that boat \- you aren’t coming off the boat.”

Hap gets his owl back.  Fleur noticed that there was a boat with no activity across the docks.  The group starts to head in the direction of the other side of the docks.  Darn follows the group.  Aury intervenes and tells him to go see Bordi…and say that he sent him (trying to buy Darn’s silence).  

Ky catches up with the group and they make their way around to the small ship.  As we walk out to the pier, everything else seems to evaporate…as we hear the creaking of the ship on the water.  We all think it seems a little odd (though none of us has ever been on a ship, maybe Hap has).  We stop and see the ship is roped off. Hap sends the owl to get a closer look. 

We put the ramp on the ship and board.  Ky, then Og, Hap, Aury, and Ainslie.  Hap listens to the trap door, but doesn’t hear anything.  Og takes a listen and hears water inside the hull.  Og pulls the hatch…it is dark.  Hears a splash. Ky looks in and sees water in the hold with evidence of broken barrels.  She tells the group what she sees.  And she also spots a dead body, a crew member.  Ky starts to walk down the steps. Ky calls Ainslie and Og passes a torch down.  It is initiative.

Dead bodies lunge at the group.  Ky is pushed off the stairs and into the water by a dead body.  A tadpole-like creature crawls across her face.  Ainslie hits the creature on Ky.  Aury lights up the stairs.  Og hits a dead crew member.  Ky kills the thing on her face and moves back toward the stairs. Hap goes to the water and firebolts a crew member. A tadpole attacks Ky and misses, another attaches.  One attaches to Og.  A crew attacks Og.  A crew attacks Hap.  Ainslie kills the tadpole on Ky.  Ky kills the crew on Og.  Og misses the tadpole on him.  Aury tolls the dead on the remaining crew and misses. Hap attacks the zombie with a sword and hits, then uses another action to hit with his scimitar and kills it. The tadpole hits Og again…but is blocked in part by Hap. The tadpole attacks and takes Ky down.  Ainslie kills the tadpole on her.  Og hits the tadpole on him and crushes him.  Aury heals Ky.

We see all the crew in the hold \- dead.  Looks like the broken barrels were full of tadpoles and once they broke out, they killed the crew.  Ainslie realizes the tadpoles were “otherwordly.”  Aury prays to his goddess for help.  We approach the crates that are still intact.  We find one that has iron ore, several with supplies (spoiled rations).  We start to bail out the hold; organize the cargo.  We put the bodies in an empty crate (to take off the ship to burn).  Ainslie goes to the Anchor Point to find Berg and tell him the job is completed. As they walk to the ship, Ainslie clues him in.  Berg said he has no idea who the ship belonged to.  We start to offload the ship with crates.  We are paid 85 gp for the job and given a crate of iron ore (50gp) and another 10gp to haul off the crate with dead bodies.  We take the iron ore to the blacksmith, Ainslie gets a 50/50 split for it.  We take the crate to an area near the temple to burn the bodies (after Ky gets a mummified tadpole to keep).

Aury heals Ky and Og fully.  We all split up.  Ky goes to the Hydra; Aury and Hap go to the Temple; Og and Ainslie ask around about Emlee’s whereabouts.

At the temple, Aury is approached by the priestess who asks about the mischief he’s found himself in the middle of.  They speak of the goddess and he is asked how much longer he will be living at the temple. He would like a couple of additional days and she agrees.  They can both stay at the temple.

Ky returns to the Headless Hydra.  It is lively.  She asks if there is a message for her.  There is none.  She heads to the bar and hears about a cultist group that is rumored to be around.  A group of adventurers came through that were acting under the Lord’s guidance to keep the city safe.  The group found out the cultists were operating near the cemetery/and in the sewers.  Group has to do with **Si’julous**. The group has been compromised.  They were in the sewers under the city.  After a bit, Tessa makes an appearance. Ky feels a weight in her pouch…it is a broken, triangular piece of a rock.  She heads to her room.

Og and Ainslie make their way around the town \- trying to get info on where Emlee might live. They decide to visit the **Old Oak & Grapes** pub up in the hills. This area of town is very nice.  This pub has a very nice, warm feel. They are seated in a booth. Each seating area is somewhat private. They order wine. Og hears rich people talking about rich things.  Ainslie hears more economic talk \- this is our last harvest \- as well as specific talks about the vineyards.  He hears “I’m not sure if **Tarathial** is aware” \- and also picks up on a spat, about that comment. This is just outside of their vision.  They hear “this is not going to work; someone will fix this.”  Then “no \- it is too late.”  Ainslie sees someone start to get up and walk out, the person who is left looks surprised.  As he looks on, they see someone walk up and assassinates the person who is left sitting in the booth.

– The End of the Session – 

# **02 \- Bad Wine**

**Session Date**:  April 22, 2025  
**In-game Date**:  14th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  The Old Oak & Grapes tavern in Emberstran

## **In the Old Oak & Grapes**

Old Oak \- very nice establishment; barkeep, 2 barmaids, stage  Og and Ainslie see body fall to the floor, observing that the person’s throat was slit.  A person stands up and bows and people clap; people say “I knew it.”  But there is actual blood and she is startled and she looks down and sees a dead body and she screams.  Barkeep rushes over \- “**Emelee** \- are you ok?  **Pim** fetches a guard.”  Og tries to help the injured body, but realizes the person is dead.  The dead body is a female and she is dressed like the other lady.  They look for the assassin…they don’t see one, but Og notices that the blade that killed the woman had poison on it. Og takes charge \- “no one is leaving.  We have a murder on our hands.”  Someone else rushes over and says the murders are part of the act…then they say the real dead body.  Questions about who was supposed to be the murderer in tonight’s act.  Barmaid is tending to Emelee. A man stands up and says he was supposed to be the killer, but this is not who Og and Ainslie saw kill the girl. They observed a cloaked figure in dark gray talking to a lady (could be Emelee or the girl killed).  Ainslie asks who the dead person is \- Emelee says, “That is Kelona.”  Both she and Emelee are wearing similar clothes, trimmed out in vineyard-like wear.  The barkeep asks who Og and Ainslie are \- they say they work with the captain; they are persuasive.  They say they will take the dead body to the temple.  Ainslie cleans up the blood.  Barkeep \- “I’m sorry, **Meglan** (little gnome)...maybe we won’t continue with the event tonight…drinks on the house.”  He promises Og and Ainslie a free round…they instead get a carafe of wine to go.  Emelee is crying over her friend. They ask for a curtain to wrap the body in.  **Leedra** (barmaid) gets one and they wrap the body up and head out, going to the temple.  

Hap and Aury hear commotion at the temple…as Og and Ainslie show up with the dead body.  High priestess walks up as well.  Aury says he will take charge.  They describe to Aury and Hap what happened.  Aury fills in the priestess. They take body to prepare…and discuss having a service. Ainslie claims that Kelona and Emelee were sisters (trying to deceive…not nefarious…but to act like he just knows what is going on).  Priestess says word will spread that someone from the vintner’s guild was killed fast enough…so Ainslie doesn’t need any proof of dropping off the body.  Priestess stays with the body.  They decide to sleep and talk to Emelee in the morning.  Og and Ainslie head to the Warm Bear Inn to rest.  Aury prepares the body for burial (he also notices that there was poison involved in the murder \- knows it was a contact poison, used on a blade).  Then Aury and Hap go to sleep at the temple.

Meanwhile…Ky examines the fragment that Tessa put in her pouch.  It appears to be part of a second tablet. Has a vine-like pattern…this is a corner piece.  We think this could be the “vine” mentioned on the piece from the empty cask.  Ky goes to sleep.  All get a long rest.

## **Emberstran \- 15th of Talil**

Ky wakes up and sees at the front desk if there are any messages for her \- there are none.  She heads out toward the docks…heading to the **Warm Bear Inn** to find her friends.

Ky runs into Og and Ainslie…they start to tell her about their evening.  They all then make their way to the temple. Ky is impressed by the construction and takes a hard look at it…she sees that there are patterns in the base of the rock made to look like flames/fire; sees that the temple was a reconstruction of a building of something else originally there.  

Aury mentions the poison and Og says he also knows.  Ky tells the group about the fragment of a tablet she has.  Discussion about the next steps:  we know Emlee needed help with the tainted vineyard, we know there was an empty cask save a tablet, and now a murder tied to the vineyard.  We decide to head to the Old Oak.

Og and Ainslie walk in with the curtain and carafe. They ask for a refill…and the maid says she needs to check with Odith (the barkeep). They get a free refill. Then Hap, Ky, and Aury walk in a few minutes afterwards \- ask for a breakfast and if a murder happened last night.  Ky asks the maid about the murder mystery \- they say it happens every 6th night of a stretch.  Are told the logistics of the play, how it works (anyone in the bar can take part, names are drawn to be victim, murderer, maybe 10 other roles).  Are told Kelona was a friend of Emelee and part of the guild.  That last night Emelee was supposed to be killed.  The two women look alike.  We pretend that Ky, Hap, and Aury don’t know Og and Ainslie.  We talk.  All decide to go to the Guild.  Ainslie asks about where Meglan lives…is told to visit the Collector to get the information they seek.  Og interjects and is told exactly where she lives.

## **Meglan’s House**

We head to Meglan’s house…on the way to the Guild.  We knock, Ainslie introduces herself.  **Meglan Whistlebell** asks why we are at her house.  Ainslie and Og present themselves as the Observer and Analyst \- part of a new initiative the Captain has created.  They start to question her. We end up going in for tea.  Ky asks questions related to the plays in general and then to specifics about last night.  Ainslie asks about props.  Meglan takes him to the prop closet. Ainslie stealths to her bedroom to continue the search.  Meanwhile, Ky tries to connect with her on a personal level and asks questions about Kelona \- does she have enemies? Meglan takes a pause and indicates that she probably had some enemies, but doesn’t want to talk about it \- clearly scared to get on the bad side of those people.  She changes the subject.  Before that, Ky also finds out that a murder of a town guard happened last stretch \- supposedly done by a cultist group (Ghone…Si’joulous…dwellers of the sewers).  But she says that the cultists were brought to justice.  Ainslie searches all over the place \- trying to find evidence about last night’s play.  He finds written documentation about the play that was performed (about Betrayal and Love) and keeps it.

We all thank her for her hospitality. Ainslie asks about a pirate hat. We leave.  We discuss what we found out \- info from Meglan and stuff from her lockbox.  

## **Vintner’s Guild**

We walk to the north toward the Guild.  Ainslie reads the play as we walk…doesn’t discover anything much from it.  We have to go outside the gates…and through farmlands and vineyards for about a ½ mile.  We finally find a central, large establishment \- the Guild. We go up the hill to the building \- it is a large, mansion-style property.  We knock and hear barking…a dog emerges.  Ainslie tries to pet the dog…it doesn’t budge.  The owner comes up behind a few beats later…and asks him not to pet the guard dog.  Ky asks his name \- **Haggis**.  He tries to establish who we all are.  Aury interrupts to ask about the next of kin for Kelona.  He tells him that she was adopted by the Guild many years ago…and therefore the Guild is her next of kin.  He tells him that we should talk to **Lady Tarathial (Rulendor)** to arrange the service.  Ainslie says that they need to talk to Emlee \- and Haggis says no \- no one is talking to Emlee because she is in mourning.  Discussion.  Hap speaks up and says we need to talk to Emlee about the posting.  Haggis says that Hap can talk to her…that we can talk to her…as long as we are kind (gives us rules) and invites us in the foyer.  We observe that as big and nice as the place is, it is not well kept and seems too big for the few people that are here.

Lady Tarathial shows up first.  Older woman in gray robes, unbrushed hair.  Aury steps up to discuss the service. She confirms that Emlee was Kelona’s closest friend and might have words to speak at the service.  He tells her that we are trying to find out why this took place and also guide her through this process by reaching some closure.  She asks Haggis about showing us the cellars…he warns that no one has been there for a while and we may not come back.  Ainslie asks about servants.  She mentions that they are all dead or dying. Haggis tells us that they are no longer able to house the various casks because of the issues in the cellars.  The staff that have gone down have come back with abrasions and piercings and bruising and have died shortly after returning.  They just cautioned not to go in.  Ky asks about barkentea, casks, etc. \- thinking about the empty cask that held the tablet.  He walks us to the doors of the cellars.  Ky also asks if this is the “last harvest” \- something that Og overheard in the argument before Kelona’s death.

Haggis hands us keys that unlock doors to their best vintage \- Barrels of Tears (**Tears of Baltwood** \- aged, special).  They need to know if those casks are ok.  Haven’t been able to get there for harmons.  Hands Ky the keys…

## **Cellars of the Guildhouse**

These are lean to doors with steps that go down 10-15’.  Cellars appear to be well constructed, however, no sign of disturbance in a while.  We see old blood stains, mixed in with wine stains.  A putrid smell bothers us…stale and thick air.  No light.  Ky travels down a bit to cast tremorsense…senses a slight vibration on both sides.  Hap sends Fleur down to take a look. She sees movement on the left.  Then she goes to the right side and we hear a barrel bust on the left side.  She flies to look and sees a snake-like creature.  We proceed down the corridor.  Hap and Og at the front, followed by Ainslie and Ky, then Aury in the back.  INITIATIVE.

We see tentacles/vines pop up and grab pieces of barrels on both sides as they emerge from the debris.  One on each side in each room.  Og attacks one on the left and hits.  Wood flies (from what, he isn’t sure).  Ainslie casts a spell (fire) on the same one on the left. Hap runs into the left side and attacks the same one with a sword.  Aury steps up 15’ to see inside and casts a spell.  The creatures throw wood to attack Og and Hap…and miss.  Ky takes one out (the one Hap was attacking). Another attacks and misses Hap.  Og attacks the one in front…and hits it hard.  Ainslie attacks and misses.  Hap hits it and cuts it down.  He then runs to one of the ones from the right and hits it.  Aury casts a spell and the vine saves.  The vine attacks..  Ainslie and Ky are restrained.  Og is in difficult terrain and not restrained.  The other one attacks Hap and misses.  Ky breaks out of the restraint and moves into the hall and 10’ back from the vines. Og moves and tries to hit a vine and fumbles.  Ainslie casts a spell on a vine and it saves. Hap hits with his sword and again with his scimitar.  Aury casts a spell on the vine and it saves again.  Both vines attack Og \- one fumbles, is knocked prone and stands back up; the other hits him and grapples him. Ky hits that one.  Og attacks and misses.  Ainslie casts a spell on the one holding Og and hits.  Hap attacks and hits with his sword and then again with his scimitar.  Aury heals Og with a bonus action and then casts toll the dead on the vine holding Og and hits.  The vine continues to hold Og; the other one attacks Hap and hits him and grapples him.  Ky takes out the one on Og with her crossbow.  Og then attacks the one on Hap and misses.  Ainslie casts a spell on the one on Hap and kills it, releasing Hap.  

Ky senses additional movement down the corridor.  Ainslie realizes this is a **Grapevine Blight** of some type.  We see there are 5 untouched barrels left in these cells.  

Two barrels come from the corner and down the hall heading in our direction.  It is Initiative.  Og runs up and hits the barrel, but misses.  Ky shoots a bolt into a barrel and damages it.  Ainslie tries to cast a spell and misses.  Aury looks and sees some gooey substance seeping out of the bottom of the barrel.  Hap shoots a firebolt at the barrel on the left and hits it.  The barrel bursts open and large **gray ooze** emerges.  Hap runs up to be next to Og. The barrel leans on its side and tries to push Hap and misses. The ooze creates a hammer and tries to attack Og and hits, but Hap deflects the damage with his sword and the sword is damaged in the process. Og damages the barrel and ooze.  Ky takes out the barrel and another ooze emerges. Ainslie casts a spell on the left ooze.  Aury casts a spell on the left ooze and misses.  Hap uses Fleur to distract an ooze to get advantage…and attacks an ooze on the left. He hits, but the sword takes damage.  Uses his scimitar to attack and hits…it now is damaged, too.  It then hits Hap and damages his armor (-1 damage to AC).  Og attacks and takes it out.  Ky misses with her crossbow.  Ainslie attacks with psychic damage.  Aury heals Hap and then casts a spell on the remaining ooze and hits. Hap asks Fleur to fly by and he hits the ooze with his sword.  Then he hits with his scimitar and takes out the ooze.  

Aury tries to help fix the weapons and armor of Og and Hap.  While this is happening, Ky checks out the hallway and sees scores of dead bugs and two doors \- one open on the right and one closed and locked on the left.  The room on the right contains high quality wine that is all ruined.  All barrels are opened and wine everywhere.  We go back to wait for the others at the locked door.  They come.  Ky takes out the key and opens the door.  There are 4 barrels intact with ebony wood and gold bands.  Ky checks and there are no other exits.  She then calls out for Haggis…we hear him unlock the door.  He and the dog come down the hall and observe the carnage, destruction and finally, the casks that they most wanted to see. He is very thankful and takes us to Lady Terethee.

We are walked up to the house (he locks up the cellar).  We go back into the foyer and Lady Tarathial opens the doors with a tear in her eye.    

– The End of the Session – 

# **03 \- The EGD**

**Session Date**:  May 6, 2025  
**In-game Date**:  15th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  The Vintner’s Guildhouse in Emberstran

## **The Vintner’s Guildhouse**

We approach her to tell her the good news about the cellar.  She sends Haggis to get us our earnings.  Confirms that she owes us 50gp.  She offers us a carafe of the fine vintage as an extra payment \- selfish motives.  The **Cetaen** family has sold their property and they will no longer be selling wines \- they were the largest member of the guild.  She thinks there is something else at play and would like to get to the bottom of it.  A crow just delivered the message…she thinks it is authentic.  

She asks how familiar we are with Emberstran…Aury knows…that it was rebuilt when Mt. Emberstran erupted. Notable about the city is that the northern part of the city sits up higher because the old town was covered by lava…the area by the sea has flourished as the new town, while the north was settled by wealthy folks and the vineyards.  

When it was Winbalt (pre-eruption), there might be records from the land that would have been buried.  Would be valuable to find records of the original lands.  She thinks that perhaps the Cetaen family might have sold \- been forced out.  But she seems a little more emotional than just from business.

Haggis returns and hands her a bag of coins.  She counts out 50gp.  She doesn’t have any idea what she could or should pay us if we are able to find the land records.  

Ainslie talks to her about how long it will take her to be operational again. He mentions that maybe she could provide a barrel of wine to the church \- perhaps one per harmon for one year \- instead of the 50gp for the task we just completed.  She counters that she will offer one barrel, and if **we return within a harmon with the papers**, she will pay us 50gp and agree to the additional seven barrels.   Ky interjects and asks if we can also talk to Emelee.  She agrees, but says only one can go.

Ky suggests that Aury go.  He is led up by Haggis.  The rest of the group stays in the sitting area.  She lets him in \- recognizing he is a priest from the Temple of Diverra.  He blesses her and prays.  He asks her who might have done this to her friend.  Leaning in close, she says “*this is all I have from that evening*” and hands him a coin with 2 curved daggers engraved on it.  She doesn’t know what it means, other than “it may have fallen off the guy with the cloak, but it may not have.  I know that there is a lot happening in the city.  The Lord hasn’t settled on a secure watch.”  He thanks her and she says she will attend services.  He leaves and goes back down to the group.  He hands Ainslie the coin discreetly.  

We ask Lady T about how to go about finding the records.  She advises us to find **Vammad**, the captain of the guard.  He has been here a long time.  The coin belongs to the local thieves guild \- The **Etterset** (Aury recognizes it).  Ky knows this group \- says that she might need to talk to a friend when the group gets back to town.  Aury questions her about this.  

## **The Messenger**

We head back to town.  After passing through the gates, a wiry human who is a courier of Emberstran comes up to us and says he has a message to her from the Inspector.  “Please meet with Inspector Elise at the Old Oak & Grapes.”  The courier says he has been looking for us for a while…he says that he will tell her when we can meet with her.  We tell him “when the sun crests the Glimmering Sea” and he says “ok \- six o’clock then.”  He then pushes back that it is busy then, so Ainslie pushes it to 9pm.  Ky asks for his name.  It is Hernley.  She asks for Vamod’s whereabouts and he says “down by the port.”  Ky gives him a silver piece.  Then Aury asks him to deliver a message to Diverra’s temple to the High Priestess.  “A deal has been struck and a barrel of wine will be donated tomorrow.”  From your humble servant Aury (and the guild).   He asks if the Lady’s problem were taken care of.  Ky questions how he knew…and he says it is because he is a messenger and departs.

It is noon.  We head to the port area.  Og is able to locate him. Og talks to him about the evidence in the murder.  Also tells him that we are handling a side task for the Lady of the Guild.  Ainslie and Og talk to him about him “hiring” this group and/or to give them jurisdiction to help with the case.  Aury jumps in and says that Diverra is asking for our guidance…to give these two the authority.  Ky jumps in to ask about records. He pulls her to the side.  Ky tells him that they need the records from before the eruption.  He says they are the records room…which was covered.  He says that there is a hidden entrance behind the Points & Edges (smithy) that will lead to the records room…since it was buried in the city.  He says there are unspeakable horrors in this tunnel.  At the edge of the cliff, you’ll find shrubs…and the entrance.  Guards have gone and never returned.  

Ky tells him she will buy him a pint at the Old Oak & Grapes tonight at 9pm.  He will meet her there.  Aury gives him 1gp for his help.

The group splits up \- Aury and Hap to the temple; Og and Ainslie to the Warm Bear, and Ky to the Hydra vs. Charum’s.  The plan is to rest up \- since we plan to adventure during the night. Aury fills the Priestess in about the Guild (the courier has not yet made it).  They discuss using the wine barrel to help with tithing, attracting more folks to the temple.  Ainslie casts a spell before resting.  Og plans to rest.  Ky goes to the oil shop and spots Tessa.  

Ky and Tessa have a conversation about the coin.  It is something that members of the Etterset must earn and be presented with.  It is supposed to be safeguarded and hidden by each member.  She asks where it came from and Ky tells her it was found at the murder scene.  They agree that no one must know that Ky has the coin and that Tessa knows nothing of it, but that if either one finds out any information about it, they will reveal to the other.  Tessa also talks about how maybe it is time to introduce Ky to the higher ups in the organization, but mentions that intro into the group will mean danger for Ky (“the cloak always knows what is going on”).  Ky then gets her bath and goes to the Hydra and rests.  

## **The Inspector**

Everyone in the group meets at the Old Oak at 8:30 to eat before the meeting. Ky tells the group most of what she learned about the coin and tells everyone to not mention it again nor use it as a bargaining chip with the Inspector.  Aury tells Ky he is worried about Emlee’s safety as a result, but Ky convinces him that he could ask Vammad about hiring someone to protect the “murder victim’s best friend.

A halfling pops up as we are ordering food…feathered cap, burgundy coat and vest.  She says she is looking for us and introduces herself as the **Inspector (Elise)**.  Wants to talk about the murder.  Asks if we are curious how she knew of us…Ainslie says that it must be because we are part of the **EGD (Emberstran Guard Dept)**.  She knows not of this group.  She knew of us because of Meglan \- she was not happy about being questioned.  She asks why Og and Ainslie were investigating Ms. Trendrel (Meglan).  They talk about the play, how they were at the tavern, and wanted to ask everyone who were there \- but the playwright would know the most (the perfect cover).  She asks what we have uncovered.  Ainslie asks for her creds and she says she was appointed by the Lord himself.  Ainslie brings up that her sister is in mourning.  Ky mentions that Og and Ainslie saw a hooded figure that night.  We tell her we have questioned Emelee, but don’t have a motive.  She seems to have more information.  Vammad shows up as Aury says that the “Inspector was just leaving” and she leaves and he sits.  Og and Ainslie go to follow her.  Vamod says I wasn’t aware that an inspector was assigned to the case.  They lose sight of Elise after she leaves the tavern.  Og knows that she was not an investigator because she didn’t know about the EGD.  

Ky also realizes that she was not legit.  And mentions to Aury that we need to get a guard hired and up to the Guild. Aury talks to Vammad about hiring someone immediately...for 2gp he begrudgingly agrees.  He wonders if the new lady \- and uppity up \- might have something to do with all of this.  Her name is  Lady Bidva.  He says we can’t miss her.  He then leaves to find a guard to hire.

Meanwhile, Ainslie saw a marking on Elise’s neck (2 small horizontal lines and 2 vertical lines shooting up from them (like 2 upside down T’s that were arrows/points)…but only saw part of the symbol on the neck).  She also had raven black braided hair.  They cannot find her at all and head back in.  They describe the neck markings and none of us know what it is.  We look around the bar to see if anyone bears these same markings.  Hap suggests that we say “ree ga day” (Regardez) if we see this on anyone else.

## **Beneath Emberstrand**

### **Secret Entrance**

We decide we are going to find the secret entrance to the tunnel next to the smithy.  We head out of the northern district, and go to the copse of trees behind the smithy.  Area is very overgrown.  We find an area that looks like it could be the entrance and Og moves stones around…and he finds a tunnel.  Jagged walls made of lava.  Thinks he might hear water through the tunnel, feels a light breeze.  Sees cobblestone flooring…what might have been an old road. Ky looks in and sees it goes at least 30’.  There are areas that were covered in more lava than others…buildings and beams…road is fairly straight.  Moss on the road.  After about 40’, there is a broken area on the left as it curves to the right.  Nothing seems to have been disturbed for some time.  

Og and Hap lead, followed by Ainslie and Aury, then Ky in the back, checking over her shoulder every now and then.  Og carries a lighted axe.  As we pass buildings, Hap tries to determine what type of place each was (specifically looking for what might be a library or records room).  Og uses a maul to hit the door of the first place on the left. While the wood breaks, there is no opening to the room because the lava filled the room in.  We look to the right and see a passage that goes 30’ and an opening on the left and then another door to the right.  We go down to the T \- do we go left or go right (to the door).  We go to the door and Og shatters it, but there is more lava…and no way in.  We see that this passage goes to the left after 20’. We head that way.  There is shattered wood there and then the passage turns and goes north.  We see another door to the right.  This door is marked by a spear and shield.  As we get to that door we see a large crumbling to the left, 10’ beyond the door to the right.  

### **Old Guard Post**

Og busts this door down.  There are thick stone walls, rusty iron bars \- this might have been a watch post of some type.  There are still weapons in here, rotted cots, and a sign that says “Warden’s Post.”  There are manacles and a key, a **flail**, and **halberd**. We all enter the 20’x30’ room. There is an **undamaged quiver**.  We take all of these items with us.  Ky looks for a hidden door in this area and finds nothing.  

We leave and head to the area that is 10’ away…this area is a little more crumbly.  We see another door that is 30’ down on the left.  We head in that direction.  Og is looking for bodies, bones…he sees that mixed in with the lava rock are some bones that are broken up and scattered along 30’ on the right. We decide to investigate this area and move the rocks to see if there was something hidden.  We find a skeleton that is not intact.  Ainslie examines the skull and it seems newer than from the lava/eruption.  Hap investigates this area to see if there is anything to these bone pieces and sees and indentation on the north wall.  Ky and Hap look and find a secret door.  

### **Secret Chamber**

The door pushes open into a narrow chamber…walls are intact and smooth (this was built to withstand the lava).  We see a ladder that goes into a trap door in the ceiling (that leads to the northern district).  Ainslie decides to check out the opening and Ky looks at the lock and thinks it is trapped.  She disarms the trap (hears gas going the other way). Before opening it, the two listen and hear in the distance some singing and soft beats.  This is a dark room and Ky notices that the door is hidden behind barrels (these are wine barrels).  We decide not to explore the Lord’s Keep right now, but to continue in tunnels for now.  Og tries to open the door across the hall…and it busts open.  

### **Old Residence**

This looks to be a small one room habitat.  Og notices a small glint of silver and goes to pick it up.  He finds a **silvered short sword** and he hands it to Hap, who keeps it.  We leave and keep going and it joins up to where we could have gone.  There is another hallway that juts off at an angle and we follow it.  We go 50’ and turns and goes north and there is an opening \- goes into a room, as we notice a cave-like tunnel through the lava to the left.  

### **Old Store**

In the opening, we see charred shelves in a crumbling storefront.  Heavy ash, spoiled merchandise.  Og finds a **bullseye lantern** (Ainslie), a **dungeoneer’s pack** (Og) and a **poisoner’s kit** (Ky).  

As we exit this room, Aury feels something land on his back.  

– The End of the Session – 

# **04 \- Beneath the City**

**Session Date**:  May 28, 2025  
**In-game Date**:  15th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Ruins beneath Emberstran

## **Beneath Emberstran** 

IT’S INITIATIVE:  Combat vs. Giant Tick  
Og sees a “slimy tick” covering Aury’s back and smashes it off of Aury’s back.  Ky misses twice.  Aury hits it \- a little.  It then jumps back on Aury’s back and attaches to him.  Ainslie thinks damage to the “tick” might also hurt Aury.  Ainslie delivers 3 points of psychic damage to the tick.  Hap tries to grab the tick and pull him off and can’t do it.  Og tries to pull him off, too, but can’t. Ky then pulls him off and Aury heals himself.  The bug then jumps at the fighter and misses.  Ainslie attacks him and does psychic damage again.  Hap stabs the tick and hits twice and bloodies him.  Og  smashes him with his hammer.  Ky then slashes his head off with her shortsword and kills it.  Aury then flames it up after being grossed out.  

As we step out and turn right, there is a V ahead \- lava rock passage that breaks off to the left and a cobblestone path to the right. We discuss which path to take.  Og looks both directions to try to determine which might be the best.  Ky thinks no one has been in this area for a long time.  Group decides to go to the right.  Ky offers to scout.  Hap sends Fleur to go with her.  She goes down a way and tells Fleur to go back to get the others.  She comes to a fork in the path and you can go left (north) or right (east).  To the north Ky hears soft water trickling.  Same to the right.  The group catches up and Ky points out the sound.  Ainslie thinks the sound is muffled in one direction and echoed in the other.  Group decides to go right \- Ky and Fleur go first.  Water sound is louder.  Come to a grate is on the side and waist height and an alcove with a ladder going up.  This ladder is not as well maintained as the one they previously found.  Ky hears talking from the alcove \- she sends Fleur back.  She backtracks 15’ feet to meet the group and tells what she’s found.  They go back to the alcove and Ky goes into see if she can source the talking. She finds a hole behind the stairs and hears “bitch, behind this, backing the Etterset, gathering them” and Ainslie hears a few different voices and hears “**Lady Rook**.”   Voices fade and we hear a door close. 

They pull out of the alcove and clue the group in to what they heard.  There is a foul stench and Ky goes to check out the grate. We know the cultists of **Sijulous Ghone** are occupying the sewers of the town \- the grate would be a way in.  We decide to check out the ladder \- Ky will go up to check.  At the top it is dark and covered in stone.  She pulls out her lantern and looks and sees this area covered with a stone.  This is a faux stone. She moves the stone and goes up \- she is behind a building in the Northern District of the city \- this building that is surrounded by a lot of trees.  She is near a wall.  The building is a 3 story tall building \- she thinks this is the Crimson Swan.   Ky marks this area with 3 ball bearings then goes back down and replaces the stone.  Tells the group what she found.  Hap tries to figure out if the talking was from up above or from somewhere down here.  

We go back to area where we decided to go right…and now go right (to the north).  This opens into a 10’ cobblestone path.  It goes 30’ and turns right. Ky travels 10’ ahead.  She sees a very nice oak door to the left. She hears water flow through the door, sees rocks and rubble under the crack.  Og pushes the door open.  There is discussion about taking the door with us when we leave.  Two claws appear.  Initiative.

A lanky, hairless, pale-eyed creature lunges at Og and misses and at Ky and hits, as Hap fends off some of the damage she takes. Hap attacks that creature and hits twice.  Aury casts bless on Og, Ky, and Hap.  Ainslie glows purple and casts spell and hits and kills him (ghoul).  Og steps up to hit the other one and hits.  It then attacks Og.  Ky then runs in and kills it with her sword.  

We now focus on the room.  This was clearly a nice tavern.  Half of the room is standing and the other half is lava damaged and separated by a flowing stream.  We see broken bits and pieces, as well as bones.  The stream seems shallow enough to walk through (west to east) and leads to tunnels on both sides. Ky looks around (with Aury’s help) to find valuables.  She sees a fragment similar to that found in the barrel.  This is the “**BONE**” fragment.  Can’t make out the messaging on the fragment (VINE, FLAME, SEA, and BONE…we have Vine and Bone now). Hap wants to send Fleur to check out the stream.  We decide that the stream will likely lead back to the port district.  

We go back outside to check out the rubble \- fallen molten rock.  Og checks out and sees bronze protruding from the rock.  He notices a curve of a blade and a dead body with armor.   Hap is intrigued and he and Og work on the rock and pull forth the skeletal body.  There is a nice breastplate and a glaive.  We also find a pouch stuck under the breastplate with 13gp (Ainslie takes the pouch).  

We make our way back to the initial fork (near room D).  This is a natural path.  Og leads the way this time (Ky wasn’t feeling stealthy).  After a while, we start to hear water again, as the path opens back up to cobblestone and a chamber ahead \- long cavern, arched, “bubbly,” moisture and moss. We see a stream on the north end and a large door in a stone wall on the left (rusty).  Hap sends Fluer ahead to survey the area \- he says there is something across the stream \- it is a skeleton. 

We go to the door.  Ky uses tremorsense to see if there is movement on the other side.  Instead she feels lots of skittering feet on this side.  Hap sends a firebolt over to light up the area and many spider-like crabs hide.  Ainslie lights up the area and we can all see a skeleton that seems to have something hanging from it.  Hap walks forward to try to get the sheath/wand….using mage hand.  As he grabs the wand, a tentacle grabs the skeleton and pulls it into the water.  The wand has an orb on the end of it.  Hap aims a firebolt into the water where he saw the tentacle.  It is Initiative.

There are tons of crabs and a giant octopus.  The Octopus attacks Ainslie and hits.  Hap intercepts most of the damage, but it grapples and restrains Ainslie.  Og rages and attacks the Oct and hits it with his glowing great axe and knocks him prone. The crabs move and attack Hap, Ainslie, and Og.  Hap attacks the Oct and hits twice.  Ainslie casts thunderwave at 3 crabs, Og, and the Oct (kills the crabs).  Ky kills one and then falls as she tries to hit another.  Aury moves back and kills a crab.  Oct stands up and misses damaging Ainslie.  Og tries to hit the Oct and does.  The crabs attack Hap (3 misses), Aury, (1 miss), and Ky (1 hits).  Fleur tries to distract the Oct as Hap runs to attack the Oct (3 crabs try to hit \- one does) \- he hits. He kills the Oct…slices him and a little ink squirts out.  Ky kills another crab.  Aury attacks a crab and misses.  Og attacks a crab and hits two crabs and kills them both.  The crabs attack Ky and Aury and miss.  Hap attacks a crab on Aury and kills it.  Ainslie tries to mentally attack it and it takes 1 point of damage.  Ky then kills the final crab.  Hap walks around and slices up and takes crab meat. 

Ainslie wants to fetch the sheath for the wand on the skeleton in the stream.  He jumps in the water and gets it.  We then go to the door.  Ky tries to open it and uses thieves tools and realizes it is just rusted.  Ainslie uses a spell to clean up the rust on the hinges.  It takes a while, but he is able to make the door openable.  Og then pushes it open.  There is a large round chamber with an intact arched ceiling.  This appears to be a records room/archives vault.  We split up to start going through all the records and we finally find records about the plots (tome).  We also find neatly stacked scrolls…and one in the middle looks special.  Ainslie examines it \- it is a **scroll of Knock**.  

Group takes a short rest here.  The wand is a **Detect Magic** wand.  

## **Studying the Records**

Ky takes some time to go through the tome:  1\) the property once owned by the **Cetaen** was properly owned by them, but was left deeded when the original owner died to someone with the last name of **Garemon** (he was a servant of the family) that died shortly thereafter who didn’t have a will, so the Citean picked it back up.  So the reality is that they don’t own it.  Also saw where the Guild started to form and where there were write offs to contribute to the Guild.

We also find other papers and tomes that talk about inheritances and stories from the town.  There was an investigation into Garemon’s death.  He was referred to as Mr. Garemon, and Hap thinks it is his father, Benoit Garemon.  He was found dead on the streets, but no one knows who killed him…there were no other clues.  It was a mysterious death.  Hap finds out that Garemon died when the Cloak of Defiance took over the Malfease.  Garemon had a big family and apparently they were wiped out during the Awakening.  There is one person that left Winbalt who didn’t want a claim to anything in their name \- this person is Phira Garemon.  These are Hap’s kin.  He was told Phira was a cousin, but she was probably a sister of Hap.  He thinks Mr. Garemon is probably his father (Benoit) and faked his death to flee to the swamps to hide, but was eventually found in the swamps and killed.  We look to see who wrote the entry about  Garemon dying in the streets…maybe this is someone we can talk to in the town to see why and how this falsehood was orchestrated.  Clearly, Garemon wanted to flee the Cloak.  Jae Vynaar appears to be the bookkeeper from that time period. **Does Jae still live in the town**? 

As we get ready to leave, Ainslie casts detect magic.  Glaive, wand, quiver, silvered short sword, and scroll.  As we leave, Ainslie “soils” the door hinges again.  We all leave the area and head to our own beds.  Long rest.  Plan is the next morning to go to the Dyed Cane (9), eat, and try to find the Captain to seek out the old bookkeeper.

## **16th of Talil**

We all get a long rest, but in the morning, Aury and Hap gets a knock on the door at the Temple.  “Excuse me….and they open the door.  Meladra is missing.  And the rest of the priests are meeting together to discuss this and what is going on.”  

– The End of the Session – 

# **05 \- Beneath the City**

**Session Date**:  June 10, 2025  
**In-game Date**:  16th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  The Temple to Diverra in Emberstran

## **Temple to Diverra**

**Acolyte Tarley** (elf) says **Acolyte Coken** (human) and **Altarboy Arnol** have been looking…no one has seen her.  She left after normal hours (midnight) last night in full gear (service garb). Tarley tells Aury they have looked all over the temple.  They all walk to the main area.  She’s never done this before.  Aury talks to Arnol about cleaning her room…he says he doesn’t really go in there.  She has said it is off limits.  Aury asks him to check out the room to see if it is neat and tidy and he leaves.  He sends Tarley out to the gate guards to see if she left town.  He asks Coken to stay here and see if she comes back…and send word to me if she comes back.  Discussion about Aury’s new group affiliations.  He says he will be at the Dyed Cane or Bear. Arnol returns to say it looks like she went to bed and then left (it wasn't made up).  Arnol and Coken whisper about Aury fighting monsters.  They will wait for Tarley to return.  Coken dubs him “Diverra’s Hero \- Champion of Emberstran.”  Tarley shows up and says the guard said she went through the North Gate after midnight.  

Hap and Aury tell them they will look for her when they go that way. 

Ky gets up and walks around the docks.  Inquires about the Captain…and then about Jae.  They tell me the captain, Bordi, or **Devron** (dwarf owner of the Hydra) might have info.  Ky heads back to the Hydra.

As Hap and Aury head to the Dyed Cane, Aury feels like they are being followed and tells Hap.  They look to see who might be tracking them and see Arnol.  They then have a conversation…he was hoping to be a hero.  Hap offers to “bring him a finger” and that seems to make him happy.  Aury asks the kid to run his finger across the blade \- he pulls off before it cuts him.  They then agree to train the kid once they find the high priestess.

## **The Headless Hydra**

At the Hydra (**Leanna** \- hostess, **Jasline** \- barmaid, **Macie** \- housekeeper).  Ky talks to Jasline \- why have you not introduced me?  He normally is there around lunchtime; he lives in the north district.

## **The Dyed Cane**

Ky heads to the Cane.  Ainslie and Og go to the Cane.  Bordi asks Aury about his barrel. Ainslie and Ky step in to ask about rumors.  Ky asks about Jae \- he lives down at the docks.  She asks what he looks like (old, thin, blotchy skin, faded vest with ink spots, big drinker).  Ainslie asks him how much the barrel would be worth that he needs to replace the one that held the tablet.  Bordi says someone lost their vineyard.  Ainslie and Og talk about refilling the carafe… Bordi has offered to refill it for free. 

Hap talks to Bordi about Phira.  He tells him that Phira was Benoit’s daughter.  Hap reveals that he is Benoit’s son…and Phira’s brother.  Ky asks what he knows about Benoit.  Brody says he was tight with the **Cetaen** family.  Hap says he is looking for Phira.  Brody hasn’t seen her in ages.  Brody says Benoit was killed in town.  Brody talks to Aury about his wine patronage.  

We look through the papers and Hap realizes that he saw people kill his dad a stretch after the papers reported he was killed in town on the streets.  Something is fishy?  We need to find Jae.

Hap tells the others about the missing priestess.  Group decides to head to the docks.  Ainslie finds his contact and asks about Jae.  He points the way to Jae’s house.  

## **Jae Vynaar’s Place**

The group arrives and Ainslie knocks on the door and Jae starts talking to the group about tea time.  The group helps him get the tea ready.  It is cramped. Hap pulls the book out and flips to the page about Benoit.  Jae is glad to see “his book.”  Hap asks about the murder of Benoit Garamond.  Ainslie talks in his head “tell him all you know.”   He says:  Benoit had a lot of money and a lot of influence.  He helped the Cetaen’s keep their vineyard \- he loaned them the money to keep it.  Hap asks why he recorded that Benoit died \- who told you to do it?  He says he doesn’t know who told him to record it.  Hap tells him we have reason to believe that Benoit didn’t die that day.  He says that it could be true \- perhaps someone would have wanted to kill him so they wouldn’t have to pay him back.   Various conversation.  He doesn’t think Benoit loaned money to anyone else.  Ky asks him about the Cloak and he claimed to have no knowledge (but Ky knows he does).

He asks about the current recordkeeper and find out it is Doit Torwell.  Ainslie asks him about a female with burgundy cloak and feathered hat…he says that she has tea on the 4th day (today is the 5th day).  He says that she was asking about the “new people” in town \- are we they.  He mentions that he often gets paid for “tea.”  Ky gives him 1gp.  He then mentions that the Lady Rook is probably the one that is taking over the Cataen family land.  She is a visitor to the tower…and that Imonorra never leaves her tower (she is a reclusive wizard).  He says that if we see the lady in town she might give an invite to the tower \- to the likes of Aury (an uppity up who is a local).  Hap asks him if he looks like Benoit…and Jae doesn’t recollect.  He also asks about Phira and he doesn’t know.  Ky supposes that “what if Benoit had an heir and she showed up to make claim to the Cataen land” and he asks Jae to walk with him.  He tells her that he was asked to change the records to show that the land shifted back to the Cataen’s upon Benoit’s death since there was no heir.   He was asked to change the records by the old Lord (who is now dead)...he was influenced by either the Cloak of Defiance or the Vintner’s Guild.  

Ky shares with them what she learned as they walk to the north.  Hap shares that the new Lord came to power \- appointed by the people \- as the town was changing after floods, etc.  He was a prominent figure and was anointed.  His name is **Garrod Dusklight (Lord Protector of Emberstran**).  

We discuss options of what to do.  It is now lunchtime and we see a cart heading from the north down to the docks.  After it passes us it stops and the driver yells at our group \- “excuse me.”  And he knocks on the cart and someone jumps out and walks over to us. This person asks “are you the one that is looking for the priestess” to Aury.  She would like an audience with you at the Tower tonight.  The note is signed by Lady Rook.  

Group is going to talk to the Guild lady as Ky goes to talk to her contact.  Lots of strategy about what to say and reveal…as Ainslie reads her mind.  

## **Charum’s Oils**

Ky goes to Charum’s Oils to find Tessa.  She talks to her about what she overheard in the sewers.  Tessa tells her that it sounds like the Cloak was talking.  That Lady Rook is a strange person of some influence \- she’s had a huge impact on this town in a short time.  Lady Rook arrived at the town, involved in trading at the dock for odd antiquities.  She seems to be leaning toward supporting the Etterset.  That said, the Cloak has provided stability to the town \- good and bad.  And their reach is all over Myrdae.  But that for the Etterset to gain ground in Emberstran, they will need Lady Rook’s help.  Ky asks her if she knows anything else about the murder.  The Etterset seems to think this was a distraction \- but not sure why yet.  Ky asks if she has heard of Benoit and she has not but will inquire quietly.  She says she is going to set up a meeting between Etterset leadership and Ky…and will leave word of that with a note from Ky’s “brother” Seanathan at the Hydra.  Ky then leaves to walk to the Old Oak & Grapes.

Meanwhile….the rest of the group is heading to the Guild. 

## **To the Vintner’s Guild**

Ainslielee notices on the way that some of the vineyards aren’t looking so hot.  So the issue isn’t entirely the problem in the cellar.  He investigates the problem with the vines, and while the death of the vines looks natural, he is uncertain about the cause.  Essentially there are some spotted leaves among them.  Disease?  Soil?  Curse?  He asks a nearby worker about the condition.  He says the condition looks bad, but hopes it will go away, but seems a little guarded about it.  Off the record, (person is named Nonee \- they call him Picker Picker)...After throwing him a gold piece, Magus is his boss, Noni goes to town on information, most irrelevant, but has never seen this problem with the vines that started maybe only a stretch or two. Ainslie convinces him (with a silver piece reward) to give him an update at the Bear should he discover anything else about the crops or rumors of the land being sold. He mentions finally that there was an issue about “not getting paid for the work they’ve been doing” (maybe something Magus said?).  

Group then heads on and Ainslie and Og tell Aury and Hap what they learned. Ainslie agrees to tell things to Hap telepathically \- he “has to get used to this.”  They find Haggis.  He notices that Ky is missing and Ainslie tells her they want to see the lady…and will tell her about the recon work that Ky is doing at the moment.  They  go in and Lady T eventually comes down the steps (Ainslie casts Detect Thoughts spell just before her arrival…out of sight…he gets it off unseen).  Lady T comes in.  Ainslie starts to talk.  Says Ky is away looking for records. Ainslie says that 

Ainslie mentions the Cataen family and that they are being forced off the land (she thinks:  No \- they are not against me \- they are helping me)

He mentions Benoit (she does not know the name)

She asks what Ainslie has heard rumors about (she thinks \- this guy is messing with me; no \- maybe he is right; maybe there is a bar guy who knows something and maybe I can get something out of this and can work to stabilize the lands again).  

Ainslie asks her about the record keepers \- she said that would be The Collector.  He then mentions that he knows who it used to be. (she thinks \- who do I know that keeps records).  The current collector is the one I pay my taxes to. 

Ainslie says if the land does not belong to the C family then are you worried about who it might belong to (she thinks ?  (Chip sent a private message)...Ainslie says that she doesn’t want to have her job taken from her…doesn’t want to lose what she built up).  

Ainslie asks if she knows who the land is being sold to and she says that someone is trying to displace HER in the Guild.  She is revealing this because he has proved trustworthy and because she wants to continue the success of the lands.  She thinks someone is coming in to corrupt and disrupt and she is worried the town will suffer.  (she thinks \- I’ve busted my ass to be here and I’ve done very well…I want to keep working here).

Ainslie mentions Lady Rook and she says that she’s heard of her.  Aury asks if Rook works for the Lady in the Tower?  She says I do not know if she works for Imonorra…I do not know Imonorra.  Does the Lord know all of this?  Do you think she is tied to the vineyard purchases?  Ainslie says that he’s heard that rumor.  Lady T calls Haggis over to whisper to him.  Og catches odds and ends but can’t make out what she says.  He then nods at her and leaves the room.  Finally, Ainslie asks her if she has seen the Head Priestess.  She says she has not seen her.  Ainslie believes her.  She asks if the payment of 50 gp and a barrel of wine for the church is enough \- she offers 75gp.  Ainslie says that the initial payment was good.  They then ask about the rot and she says she doesn’t think anything is out of the ordinary.  They then ask to purchase an extra barrel of wine (for Bordi).  Cataen family was responsible for 15% of wine coming out of the city.  Og and Hap thank her for her help and dispelling/confirming rumors to get them on the right track with their investigation.  Condolences to Emilee as well….and that the group is still working on the investigation.  Lady T walks the group to the door…as Hap asks one more question “You supply Bordi with his wine, right?  Did you know he got a barrel that had no wine in it?  Thought it came from you.”  She offers to send him a new barrel in exchange for the empty one if it came from her.  They said they will ask Bordi where he got that barrel.  Ainslie casts detect magic as she is closing the door on them.  He picks up Abjuration on the door.  He also picks up on Abjuration on Lady T.  And he sees Evocation magic as she pulls away.   Ainslie tells the group about the magical door.  And that he doesn’t think she is good or bad…but is true to her purpose.  Lady T doesn’t know who is buying the land from the Cataen family.   They head to the Old Oak and Grapes.  

## **Priestess Maladra & The Grove**

As Ky is walking to the Old Oak and Grapes, she sees a robed figure…it is the Priestess.  She confronts her and she says things aren’t as they seem.  Ky offers to talk to her.  They go to the temple.  The priestess reaches out to Diverra and asks for help.  She is rambling.  She says I hope I have not caused alarm.  I found the wisp and she spoke to me.  “She (the wisp) knows of her (Diverra) presence” Diverra told the wisp that this is the ultimate sanctuary of all followers, but there is an issue at hand.  “You can’t get to it(…the sanctuary).” The wisp says it is guarded.  All she could find is that in her quest, the one **Blackrend**, prohibits us…he has taken the sacred grove and in so, has captured the wisp.  Diverra spoke with her in dreams as she was escorted to the sacred grove. If we can pull this corruption/darkness from the grove that poisons the land, then we can find where Diverra wants us to praise her. Ky asks her where the Grove is.  She tells her with landmarks and Ky feels like she can find her way to the corrupt grove.  Ky says she is on her way to meet Aury and will tell him immediately.  Ky then heads on towards the Old Oak.

Ky meets the group and tells them about the priestess.  There could be a link of the rot in the land tied to this corrupt grove.  Tells them about what she learned from her contact.  

We decide to clean up to prepare for the dinner at the tower.  Ainslie and Og go to the shop to buy clothes, sells Ky’s cases.  He buys Ky a white cloak.

## **Imonarra’s Tower for Dinner**

Group heads to the Tower (it has a rectangular base).  Path there is winding, dark, has strange appearance here and there…arcane items, shifting leaves, etc.  We knock at the door and a familiar face answers \- Dathreil Runewish at your service.  Sorry if I’m uncomfy \- this is uncommon b/c we don’t usually have guests, but the Lady’s guest invited you.  Come in.  

The room is a foyer and we are escorted to a lounge area just before the dining room.  A platter is set before us with small jars, glasses, a decanter, various charcuterie items…plates and napkins as well.  He asks for our affirmation of if this spread is ok.  We say yes and Og grabs for food and drink.  He removes himself…says She will be with us shortly (he won’t confirm which she \- guest or mistress of the tower).  Aury blesses the food and drinks.  Aury, Og, and Ainslie eat.  Hap and Ky refrain for a minute \- waiting to see if anything happens to the others.

After a few moments, we notice a robed figure coming toward us (“hooded lady”).  She pulls back the hood and we see a large feline head with the ears pointing backwards.  “Thank you for joining me.”   Ky thinks this is not a mask.

– The End of the Session – 

# **06 \- Diverra’s Grove**

**Session Date**:  June 24, 2025  
**In-game Date**:  16th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  The Tower of Imonarra in Emberstran

She welcomes us.  Ainslie and Og ask about her cat head.  She says she is a Tabaxi.  She says that she is here to acquire lands \- after dancing around a bit \- she says she has purchased the Cetaen land.  Hap tells her he knows of a rumor that the Cetaen’s do not own that land.  She laughs it off, mentioning other “rumors” in the town, which Ky challenges are all true.  Og then chimes in that it is their business to know the truth of rumors as part of the Emberstran Guard Department (EGD)…which she questions (and Ainslie can’t convince her).

She says that she doesn’t want to mince words…and talks about how the town’s primary trade is “wilting under the town’s leadership.” And how the town needs a new steward who has the vision to see through the “rot.” Then she asks us directly to help the town replace Lady T with her.  She looks around the table and specifically mentions areas where some of the group can help her \- by persuading the Lord, the priestess, others in the guild/around town, etc.  She even offers to make things “right” with Hap with the farm his dad brought to prominence.  Aury seeks clarification on her wants.  Ky brings up that maybe Lady T might not be at fault, but that the corruption could be from someone sabotaging her or the guild itself \- asking if she knew of the murder that is associated with the guild.   We ask her why she thinks she is the right one to fix the problems with the guild. Og asks her what she is going to do about the rot.  She reaches for a case…noting that what is happening to the grove is not natural…and holds up a scroll that is a ritual (from Adamont) that is ancient and requires a focus (life-focused) to use.  She also pulls out an egg-shaped crystal (quartz, milky threads) and holds it up (strange hands) saying “this is the marrowstone, an artifact of convergence.”  Use the scroll and remove the blight and you’ll know it works when the stone changes.  She hands these items to Og…use these with the key and cure the lands.  Hap and Ky question why she wants to be the Guild leader.  She responds that it is the most powerful role in the city.  

Og reaches in his pouch and pulls out the “key” he has (it is a leaf encased in thorns).  Ky asks how she knew he had it…and she says she will not reveal her sources.  Aury knows that the wizard took in Lady Rook…and the wizard is the Lord’s right hand (she is the Magistrate).  We ask if the Lord has signed off on the change of Guild leaders…and she says no…but it would be in his interest to make sure the Guild prospers so that the town prospers.  And again, heroes of the town might be able to influence the changeover at the Guild.  Lady Rook is interested in power.

Ainslie tries to talk to the group in our heads about “do we have what we need from her or should we group together to discuss?”  Aury tells her that he enjoyed meeting her, but that we need to discuss this as a group.  She then reaches for the scroll and egg from Og…and he gives it to her \- slightly begrudgingly, as he says “we will be back.” We ask if she is going to seek help from others \- she says she, like us, will not be doing nothing.  Og asks if we should just come back to the tower to give her an answer.  Conversation ensues and she drops hints that she knows where we are staying, etc.  Ky asks her if she’d like to continue their conversation briefly after dinner (a nightcap in her words, a little pussy in Aury’s words). 

Hap questions how she knows about his connection to the farm.  She responds that she hears rumors as well and that she had talked to Jae.  He then directly asks about Phira…and she pulls out some parchment and writes her name on it (saying she is not aware of her).  

The group then leaves, except Ky \- all agreeing to meet the next morning at the northern gates.  Ky then asks her about her involvement in the Etterset…and/or the Cloak.  The docks, the missing Kelona, the crops \- are not just coincidence.  She admits that the Etterset have helped her today and that she doesn’t need us, but wants us.  She tells Ky that she will help Og, Hap, and Aury…with their issues.  

She tells Ky that we need to check with the priestess before we head to the lands \- they were hunters' lands \- but have not been hunted in a while. 

As the group leaves, they talk about everything revealed at the dinner.  They ask Og about his key…what it means.  Aury questions how she knew so much about Og, Hap, and Aury…as well as details about our doings in the town.  Ky catches up to them and shares what she learned.  The group discusses whether or not they trust him.  

The group thinks curing the land has no down side.  A land with no blight will help everyone.  Ky suggests that she forge the ownership document (making an exact copy) and give those to Lady T to see what happens with her.  Everyone states their case.  Ainslie is concerned that Rook is too good to be true.  Ky and Og want to proceed with Rook…for now.  Hap and Aury are neutral.  We decided to meet here in the morning.  

As Hap and Aury enter, the priestess pulls Aury to the side as soon as he walks back in the temple \- did Ky get in touch with you?  Are you going to address the problem?  He reveals that we just came from a meeting at the tower and may have a way to dispel the magic in the grove.  She replies that “Imonorra has a way to help \- that is great.”  She says that she may also have something to help you.  Aury says no acolytes.  She says she will get a potion from the vaults that might help….and to take holy water as well.  She knows that Diverra is speaking to her through the wisp.  She says to follow hunter’s trails, follow the “feather,” try not to get lost,  look for the clearing with the wisp. I know this is the first time hearing about the vault…and I know this is your calling.  I will bring you the potion…or send it via the acolyte.  Aury says \- no \- you can bring it to me.   Asks is there anything else?  Aury gets vials of holy water and gets a regular Potion of Healing from the priestess.

## **The Warm Bear, Emberstran**

16th of Talil

As Og and Ainslie walk into the Warm Bear, they see hunters around in the bar.  They walk up to approach someone who looks like a hunter to ask about the areas to the north. Ainslie creates badges for he and Og to show the hunter.  They approach him and flash the badge.  We are on urgent business. This guy is drinking heavily.  He comments that if this is secret it is going to cost you and Ainslie says this will cost him.  Ainslie says the Lord will take care of him.  Asks him about the Southern Baltwood…and he says it has been corrupted by a blight.  He hasn’t been there in a while. Ainslie asks if any of his buddies have been there recently.  Another guy walks up. Og asks him about the corruption and he says “he’s got a feel” of how bad they are. He says it is dark, says he didn’t find the “**dralg**” (a native creature to Myrdae \- the size of an ape, very hairy, large head and fluffy tail who walks on 2 legs).  They discuss the hunting grounds.  Guy asks if he is buying the next round \- Ainslie says the Lord Protector will provide, as Og hands him some coin to pay him.  They ask if there is any other information \- he says the trails are marked, but he’s unfamiliar with the markings.  Other creatures?  Yes \- stag, boar, lots of beasts.  Not sure about unnatural creatures.  There are hunters who have gone and not returned…so hard to say what might be there.  

## **Returning to the Tower of Imonarra**

Ky goes to the Tower to collect the artifacts.  Rook freely gives them to her with a warning that Og will need to be protected while he performs the ritual. That we need to make sure that he can complete the ritual.  She doesn't know how long it will take. Ky did not promise anything else…other than that we need to be careful with the egg and return it to her when we collect the blight. She then leaves, heads to the Hydra, and goes to bed.

## **17th of Talil**

The next morning, the group leaves and and meets at the tower.  Ky tells them that she collected the items we need.  Aury reveals that the trails will be marked and that we should follow the “feather” trail.  Ainslie and Og reveal that there are beasts in the woods, including a dralg.

## **Southern Baltwood forest**

The group heads out to the northern gate, and heads to the Northeast…to the Southern Baltwood forest.  As we pass the vineyards, we are talking so much that we don’t pay attention to anything there.  We follow the winding road of the Kahlbit Trace. We see trees with dark moss, find a collapsed campsite…broken tent, fire ring.  Wooden marker at the edge of the clearing and a narrow trail.  We follow the trail and come to a T where we can go right or left.  One way is a feather, the other a frog.  We go to the left to follow the feather.  Ainslie wants to adventure and take all the paths…we say we will AFTER we cure the blight. 

Path goes deeper. Gets darker.  We come to another fork.  There is a spiral to the left.  To the right, we see a feather.  We go right. After a bit, we reach a shallow stream.  There are fish in the stream. Og steps in the stream and twists his ankle in the stream (his movement is restricted).  Og ties the rope off on the other side and Hap holds it on the other side.  Ky crosses with no issues.  Aury doesn’t want to get wet, but he also goes across with no issues.  Ainslie goes across, holding the rope \- his ankle catches, but he shakes it off.  Hap then brings the rope with him and is able to cross without issue.   Aury looks at Og’s leg and realizes he needs to be healed…but we continue forward.

## **The Wisp**

Trail comes to a clearing.  Flashes of light flicker in between the trees.  Footprints are visible.  A pulsing light glows ahead and heads in our direction.  We step into the grove.  The wisp heads toward Aury and he hears a voice \- it is like a memory “you bear her light, the blight binds the way, you must cleanse it…so the path may be revealed.”  There are three paths that lead from the clearing.  Aury asks the wisp, which path to take?  It doesn’t respond, but goes to Og as he is pulling out the tools he needs.  The wisp stays there a few minutes, then vanishes, then goes up to the center trail, then vanishes, then shows up again behind us.  

## **Ritual Site**

We head down the central trail and come to another clearing. Mt. Emberstran is in the distance.  We see a rock face on the far side and a rose carved into the stone on the ground.  Hap sends his owl up to check out the surroundings \- Fleur sees wilting plants, venom-esque, dark surroundings.  Ky casts tremorsense on the stone and feels no movement.  Og looks at the scroll and feels like he can say them \- even though he won’t understand what he is saying.  It doesn’t reveal all the words all at once…but will reveal as it goes. Og steps on the rock and pulls out the tools and starts to read.

The egg and key start to shake in his hands and the ritual begins. Og feels warm, radiant energy.  Gusts of winds swirl around all of us.  The words are not familiar to any of us.  The rose outline on the carving starts to glow.  Air darkens and black mist starts to rise and seep on the ground and we are attacked. 

IT’S INITIATIVE  
Ainslie attacks with a spell and kills one. Hap attacks and misses.  Aury uses channel divinity and hits and kills one.  It then attacks Og, but Aury imposes disadvantage.  Ky then kills one.  Og continues to read.  Ainslie attacks the remaining entity and hits.  Hap swings and misses.  Aury attacks with a spell and hits it.  The entity attacks Ainslie and misses and then attacks Aury and misses.  Ky runs over and attacks it and kills it.   We each reposition ourselves for additional attacks if needed.

The rose glows brighter, but the relief is brief.  We hear a sinister laugh from the cliffs above and figures form in the air \- they smell and the mist forms into bodies that pop up.  Ainslie attacks and hits and it laughs at him (these are darker and more evil).  The trees are starting to flutter.  Hap attacks and misses and his owl will give Ky advantage.  Aury flashes his holy symbol and they take radiant damage.  The creature attacks (with disadvantage) Aury and misses.  The other attacks Ainslie with vomit…and hit and he is poisoned. Ky attacks one and hits twice.  Ainslie attacks and hits and then moves next to Hap.  Hap attacks and hits and knocks him prone. Aury casts a spell on the one next to him and hits and then casts healing word on Ainslie. One attacks Ky and hits, but the damage is minimal.  The other vomits on Hap and hits and he’s poisoned.  Ky attacks and kills one.  Og continues to read.  Ainslie attacks and hits.  Hap attacks and misses and heals himself a little.  Aury shoots a bolt at the last one and hits. He tries to attack Og and Aury imposes disadvantage and he misses.  Ky then kills the last one.  

Another round to gather, heal, etc.  A crack emits in the air and a blight champion arises next to Ainslie and his reaction hits and does great psychic damage.  The creature calls forth a rod/mace/scepter and attacks Hap and crits with a claw.  Then misses with the mace and Hap evokes hellish rebuke and damages him.  Ky runs up and hits with her short sword.  

Ainslie torments it and makes it run away, as Ky gets an opportunity attack and crits it, killing it.  As it falls, the last of the blight is banished and a mist lifts and clears into the crystal. Ainslie grabbed the scepter off the dead body.  A single white petal falls from the cliff and falls onto the stone, which is now silver.  It radiates.  The crystal is dark ebony with dark gaseous forms inside.  The scroll is now covered with gibberish.  The leaf/key is now relaxed.  A symbol of Diverra now sits in the clearing.  Og steps down.  Aury takes his medallion and lays it on the altar, stepping on it.  All of our bodies swish towards the stone and sucked on top of the stone, as it vibrates.  

– The End of the Session – 

# **07 \- Divine Waypoint**

**Session Date**:  July 9, 2025  
**In-game Date**:  17th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  The Southern Baltwood Forest

## **At the Platform**

The platform rises…and we peer out over the forest to see the last of the blight resolve, smelling roses. The platform rises up against the 80’ cliff…stops at 60’ above the woods. There is a jagged ledge…and Aury steps forward on the ledge and walks into the chamber.  We all follow. The cave's circular room is carved with vines and roses.  

At the far end, a female head (Diverra) statue looks down and there is an arcane design etched in the floor (it is an Arcane Circle).  Ky tries to see if anyone has been here…doesn’t think anyone has in decades.   Ainslie thinks this is a teleportation circle and thinks it can be used once you learn the sigils.  Aury drops to his knees and prays to his goddess \- “should I step into the circle.” Hap walks around the small cave, looking for secret doors or other passages.  He notices the vines and roses in the design of the circle are tied to the room…different from other circles, but doesn’t know how to use it.  He notices a specific rune in the center of the circle.  Aury reminds the group that we were told that the path to Diverra’s sanctuary would be illuminated if we helped with this task.  He thinks that maybe this is the circle that leads to her sanctuary \- or at least one step of it \- that we would need to use the circle, with a priest of Diverra here, to use it.  Hap notices a smudge on the statue of the priestess and points it out to the group.  Og goes to remove the smudge and finds another rune similar to the one on the floor.  Aury thinks we could likely use the runes to travel.  

Ainslie pulls out the mace/scepter to show Aury to study.  It is longer than a normal mace…more akin to a quarterstaff, but has mace features.  In the blades of the bronze-colored mace is a soft blue orb. It has transmutation and enchantment magic.   Hap talks the group into taking a short rest.  The pedestal starts to lower as we rest.  Og goes out to jump off and once he steps on the ledge, the pedestal rises again.  Hap heals.  Aury studies the mace.  

Ky joins Og on the ledge. They notice that the woods look like Diverra’s arms embrace the canopy of the forest.  We notice a large waterfall to the left and see a path that follows the river we crossed. To the right, is a mirror copy, except the waterfall is smaller.  We see beyond these woods on the left (beyond the river), the vegetation looks different (sparse trees).  Cliffs are tiered. 

After a short rest, Aury feels this is the Scepter of Adoration, and Hap feels better.  Og and Ky share what they observed from the ledge.  We climb on the pedestal and head down.  Once we emerge, we decide to go to the east. \[Aury knows he has the ability to use the sigils…it is a level 1 spell\]

We find the path that follows the river and head northeast toward the waterfall.  Forest thins as we approach the falls.  The falls emerge from a 50’ cliff, as we see a jagged path up to the side…with a ledge about 25’ up…then a jump to the other side of the falls to another ledge.
