Ky is enthusiastic to climb up and does.  When she does she sees there isn’t room for multiple people on the ledge.  She hammers a rope into the wall….and it makes a very loud sound.  She pounds a few times and stops.  Hap asks Fleur to scout around since this made a loud noise and make sure nothing is approaching. Ky decides to stop hammering in and jump to the other side…to see if there is a good reason for all of us to keep going.  She jumps and makes it and looks back and sees a dralg about 300yds away on the tier of the cliff.  It is a slippery landing…and she notices a niche and a carved in spot, clear of moss and a spot of silver deep within.  Ky yells to the group about the creature, as she tries to grab the silver that she sees.  She pulls out a silver bird-like figurine with quartz eyes and drops it in her bag.  She then looks around for a hidden entrance and finds nothing.  She then jumps back and is caught by the falls, taking damage.

At the bottom she shows the figurine, as Ainslie cleans and dries her up. She tells them that maybe there are other items up there, but not sure. Aury thinks maybe it was just a random hiding spot.  Ainslie thinks the figurine is magical, but Ky tells him to wait in case they find other items.  Hap sends Fleur to scout to see if there is a hidden cave behind the waterfall \- there isn’t.  Fluer thinks the creature was a “mama.”  

We decide to head west and try to explore all paths on this side of the river.  We see the path heads northwest.  Fleur flies up to check out the other falls…to see if it is worth trudging through the forest off the path to get to the falls.  She notices Rongri in the trees around here, but nothing around the falls (no ledge, nothing in the pools).  We decide to head through the forest and towards the falls.  We see the Rongri jump out and glide and watch us, making some noises.  Ainslie tries to talk to one….using Animal Handling.  Ky notices that the creatures stop chirping…and Hap feels something land on his shoulder.  Ky notices and tells him not to swat it.  It wraps himself on Hap’s horns.  Meanwhile, Ainslie walks forward into the bank of the pool and opens his eyes to see them all look at him.  Ky tries to talk to the one Hap “hello wee beastie” and feed it a ration…which is doesn’t want.  Ky eats it.  It then glides over to Ainslie and flies and then glides over the water and walks on the water and then slaps at the water.  It then gets Hap’s attention and runs up a tree and glides and leads us down a path adjacent to the river.  We all follow Hap.  This appears to be the only Rongri leading us somewhere.  

When we get to where the path crosses the water, we hear loud water (it is wide here and difficult to cross)...and see a path of slick stones we might be able to use.  But we also see a whirlpool in some shallow rapids that the Rongri points to \- is it alerting us to danger or a place it wants us to see. Fleur flies over the whirlpool and sees bones.  Og decides that he wants to jump in the river \- tied to a rope which we tie to a tree \- and investigate the bones.  Og jumps into the water and successfully dives in.  As soon as he does, the rongri jumps on Hap’s shoulder and looks at the body.  Og sees a skeletal body, a skeletal primate body, and an amulet on the body.  Og grabs the skeleton and the amulet.  He gives the skeleton to the rongri and he takes it and walks on two legs and bounces back towards his camp.  He then shows the group the amulet \- which is golden/bronzy rosary beads with an eye-shaped pendant with a rune on the pendant.  Hap looks at the rune \- it is arcane for the word “secrecy.”  Og puts it on, but doesn’t notice anything different at the moment.  

We decide to use the stones to cross the river.  Og starts, using the rope…slips and dives into the water and swims to the other side.  He emerges and now we can use the rope to cross.   Ky tries, slips and falls into the water…and is pulled under.  Og dives in to save her.  Ky feels a hand reach in to get her and takes her to the other side.   Ainslie goes next and is sure footed crossing, twirling as he goes.  Hap goes next, holding the rope.  He slips and falls, but uses the rope and Og is able to pull him up.  Aury catches the rope Og throws and tries to cross and slips as well…and Og pulls him up.  We are all now on the other side.  Aury dries himself, as well as Ky and Og.  Ainslie dries off himself and Hap. 

We follow the path now and come to the place that would lead back to the clearing…and take the other route (not to Emberstran, but centrally).  We follow the frog path. The path winds around begins to thin out…leading to a marshy clearing.  There is a chest-high fog here.  We see mounds of dark, slimy vegetation and thin walking paths…this is just a game/single-file trail.  We hear a splash or two ahead.  Ainslie casts detect magic (transmutation magic on the figurine, and abjuration on the amulet).  We go right around the circle/swamp.   Ky picks up a long staff to poke with; others make sure they are armed as well.  Hap leads with his trident.  We travel a quarter around the circle and hear a splash \- there is a path to the right here \- but we continue on the circle.  We hear another splash…

IT IS INITIATIVE:  Combat vs. Swamp Creatures 

* ROUND ONE:  Og touches Ainslie and gives him temp hit points…then moves up to stab one near Ky.  Hap attacks and crits, then pulls out another weapon.  Ky then misses \- natural 1\.  She then jabs her stick in the ground and pulls her dagger for the next turn.  Ainslie attacks with a spell and hits one.  Aury casts burning hands on one and hits. They then attack: the first to Hap, who fails his wisdom save, and is incapacitated (sleepy); another becomes a whirlpool and attacks Og, who passes his strength save, and takes half damage and is pushed away; and the last one who turns into a whirlpool and attacks Aury, who passes his strength save, and takes half damage and is pushed away.   
* ROUND TWO:  Og gives Ky temp hit points and then attacks the one on him and hits.  Hap fails his wisdom save again and is now unconscious for a minute.  Ky then kills the one on her with her short sword…and steps up into the swamp to attack the other with her dagger and hits, breaking its concentration, which wakes Hap up.  Ainslie casts a spell on the frog next to Ky and it fails, taking psychic damage and must run away.  Ky and Hap both miss their opportunity attacks.  Aury then attacks the one on him with his new mace and misses.  One attacks Aury and he fails his strength save and takes damage and is grappled.  The other guy moves back to the area and attacks Ky and Hap with a spell and they save.  
* ROUND THREE:  Og gives Aury temp hit points then tries to attack the one on Aury and hits and knocks it prone.  Hap stands up and throws a firebolt at the one in the swamp and hits.  Ky then gets advantage from Fleur…and hits and kills one with her dagger.  Then moves into the swamp with a dash to retrieve the dagger on her next turn.  Ainslie attacks the last one and hits. Aury is grappled by the last one…and casts an inner radiance which will cause damage to creatures within 10’.  The creature turns back into a frog and Aury is no longer restrained…but Aury, Ainslie, and Og need to make wisdom saving throws.  Og saves, but Ainslie and Aury are incapacitated…and he tries to hop away.  Og hits him as he tries to escape and kills him.  

We are out of combat.  Ky grabs her dagger and loots the body and finds 3 daggers \- gets 6 more from the others.  We feel these are fey creatures (kinship with the wisp).   We follow the circle and head north…following the path and it comes to a T so we travel east.  We know going that way is a trail created by game…Og can tell there has not been a hunter in a long time.  He knows that we can go hunt creatures or not.  Ainslie is eager to go that way to talk to the creatures, so he becomes invisible and tells us to wait and he forges ahead to check them out. 

Ainslie follows a narrow trail that ends at the base of a sheer cliff.  There is a cave ahead…with gouged soil, scattered bone.  He walks up to the mouth of the cave and sees a massive beast inside with two smaller forms nestled against her.  He looks around the rest of the cave…and notices bones, fur, rubble, and places where the creature dug underground.  He goes to a dirt pile and scatters it…catching the attention of one of the kits.  Ainslie finds a large bone…not sure what animal it is from.  Bone elevates in the air and the kit lunges at it, just as it disappears.  It then goes back to its mom. Ainslie tries to find a place where he would not be detected…behind a rock.  He is trying to be stealthy, while keeping invisibility.  He looks for bigger cover and there is another large rock.  He starts to leave the cave and the small kit starts walking to the face of the cave.  He is sniffing the air and scraping the ground…like he knows Ainslie is there even if can’t see him. Ainslie steps just back inside, hiding behind the rock, inside the cave and casts detect magic with his wand.  As he whispers, the invisibility drops and he sees the kit next to him looking at him.  The big bone in his hand has magic.  Ainslie pulls out rations to feed the kit and uses animal handling to hand it to him…and then walks out of the cave, hearing noises behind him…the kit is following him.  He throws rations to the cave mouth and the kit chases it to feed…as Ainslie runs back to the group.  

We ask what happened and he tells us and we ask about the magic on the bone (abjuration).  He tells us he befriends a kit.  

We decide to backtrack and then follow a trail to the south. It is twilight…we walk that way.  We then decide to head to Emberstran and the sun has set. We discuss that we are going to head our separate ways \- keeping a low profile.  

Aury and Hap go to the Dyed Cane to eat. Bordi asks Aury about the tablet \- any leads?  Aury says we are getting closer to having an answer, but that as far as the wine goes things are going to get better, but he won’t reveal all of the details yet (isn’t going to spill the beans). Bordi gives him each a glass on the house to start their meal.  Aury gives him 1gp for food.  Then they head back to the temple to turn in.  

Ainslie and Og go to the Bear to get a meal and pay for another night of lodging.  After dinner, they go to their rooms to study the amulet (Og \- it is an Amulet of Proof Against Detection and Location) and bone (Ainslie \- it has a circular crack all around it). Og gives Ainslie the amulet and Ainslie give Og the bone…and it pulls apart.  This is a casing holding a scroll.  He hands it to Ainslie and they find it is a Protection Against Beasts scroll.  The bone is an easy way to hide something.  Ainslie holds the scroll and casing and amulet.  

Ky heads to the Hydra \- checking for mail, grabbing a bite to eat, and settling her tab for another night.  She stops at the desk and asks if there are any messages from her brother.  He tells me that there is a message for me from the girl at Charum’s Oils \- “she’d like to talk to me about the Lady.”  Ky tells her it must be in reference to her massage appointment.  She pays 4sp for food to be sent to her room…and goes up to her room.

As Hap and Aury arrive at the temple, they notice the altar boy outside swinging a sword and he says he’s ready.  Aury tells him when he can wield a sword without cutting himself he will be ready.  He wants to accompany Aury on his missions. NAME???? As he opens the door to let him in, the acolyte says “she is waiting for you,” as Aury flips him a gp.  He sees him run and put the coin on the altar of Diverra and yells thank you to Father Auruleius.  They then run into the Priestess who asks if they found the wisp.  Aury tells her about the area, the statue of Diverra.  She asks what he learned and he says “the quest for the temple has just begun,” and tells her about the circle.  She says that she can provide him a scroll of Arcane Waypoint (which is the spell Activate Sigil).  She says she will go down into the reserves to fetch a scroll and will have the altar boy deliver it in the morning.  Aury then mentions that he is carrying a sword…and probably shouldn’t have one without training.  She asks who he recommends to be his trainer.  She says she is part of a Council \- and asks which of the captains he thinks they should pick.  Aury replies that he should use Captain Vamod (at the docks) to learn that he is properly skilled…and always keeps his eyes and ears open around town for the truth (since everyone is not always truthful).  She says she will send him in a day or two. She thinks between she, Imonorra, and the Captains they can keep the town safe.  

### **18th of Talil**

Og and Ainslie wake up and leave \- heading to meet us. Hap and Aury are told about the Reservoir.  Then walk outside.

Ky stops by the desk of the Hydra and then steps outside. All of us are met by carts at the same time…told to go meet with the lady.  Will you get inside?

Og and Ainslie ask if this is important…and their courier pulls out a scroll and confirms he is looking for them.  They say they are not the ones Lady Rook is looking for…Ainslie trying to persuade.  They do and the courier apologizes for interrupting and they walk away.

Aury and Hap ask who is asking for them.  He doesn’t say who but says he will take them to the Tower.  Will they go?  They say they have things to take care of…and he says he will return and tell her they are busy.  He asks when they will arrive at the Tower and Aury says they will figure it out and will not need a driver.  The driver confirms that they will show up later and leaves.

Ky confirms that this is a cart from Lady Rook and asks if her friends are going to be picked up and then jumps in and is carted to the tower.  \[Aury and Hap see 1 cart ride by\].  Ky gets there and sees people talking at the doors and is told your friends are not here.  She questions that, but plans to enter.  He opens the door and sees Lady Rook reaching to hug her.  

– The End of the Session – 

# **08 \- Cemetery Mystery**

**Session Date**:  July 16, 2025  
**In-game Date**:  18th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Various Locations in Emberstran

### **Meeting with Lady Rook**

Ky receives the hug, as Rook tells her she is not surprised to see her first \- she expected it.  They go to the top floor of the tower.  Domed ceiling, floating lanterns. Can see the entire city from this vantage point.  Polished table in the center of the room with moving symbols on it.  Asks Ky about the forest. Ky gives her most of the info (but not all \- no mention of the hidden chamber with the arcane waypoint).

She gives Ky a potion of invisibility \- in thanks. Ky asks about her standing with the Etterset…which Rook says is good.  Ky brings up Hap and his land petition.  And also about his dad’s death and the mystery around it \- was it the Cloak?  She talks to her about politics \- and she asks about Ky’s family (and drops that she knows her real name \- which Ky quickly “corrects”) as the door opens and a silver-haired lady comes in.  Imonarra enters and needs to speak to Lady Rook.  Apologies are made as Rook invites Ky and companions to dinner she is hosting at the Old Oak & Grapes at twilight…with an interesting group of like-minded individuals.  Ky leaves…in search of her friends who have not yet arrived.

### **At the Temple**

Meanwhile, the others all meet up and talk at the Temple. Discuss that a coach and driver showed up.  They notice as they talk that some people walk by and are whispering \- clearly talking about the four of them. They talk about how good they all look.

Aury feels a tug on his cloak and it is the acolyte..who tells him that people in town are talking about them (forest cleared for hunters, helped with the vintners’ guild, were seen in the tower the day before).  Aury tries to coach him and tells him that he is going to be sent twice a week to the captain of the guard (Vermond) for sword lessons. He is excited to hear the news \- he’d love to be like Hap someday.  Hap, pleased to hear this, creates a minor illusion to show him what he can look like.  Ainslie, not to be outdone, gives the boy money (10gp) to go to the market to buy him a red outfit.  Aury tells him that he needs to stay on the path of Diverra…as the lad says he will spread the good word about the EGD.  He starts to walk away and then realizes he doesn’t have his blade so goes back to get one.  When he comes back, Og says “Scarlett Knight \- here you go and gives him a hand ax.”

They hear someone snickering nearby…big guy with leathers, grungy, with a bow and sword.  “You must be the ones who lifted the blight.” Word travels fast because the hunters want to get back into the woods.  He talked to Lady T…and told her that he saw the group heading into the forest shortly before the blight lifted…and presents a scroll from her in thanks:  “Keep it up, Emberstran is proud.” 

Ainslie opens the scroll.  “To the champions…guild owes you gratitude.  Requests presence at the Suncrest Cellarium this early afternoon (after lunch) to give thanks.”  Aury knows that this is a special place in the hills near the vineyards…only people in the Guild use this place/get invited usually to taste fine wines, etc.  He knows this is a big deal.  About that time, Ky shows up. 

Ky shares what happened at the Tower.  They share what happened in the Temple.  Discussion about whether or not to trust Rook.  Og questions the arrival of the blight on the crops vs. the arrival of Rook.  We find out that the blight arrived after Rook arrived.  The Priestess told us that the blight was from Blackrend…and we don’t know who that is.  We know we put out this instance of Blackrend’s influence.  

More discussion.  Ky tries to convey that we shouldn’t close any doors at this point with Lady T or Rook.  Group goes to the Dyed Cane for breakfast…with Ky and Ainslie stopping by her room on the way for a little while (with the land records we gathered for Lady T about the vineyard in question).  Ainslie brings up the need to have more attractive clothing to wear to the festivities today.  We decide to go to the market before breakfast. 

At the market, we look for a clothing shop/tailor.  He recognizes us as the Heroes of Emberstran and offers us a 10% discount.  Ky pays Ainslie 7gp; Ainslie pays the tailor 24gp for a white tux for Hap and a green form-fitting dress for Ky.  Ky also buy a green cloak for 2sp.  Aury buys a silk robe for 12gp \- white priestly robes with Diverra red and roses.  Group sees the acolyte in the market spending the money on the items he was supposed to buy.

Ky takes Og to Charum’s Oils.  The rest of the group goes to Dyed Cane. Ky asks for Tessa \- talk of our efforts.  Tessa says we need “Bru” to fix up Og.  Ky leaves Og there and goes to the Dyed Cane the others for breakfast.  Bordi welcomes “the talk of the town.”  Aury mentions we are there for breakfast and Bordi says he will set the group up.  Bordi asks us about if we learned anything about the fragment…and we told him we are still working on it.  Aury mentions the murder and Bordi says he heard that the lady was killed because she knew who was poisoning the wine \- but could just be a rumor.  He mentions her friend in the guild…but unsure if there is a connection there.  Either way, the talk is that whoever poisoned the barrels is probably behind the murder.

Ainslie talks to everyone of their “metal” colors, piercing Haps’s horns, etc.  Aury brings up to Bordi about our invitation to the Cellarium…and he is surprised we got invited…and would go dressed like we are.  We tell him we have acquired new clothes.  Aury more concerned with who might be there \- guild members, nobles, perhaps the Lord himself…but Bordi doesn’t know.  Ainslie asks for a private table…Bordi doesn’t grant it freely so we tell him nevermind.  We discuss plans \- Ainslie brings up perhaps bringing up the “dead body under the Lord’s Keep” to the Lord if we see him today.  We decide to mention it as a “rumor” and to have Ainslie get his reaction.  Ainslie would like to go back down there…but we decide to wait and see how the event goes tonight (are people otherwise engaged, do they get drunk, etc.).  After breakfast, Hap goes back to the Temple.  Aury goes to pick up his robes.  Ainslie and Ky go to Ainslie’ room.  Og wraps up at the Charum’s Oils and goes to pick up his armor.

As Ky and Ainslie walk in the Bear, people stand and clap…thanking them.  They are hunters…and eager to get into the woods. When they get to the room, Ky gets the documents from Ainslie.  She copies them \- making a forgery of the main documents \- to give to Lady T (what she asked for).  The Caetean family borrowed money from Benoit to get out of debt.  Two days later, Benoit is dead.  Ky forges the documents perfectly and Ainslie is able to “age” them as well.  Ky then changes her clothing.  Ainslie changes. Og comes back to the Bear \- he feels magical and is glowing. 

At the Temple, Hap changes into the tux. Aury goes back to the Temple and puts on his new robes.  We all meet at the Temple and then head to the Cellarium.  

### **Suncrest Cellarium**

We head out into the vineyards…and the Cellarium is made of glass and golden stone up on the hillside. We are met with the aroma of wine, candle-lit chamber.  Casks along the walls.  A half-elf welcomes us and takes us to Lady T who welcomes us and encourages us to try the various wines.  She expresses her gratitude and beckons us to sit and talk. Aury walks around to sample wines.  

Lady T asks exactly how we rid the lands of the blight.  Ainslie talks to her about how we did a lot of research, acquired an ancient artifact that helped us, Og’s ritual.  Ainslie leaves out any mention of Lady Rook.  Lady T then asks Aury about extra details…and specifically mentions that she thought we visited a grove of the goddess Diverra. Aury asks “exactly what do you want to know.”  Lady T was so upset last time…and now things are looking up so she just wants to know what happened.  Aury gives her a few additional details…that we were in the grove, that Og performed a ceremony, perhaps to Diverra…and that with Diverra’s guidance we were able to rid the blight.  She introduces the half-elf as a sommelier named Vargaro.  She then brings up the issue of the Caetean family and Ky tells her we got her the records she needed.  Ky gives them to Hap, who gives them to her.  She then reads and says that “Benoit” might be the rightful heir to the lands.  Then she sees that Benoit died.  We then tell her that Benoit had heirs.  Hap tells her that he had a daughter named Phira (Ky mentions he had a boy, too).  

Lady T, after some thought, seems to recall that Phira passed away a few years ago (maybe in 1241…5 years ago)...and is buried in Crael cemetery.  Aury thinks that Lady T is unsure of her story, but is being truthful. Hap asks what Lady T would do if we were to find the heir \- esp. since Benoit and Phira might have been killed over the lands.  She leans in close and says she’s not sure because she’s building a case for how she can keep these lands out of the hands of the person who is trying to buy it.  Aury, drunk, says to Hap…so if your “sister is buried in the cemetery…” and Ainslie tries to cover with the use of the word “sister” by a priest.

She says there are votes that don’t support me out there.  My stewardship may not last long in the guild, but she asks us for our support to help her keep her position.  Ainslie brings up Lady Rook…and she claims she hasn’t met her…but that Rook is the one who wants to buy the lands. Hap brings up “are these records” what you wanted us to bring you.  Ky brings up the fact that the lands could be owned by the heir…and that wouldn’t necessarily influence who is the steward of the guild.  Lots of conversation about Rook (Lady T thinks she is nefarious), the lands, whether or not the Caetean family is good or bad, etc.  The patriarch of the Caetean family \- she heard \- may have had Benoit killed.  Ky asks if Verellen is a member of the Cloak.  She says that the Cloak has been here a long time \- before Emberstran became what it is.  She may have even had dealings with the Cloak herself, because she had to.  She offers to pay us double for the records \- she gives us each 25gp for providing the records. We tell her that we will do some additional research about the heir.  Ky asks her what she would do if she was displaced.  She doesn’t know. We thank her for the hospitality and leave.

We walked back to the Temple. Talk of keeping Hap’s identity a secret for a little longer. We talk of going to the cemetery to see if we can find Phira’s grave…and even speak of “speaking with the dead.” We discuss who we are going to support \- Lady T or Lady R or neither.  Ky knows that the Cloak are the “hammer” in this town \- not just the hired assassins.  We decided to go to the cemetery to find Phira. Ainslie would like to stealth around the Lord’s Keep to look for any connections to evil or any major influences (Cloak, Etterset, etc.).

### **The Cemetery**

We change and then go to Crael’s Cemetery.  On the way, Darn (dockhand) runs into us.  Is it true \- you guys are responsible for so many good things in this town?  Ainslie says yes.  Darn says that this info was handed out by the Town Crier this morning (Ainslie confirms that the Crier is given news from the Lord).  Vamod comes out and talks to Og \- “great job \- I knew you had it in you to be this helpful.”  He asks Og (and the group) to join his team to keep the streets of Emberstran safe.  Og declines \- says he is “rocking with this crew” now.   We continue on.

We make our way into the cemetery and the guards ask us why we are there.  Ainslie says on official EGD business and asks if they will keep folks out…and they said they will try.  Ky asks about who might know where folks are buried and they tell us that Guy, the groundskeeper, will know.  We decide to split up to look for gravestones.  Ky finds stones for Phira and Benoit…and next to them Eldermer Caetean. Ainslie finds Guy…his name is actually Rono.  Ainslie compliments him on his work. Rono starts to tell him all about his craft.  Ainslie asks him about burial procedures.  Rono says he is the one who digs the holes, maintains the grass and grounds (no weeds).  Rono blames the cultists for the discoloration of the grass (Eradic of Ghone).  Ainslie then leads him over the Phira’s and Benoit’s graves.   Rono says the body in Benoit’s grave does not have horns.  Ainslie pays him 1gp.  He finds out that Eldermer’s body is buried in Benoit’s grave (he says he was paid to put that body in that grave).  Ainslie pays him an additional 1gp. Rono says that the Phira grave is actually empty…no one is buried there. Ainslie paid him another gp.  Rono says that he is paid to do various things and always has been. Ruthless Jerik is the one who paid him back then to bury folks where and how he did.

### **Jae is Dead**

Ainslie then walks over to the group and tells us what happened.  Ky asks Aury if he knows who Ruthless Jerik is.  He does not, but knows that people have often referred to a “” as someone of influence in the town.  On the way to dinner, we swing by to see Jae. He is not there.  Ainslie opens the door (which is unlocked).  We see bloody footprints and Jae’s body on the ground.  We see Jae has been stabbed in the back.  Ky determines the footprints look like someone who is 3’ tall…and stride seems to match the prints.  Ainslie starts to investigate the scene and finds that some books have been disturbed.  He casts detect magic and senses “transformation” magic hidden underneath the floor near where Jae previously made tea (when he was still alive the first time we visited).  He finds a trap door and finds a small wooden box with 15pp inside, as well as a small vial \- crimson liquid that pulses like a heartbeat.  Og left to find Vamod….and comes back without him.  A few moments later, another captain comes in \- what are you doing here?  We tell him that we went to report the murder of Jae (this is the captain of the SW).  We ask him to stay on scene until Vamond arrives.  We ask him to leave us out of this until we solve the murder.  Aury asks if he will make sure the body is delivered to the Temple of Diverra.  Ainslie says \- or you can take him to Guy. 

### **Dinner with the Lady**

We make our way to the Old Oak & Grapes.  As we walk by the Temple, we see the acolyte outside.  Og encourages him to do push ups and we keep going.  As we arrive, we are met by an attendant who shows us in and guides us around through a narrow archway behind which is a tapestry of a weeping dryad. There is a room here with a chandelier and a table with goblets that are filled with wine.  Lady Bodiva Rook is here (black silk dress).  “You’ve come.”  Lots to discuss and great wines, too.  Folks here are:  **Ehkel an’Davo** (dark hair and skin, polished leathers).  Before we start, do you have the stone?  Og says it is here and takes it out…but says he has questions first.  Ky asks who Master an’Davo is \- he is a traveller.  And she asks if she can make sure that the blight is in the stone.  She looks at it.  “You’ve done something remarkable here…it may be more potent than I imagined.”  She is interested in this stone.  Aury thinks that no one is getting out of here without her having that stone.  She reaches to touch the marrowstone and it flashes an indigo, brighter than we’ve ever seen before.  Ainslie surroundings suddenly disappear…he sees 2 people:  a golden mantle, familiar to him with spells arched around him in flame, the other dark robed adversary (flail with magical stars) as he hears “bound, bound and not broken”.  Lady Rook didn’t flinch at the flash.  “It is what I expected \- shall we eat.”  Ainslie feels like the vision connected him with the man. 

– The End of the Session – 

# **09 \- Sides & 3rd Piece**

**Session Date**:  August 11, 2025  
**In-game Date**:  18th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  The Old Oak & Grapes in Emberstran

### **Dinner with Lady Rook**

Ky asks \- what was that flash?  Lady Rook-perhaps something to do with the blight \- to that end, perhaps we should let Imonarra study it?  Og says yes \- that would be good, we aren’t smart enough to figure that out, but perhaps we should give it to her ourselves.  Lady Rook \-I detect a little mistrust, but that would be okay with me.

And with that dinner is served \- rich foods, very nice, nice red wine.  Ehkel decides to entertain us with a story.  Trailport \- breed fast, spirited horses.  Ghost Gray  \- was a special horse who would watch the road like she was waiting for something.  Then pauses \- Hap \- you look familiar \- but maybe not.  And he continues.  The horse followed him for 10 miles…but in the morning, she was gone.  He proposes a toast \- to things that follow trust and others that follow silence.  Ky then makes a toast to odd friends \- and talks a little about how she and Og became friends such that she followed him to Emberstran.   He asks for additional stories and no one speaks up.  So then he asks us where we plan to go after dinner and we talk about where everyone is staying.  He is at the Anchor Point (docks).  Ainslie then asks how old he was during the Awakening (Ehkel is now in his 40s).  He says around that time he was in the Mahruud.  Og asks what brings him to Emberstran.  He says it was Lady Rook.  They met a decade ago \- he says Rook is reliable. Hap brings up how Rook brought up that she knows something about Hap’s family \- did she mention that to you \- is this a good time to talk about this?   

Aury interrupts to tell a story about a merchant named Phalen in another town. He tells that Phalen did not trust spell weavers.  And notes how several magic wielders have taken interest in town…politics, costs of things around town, other conflicts…and the mages had been the mayor’s advisors. Phalen went to the mayor and mentioned how magic should serve the town…vs. serving but a few.  Ehkel comments that Rook has chosen friends well.  

Rook then asks Og where he got his thorn leaf.  He tells how he does odd jobs and on one job was drawn to a cave…where he then found this special leaf.  Ky comments how it was fated.

Ehkel then talks about he was chasing a stag and found a green-eyed woman,  Dandelia of the Veil in Kahlbit’s Veil (name always changed).  She told him that the forest keeps it own keys.  And Og’s story made him think of that.  Maybe she would be able to help you figure out the origin of your leaf.

Ky asks him if he is member of the Advents of Harmony?  He says no \- he is more spontaneous.  And then says that makes him think of Hap again.  And how familiar he looks.  Is he sure he has never been to Trailpoint.  He then talks about a lady who looks very much like Hap who he met in Trailpoint.  This woman said she impulsively left the city to buy the inn in town.  Ky says that it was the Shimmering Stag Inn…she’s been there before.  But she doesn’t remember the proprietors of the establishment.  Ky comments that she is sorry she can’t place them since this could be her sister.  

Hap then asks Rook what she knows about his family.  She comments that the documents indicate that he and his sister are the rightful owners of the land.  

She then says if we choose to support her, that property that she purchased could find its way back into Hap’s hands.  Ky mentions that we have documentation that would show that the land already belongs to Hap.  She thinks that would be great \- and thinks that Hap should try to find his sister.  Ky then shows Rook the documentation (the copy that she made)...and Rook says that is wonderful.  She says that we should meet with the magistrate to formalize that the land belongs to Hap.  Again \- asking for our support.

Hap brings up that we met with Lady T.  And Rook talks about how she is of the old ways, but that she helped us stop the blight.  Hap says that he just hates to see bad things happen to either one.  Ky then asks why she doesn’t set her sights on higher leadership.  And she doesn’t want all of the responsibility. She wants to help the Lord with trade, raising an army…making things better.  

Aury is skeptical of everything.  Hap tries to be neutral. Ky asks what will happen to Lady T if we support Rook.  For example, would she make sure that Lady T still has a position of some importance?  Rook says she hasn’t thought about it, but would hope that Lady T wouldn’t be vengeful.  

Hap asks where the wine is from.  This establishment always gets the oldest and best.  Hap wants to know which vineyard.  Ky asks where an’Davo is heading \- he had already said across the Glimmering Sea but didn’t say where and asks us for recommendations.  We have none.  Og asks if he ever found the horse \- and he never did.  

Ky escorts them out.  Asks Rook if she can help with a favor…and asks her if she can ask Immonarra to send a message to the proprietor in Trailpoint…trying to find out who she is (a family member wants to find her).  Rook says that she will help and hopes the group will take her favor to heart.  She then tells an’Davo that he should visit the dwarven areas to the SE of Mount Emberstran.  He catches her meaning and asks if he should take a guide…and she says to look her up and perhaps she can take him.

Ainslie disappears after they leave and follows an’Davo to Charum’s Oils…and then comes out to talk to him.  He asks if he saw the flash when Og showed the stone to Rook.  He said he just saw the flash and nothing else, but Ainslie reveals that he saw a vision. \- and he told Ainslie not to tell anyone else the vision, and that this is something that has been played out before.   And he asks an’Davo’s advice on what to do.  He suggests that he finds an archivist that he can trust to get more info about that vision.  He also told Ainslie that there is probably someone in the town who already knows who Ainslie is…even though he hasn’t told anyone.  But he doesn’t say that is Lady Rook.  He thinks it is probably someone else. 

They make it to Charum’s Oils.  An’Davo walks up to one individual and asks for a private session from a petite female human (Tessa) and offers to pay for a session for Ainslie \- asking that he gets the Pearl Oil and a female tiefling (Kia) shows up to help him.  She applies Pearl Oil (looks like inside of oyster) to Ainslie as she tends to him….his skin has a glow of that when finished.  “You must have a friend \- this was a gp for this special oil.”  She asks Ainslie who An’Davo is \- is he new in town?  She seems to know Tessa.  Ainslie says he is in with the nobles…and she says that makes sense.  Ainslie asks her not to reveal what she has seen under his clothes and tips her well.  She says she will keep the secret. 

Hap writes a letter and gives instructions to Fleur:

Instructions:  Head to Trailpoint and find the Shimmering Stag Inn.  Spend a day looking for a Tiefling woman that resembles ole Hap.  If found, give her the letter and wait for a verbal response.  Otherwise, return.

| If you are Phira Garemond, you have a brother who would like to meet you.  Simply tell this owl whether or not you are Phira, and if so, if you would like to meet him. |
| :---- |

Hap and Aurelius discuss going back to the portal to see what is on the other side.

Aury writes a letter to the lord …

| My Lord, With all due humility and in the service of truth, I feel compelled to speak of Lady Rook. I would not presume to judge her heart, for only the gods may see its true depths. Yet I must share what has weighed upon my spirit. This lady has come to our city but recently, and in that short span she has gathered wealth and influence with remarkable swiftness. She is often found in quiet counsel with those who guide our halls, her words soft, yet persistent. I have no proof—only a growing unease—that her ambitions may stretch beyond what has been spoken. The Vinery, I believe, will not be the last of her requests. I fear that, should she be granted it, her reach may continue to extend, perhaps beyond what serves the common good. Moreover… though I lack any evidence, I cannot banish the troubling thought that she may be connected, in some way, to the blight that has afflicted our lands I pray I am. mistaken. Yet my duty compels me to place these concerns before you, so that wisdom may guard where certainty cannot. May the Light guide your judgment,Father Aurelius Valeheart |
| :---- |

Ky asks Og and Hap what they thought about the information about Hap’s sister. 

Hap says he has never met anyone who would buy land and then give it away.  Ky tells him that there are people like that \- those who are so wealthy that they just look for an entry into a town.  Discussion about the land and Hap would be happy but okay if it doesn’t work out.  He is going to write a letter and send it with his owl to Trailpoint.  Plans to send it tomorrow.  Ky mentions that she didn’t want to show the original document to Rook (or anyone yet) and Aury catches this and asks about it…which Ky says it would be best if he didn’t know.  Og talks to Hap about Phira and what it might mean if he finds her.

Meanwhile…Ky goes back in and Aury hands her a sealed letter addressed to the Lord, asking her to take it to her contacts to make sure it gets to the Lord.

Discussion about what to do next \- visit the portal of Diverra and investigate further (Hap and Aury).  Ky mentions that we need to decide about the Rook vs Lady T issue.  Ainslie comes back in with a tale about why he was gone for a long time \- leading to him going to the bath house.  He says that he has decided to support Rook \- better to back the stronger horse, than the one limping to the finish line. Og said that even if he felt a little used by Rook, she scratched his back so he is fine to back her.  Hap says he doesn’t understand why anyone would value his opinion.  Aury doesn’t see anything wrong with Lady T, but thinks Rook is seeking ambition above her station.  He wants to support neither. 

Back to what to do.  Take stone to Imonorra…ask Rook about solving the murders…give Aury’s letter to Lord. Ky goes to Charum’s Oils.  Aury and Hap go to the Temple. Og and Ainslie go to Jae’s house to investigate.  

**Ky** \- sees an’Davo come out with Tessa and he recommends her to Ky…then pays for Ky.  She asks Tessa about Rook.  Tessa said the an’Davo thinks that Rook was supposed to be the hit at the show that night.  And that the hit was the Cloak of Defiance.  She says that “the whisper”  did it (via poison in a drink).  Whisper is a small female who disguises herself.  Whisper likely killed Jae as well.  As a member of the Cloak, they don’t want any changes in the town.  So they would want Lady T to stay in her position.  Tessa liked an”Davo \- great stories (lost caravan, etc.).  Ky leaves \- taking nothing \- heads to bed.

### **Temple**

Aury looks for the Priestess and finds her lighting candles.  She is so happy to have heard the stories and says that she has something to give the “champions of the Ardent One.”  She gets a key and leads Aury down to a place in the temple he’s never been…large oak door (Hap went to bed).  This is a vault with magical sconces, lots of scrolls, banners, many rose items.  She goes to a low chest (large) with the sigil of Diverra on it.  She opens it and inside is a breast plate that needs to be inscribed with Diverra’s new symbols.  It is polished and silver and warm to the touch.   This will help with any additional efforts for your assistance with Emberstran.  Aury tries to get out of taking it, but she insists.  Aury noticed a broken piece of tablet in a bag on a shelf.  She sees Aury looking at it.  “That old thing \- it has been here for years, and it seems important so we kept it.”  She says that Aury can take it with him if he takes the breast plate.  And I hope you have more strength at the Silver Spaulder than I had….I hope your prejudices do not pass on from me.  She hands Aury the **third piece of the tablet** wrapped in cloth.  Aury heads to bed.

### **Jae’s house**

Og and Ainslie show up \- EGD \- here on investigative business.  What do you know \- where are the reports.  Vamod has them \- his team investigated.  Why do you need to be here now?  We had other business to attend to.  They try to talk the guy into leaving.  They follow the small footprints down the road and off the edge of the dock.  They ask about another record keeper…and are told the Magistrate (who is in the High District). They discuss how the murderer probably fled to the sewers to escape. They then go back to the Bear.  Innkeeper \- the hunters are back in and I’ve saved your key…they are coming back because of you guys.  Some discussion about the price of their room, saving their room, etc.  They go to bed.

## **19th of Talil**

Everyone wakes up.  Aury goes to see Hap with his new breastplate and also shares about the tablet (FLAME SECTION).  Hap is excited to see the tablet.  They want to share with the rest of the group.  Aury looks for Arnol the Acolyte…but he isn’t there.  

Ky gets up early and walks to the Tower. Datheril greets and gets Rook.  She asks them to convey Aury’s message to the Lord.  Imonorra will take it today.  She then tells her that she and some of her companions will support her.  And finally she talks about the Whisper and solving the murders.  Rook conveys that it will take some time for Rook to out the Cloak for those crimes.  Rook asks that we stand down the investigation of the murders. But also says that if Hap is upset and seeks vengeance against the Cloak…let her know how she can help.  She then offers to take Ky to Imonorra.  They go to the top of the tower.  Pardon me sister \- and Rook then asks for her help to get Ky to Trailpoint to validate.   Imonorra takes her via a teleport spell to the Spinning Stag Inn.  Ky goes inside the Inn and finds the owners.  There is a human male and a tiefling female.  Ky approaches the female and it is indeed Phira…Ky tells her everything.  She is very scared for Hap…says that he needs to leave.  Ky tells her that she is aware of the Cloak threat.  And that Jerik is her husband’s brother.  And gives Ky a ring and an apron to take to Hap….says to return with the ring someday.

Bear \- the Town Crier is there announcing that the blight is gone.  Trade is strong.  Coin is high.  Vendors are happy.  Stop by the Temple…lady luck.  Sailors mock him.  A man (ordinary in dress, but uneasy) slips up in the crowd and stands next to Ainslie (you feel it don’t you…he says…the weight of being seen even when you wish you weren’t.  Carrying things that don’t belong to you.  We don’t want to hurt you.  If you tire of being a vessel of their lies come find the silence beneath the stones…the ones who understand).  He carries a bone carved token dangling from a thin cord \- cult representing Sijulous Ghone.  Ainslie knows that Eridict is a group of cultists of Sij Ghone…and feels like he was just recruited by them to join.   Ainslie knows that SG is an all powerful immortal wizard.  Ainslie tells Og what is going on in his head.  Og watches and sees the guy go into an alley…and looks back to see if Ainslie is following him. He knows that the two are tracking him.  They go down alleys to the east of town…and come up to an area in the shadow of the cemetery.   A boarded up building with cracked shutters and an iron gate is there…they need to go around back…if interested in joining this cult.  Ainslie knows that going in alone is probably not the best idea…unless he is prepared to join.  Ainslie asks Og not to tell anyone about this recruitment (pinky swear).

As anyone is heading to the Temple, folks in the crowd acknowledge the “champions” as they pass.  Og and Ainslie notice Arnol in the dock/merchant area as they pass.  Ainslie mentions the new cult to him (a secret mission was revealed to him that morning since he is magical…and asks him to keep a secret or his head will explode).  Ainslie asks Arnol to join this organization, too (they will be brothers)...tells him to join and learn everything he can.  Arnol agrees to this and Ainslie gives him money to buy a dark cloak.  Ky arrives at the temple first and is super excited to talk to Hap.  

Ky arrives at the Temple \- tells Hap everything, gives Hap the ring and apron that she got from Phira.  Hap is stunned that not only does he have a sister, but that she is alive.  Ky notices the breastplate.  Aury then shows her the tablet.  Ainslie and Og show up and Aury shares the news of the tablet with them.  They then share that they learned that the tracks at Jae’s house lead into the water.  Ky then shares all she learned about the murders and Cloak. 

Discussion about everything.  We all agree that the Cloak is bad.  Agree that the Whisper needs to be taken care of privately; that we cannot take them out publicly.  Blight has a weird association with the thorn leaf (which we can talk to the druid about).  

* We need to go into the sewers \- to find the Whisper.  Crimson Swan/moveable stone is a way down. Ky says she can find out about the sewers.  
  * Magistrate might also have information about the Whisper (but we don’t know what her association is with the Magistrate).  
* Find the fourth piece of the tablet (has something to do with the Eradic of Ghone; cultist group clue from early on).  
* Go to the Magistrate with Lady Rook and the documentation about the land.  Then follow up with Phira in Trailpoint.    
* Go to the portal of Diverra/sanctuary.  
* Follow up with the druid about the blight.

Ky presses her connections and gets a map of the sewers.  Group decides that we use a room at Bordi’s place tomorrow to meet and look at the map.  We see the map of the sewer system.  

– The End of the Session – 

# **10 \- The Sewers**

**Session Date**:  August 20, 2025  
**In-game Date**:  19th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  The Dyed Cane in Emberstran

### **Inside the Dyed Cane**

Sitting at Bordi’s (Dyed Cane).  Lella is our server.   We eat, decide to head to the sewers \- to find the Whisper/Cloak and question about the murders (of a girl at Old Oak and Grapes, Jae) and to look for the fourth tablet.   We decide to go to the Temple of Diverra to look for a sewer entrance to avoid having eyes on us entering the sewers since it is morning.  Aury isn’t sure where the sewer entrance might be…but also didn’t know about the vault.

We go to the Temple and find Acolyte Tarly…asking her about the sewer system and vault (she knows of neither).  We ask for an audience with the Priestess. Meladra comes out and hears our request…and tells us there is a locked door that leads to the sewers.  Hap explains our needs; Ky tells her this is the best way.  She tells us that she will have to lock the door behind us.  She leads us into the vault area and moves a rug which uncovers a hidden door.  Then she goes to a lock box and asks us to turn away…we do.  Ky hears the shuffle of coins in the box and she looks for the key.  She comes over and offers to unlock the door, asking Aury to stay here by the door in case we come back.  

### **Into the Sewers**

The door has not been opened in a long time (makes a sucking sound).  There is a ladder heading down.  We notice pipes from sewage that dump into the sewers (the smell is also horrid).  Aury will stay and the password will be “The Flower Blooms”...for him to reopen it with the key.  

Ky starts down and starts to gag, but is not impacted by the odor (poisonous).  Ainslie makes nose clamps for the other three.  Ky sees slope as she gets to the bottom of the ladder.  We will have to trek through muck.  Hap goes next \- also okay.  Ky gathers her bearings \- knowing she needs to go north.  Ainslie and Og then come down \- they are okay, too.  Ky and Hap stay about 40’ ahead of the other two. 

### **Rats**

As they head north and reach the T, they see/hear rats.  IT IS INITIATIVE.

Hap goes first and crits, taking one out.  Then Ky kills one.  One attacks Hap, and hits.  Then another attacks him and hits.  Og dashes and things happen. Ainslie goes, things happen, a rat dies. Hap goes, misses, hits though does not kill, hits again and kills. Combat ends.  

Ky looks into the alcove where they came from \- there is a skeletal creature (chewed up) in there.  It has been a few days at least.  Ky opens the pouch \- it has coins (3gp, 12sp \- splits on the spot).  Ainslie cleans it up for Ky.  Hap looks in the backpack…there is a tender box, 4 torches, 2 flasks of oil (split all on the spot).  Ky decides to crawl into the rat tunnel to check out the remaining scattered gear, looking for clues who this person was. She finds a lock picking kit (which Hap takes), bedroll in pieces, another piece of cloth…then a nest of bones and two babies (which she kills with remorse).  On the cloth \- which is a cloak \- she sees the symbol of the Cloak of Defiance.  She backs out and shares what she found.

### **Lockjaw & Dust**

The group then proceeds to the left to a dead end, finding nothing.  The group goes back to the T and heads east.  Nothing much to find other than holes that drain into the sewers…and an occasional grate that allows daylight to filter in.  Come to a T \- to the left is an immediate dead end (large storm drain) and dead end to the right…so we follow the path east again, until we encounter a circle.   Flow of sewage is slow…there has been no rain lately.  Ky and Hap notice a flicker of firelight to the right side of the circle.  Ky motions to Hap to be still…that she will try to stealth that way (and telepathically tells Ainslie/Og to still).  Ky sees a guy in a hood/cloak with a lantern.  This is Lockjaw.  He sees Ky (draws a bow on her) and the two converse.  Ky tells them that she is with companions looking for their “priest” friend.  He says he is looking for a friend, too.  Ky tells him she found someone and that she will take him to him.  His companion Dust shows up…and Ky takes them to the dead body (encountering her companions along the way).  They confirm the dead body was their acquaintance (Smiley). 

Ky introduces the group…but uses Hapenstance McGrady (McG) for Hap’s name.  They tell us to turn around because Jerik won’t be happy we are in the sewers.  Then they mention Quill \- is a “she.”  The Black Quill…to us.  Ainslie asks about the Whisper.  We know that they know of Quill.  They say they are going to tell Quill we are down there.  Hap tells them to hold on…that we need to see Jerik.  They say that Jerik is too busy and won’t give us the time of day.  They insinuate that Quill is under Jerik.  As we are trying to talk them into not leaving, Ainslie casts a spell, which hits Lockjaw psychically.  It is INITIATIVE.  

Lockjaw attacks Ky and hits once.  Og then moves up and shoves him, and does great damage to him.  Ainslie leans forward to cast a spell at Dust and hits, killing him.  Lockjaw interacts to beg for his life and Hap says if he will answer questions he can live.  The guy asks to put his weapons away and pulls out a vial and holds it to his mouth. We ask who/where Quill, Jerik and Whisper are.  He will not tell us.  We ask why the girl was killed at the Old Oak and Grapes…it was because she knew of the plot to kill Lady Rook.  Ky asks for confirmation:  Jerik ordered the death of Rook, but that hasn’t happened yet.  Imonorra (he spits), Magistrate and Hand…and others are ok with the Cloak.  What does Quill look like?  She is half-elf that blends in with the nobility…she changes her appearance frequently.  He says he will leave the town if we will let him.  Ky offers to fake his death.  He says we might be able to get some bodies from the cultist group. He says they are sick \- hanging out in the cemetery.  If the Eradic of Ghone gets your body…they will give your soul to Sijulous.  We ask him to strip off his weapons and cloak.  He can keep his money…he says he will board a ship and leave.  Ainslie asks if we will encounter other Cloak members in the sewers.  Ky asks for his real name…**Dren Velt** is his real name.

We then take the loot from Dust (split it) and drag his body to the rat alcove.  Ky retrieves the rat eaten cloak and then Ainslie sets fire to the alcove.  The plan is to be able to tell people that we found an infestation of rats that looks to have killed 3 Cloak of Defiance members (cover for Lockjaw).  Hap (Ivory) puts on Dust’s Cloak, Og \- Lockjaw’s cloak (Biggie)  and Ainslie puts on Smiley’s tattered Cloak (Talon) don cloaks.  Ainslie takes Ky’s clothes and rips them up to match Smiley’s cloak.  Ky pulls up her cloak (Smalls).  Should we encounter anyone then we are Cloak of Defiance from Paendley…Smiley asked us to come (he was a childhood friend with Edge (rank)  Rock our superior.  

We go back to the circle and head clockwise around the circle (toward a).  We find a passage on the left (northwest) and follow it to the dead end at the guard house at the Upper City Gate.  We see a ladder that leads up into a trap door into the guard house.  We go back to the circle and head clockwise. We take the next passage to the left (northeast).   Ainslie leads, followed by Og, Ky and then Hap in the back.  

Down the passage, we notice there is no sludge…this is a clean passage.  We start to travel to the next circle. Ainslie fails a DEX Saving Throw…his body is pulled forward and starts to float in the air, with acid on his skin– restrained.  

### **Gelatinous Cube**

IT IS INITIATIVE:  Combat vs. a Gelatinous Cube

* ROUND ONE:   Og has to make a dex check and succeeds.  Og then uses his glaive to swirl around the floating Ainslie…looking to see what is holding Ainslie up. This action lodges his weapon and he calls that out. Ky then looks and doesn’t see anything…and jumps up on top of Ainslie and finds herself stuck and with acid damage.  Hap then attacks the air with a firebolt and does damage…and reveals that they are stuck in a gelatinous cube.  Ainslie takes damage.  Ainslie then uses his telepathic feat to shove the cube away from him (Ky and Og’s weapon are still inside)…dislodging him from it.  He then casts a spell and does damage.    
* ROUND TWO:  The cube tries to hit Ainslie and misses.  Og attacks with another weapon and hits. Then tries to punch it and do additional damage…getting his hand stuck inside.  Ky attacks from inside with disadvantage and hits, then attacks with an offhand dagger and kills it.  

Ainslie cleans us up.  We see rusty weapons, 1pp (we split), a piece of topaz (50gp to split later when sold \- Ky keeps).  We decide to take a short rest to bandage ourselves. WE THEN GO BACK AND GET AURY…AS WE KNOW IT IS DANGEROUS HERE AND WE NEED HIM. As we do, we hear critters, but don’t encounter anything.  After a while, the muck comes back. We go around the next circle…changing the marching order:  Ky, Og, Ainslie, then Hap.  This area is a little wider and cleaner-probably cleaned by the cube.  We then come to a passage on the left \- due east.  It is a little darker.  Ky notices some cracks in the walls (normal size rats, spiders).  Ky and Hap look for hidden doors/passages in this area…find nothing unusual.  

### **The Scraping**

The group heads down, also looks in passage on the right (due west).  We haven’t seen signs of anyone traveling. Ky spots signs of footprints in the muck that lead toward Imonarra’s Tower.  We go that way and hear scraping metal on rock.   Ky stealths down and sees someone trying to get into I’s Tower, messages to the group to catch up.  Ky gets to the bottom of the ladder and looks up and sees a 3’ tall figure wearing a Cloak of Defiance cloak and plague doctor mask…they drop the pick axe.  

– The End of the Session – 

# **11 \- Confronting Cloak**

**Session Date**:  September 9, 2025  
**In-game Date**:  20th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  The Sewers of Emberstran

Aury’s “cloak” nickname is Flower.  He is with us.

We are in the sewers near I’s tower. Ky tells her to stop, asks what are you trying to do to get into the tower.  The figure sighs…

IT’S INITIATIVE:  Combat vs. the Whisper

* ROUND ONE:  Ky asks her to stand down and then fires her crossbow and hits.  The figure throws two marbles that shift into rats.  She then fires a crossbow and misses Og, but hits Ky (piercing and poison).  Og runs forward (rat misses him \- opportunity attack; other one hits) and then dodges caltrops in the sewers.  He attacks her and hits once.  Hap moves forward  and dodges the caltrops and attacks her, crits, then hits her again, then crits again. Aury says “we need to question her” and casts ‘you shall not move’ (and she saves).   The rats attack Ky \- the first one misses, then hits.  The second one hits twice \- almost taking off her ear and then drops her to unconsciousness.  Ainslie steps back and pulls Ky toward him.  Then casts sleep on the rats \- both save.    
* ROUND TWO:  Ky rolls her death save (hit or miss \- who knows).  The figure adjusts her mask and her body turns into a mist and the form moves up the ladder towards the hole she created (but still there).  Og gives Hap temp hit points then moves back to the rats and attacks one. He hits and kills one of the rats, then faces off against the other rat and hits it.  Hap thinks the mask initiated the misty form, but she’s not invincible.  He runs forward to chase her and tries to climb up to swing at her, missing with his first swing, but hitting with his second attack.  He then misses with his scimitar, but hits on his second attack, killing her.  The rat disappears and she falls onto Hap.  Aury then heals Ky.

We move the caltrops out of the way; Ainslie moves the dirt to make it happen faster.  We all move forward.  Hap removes the mask, as Aury casts a spell on her.  It is the Inspector, aka **the Whisper**.  She is wearing studded leather, has a small pouch with 9gp, a small crystal about “quarter” size, a very nice dagger, fancy, blue cloak (no markings), plague doctor mask. Ainslie notices that the dagger (enchantment), crystal (divination), mask (transmutation) are magical.  And she has a magical ear cuff with divination magic.  Ky takes the dagger (+1 dagger) and studded leather. 

As we look at the hole, it reminds us of a peep hole and there seems to be a place where she was going to set something.  Ky thinks that she was going to set the crystal there and listen through the ear cuff.  As we are checking out everything, Aury animates the dead figure. She is under his control for 14 hours.  Aury and Hap study the crystal and ear cuff (Listener’s Coil).  Ainslie studies the mask and takes it (Mask of Withered Breath).  Ky takes the Coil as well (party inventory).  

We discuss how we will proceed.  We decide to proceed in the sewers, heading eventually to the cultist group

Marching order:  Ky (stealth 28), Og/Hap, Ainslie, Aury/zombie

Ky stealths \- she checks out the dead end where the Old Oak and Grapes are…doesn’t see any openings, just pipes.  Heads back up and makes a right and starts into the curved sewer.  At some point, Ky sees something moving about 10’ up (sees a shadow)...she determines it is a humanoid, cloak member.   Ainslie moves ahead of the group to talk to them, Ky guiding him about distance and how to walk.  Ainslie falls and the Cloak of Defiance member snickers…and they start to talk.  Ainslie trying to deceive him.  “I’m Talon \- a new recruit \- Lockjaw told me where to find you.  I’m from Paenley…and have a message for Jerik.”  He is told to take the ladder and go in through the front door. 

IT’S INITIATIVE:  Combat vs. Cloak of Defiance Agents

* ROUND ONE:  Ky then attacks him (surprise) and hits him.  He then attacks Ky and another shoots a crossbow bolt at Ky and misses.  Ainslie shoots a lightning bolt and hits both guys, killing the one that ran away, as well as another down the hall, and the one that Ky hit.    
* ROUND TWO:  Hap uses firebolt to try to hit the last one and misses, fumbling and hitting himself.  Ky throws a dagger and misses and yells at him to help her.  He runs back towards the ladder and Ky gets opportunity attack and hits him.  Ainslie dons the mask and moves up and tries to shove him from afar and he gets pulled off the ladder onto the floor.  Og runs up and gets on top of him, but can’t attack.  Aury runs forward and casts a spell on the guy, and the guy fails his save, dying from a sacred flame. 

The group loots these bodies (20sp total), heavy crossbow, short sword and leather armor on each.  We look at the ladder.  The top has a grate in place that the guy was trying to move.  Ky moves up the ladder to see where this grate leads.  It is a hidden entrance on a house next to the Crimson Swan.  She comes back down.  We hear footsteps approaching from ahead.  Ainslie runs forward to grieve as two approach.  Ainslie convinces them he’s a new recruit. They tell us to watch their post and hold position as they go for more help. 

We decide to attack.  Aury holds one, as Ainslie casts lightning bolt.  Aury then sacred flames the guy on the ground…and he saves.  Og moves up and attacks the guy on the ladder and hits him, killing him.  Then climbs down and attacks the other one, killing him, too.  We loot…10sp. 

We hear someone above and decide to retreat…back the way they came and back to the Temple.  We knock on the door there and Arnol is the one at the door.  He is excited, but doesn’t say anything (as Ainslie reminds him telepathically not to speak about the cult).  We tell him he missed a fight in the sewers.   We go to up into the Temple.  Aury asks Arnol to go get the group food (but Arnol has to be back at the cultist group in an hour and can’t)....he says he has a family emergency to take care of \- a lost brother.  Aury asks if the Priestess knows. Aury knows something is off, but doesn’t know what.  He demands that Aury goes to get him food.  Arnol says he is going to prepare the food at the Temple and Ainslie says he has to go to the bathroom (he actually goes up to talk to Arnol \- says I’ll take the food down and you go to your meeting).

We find a place to sit and eat and talk.  The high priestess walks in after a bit.  Asks us if we know about the chaos in the northern district \- that lightning came up out of the ground.  Near the Crimson Swan.  The Emberstan mercenaries have been sent to investigate. We ask her if we should help and she says yes.  

We decide to head that way.  Ky, Aury, and Hap will go to the Tower; Ainslie and Og to the crime scene.  We establish our alibi (we’ve been in the vault).   On the way to the Tower, Hap talks to Ky about Phira.  He recalls Fleur. We decide that Phira was communicating that Jerik is BAD…which is why Hap needs to get out of town.  Hap sends a note via Fleur to Phira (asking about Rook).

| Phira, happy to hear you want to meet.  I will come when I can.   What do you know of Lady Rook?  Can we trust her? |
| :---: |

Ainslie and Og arrive at the scene and see a zombie with a crossbow bolt. They see a guy walk up \- everyone moving away from him.  He surveys the scene and is shaking his head.  They assume it is Jerik.  They walk up to Captain Amilla (female, human).  “Captain, EGD \- fill us in on what happened”.  She asks what is the EGD.  They tell her they work for Captain Vamond and that they want to help.  She rushes over to the man and says Jerik \- stop for a minute.  They can’t hear what she says, but they hear his response is “nothing.”  She comes back over and says she won’t need their services.  They respond that this is a big crime scene and that they were on the Old Oak and Grapes case and would like to help.  She says there is nothing to solve here.  She says the killer was the zombie.  Ainslie mentions the lightning and she says there is nothing to see here.  The case is solved.  They say they are freelance workers.  She asks them to go stand over here (and motions) and says she might have freelance work for them.  Ainslie casts detect magic to get surface level thoughts from Amilla:  “Where’s Torrick?  He will take them in \- I think it will be perfect” Torrick:  “what does she want?”  Torrick has a jacket full of pockets (ink stains).  “I guess I’ll talk to them”  Torrick then comes over to talk to them.

What is this facade you are putting on?  They say they are with the EGD.  He is asking under who’s jurisdiction are you working?  They say they report to the captain.  He says they investigated the murder near the docks…and tell him about the footprints they found leading into the water.  He then asks them where their headquarters are….where would he find them if he needed them?  They tell him “Headless Hydra.”  He says that he will leave a message for them there in the morning.  They find out that he works for Jerik.

They are trying to figure out if they are being watched or followed.  They start to walk away and someone comes up to them.  What are you doing?  I thought you were the heroes.  Why would you get messed up with those people?  And the guy walks away.  Ainslie and Og walk to the Tower to wait for us.

We go to the door and are met by the servant.  He tells us that Rook and Im are at the Lord’s Keep sorting out what happened today in the quadrant.  We decide to leave…asking him to tell them we came by.  As we leave, Ainslie and Og show up.  They tell us that they think the captain in this area is part of the cloak.   And that maybe they are now persons of interest.  Aury talks to them about the EGD and how people may question that they are not part of a real group. 

Discussion about what to do.  Knock on the front door of the Keep or go underground to the Keep. We walk that way…and eventually end up in front of the Keep.  Lady Rook sees us and comes over.  Ky tells her she came to find her and Rook offers for her to ride back to the Tower in their carriage.  

They leave and start to talk, but Ky expresses concerns about stuff and things. Rook and Ky are yapping on the way back to the tower. Ky reveals a lot of information about what we have learned: tells her that Whisper is dead… Rook drops a bombshell of a quote of “Time to change the status quo” 

She wants blood (at least Og assumes). Rook is definitely going to cause problems. Ky tells Rook to tell the Lord to make the EGD a real thing (even though Ainslie said not to).  Rook tells Ky that she and Im go way back and she trusts her with her life.  That her allegiance is not with the Cloak of Defiance, but to her.  Tells her that the Lord just wants to keep the town safe and does what he has to do.  She thanks Ky for the info and says she will think on it and contact her if needed.

Aury and Hap go to the Dyed Cane.  Ainslie and Og go to the Warm Bear in (Og and Ainslie are both invisible).  All are listening at their various establishments. Aury talks wine at the DC, blesses them.  He is looking around for someone who needs healing \- doesn’t see anyone.  Og and Ainslie look for shady characters around the WB…see no one.  They drop invisibility and go into the WB.  They talk hunting.  Ainslie offers to buy a round for everyone (gives the barkeep 1pp and says to keep everyone happy).  The crowd is happy to see them and excited for the good favor. Og drinks with the crowd. At bedtime, they go to room, draw the blinds and put a chair under the door handle (mage armor). Ainslie gives Og the Mask.

Ky walks into the DC with her ear falling off and asks for help.  Aury offers to help…and also to heal anyone else who might need anything.  He prays to Diverra…makes a scene.  And heals several of us.  They then drink.  Ky tells Aury and Hap about her conversation…then goes to the Hydra, saying they will all meet for breakfast at the Temple in the morning.

Aury and Hap go back to the Temple.  Aury talks to the Priestess about Arnol…telling her about a lost brother that he found.  Tells her that he’s been excited, but evasive…but seems stressed.  Aury doesn’t know what is going on or what to do, but that he needs to be watched.  She tells him that he has a good relationship with Tarly (another acolyte)...she says maybe she can get Tarly to figure out what is going on.  He says he will pray to help guide his spirit.  She says she will talk to others in the Temple to ask around and that hopefully the two of them will get to the bottom of this.

Aury prays to Diverra.  He gets a warm feeling as if her spirit is with him.  Then a chill.  As the prayer leads into what should I do…he gets warm again.  He doesn’t get direct message.  Just feels like he’s correct that something is wrong and that he needs to worry about him.  

### **21st of Talil**

Next morning, Emilee and Haggis are sitting there at breakfast.  They jump up and say I’m so glad to see you \- how has it been?  And she pulls out a scroll and hands it to them.  It says:  I am pleased to announce that you are the official investigators of Emberstan…the EGD is official, etc.  Mentions their names, specifically.  It is signed by the Black Quill.  She pushes the box in their direction and they leave.  The box is full of gold pieces (500gp).  Inside they find a note that says \- I will expect you for dinner at the Crimson Swan.

– The End of the Session – 

# **12  \- The Eradic**

**Session Date**:  October 7, 2025  
**In-game Date**:  21st of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Various Locations of Emberstran

### **At the Headless Hydra**

Ky’tha goes to the desk at the Hydra to see if there are any messages.  Desk man tells me that Tessa stopped by and asked to meet me.  Ky goes over to Charum’s Oil to see Tessa and she says she is glad to see me and has someone for me to meet and walks her out of the shop and across the street to the Dyed Cane.  They go up the stairs and into a private room and Bordi is there with a female pale elf with silvery eyes.  “Your friend Tessa has told us so much…and have played Lady Rook’s games without becoming her piece.  This is what the Etterset values.”  Ky asks for her name  \- **Belavie Lightstrider**.  Ky asks if she lives here (she does) and how does Bordi fit in.  He says “I’ve seen the Cloak of Defiance twist people…we have to be careful no matter who runs things.”  Discussion about the hit with the Cloak of Defiance last night.  Bella offers Ky a coin and she accepts it, asking how Rook fits into those plans.  She also asks about the cultist group. Bella says she doesn’t know much \- she’s been too preoccupied.  Ky tells her why she’s asked \- that she wants a tablet that I think they have.  Bella gives Ky a hug.  Coin is rank 2\.  Ky and Tessa walk out and Ky tells her that they were in the sewers and responsible for the chaos.  Describes how to get to the Cloak of Defiance back entrance.  Says that he is on board, but can’t say that her party is all in (Aury is skeptical).  Bids her farewell and heads to the Temple.

### **At the Temple of Diverra**

Aury and Hap wake up and want to have breakfast…are told that the High Priestess wants to meet them.  She looks like she’s been up all night.  “Something is shifting in Emberstran \- blight now fixed, people changing personalities (Arnol), and now lightning has shot up from the ground.  These are not coincidences \- they are signals.  I keep praying, but have heard nothing.  What is your assessment of the situation, Aury?”  He replies that there are changes in the power struggle in the town.  Mentions Ms. T, Lady Rook…she questions how Diverra has led him to this.  He says that Diverra has many paths and sometimes it is about power.  And that power can corrupt.  People need humility.  Mentions the Lord and Cloak and Etterset.  She says that his calling is now far beyond that of Diverra…that he is bigger than that.  He says that he has been called by Diverra to keep the city safe and protected.  She asks him about the Grove and he says he is still focused on that…but also is looking at other things.  He reveals that the lightning was a result of he and his companions (she suspected).  He makes sheep analogy.  Ol’ Hap looks at him with curiosity…doesn’t quite understand that.  She says that he is not lost…that Diverra is continuing to guide him.  And asks if she can join them for breakfast.  They then go to the dining room to eat.  Aury asks about Arnol \- are we keeping a watchful eye on him?  She mentions that she has noticed that he’s changed and Aury asks to have him followed to find out what is going on.  She agrees to have Koken follow him (Aury makes a bee analogy).  

Ky makes it to the temple and joins the group for breakfast. She thanks the Priestess for her discretion letting us use her cellar.  She asks Ky about her background and Ky avoids it for now, saying how Og was the one who told her to visit Emberstran…but that all cities have complications and politics.

### **At the Warm Bear Inn**

Meanwhile, at the Warm Bear Inn, Og and Ainslie discuss their box of gold and what to do with it.  Spend it, save it, give it away?  Lots of discussion…Ainslie trying to convince him to buy armor, Og saying he wants to wait.  They discuss whether the money is a gift or a payment. They decide to wait for now to buy anything.  They want to look into using the locker service at the Bear.  Og decides that taking it to the church might be the best option.  Ainslie mentions taking it to the crime scene to hide it there (where the man was murdered).  Og agrees.  They decide to go to Jae’s house.  Og carries the chest with a cloak over it. 

As they walk, they decide to go to the Gems of Emberstran and talk to the proprietor (Befast Umberfist) about buying gems (500gp worth).  Some discussion about trying to find pink gems.  The guy is on the up and up.  They agree to buy five 100gp rubies (much easier to carry than 500gp).  They thank him and head to the temple.

As they walk there, they buy a gertot leg (a gertot is a livestock animal similar to a sheep) from a vendor.  They meet up with the rest of the group and Ky shares what she’s done that morning.  Ainslie then shares that they received notice that they are now an officially recognized group, pulling out the document.  Hap notices that the document was not signed by the Lord.  Ainslie shows that it was signed by the Black Quill.  Hap and Ky both say “no” \- that isn’t the Lord.  Or is it?  Maybe the Quill did that for the Lord?  Discussion about the two being hand in hand.

Discussion about using their new found power to “investigate” the sewers “all over the city” and to talk to the cultist group to get the tablet. Discussion about the EGD going vs. the entire group going.   We decide to split up and some go shopping/selling while the EGD investigates.  We decide to meet up at the Cane…if they find anything.  Otherwise at the Temple in the morning.

Ky, Aury, and Hap head to the gem store.  Nice store.  We notice a dwarf going through a big box of coins.  We see him shake off a look and then welcome us to the store.  His assistant is short, gray gnome with violet eyes…quiet.  Ky pulls out an opal.  Emiffi \- bring that to me.  She says “you’ve got one, too”  And Ky asks what she meant..but she doesn’t explain.  He offers 10gp for the opal and Ky isn’t sure about the price.  Aury thinks it is good.  Emiffi is going crazy wanting to talk…and Aury drops to his knee and says “Child \- are you ok.”  She says \- can you help her find the other side of her necklace?  They notice that Ky is wearing a locket that is in half.  He asks Ky if she is from around here and tells his name and Ky says that price is fair.  Hap tries to intimidate Bafast…and doesn’t.  Aury asks Emiffi if that is a fair price and she is squirmy…doesn’t contradict her boss.  She is a deep gnome hanging out with a dwarf…Ky knows that Umberfist is a clan in Dunduaar.  He agrees to get the gold and opens up the chest and gives Ky 11gp.  Discussion about Ky’s locket.  Bafast tells her that he had the other half of the locket…until it was taken.  He walks over and locks the door \- I can’t be talking about this.  He says that a “cloak rat” came in and stole it.  Emiffi saw who stole it.  It was someone with a red cloak. Bafast says there is too much that is strange around here…I’m kind of trapped.  Caught between two stones.  Ky tells him they are not affiliated with the Cloak.  He says that he gets shipments…he wants to see the town where it should be.  He isn’t happy about having to be here.  Lady Rook has pressured him into supplying materials to the drow at the Spaulder (iron, steel, and dunduaar).  He is not comfortable sharing more right now.  He doesn’t know what Rook’s plans are for the drow.  Ky asks him to acquire the locket if he can…and hold it for her.  Ky says that she doesn’t like the Cloak either, but is on the fence about Rook.  He’s not convinced that Ky is not going to turn on him.   They leave and head to the Silver Spaulder.

The go in.  Well kept.  Suits of armor.   We see two guys who look just like Emiffi…they are muscular; and a tall silvery-white haired, dark skinned individual.  Ky tells him they have armor to sell.  Aury says he has a chain shirt to sell.  How much to trade chain shirt, Hap’s chain set for the splint mail?  He makes an offer that is not fair, calling the trade chain “shitty.”   Hap tries to persuade him…and does.  120gp and the items in exchange for the splint mail.  Wimplerags…one of his helpers gets the items.  Aury offers him a mace in exchange.  Hap wears a white tux…while he waits for the armor to be finished up later today.
