They walk out and head to the Rarities shop. Shop area and warehouse area.  Half-orc guy with glaive guarding the place. Half-elf walks up to help us “looking for something you didn’t know you need?”  Aury likes the sound of it. Ky says that she dropped something and will be right back.  Aury and Hap believe her. Ky leaves.  Aury says he didn’t know what he wanted.  Raddic mentions that he needs a censer.  This one has holy water and will shower someone (for one round it has self levitation) is 30gp.  Aury buys it.  She asks if Hap needs anything.  She asks if people ever mistake him.  He says no.  She then says I hope I don’t offend, but I have this balm of removal…you rub this on your horns and they are not there for an hour.  The balm is 5gp.

Ky goes back into the silver shop.  Tries to ascertain what the deal is with this guy and Bafast and how that ties to Rook. He says that it is a business deal, but Ky isn’t able to get to the whole truth. She knows that he isn’t paying Bafast much for the supplies he’s getting from Dunduar. And that he doesn’t share the profits with him.  And that he and Rook go “way back,” both arriving in town around the same time. She learns that his assistants and Bafast’s assistant are brothers/sister.  Ky storms out and goes to the rarities shop and convinces Raddic to sell Hap the balm for 3gp and she buys it for him.  The group then leaves and heads to the Dyed Cane.

### **Eradic of Ghone Hideout**

Og and Ainslie follow the group and see them going into the gem store.  Then proceed to the Eradic of Ghone area.  They go to the door and see that it is locked. Og knocks…many times.  Someone opens the door “we just paid our taxes last week” and then shuts the door.  They knock again.  Someone opens it.  We are here for recruitment.  “For he who is great…he who cannot be killed.”  The guys asks who recruited them…and they describe Arnol.  The guy says you can come in but need to stay in the front area (5’x10’ area).  Area is unkempt, cobwebs, etc.  The guy leaves them in there and goes through another door and locks it.  Og hears some noises coming from another room (very odd).  After a bit, the door opens and they are asked “are you committed.”  They say yes and they are admitted through the door.  There is a hall with many doors and a staircase.  They are told to go into another room, take off all your belongings (not clothing), and stand in the middle of the room and wait for us.  Og challenges “naked” and they say no \- just take off your backpacks, pouches, etc., and maybe sit or kneel.  As the door closes, they see Arnol walk past.  Ainslie says in his head “how’s it going” and he responds “it is going fine.”  

They take off their belongings and do as asked.  A little while later people come in dressed in purple cloaks. “You have chosen to bring forth Sijulous” \- and Ainslie responds “praise be” but they say that is over the top.  They say they are going to go to the cemetery to dig up bodies to try to capture the trapped souls for their god.  You will need to wear these robes.  Your belongings stay here.  Ainslie thinks that these people will go through their stuff so he reaches out to Arnol to come and watch it all. Arnol says \- do you report to me now?  And he says yes.  Arnol says you know they are going to take it to the caves. That stuff is no longer yours.  Ainslie and Og have a conversation in their heads about not giving stuff away.  They offer to let him keep the glaive. They start to resist. Ainslie tells them they need to fight.  “Rip and Tear.”  Og goes over to get his glaive and then surprises the guy. IT IS INITIATIVE.  

ROUND ONE:  Og kills the guys immediately.  Ainslie says “Your soul for Sijiulous.” The guy makes a noise that travels out of the door.  They close the door, put their belongings on, then put the cloaks on over them.

They take the dagger and put it into his chest and smear the blood on the ground in a circle.  There are two guys outside the doors who ask what the noise was…and Ainslie says one of their fellow members wanted to dedicate his life to the cause.  Ainslie is able to deceive him.  The guy comments that “he jacked up the recruiting room.”  Says he will go and notify “Vada.”  Ainslie says they will take the stuff downstairs, um I mean across the hall.  Og and Ainslie see them go through another door (not up the stairs)...the door on the left. They go into the room across the hall and see open crates with stuff thrown in all (clothes, daggers…nothing of value that they can see).  They throw the dead cultist’s stuff in the crates.  They then leave the room and go down the hall to the door on the far right.  It is empty, save bed rolls.  They listen at the door where the guys went. Og hears a door close.  They open the door and see an empty room, except for a few crates with food and wine. They don’t see another door.  So they look for another door \- since they heard one close.  After a few minutes they find a trap door in the floor…with a ladder leading down to the sewers. 

Once down, they think about the map and think they are dropping in at point K. Once down, they see the long sewer hall ahead. Og notices a small symbol sitting on one of the stones with a small skull with a crown on it.  It is dark down here and Ainslie can’t see anything.  Og describes what he sees, but neither is sure what it means.  Og goes over and touches the symbol.  When he pushes, it sinks into the stone and the outline of the bricks open and leads to a path, under the city….maybe directly under the cemetery.  Ainslie and Og proceed forward with Ainslie holding on to Og’s shoulder.

As Aury, Hap, and Ky walk \- they talk about her necklace, she admits it is tied to her family (and Hap says, like my ring).  She says she is confused why the Cloak of Defiance would want the other half.  And also tells them that she went to get more info from the silversmith about the deal with Bafast.  She commits to Aury that she will try to gather more info to try to get to the bottom of Rook’s involvement.  They then get to the Cane and Bordi gives them the regular table, asking Ky “are we all good.”  Same for Aury.  They sit and eat.

Back at the Eradic of Ghone, Ainslie asks Arnol via message what the passwords are for the cult group.  Arnol is confused and doesn’t answer directly.  Says he misses Aury.  Says “I’m coming.”  The guys light a torch and Arnol suddenly appears with a torch and Gertot leg (headed for someone else)…and they follow him.  Arnol says “are they making you dig today” to the guys (not knowing who they are).  And Og replies with a female voice..  Wow \- you are a big woman, Arnol says..  Og tries to deceive him and doesn’t and then Arnol realizes who they are…and says welcome.  Arnol asks if they are higher rank than him…and Ainslie tries to persuade him. Arnol says he has to drop off the leg with someone.   They travel for some time, passing another ladder, as Og sees an outline of a door carved into the sewer wall (doesn’t seem to be carved by pick axes).  Ainslie asks about the door and Arnol doesn’t know.  They walk another 20’ and Arnol hands the meat to another cultist.  The guy tips Arnol.  He is not dressed in a robe, but a violet tabard (has sword,shield).  He says “new recruits” and points down the hall.  Arnol says “I thought you were higher rank” and the guy laughs as Og and Ainslie say they never said that.  They decide to go into the door that they saw carved into the wall.

They open the door to a large, circular room with a runic circle.  There is another robed figure in the room with a large creature (A **chalyth** – *like an armadillo the size of a mastiff*) with a skeleton in its maw and some acolytes here as well.  The lady says “I am assuming you are here to proceed with the work, please proceed forward.”  She points to another door.  They go forward into the other room. The room is misshapen.  There are two tunnels that lead from here.  Lots of bone-skeletal creatures lying about.  She says there are creatures floating, ghostly guys and two others with long tongues.  She says she will hold them to allow them to pass and points to one of the tunnels.  Ainslie says the person who recruited us killed himself and didn’t tell us what to do.  She says no worries and gives them pick axes…Ainslie barely holds his so she says we messed up.  And tells Og to take the ax into the tunnel…and he does.  He follows the passage and sees others chipping away with axes.  He sees debris of caskets and dead people.  They are digging out the dead bodies from below. He gets the impression he is supposed to dig.  He introduces himself saying he doesn’t know what he is doing.  And they tell him to dig for bodies.  He mentions a tablet and they have no idea…just say to dig for bodies.  Meanwhile, Ainslie is with the lady.  She says “so you can’t dig” and he says I can do magic and produces smoke in his hand.  She says, can you detect magic and opens a door and tells him to “detect,” as he really thinks she wants him to truly detect magic.  He casts the spell.  And sees that this is funeral treasure…goblets, burial items, strings of pearls, other items.  One thing stands out \- the 4th piece of the tablet, as well as silver and gold items (urns, jewelry, a purse with coins).  He thinks there are 500gp worth of valuable things.  He also sees magic on the cultist lady (bracers-abjuration or enchantment).  He starts to sort through items…picking things up and looking at them, appearing to study them.  She watches him.  He is trying to find a moment to grab the tablet as he studies and handles many items.  Og tells him he has found three skeletons.  He holds up the ivory symbol of Goldraen…and Ainslie says it seems to have divine power.  He says he will continue to look.  She says they will pray on that symbol…and use it for tonight’s ceremony.  She says I’m assuming you will be at the ceremony tonight, right?  If you find anything else, please let me know.  Ainslie messages to Og to “get out,” as he pockets the tablet and then takes the onyx box (120gp) and starts to leave. 

Og starts to head out of the tunnels, crouching as the tunnels are only 6’ tall. As he approaches the misshapen room…he casts gaseous form and floats through the room.  As soon as Ainslie knows that Og is okay, he asks the lady if he can study the onyx box…saying he thinks there might be magic in it.  She says, “good \- I expect a response tomorrow of its capabilities.”  The beast makes a noise and looks at Ainslie, but does nothing.  Ainslie goes to the door to leave.  They don’t see Arnol.  Ainslie gets into another room and lights a torch.  He sees the guy eating the leg and asks him how good it was, telling him that Arnol is good for helping him with anything he needs.  The guy says “can he swim” and laughs and says I’ll ask him to swim \- and points to a door.  Ainslie asks him to open the door to show him.  And he sees a 5’ wide tunnel that leads down the passage and he hears water. Thinks this might connect to the river that we know about.  He says that she has a good guardian over there who will shoot anything that disturbs her.  Ainslie and Og end up leaving the hideout \- successfully.

Og feels remorse for grave digging…speaking about a child who was still decomposing.  They walk away \- a couple of hours have passed.  It is not quite dinner time.  They go to the Warm Bear Inn.

After a couple of hours, Hap and Aury go back to pick up the armor.  The armor fits perfectly. He compliments Hap on his shape.  Hap is happy.  He asks where Ky is and Hap says she is finishing up eating.  He asks him to pass a message to her that he will help “with the wooing of Bafast.” 

Meanwhile, Ky goes to the gem store and asks Bafast what he knows of her necklace.  Where did you get the half you had?  He says it was a good luck charm that I’ve had for some time…given to me from my nephew (he’s not my nephew, but still).  She asks if he was an Umberfist and he says no \- a Cragfury.  He says “they left us and I miss him and I want the other half of that back.”  I don’t know where you got yours but I want mine back.  She asks where they went and he says…the vein drove him away. She asked how he found himself involved in the enterprise. And he says that Rook persuaded him.  She tells him he could get more coin.  He says he hates the drow but likes the smith.  She questions how the gnomes ended up working for both he and the drow.  They had expertise in gems.  When the vein was found and needed to be suppressed, the Cragfury disappeared.  So he left and made his home here…because he doesn’t like change.  He says that the drow knew Rook back when they lived in Dunduar.  She promises to be on the lookout for rubies.

Ky catches back up with Aury and Hap.  Tells him he looks great in the new armor and suggests the Old Oak and Grapes for dinner.  They walk there.  As they walk in, they find it is Ladies Night.  They had Ky a goblet and say as a representative from Lady T, the original member of the Vintner’s Guild.  And others hand goblets to Aury and Hap and say “to a new future.”  These people are trying to be happy, but are also sewing division…maybe.  Others in the room have these same goblets.  We get seated, paying a cover, and enjoy a fine meal.

Meanwhile, Ainslie and Og go to the Crimson Swan to meet with the Cloak of Defiance.  This is the nicest place they have ever been.  Lighting is perfect, candles give feeling of warmth, they hear music, smell of cherry BBQ, soft but rich fragrance.  As they walk in, someone locks eyes like they were expecting you.  They come up to them and welcome them, “right this way.”  She waits and follows them.  They go into a private dining room, no sharp angles.  A lady dressed in dark leather stands and says recognized and rewarded and perhaps ready for more.  Love me some EGD. They see a fantastic meal…exotic and upscale foods (you don’t want to eat too fast).  She says I really hope you can prosper in this city. Og grabs food and she says ‘that is only the beginning.’  You see that Emberstran can eat honest folks alive, but not you.  Og introduces himself, Ainslie does, and she says I am Selvara Nendris, but everyone calls me the Black Quill.  You know my friend Torrick.   They eat.  She says you’ve seen what happens here, you have been around.  My friend can speak on your behalf. As you fill your bellies, I’m asking you to join us…not as pawns, but as peers.  And believe me the next chest you open won’t be so light.  Og says you have put effort into making us official EGD…what exactly are you looking for us to do?  She says she needs to clear the objectives with her authorities…and then will let them know.  She is trying to establish a partnership to increase their efficiencies in the town.  The two talk in their heads and agree with her that an alliance could be beneficial to both sides.  She raises her glass “Shall the Rook topple.”  They both raise their glasses as Ainslie says he is trying to come up with another chess-related pun…other than “down with the queen.”  They realize that was a toast to “are you in or out” and they both agreed to be in.  She says you did good…there is always a choice and there are certain ones that lead you to the correct future to insure that you are still standing.  Glad you enjoyed the meal.  Once Jerik gives the word, I’ll be in touch.  I hope next time we will meet again as kin.

– The End of the Session – 

# **13  \- Emberheart Tablet**

**Session Date**:  October 23, 2025  
**In-game Date**:  22nd of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Various Locations of Emberstran

We have become known in the town–heroes to some, problems to others.

At the Temple … Aury and Hap come out for breakfast and they hear someone enter  “Aury \- you here, I got something you should see.”  Arnol \- “I found this…I think this is tied to you” (to Hap) and sets it next to him.  This is a round medallion of black obsidian hanging from a copper chain rope.  “This was in their stash…I took it from them when they were gone.  I think they took it from a grave they were robbing.  Your dad was buried there, right?”  It has a “G” on it.  “You recognize it, don’t you?”  The second Hap touches it, the brazier in the room flickers. It is not familiar to him, but the G does look like his family’s “G.”  “It didn’t do that when I touched it,” says Arnol.  Hap says it looks like something his daddy had….but the chain is what seems familiar.  He realizes that Ainslielee wears a chain just like this, but isn’t sure about it.  Wants to spend time with it.

Aury wants to figure out what is going on with Arnol (after hearing his tale of what he just described).  Hap tries to pay him and he declines…Hap thinks Arnol is guilty.  Aury repeats some of his words to him…and asks if there is anything Diverra should know.  Arnol says he will take it up with his goddess.  He won’t lie to Aury.  Aury says he needs to go see the Mother.  He asks if he can clean up or eat first.  Hap tries to defend Arnol.  Arnol says he didn’t tell because he was told not to.  Word play between them all about what he should tell…and Aury lands on “break your promise if you are breaking our vows to our goddess.”  Arnol says he didn’t do the bad things…killed people, robbed graves.  Aury and he come up with “guilt by association” and he says he will talk to the Mother and probably shouldn’t go back (to the bad folks).  Hap asks who “they” are and he says he can’t tell.  They two offer to help Arnol…who now feels bad about ever associating with the bad people.  He says he needs to think of this.  Hap says he owes him since he did “him a solid” and will help if needed.  Aury agrees…he waves his hand and magically cleans up Arnol.  Hap is excited to sit and reflect and study his new medallion.

Meanwhile, at the Warm Bear, Ainslie and Og wake up and discuss their day yesterday.  Getting the tablet piece, their dinner with the Cloak.  They think Aury is 50/50, Hap and Ky dislike the Cloak…but now that they are “employed” by them…what does that mean.  Are they Cloak agents or maybe Double Agents?  They discuss how Og is not great at lying, but Ainslie can lie for them.  Ainslie thinks that maybe they should convince Rook to leave town; Og says they could via burning down the vineyards or the town.   They talk about letting Rook know they (the Cloak) want to get rid of her.  But at a minimum, decide to meet the party at the Temple to show them the tablet piece they acquired.  Will avoid the Cloak talk with the party unless it comes up.   Og brings up that Ky may talk to Rook about all of this.  They agree that they don’t want to lie to the party.  They decide that they will share their interaction with the Cloak, but that they don’t agree with the Cloak’s goals.  They decide to hit up the gertot leg vendor on the way to the Temple.

As soon as they walk downstairs, everything seems still and quiet with Og.  He feels a motion as if something is crawling around in his backpack. He stops to go through his pack and a vine of the thornleaf grips onto Og and to the inside of his wrist and he feels a thorn prick him and blood is drawn. It grasps like a tick momentarily and he feels like his skin is changing (lizard shedding skin) and in his mind he hears a soft sound (old tree gets pushed by the wind and you hear creaking timbers) and then it forms the word “Alferumn” (a couple of times).  He reaches down and it releases from his wrist, and it goes back into pendant form.  He has a feeling that it was asking for something that he didn’t answer.  When he looks at where the thorn was, he now sees that it is like tree bark on his wrist.  Og tells Ainslie about what just happened and the word that he heard…Ainslie tries to see if he knows this word, and he does. He thinks back to stories he’s heard when he was captured by the cult and he doesn’t think the name has anything to do with the cult, but more of a name that he heard from that group about someone who spoke about an axis of perpetualism…like the circle of life everywhere it sits.  He thinks it has something to do with the Ascension Tree (tree of life in Myrdae). Maybe this leaf is from that tree, thinks Og (who found it in a cave).  Og puts it on his wrist and it latches on again…but he can tell this doesn’t want to hurt him…it is almost asking for permission to stay on him.  Og talks to it “hello, Mr. Leaf, can you hear me?”  And it grips tighter.  He says he will talk to it later, after he eats.  Ainslie tells him that he needs to feed the leaf, too.

Meanwhile, at the Hydra: Ky wakes with a sharp knock at her door and Tessa comes in, uninvited.  She is damp and looks tired “we need to talk.  Tell me your friends did not have dinner with the Cloak.”    Ky says she doesn’t know what’s going on, but that they probably have a good reason.  They have a discussion about how someone needs to change \- them or Ky.  Ky tells her about the EGD and how she asked the Lord to give them official standing.  Ky says perhaps they can be double agents.  Tessa says that if we need the guys to go before Belavie, we can do that.  But that our group is too well known to be in two different opposing groups.  She apologizes for barging in…and notes that her skills to do so were good.  She leaves and Ky leaves, heading out without eating, to get to the Temple.

It is misty as the groups walk to the Temple to meet.  Ainslie notices a torn parchment hanging on a lamppost “by decree of Lord Dusklight…new stewardship for the VG will be happening soon.  Lady T will be reappointed or revoked…this will take part at a Council meeting next stretch.”  They get the sense that this doesn’t happen often.   Ainslie tells Og.  They arrive at the Temple.  They see Arnol sitting with Aury and Hap…and see Ky arriving at the other side.  Arnol says, “I should probably go speak to her” and walks away.”

Ky says “what happened last night?”  rather loudly.  They gather closer and she says I heard you had dinner with the Cloak.  Ky questions what the Cloak’s goals are and why they asked them to dinner.  She tells them that they cannot be Cloak members or be seen to be Cloak members…unless they are secretly getting information from the Cloak to feed Ky.  The party talks about the politics of the situation \- who to align with, etc., as Ainslie shares what he learned about the meeting that has been called for the next stretch.  Aury thinks through his experiences as a resident of the town…and thinks the Council aligns 3 (non-cloak) vs 2 (cloak focused)...and that the Lord leans toward the side of the 2/Cloak.  He’s not sure how the vote next week will go.  Group talks about how we could tip the scales with our influence, but Ky points out that we cannot be both Etterset and Cloak.  Ky mentions how they could be double agents.  Ainslie and Og aren’t sure they could pull that off.  

Ainslie ends up changing the subject and shows the tablet piece they found.  They tell the other three that it was found with the cultists.  Arnol makes a noise and Aury thinks in his head that he might be tied somehow to the retrieval.  We need to go to a safe space to put it together.  Decide to go to the Dyed Cane to have a safe and private room to assemble the tablet.

We head to the DC and see if we can find Bordi.  He says he will send up the tea and some food and throws us the key to the room.  Aury says he needs the brandy.  We go up to the private room.  As we walk up, Hap shows Ainslie the chain/medallion and he knows they have chains that are just alike.  He thinks the onyx medallion looks like it has magic drained from it, but not broken…as if it could be restored.  Like it needs to be recharged.  Ainslie now thinks that his chain is missing a pendant or medallion.  Ainslie tells Hap about it…and we discuss how Hap got his from Arnol (and tells the story).  Discuss how maybe the two chains came from the same source.  Ky suggests a trip to the jeweler in town.  

We lock the door after the food/drinks are dropped off.  Hap suggests getting the tablet pieces out \- and we all do.  As we do, we talk about the cultists…Og says they were digging graves.  Aury asks if they saw Arnol there at the site…and they said they did not see him while they were getting the tablet piece.  Og even suggests that Aury should talk to Arnol about the cult group.  

We push the pieces of the tablet together and nothing happens….then the rooms feels of energy and the tablet vibrates.  Orange light traces a sigil on the surface…edges glow.  Starts to smell like burning.  Warmth coming from tablet.  The runes then set into place…can any of us read it?   None of us can.  Ky does a rubbing on it and feels the arcane magic.  Hap tries to copy it.  Ainslie casts a spell to see what the magic of the tablet is:  transmutation, divination, and illusion.  The tablet is roughly 6-7” by 8”.  Ky feels like the magic is starting to enter her…and she stops and hands it to Aury.  Ainslie suggests that Ky ask Rook (Im) for a scroll of Identification to see if she can give it to her.  Aury gives it to Hap to hold. 

Group talks about next steps. Ainslie mentions that Hap can wear his necklace that hides him from being scryed upon (and gives it to Hap).   All think it is a good idea.  Ky says she will go visit Rook.  Og offers to go with her, but since he has a stone that Rook wants, will stay back.  Discuss who else should go.  Ainslie will go with Ky.  She will carry the stone and will leave the coil behind for someone to listen in.  Aury, Og, and Hap will stay behind (and plan to stay at least an hour).  

As we sit there, we notice that Og’s skin is starting to look strange.  As he tells us the story, the appearance of the bark disappears.  He is now one with the bracelet (+1 to armor class).  

Ky and Ainslie head to Im’s Tower.  20 minutes later they arrive at the Tower.  Ky knocks on the door.  Usual arrival. We are escorted to the dining area.  Ky starts to question Rook about Befast and the gem operation and reveals that she is an Umberfist and was sent out from Dunduar to find out who has been stealing from her clan. Rook gets defensive and upset with the accusations…Ky didn’t mean to get loud and upset…but did.  They smooth it out as Rook offers to help her investigate who might be stealing from her clan.  She suggests it might be the Cragfury clan.  She says that she is helping Befast and the dark gnomes make a life for themselves outside of the underdark.  She asks for Ky’s support and Ky says she has it.  She then asks Ainslie for his support.  Ainslie then tells Rook about how the EGD was not sanctioned from the Lord, but from the Cloak.  He asks in a round about way for an identification scroll.   He then asks Rook about the **Order of Ahn**.  She has heard of it and says that if she could provide him with information about it or the **Ahn’tsuel** \- she will have his 100% support.  She asks for Ky to be escorted to her quarters to get the **Scroll of Identify**.  Ky goes.

Rook asks Ainslie why he is interested in Ahn and he said he might be getting his power from there.  He shows her his tattoos.  She talks to him about a dragon that used to fly over Emberstran named Irragosa.  He came from the same hatch as **Annagos** and was the mount for one of the descendants of Ahn’tsuel.  **Irragosa** will have answers for you.  She will be at **Mount Emberstran**.  Rook has not seen her for some time.  Ainslie then tells her about how the Cloak wants to take her down, by recruiting Ainslie and Og.  She asks about the nature of their pact/contract.  And she’s asking what would happen if he betrays their dinner.  She is now concerned that he is bound by the Cloak.  

Ky reenters with the scroll. They talk about the Cloak and how Ky wants them to be double agents.  Rook presses that she will lobby for her support to her friends.  Rook asks how she can help him out of this situation and he says he has it handled.  

Back in the room, the group heard a lot of crackling…and only part of the conversation.  During the 1 hour and 10 minutes Ky and Ainslie were gone, the other 3 were in the room with the tablet on the table.  Aury starts to feel warmth and the runes on the tablet are starting to give an orange glow (molten lava).  He feels grounded like something is calling him into the world of Myrdae.  He notices that he feels more connected to Emberstran than he ever has before.  He feels like it is coming from the mountain, the volcano, the heat of the mountain and the energy there.  The tablet attuned to Aury during that period. 

Aury can now read the tablet.  It is a poem. 

| *“Where ember roots and thorns entwine, Beneath the mount, the vault shall shine. In Baltwood’s breath, you’ll find the trail, My warmth, a heartbeat without fail.”* |
| :---: |

When he finishes reading it, everything around him goes quiet as he goes into the zone.  He feels the heartbeat of the mountain. He feels the warmth pull at him and it calls you towards Mount Emberstran and he feels like it would lead him to a particular spot there.  Hap and Og notice and ask what is up.  He says “we have to go to the mountain \- there is something there.”  He doesn’t know why…just knows he is being called there.  

Ky and Ainslie come back.  We share that Ainslie and Og are not going to be Cloak.   Aury tells the other two that he attuned to the tablet and about the poem and that we now need to go to the mountain. Ky asks Aury which is stronger \- the pull to Diverra’s portal or the mountain.  And Ainslie answers “the mountain.”  Ky realizes that Ainslie has a strong personal reason for going to the mountain.  Hap argues that his vision is what brought him to Emberstran and brought our group together…and thinks that it was tied to Diverra.  Og feels that the portal is more risky because coming back might be hard, but also leans that way because of his initial vision, too.  Ky says she will go that way, but recommends that we endorse Rook before we go.  Aury and Hap agree…as do Ainslie and Og.  

Ky suggests that we meet at the gate; Aury says out of town is better.  We discuss and the group decides to meet at the Lord’s Keep.  

### **At the Warm Bear Inn**

Og and Ainslie head to the Bear to collect their things (papers about Hap, gemstones, etc.).  A half-elf female bumps into them.  They don’t know her but she is striking.  She bumped into them on purpose.  She looks dark-skinned, blue eyes, red cloak.  “*I was sent to see how things are going.*”  Og says they are going out of town.  She asks if they are aware of the meeting next stretch...and they are.  She asks how she can secure their support from the EGD against Rook.  Ainslie says it would be in the best interest of the EGD to stay out of politics in order to keep order established.  And to prove that they are completely impartial, he returns the money that they were given.  She declines and says she is not going to take their money from them.  They ask if she is going to report to the Quill.  They get the sense that this person is probably here to take care of bigger things.  She isn’t going to do any reporting, but was sent here for other tasks.  She says she is here to ask the EGD to help solve the mystery of who killed the Cloak members in the sewers.  She says she would like to tell Jerik when they will return from out of town.  Og thinks it would be no more than a stretch.  She says that might not be good, but leaves (this is Silverheart).

### **At the Temple to Diverra**

Aury and Hap go to collect their things at the Temple.  Aury tells the Priestess that they are heading to the Portal.  Aury bows to her.  She talks about how Arnol is troubled.  She asks if he has heard about the grave diggers.  Aury tells her that he thinks that Arnol won’t reveal everything because he thinks his head will explode.  She asks Aury to take him under his wing.  He says that he can’t because he is going to the portal.  “Ahhhh….Diverra’s work.”  Aury offers suggestions for Arnol \- make sure Arnol reevaluates his vows.  He says to research the head exploding thing.  And that he might be involved in a “cult outside of us.” She senses that calling in him to leave…and takes away the task to take over Arnol.  Aury mentions the vault and asks if there is anything magical there that Hap can wield.  She offers to fetch him the Great Magical Pruning Dagger of Diverra (+1 dagger)...and goes to get it. Aury gives that to Hap. She also gives him a vial of holy water. 

### **At the Headless Hydra**

Ky leaves to meet with Tessa, then goes to the Hydra to get her stuff.  Ky tells her that we will support Rook; that Ainslie and Og will be double agents.  They can’t openly betray the Cloak.  Tell her that we are leaving town and aren’t sure when we will be back.  Aury is listening in…but this is happening while he is also talking to the Priestess about Arnol, the magical weapon, the call to the portal, etc.  

Back to Og and Ainslie \- Ainslie tells Og that he will need to go to the Mountain at some point \- that he has personal reasons.  They discuss the visit from Silverheart.  They discuss needing to remain neutral. Ainslie casts sending to Rook…tells her that EGD needs to be apolitical.  She responds to Ainslie:  that is unacceptable, I have already engaged.  Jerik’s brother is held in your defense.  (I have Phira’s husband…he is a hostage…if they make a move on you, Jerik’s brother will be killed).  

– The End of the Session – 

### **Out of Character Plans for Next Session**

*Ky goes to the Lord’s Keep.  She finds xxxx…and tells them:  As one of the Heroes of Emberstran, I want to give my endorsement of Lady Rook in the upcoming Council meeting.  I have found her to be intelligent and someone who is ready to help take Emberstran to the next level. While I think that Lady T is competent and has been good for the Vintener’s Guild to this point, Lady Rook will shepherd the guild into the future.* 

# **14 \- Drowned Waypoint**

**Session Date**:  November 12, 2025  
**In-game Date**:  22nd of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Various Locations of Emberstran

Ky heads to the Lord’s Keep; Og and Ainslie heading to the Gate from the Bear; Aury and Hap at the Temple, about to head to the Gate.

### **The Lord’s Keep**

At the Keep, Ky asks the guards to see Lord Dusklight or someone who could rep him.  They agree to get Batheus (male, half elf). She proclaims her support for Lady Rook \- signs document. He would like to set up an appointment with Ky when they return to town.  She says they will likely be back within the harmon.  As she leaves, she sees the guard cut his eyes at her…and tells Batheus to be mindful of him.  She leaves and heads to the gate.

### **The Gate**

Og and Ainslie pause at the Gate…and talk to a guard. They discuss the name of the gate. Ainslie suggests the “Eye of the Mountain” gate.  The guard calls him “the great namer” and they discuss a title for Ainslie.  Hap and Aury arrive at the gate; Ky’tha just behind. Guard walks by “going for my downtime,” as he slaps Ainslie on the back.

At the gate, Ainslie tells everyone in their head about Rook taking Hap’s brother-in-law hostage. The group leaves the town and then talks about the situation.  Og and Ainslie told the Cloak rep they are going to be neutral \- and they are okay with that. But then tells us that Rook has Og and Ainslie under their protection…by taking Jerik hostage.  About that time a guy walks up with a saddlebag with 10 rations…from the Lord’s Keep and Batheus.  We talk about the fact that Rook could have taken the guy easily with the circle in the Tower.  Ky calls the guy to come back…and meets him halfway.  She tells him to add Ainslielee, Hap, and Og to the roll.  Tells him that Aury is to remain neutral.

### **The Lower Baltwood Forest**

We pass people here and there and the road fades behind us.  We find ourselves on a familiar trail leading to the grove.  The woods are humming with light, better than they were last we were here.  We see movement on the trail.   There is someone crouched by a fallen log \- it is someone who knows us.  It is a hunter from the Warm Bear Inn.  Ainslie thinks he is sincere.  The guy says he has not seen anyone go into the heart of the grove since the blight cleared, but if the heroes are going in, it must be ok.  Ainslie gives him 1gp to drink on their behalf at the Warm Bear Inn.  Ky asks his name…it was Kale Noven (a storyteller about hunting the golden stag).  

We follow trails and cross the creek…starting to get darker. As we enter the grove, there is a calmness. We hear a crack of branches and a guttural growl.  We see the animal that we avoided last time.  

IT’S INITIATIVE:  Combat vs. Dralg (beast)

* ROUND ONE:  Ainslie recognizes the creature, casts animal handling and steps forward, pulling Og closer to him.  The creature (dralg) steps forward and stops in front of Ainslie.  Hap steps forward and casts a defensive spell.  Ky moves up and draws her crossbow, holding her action (that’s a good beastie).  Og moves toward Ky and has his weapon ready.  Aury stays put.  Three more dralg step forward and growl \- looking menacing.  
* ROUND TWO:  Ainslie pulls out rations and steps forward with food.  The OG dralg walks up to Ainslie and starts to eat rations.  Hap looks at the creatures to see who might be hostile \- but isn’t sure what the situation is.  He moves up to ready an attack.   Ky moves up \- heading toward the area with the portal \- crossbow still at the ready.  Og steps up and gives them rations…and then moves next to Ky. Aury moves forward a little bit. The two other dralgs run up to the rations and starts to eat.  The last one eases up and starts to eat also.  

We are out of combat.  We travel through the grove and make it to the pedestal.  The stone starts to rise up about 60’ and is aligned with the stone face adjacent to the cliff face.  Sun is setting \- fantastic view.  Aury feels like this lift would not work unless there is a worshipper of Diverra on it.  He thinks that this waypoint would not work without having a Diverra follower in the group (has a divine restriction). We decide to camp here at this cave and go through the waypoint in the morning. 

Ky perceives that the cave is safe \- just like we left it.  Og doesn’t see any tracks other than ours \- thinks no one has been here since we were last here.  Og falls asleep. Ainslie decides to message Phira on Hap’s behalf before we retire for the night.  They go back and forth several times.  Phira says Rook is good and that she is protecting her husband because of his troubled past.  Ainslie thanks her and wishes her well.  Group takes a long rest for the night. 

Ainslie reaches out to Arnol and sends another sending spell…telling him that he is on the right path, one of his most loyal followers (trying to mimic a lich).  Fleur is on watch…as the group rests. 

### **Arcane Waypoint for Diverra’s Faithful**

We wake up to a beautiful sunrise.  This is what you’ve been waiting for, Aury.  We stand in the circle.  Aury casts a spell for the first time \- to activate the sigil.  He is stumbling a little, but then feels warmth and pulls himself together.  We gather close…and the light changes.  We feel like we haven’t moved, but yet the cave walls melt away.  They come back as if they are liquid.  The light flickers. We see something move by us. We realize that we are in a protected dome under the sea.  We see the opening in the dome leads into a cave.  This symbol is correct and is what was on the wall.   We think we might about 100’ under water.  Ky puts her hand through the membrane \- it passes through and gets wet.  She suggests going into the cave.  

### **Underwater Cave**

Og looks for tracks and sees enormous tracks that have been here a stretch or two  \- he thinks the circle hasn’t been used in 20 years.  We think this circle hasn’t been used since the Awakening…and that 20 years ago this place might not have been under water. Aury knows that a sanctuary to Diverra exists historically \- he just doesn’t know where.  He thinks this is at a “hub” location.  

Ky stealths into the cave (27).  It slopes downward…feels seawater seeping through the walls. She doesn’t leave footprints…communicates with Ainslie what is going on.  After traveling for some time, the path winds; after 30 minutes, we are probably another 150’ down.  Aury casts light on Og’s weapon to help light the passage.  Eventually a smell starts to emerge…spoiled meat.  It opens into a large cavern.  There are hundreds of bones.  There is a hulking figure with mottled green skin with long arms and a nose.  These are the tracks we spotted in the bubble.  Ky comes back to stop the group and describes it.  Hap thinks this is a troll.  

### **Troll Cavern**

We walk into the cavern.  Og gets big.  Ainslie speaks in giant with a menacing voice.  They say there is no need to fight.  Ainslie tries to persuade the trolls to let us go into the pool \- which he says is his potty place.  There are 3\.  They say “maybe” and step back.  They continue to eat and watch our group. Ky steps forward and Og picks her up and she goes limp (like food).  Hap walks in and Ainslie picks him up and licks him.  Og then picks up Aury and tries to lick him and Aury slaps Og and he says (in giant) I like my food spicy.  The trolls laugh.  Aury looks around \- this place smells horribly \- he notices a fishing net with some items in it (pouch, clay pot, etc.).  There doesn’t seem to be another passage \- just the pool.  Og takes Aury and Ky and steps into the pool…they all hold their breath and start to swim.  Ainslie notices a piece of paper next to the water and grabs it.  Hap and Ainslie step into the pool and hold their breath, too.  Not long after swimming, about 10’ down and 7’...it opens into a huge body of water.  We surface on the water…there is water as far as you can see.  The ceiling is higher than 120’ at some points.  

We think this could almost be a passage into the underdark.  We see a ledge \- that would be a path.  We all get on to the ledge.  The piece of paper that Ainslie grabbed:  “lights under the water at night, moving like roots.”  Ainslie pockets it.  We all get dry and start to walk on the ledge.

### **Shenog the Fisher**

We follow the path and it narrows.  Sounds are muffled and echoed here…almost distorted. Even dripping water sounds odd.  We see small ripples in the water below us. The area is dimly lit by lichen. We see a glowing lantern around the bend and see something that looks like a boat.  This seems like a boat in a raft.  We see a figure standing on the side of the bank.  We keep going and hear “careful there, the bank drops off fast.”  The big woman with red braided hair and gray skin and yellow glowing eyes starts to pole the boat our way.  She grabs the lantern and pulls it out front as she nears us.  She has a leather jerkin. “Shenog \- been fishing this lake for a long time.  You travelers look lost.”  Aury says “I”m a priest of Diverra and looking for our temple…maybe a grove…something that will lead me to my goddess.”  She doesn’t seem to recognize what he is saying.  “Is there a place with buildings or ruins.”  She knows of some.  Says they are in Nunglthil. She is just a big half-orc.  Ky says “Shenog \- is that your name”  She says “was i not clear.”  Ainslie asks why she is here \- I live down here and trade in Nunglthil.  Og says “where do you live” and she turns and looks at her boat.  Ainslie asks about the dangers in the waters.  Do you not know these waters?  Welcome to Rothenloch.  What are the dangers \- some would say the hums at night get louder.  Ainslie asks about what he saw on the paper.  Are there moving roots in the water and she says yes.  They ask about how to get to the surface and she knows how.  Ky asks her in orcish about what she trades (riches, magic, etc.).  There is some discussion about the fare to take the group on her boat to Nunglthil.  Ky (in orcish) agrees to pay her 6gp for the group \- after some intimidation.  

It will take an hour or two to paddle to the hamlet. On route, Ainslie reads a book to the group.  Og gets tired after an hour and Hap takes over.  She pulls out some smoked eel to share with the group.  Ainslie asks her about thieves guilds…and she confirms that the Cloak is in the town.  Mostly there are quaggoths in this area (large humanoid creatures).  Ainslie starts to tell her about Emberstran.  She then tells the group about the Shade of Rothenloch…it takes people’s minds and they never return the same, if it all.  The eel was tasty.  We finally start to see flickering lights in the distance.  We start to see a wall come into view.  There is an outpost that is half carved into the stone. Houses lit by blue-ish light.  “Best to keep your eyes on your purses here.”  She pulls up to the docks.  This seems very shady. 

We think this will be an interesting place to seek answers \- about Diverra, about the underdark, about where we actually are. 

– The End of the Session – 

# **15 \- Spitespirit Cache**

**Session Date**:  November 28, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  23rd of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Nunglthil

Aury thinks it is late in the afternoon

This lake is huge \- can’t even see across.  Shanog takes us to the town and we see buildings carved into the side of the cliff wall. We see a large cave in the middle of the cliff/town…and we follow it and it opens into an underground harbor, well lit by sconces, fungi, etc.  We see docks with a staircase that leads up…but it would also continue and narrow into a river that goes under a bridge from where the stairs lead up.  She says most of the trading is over there and points to a dock.  We see a door on the right and an open passage…and we see other doors (wood, stones, various substrates) set or carved into cave walls off the docks.  Aury thinks this city is old…well older than 20 years since the Awakening.  Some wood is not recognizable, but looks “weathered.”  Could even be centuries old.

### **Darkbottom Draught**

Shanog says this is the Darkbottomed Draught (pub and inn). She says to get a meal and start there…try to get some favor. We go in…it is warm, with a blue-green glow.  Aury guides Ainslie (persuasion).  We see stairs to the right and left (think they go to rooms) and straight ahead is a large stone bar, carved into the wall. Notice 3 doors \- R, L, S. Door on the right is propped open, tiefling there watching. Dwarf (female \- Bahwa) walks over to welcome and seat us. Tells us about both food and rooms…and shows us the broker’s wall where we can get favor.  We see two massive gray figures as we turn and look back toward the staircases…we missed them coming in (Thak and Drogon \- guards).  She says I’ll be working here with Fafi (ulbin) and points to the tiefling door and talks about trying our luck.  Ainslie gives her a gold for meals for all of us and 4sp for 2 rooms. We get the special (eel and rootmash).  We settle in to eat.  We see a drow female, silver hair, red eyes, lots of piercings, rings on every finger walk over.  She comes over and greets the group (Zymve \- Zym \- phan \- e).  We talk \- introductions made.

She says that she knows a guy named **Scalamank** …I need some coin and a way out of here, you look like you could use a guide.  Food arrives \- metal plates \- food looks good.  We hear someone talking and look over and we recognize him (Ehkel An’davo).   Ky asks about Scalamank \- Zymve tells us he is a gem cutter.  She tells us that this place operates with 5 groups (enclaves) who all cooperate:  Kar’Solar (surface people, including members of thieves guilds;  Grol;Thun-dwarves, ulbin \- metalworking, stonecraft; Selraeth \- drow known getting stuff that is hard to get (blackmarket); Durrak \- duergar (forging); Glimmerdale \-- deep gnomes (gems).  Coppereyes runs the gem operation group…Scalamank is his nephew.  

### **Ehkel an’Davo**

Ainslie walks over the Ehkel and says hello.  Ehkel asks Ainslie to tell a tale to the group.  He asks why he is down there \- Ainslie dodges.  He asks about his friends and Ainslie says we are around.  He warns Ainslie to be careful down here.  He asks about Lady Rook and how she has fared.  Ainslie says probably well.  Zymve hears about free ale and walks over to get a free one, too.  She tells him she makes art, takes the ale, and slips back over to the group.  Ehkel starts to tell a tale about being an artist….blah, blah.  Hap asks Zymve about what kind of work, and she says that it is basically treasure hunting.  Says we can talk to Scalamank tomorrow and see what information he has.  Ehkel talks to Ainslie about where he is from, says that they are west of Emberstran and the Gimmering Sea.  He offers Ainslie a drink, and Ainslie tries to hide not drinking it, but Ehkel sees it.  He orders tea.  He won’t say how he travelled here, but says Lady Rook gave him a favor.  Ainslie asks about the sanctuary we’re looking for, and he says that info will require favor.  They asked about Zymve, and find out she is a “qualifier”, that she can help them get favor.  Some of the party go over to the gambling area. Cadifynn Ziltogga is a drow associated with the Selrayeth running a gambling racket, wears white chaps.  Ky gambles with him (Eyes Out) and wins; Og and he play the same game and end up splitting the pot, no loss.  Ky and Og decide to catch some rest; Aury and Hap head up to a room as well (Aury blesses the room).  Ainslie continues to talk to Ehkel \- he asks him about Mount Emberstran…says there is some adventure there.  He says he hopes he doesn’t beat us there \- now that Ainslie has made him aware of it  We agree to meet Zymve in the morning. 

### **24th of Talil \- Darkbottom Draught, Nunglthil** 

Next morning we go down to meet Zymve…we see there are new boats in the harbor, unloading boats (across the cove).  Zymve tells us we have to work our way to the other side to meet Scalamank.  She points out and we start to get a good sense of the people in the various enclaves. When we get to the other side, we go into his shop “Coppereyes, Gemcraft”…there is a “bell” of a soft ding and a flash of light.  There are walls lined with merchandise and a counter.  We see a gnome with copper eyes. Great lighting. He acknowledges Zymve…she says “we are here about the key.”  He locks the doors…you think they can manage it?  She says the others haven’t been able to do it.  He taps he belt.  I’ve paid good money for the third key and I’m looking to get some gems from Mandel Spitespirit.   Everyone may have heard a tale of a guy named Spitespirit.  He says that the guy hid everything in the Spitespirit cache…I’m looking to get all of the uncut gems.  We ask him why not go yourself…and he says that others went into other rooms with other keys and never returned.  I just need someone to get the one chest of uncut gems and you can have anything else you find. Is this a goosechase?  He says he will give us the key for 100gp.  If we return, we get the 100gp back, plus another 100gp and any additional loot.  Ainslie renegotiates \- says instead, just give us a 300gp gem when we return.

Discussion.  We agree.  Ainslie fronts the money.  He gives us the key and tells us where to go. Path, stone staircase, room.  Will be a half day’s travel.  We realize that we won’t get a fair deal on trades until we establish our reputation.  

We travel there and see a large room with a heavy iron portcullis there with a passage beyond that leads further to the surface.  We see a huge guy \- Glayig \- who is a guard to this passage.  He lets us through. We enter the tunnel and travel…rest every now and then.  We finally come to where a rock fall area was cleared.  The travel has been dark, but group used light to see.   We come to the area where it was rumored that people haven’t come back from.  Aury thinks this isn’t totally true.

Ky scouts ahead of the group down the spiral staircase.  It opens into a lantern-lit chamber.  There are 12 walls, as described.  There is fungus in the lantern to illuminate the room.  2 of the 12 walls have arched open passages that lead on.  The rest of the walls have keyholes.  We look at the pictures on the walls:  mage summoning, traveler making deal with a devil, etc.  One stands out and  shows a xorn showing gems.  We think this is the right one. Aury asks about picking locks for the one we don’t have keys for and Ainslie thinks they are all warded.  Ainslie tries to think about how a xorn might have been associated with the guy from before and he doesn’t.  The walls with open arches have no clues.  Ky tells the group that xorn’s eat gems.  

Ky steps up to investigate the door.  She doesn’t see anything mechanical…very strange.  It is stone \- looks like it would be perfectly suited for a skeleton key. Ainslie uses mage hand to open the lock and it glows and then fades to dust.  Then the ancient mechanism begins to work…and the walls slide down revealing the archway.  Cool air, earthy, metallic, oily…

Straight hallway \- 10’ wide x 10’ tall passage, runes carved every 10’, goes straight for some distance.  Pillar inside.  After about 50’ the height of the ceiling reduces to 2’ off the ground (or so it seems).   Ky and Og can read the runes.  It seems to be proper names…don’t know if it is places or people.  Think it is likely people. Ky thinks the names might be from old tales, but nothing that stands out.  As we near 30’ of the pillar, Ainslie (with his detect magic still up) senses magic from the pillar.  Ky bends down to check out the pillar and sees that there are large cracks in the wall…the pillar is holding up the wall. And the passage continues at the 10’ beyond.  But the cracks in the walls are significant…and a collapse could be significant and kills us.  Ky asks Og to lift her up to investigate the cracks…looking for a triggering mechanism.  She thinks there is a 10x10x8 boulder cube here blocking the way. Ky looks and sees a button on the rod (silver, horses on it).  Ainslie tries to use mage hand on the button and it didn’t work.  Hap tries to send Fleur down to push the button.  We hear a click and the groan of rock and then a pop…and Fleur is crushed.  

Og lifts Ky up and she starts to crawl.  She sees that the top is still insecure.  Hap gives her his shield and she uses the rod (which was pushed out the other side) and shield to reinforce the center of the new ceiling. Aury casts guidance on everyone; Zymve casts dancing light so everyone can see.  Ainslie starts to come across the top \- makes it with no problem.  Zymve tries and loosens some stones and takes damage.  Og comes across with no issues.  Hap comes across with no issues.  Aury is the last to try and he dislodges some stones as well, taking damage.  Ky goes back up to get the rod and Hap’s shield.  Ky keeps the rod.

We work our way down the passage and it bends to the left. A slope down starts to happen.  Ky notices it is a steep grade, and Og feels hit foot start to slide…and the hall suddenly lights up by candles and we see muck on the ground, very oily.  It is slick and Og and Ky both make saving throws to keep from falling.  Ky points this out…and Aury immediately grabs his shield to slide down the incline.  Og and Ky are blown back…dex saves.  Aury caused the oil to light up and slid through a tunnel of flame.  Og and Ky take damage from the explosion.   Aury sees a plain wall with a handprint in the middle of it at the bottom.   Og and Ainslie try to dampen the candle wicks on the walls as the flames die down.  Ky points out the trigger to the light.  Hap slides down on his shield.  Og slides down on the saddlebag.  Ainslie slides down on a cloak.  Zymve slides down on a bedroll, as does Ky.  Everything we slid on is oily and dirty.  Ainslie and Aury clean everything with their spells.

There is a human-shaped handprint on the wall. Ky investigates the wall to make sure that there are no other traps or anything else.  The room is 20’ long hall ending in a 10’x10’ room with the handprint on the wall. Ky notices that the two last runes had the same name **Ythibius**.  Ainslie and Zymve think that sounds like a dragon’s name. Ainslie puts his hand on the handprint. The stone behind it clicks and the wall collapses as if it is an illusion.  We see a small rope bridge that goes out to a plateau that has steps that go down then that goes to a dais where the ray of light hits (ray of violet light shining down) onto an ornate box.  There are other coins and trinkets on the dais.  Aury thinks the bridge could support all of us.  Ky starts across by herself, investigating as she goes.  She feels as if the ceiling appears like a night sky. About halfway across, Og starts to walk.  When Ky gets to the plateau, a vast shape appears above…two large wings form from the mist.  She says \- Ythibius is that you? IT IS INITIATIVE.

Round One:  The dragon goes first and the group must take a dex saving throw \- everyone takes necrotic damage.  Hellish rebuke from Hap.  Ky attacks with her crossbow and crits…hard. The dragon then rears back and Ky takes damage and he disappears into the shadows.  Ainslie runs up and casts a spell on Og (haste) and then runs next to Kytha. The dragon then lands next to Ky and attacks Ky with rend and misses.  Aury heals Ky for 21 pts.  He looks briefly at Aury and then looks at Ainslie and pounces on him, doing damage. Og hits the dragon and then crits him, lots of damage.  Then runs away and the dragon gets an opportunity attack and hits. The dragon pounces down next to Og…and Ky misses her attack of opportunity.  Hap firebolts the dragon \- great damage.  And will cast second wind on himself.  Zymve whips out her yarting (instrument) and casts dissonant whispers and saves but still takes psychic damage.  Then uses her beguiling magic but nothing else happens.  And she gives herself a bardic inspiration.

Round Two:  At the start of the dragon’s turn, vines emerge from Og and he has to make a strength saving throw.  The dragon then attacks Og…and hits a couple of times.  Ky dashes to the side of the dias to hide, holding her action that if he comes into view she will attack.  He then shows up and pounces on Og and Ky hits with her crossbow.  The dragon then hits Og, critically.  Ainslie shoots lightning at the dragon and he fails, this kills him.  He vanishes in a puff of smoke and the door behind the group opens again.

Aury tells the group not to touch anything for 10 minutes…while he prays over us (all but Hap get healed, benefit of short rest). Ky goes up to the dais.  There is a box, an ebon blade with draconic runes (short sword named shadow trail \- Hap takes this), small chip of a violet prism shard with a few affixed to the ceiling (creates light cantrip in violet light \- Ainslie takes it), medallion with markings (Mandel’s Adventuring medallion \- might be a group the guy was in \- value of 35gp for the metal, more if you find a collector); bone scrollcase; ring with inscription \- “luck is for the living” (non magical \- worth 80gp); 14pp (Ainslie takes 10); 112gp; 287sp.  Lock on box has abjuration magic; scrolls of feather fall (Ainslie) and pass without trace (Zymve).  Sword is very magical…words come into Ainslie head.

Everyone moves away from the box since we think it is trapped.   Ainslie steps forward and tries to open the box. There is no discernable way to open it.  He mage hands the box to raise it up and sees a key.  He uses the key and the box opens.  Inside we see 14 very large uncut gems \- Zymve and Ky think these are worth minimum 250gp/each.  We discuss that maybe we need a better deal.  Hap thinks the blade is worth much more than the gems. 

We figure our way back to the room with 12 walls.  We decide to look at the two open archway rooms and there is no opening in either one to get through, assuming that the passages continue.  We now have a key, however, to the box, so we know what keys look like that would be useful for this area.

We make our way back to the guard at the portcullis \- a day has passed.  We are now walking back into the town, ready to talk to the guy with a box.  We get rooms at the same place.  Zymve decides to stay with Hap and Aury.  We sleep here and the next day plan to go to Coppereyes.  Hap plans to study the sword at bedtime.

– The End of the Session – 

# **16 \- Favor in Nunglthil**

**Session Date**:  January 7, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  25th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Nunglthil

### **25th of Talil in the Darkbottom Draught**

We rest at the Darkbottom Draught…a little hard to discern that it is morning being down here. Will take some getting used to, but smell scents of cooking which helps us know it is breakfast.  A lanky ulbin comes over and shows us places to sit \- almost as if he was looking for us.  There is a sealed letter for us there.  Ky picks it up and it is from Zymve…she has unfinished business and she asks us to tell **Coppereyes** she will see him later.  Ains asks how everyone slept…no issues.  The ulbin, **Tickrin**, comes by to serve us \- breakfast and an ale for Aury and Og.  Aury offers a blessing to him. 

Ky notices someone sitting behind Ains.  It is **Endavo** sitting alone.  He has a small book.  She asks the group if we should invite him over to join us and they concur.  Ains messages him and he says he will be over after he finishes the chapter.  As we wait, Ains asks Hap about his sword.  He says he has never seen anything like this before.  It talks in his head. We all see the sword vibrate in the sheath next to Hap. Ky asks if it is good or bad?  He says he doesn’t know.  Og asks about a name and Hap replies “**Shadowtrail**.”  Hap says “You don’t get this type of sword in the swamp, but of course, I already have the pruning dagger of Diverra.  I’m not swimming in riches, but now have two powerful weapons.”  Ains comments that the most powerful weapon is “in your head.” Then he excuses himself to find a restroom.

Endavo closes his book and stands up to join our group.  The book says “*Misplaced a Last Time*.” He asks how we like Nunglthil.  We comment \- it is a revelation, taking time to acclimate, etc.  We ask him about his time…and he comments that he has been enjoying **Miss Placed Tomes**.  **Dririel** is the owner of this bookstore (Serlaeth Enclave) and he writes books.  This is one of a series of 4 books he has written.  He asks if we have been on the lake?  Aury replies yes \- and that we’ve seen turds.  Ky explains that we had a guide coming in.  Endavo says there is a beast in the lake that “hunts for teeth” \- speaks none, listens all, remembers, doesn’t use words to speak, fisherman vanish, dreams change, those that hear the calls too clearly are not the same.  She’nog, our guide, didn’t mention this to us \- says Hap.  Aury asks if this is a Siren?  He thinks not.  Ky asks if it dwells in the depths.  He says yes.  Aury asks if there are other cities on this lake and he says we should visit the mapmaker \- he hears there is a drow city on this lake.   Ky asks if this is his first time here and he replies it is “his second time.”  She notices that he is wearing something he wasn’t wearing in Emberstran \- it looks to be a leather strap around his neck with different beads, different varieties.  As she notices this, Endavo comments on Hap’s sword and Hap tells him a shortened version of how he acquired it.

Og asks Endavo where we should go or visit while in this town. He asks why we are here and Og says we are here for the priest.  Endavo comments on the story Aury told last time.  Aury said he’d tell a new tale, but instead tells him we are looking for an **old temple to Diverra**. He advises we see the mapmaker, maybe visit Miss Placed Tomes, but doesn’t think there is a temple in this area.  He tells us the Ascending Path leads to the **Wurmrise** \- an odd name \- but this is an ascending rock that spirals up to the surface. It is an odd shape.  Ky asks how he got down here?  He says that is a tale for another day, but that he got a favor from **Lady Rook** to get there.    At that point, he says he needs to tend to other business.  Ky asks how long he will be in town and he says “fate will tell.”

We decide to go visit Coppereyes and discuss that we will all abide by the agreement we made.  All agree. We step out to the harbor area.  We see **Gral’thune** folks walking around (dwarves and ulbin)...we see strangers wearing earrings, bracelets, necklaces, charms on backpacks, etc. with beads \- and think this is tied to favor.  

### **Visiting Coppereyes**

We open the door to the gemshop and there is a chime that we remember.  Just like before, **Scalamank** (Coppereyes) comes forward.  Ah \- you are back…and successful?  He says to show him the box of gems.  And we give him the key.  He opens it.  Ah \- look at this, as he starts to finger the gems \- “exactly as accounted for.”  We think he is sincere.  He says, let me get you a 300gp diamond.  Ains also asks for the lockbox as well.  He mentions that we did him a favor…and gives us each a **luminescent bead (this shows us that we have favor with Glimmerdel**).  And tells us that we need to display our favor \- recommends that Ky displays hers in her hair.  Og picks up the lockbox; Ains takes the key.  He gives Ains a 325gp diamond.  He looks at Ky and points to the half broken charm around her neck \- and says he wouldn’t wear that.  He starts to multitask \- mentions **Szoradryn** \- and is triggered.  Who am I to judge people with their ways?  If you can find out from Szoradryn what he would actually wear, that would help straighten things out between us.  He made something for Szoradryn and he was insulted by how Szoradryn treated him/the thing he made.   Ky buys a 5gp chain from him that she puts in her hair.

Aury asks if he can affix the bead to his scepter.  He says he can make a band for it, but it will be 10gp.   He says I will work on it.  Ky asks where we can find Szoradryn \- and he replies that he runs Silken Secrets.  We wait on him to finish the band. Ains asks if he is willing to purchase an onyx box from him.  He considers it.  He asks how much Ains wants for it and he says 125gp; Ains then says that he will discount it to 120gp and he agrees to buy it.  

Og tries to communicate with his leaf and gets no response.  But he feels the vine twist and he’s able to affix his bead to it.  Ains decides that he will use his mage hand spell to have it circle his head continuously.  Hap will figure out another way to display his bead.

We leave the shop and discuss where to go.  Ains suggests that we split up.  We all go to the mapmaker, passing by the **Glimmerdel Center** (as we realize that each group has their own “headquarters”).  

### **Paths by Stonesniff**

We go into Paths by Stonesniff.  We see curios on display by the door.  We see something large with many legs hanging that casts a shadow (a giant stuffed spider).  There is a large mushroom shaped table in the middle of the room.  There are many lockboxes on one side of the room.  Room spells like old parchment.  This is a hoarder-type place vs. a shop.   Display case made out of umberhulk shell. A stocky, deep gnome runs the place, braided beard w/copper wire \- this is Bolshack Stonesniff.  Maps \- that’s why everyone is here.  Understand this \- the underdark doesn’t stay put, creatures dig, a guide keeps you alive not my maps.  Still better to know where you are going that go in blind.  Hap banters with him \- “so we know where we are going to die.”  “You get it” and they laugh together.  Ains asks what is the point of maps if everything is constantly changing.  But he says he has to do this.  He mentions that if anyone ever has anything that I don’t have that I can display, I’ll give you favor.  Ky immediately shows him her Mummified Otherworldly Tadpole and he loves it and agrees to trade her a bead of Glimmerdel for it.  They do.

Aury then talks to him about the temple of Diverra that he is looking for.  He pulls out a map \- green-ish gray hide.  He shows us where we are on his “**Map of the Underdark**.”  We don’t see anything on this map that looks like it could be where the **Sanctuary of Diverra** is, but Aury looks at the map and thinks that we likely came in between Thallgrove and Nunglthil. He then shows us another map that shows us Rothenloch on it ... and we think we need to find a place on the eastern most edge of the **Thallgrove** area. He explains that the red areas on his map are classically evil; gold would be fine; green would be accepting of most.  Ky asks about Thallgrove and he says that you go mad there (from the spores and fungi).  He says the genling is a little creature who was affected by the bloodwars \- descendent of the myconid.  He hired a genling to be a scout to help create his map.  Ky asks if we can earn favor by finding out new geographical features, and he says he will pay favor for any corrections to his maps.  She asks if we can copy the maps and he says that we can \- so we do.  We gather that Nunglthil is “below” Hap’s home town \- the **Ahndashere**. 

The group discusses the maps of the Underdark and the surface.  Aury’s gut tells him the Sanctuary was lost and no one has seen it since the Awakening…why the question is why?  Only one way in/out?  Some reason no one comes back?  We thank him for his time and leave.  Have the sense that he could recommend a guide if needed.

Og decides to head to **Soothing Spores** and sees a father/son setting up stalls.  He notices the docks and boats heading in and out of the area, and people unloading and going to shops and stalls.

### **Miss Placed Tomes**

The rest of the group heads to Miss Placed Tomes to see if we can get any additional information.  Inside, the walls are thick and the place is quiet.  There is an out of tune chime as we enter.  Tall, narrow shop…there is a smell of brewing tea.  Shelves and books everywhere and a lofted area above with alcoves/reading room.  A silver-haired drow walks over \- this is Dririel.  Ains asks if he has a book on **Spitespirits** and he asks if we have been dealing with Coppereyes.  He says he does not have a book about that, but would put out a finder on it.  He says he will bring over a book that Coppereyes read for Ains to read.  Ains and he discuss that he has been behind a door…and then he reveals that someone borrowed a book that has some information on the murals that show what “could” be behind other doors and he will let Ains borrow it in a stretch.  Aury asks about a book on the missing Sanctuary of Diverra….does he have one?  Books about love and beauty \- and jokes with him about where he is staying.  He says he will find books about historical references and also travelers journals that might help \- and points to an alcove that Aury can use to do research.   Ains then asks about research on cults and he says he has some materials and likes that Ains is mysterious.  He jokes about where Ains is staying.  We go to the alcove and Aury pulls out his calligraphy kit to impress him (he shouts no transcription \- just notes).  He thinks Aury has good handwriting.  

Ains goes to the cult section and it is easy to find…he discovers that the group/person he is studying is crazy (off their rocker).  He reads between the lines and gets the sense that almost everything to date is recorded above the surface.  That most of the clues he wants will be surface-bound.  However, most of the surface research appears to be associated with the root area coming somewhere northwest of the Mahruud or the Broken Deep.  Almost everything has a relationship to something from there.  He is thrown off, however, answers start to appear in a dream state.  Every time something appears to be real and not crazy, it was related to a dream state.

Aury, Hap, and Ky read the research that he brings them.  We find the info about the arcane waypoint…because we know the one we traveled through was specific to followers of Diverra.  These waypoints we know are scattered all over Myrdae and their origin is very, very old \- from the **Tegenwalk Phet** in the days before they were banned.  The Tegenwald (as they were called a long time ago) historically was good with exploration. And that most of the Bathean Empire was magical back then.  There were arcane waypoints that we used widely…then as time went on, restrictions were put in place to limit travel.  Notes are found that talk about how some waypoints misfire…where some come and where they go.  We find out that some arcane waypoints were made with shortcuts by the Tegenwald…to speed up how they were created.  As Hap studies, he finds a journal from a traveler that he finds interesting.  It is old and misplaced \- shouldn’t be in a shop like this.  This is something that should be in the **Advents of Harmony** \- and not here.  *This is truly misplaced.*  This traveler documented routes pre-Awakening.  He talks about a waypoint he found in the place that is now the lake (this was originally a lowland setting before it was flooded).  He notes that there was something different about this particular waypoint (restricted access).  Hap then finds a rough sketch of a map.  It shows hillsides…path went from lowlands into the hillside.  And then shows how the path is blocked \- with an entry about how every time he tried he was blocked.  He said he saw and smelled roses.  So we know we are on the right path. We think the Sanctuary to Diverra is probably somewhere through the Thallgrove…on a path of ascension there somewhere.  

Ains asks him if there is anything he can do for favor.  He asks if he has any books.  Ains has a play that he got from Emberstran theatre that he stole…he shows it to him and says it was from the author.  He says that he will give him favor in exchange.  Dririel hands Ains an amethyst bead with veins of platinum (Selraeth bead) \- giving Ains a bit more favor. 

### **The Soothing Spores**

Og goes into the small shop, the Soothing Spores, between the market stalls.  He steps in and it smells of warm tea and herbs; crowded shelves, jars with dried fungi. An elderly deep gnome greets him…what can I do for you?  Og senses that she is “on” some type of herb.  They introduce each other \- this is **Nelka Nittabomb**.  He tells her that he was intrigued by the name of her shop.  They talk about some energizing snacks, some mood elevating items, some dream-state items.  He tells her what drew him there is the fact that he is going to be visiting the Thallgrove.  She says that she gets products from there.  Og offers to help her with anything to get favor. He asks if she has products that would help with the madness in the Thallgrove.  She says that nothing is guaranteed, but has some items that could help. He then asks about prices for some of her items.  One is an energizing herb (Glowseed allows you to skip a long rest without exhaustion) \= 5cp.  Resistance mushrooms last 8 hours (Valecap Extract \- they numb the mind….so it could hurt some spellcasters) \= 12gp per dose.  One that is a hallucinogen (Mindbloom) is 10gp.  There is another that allows you to explore memories of blocked thoughts \= 15gp.  He decides to purchase Valecap, Glowseed x 6, and a Mindbloom.

He then goes through the trading stalls.  He sees people with basic stuff to sell \- fisher people, craftspeople who carved trinkets from rocks, basic stuff \- people trying to make a living.  This is a generic day in the market.

### **Silken Secrets**

Ky and Ains visit the Silken Secrets, as Hap and Aury go to visit the Trader’s Tankard.   At the shop, Ains walks in.  The place is fairly ornate outside, metal door with shiny steel features.  Purple/violet color scheme inside.  Ains makes the wind blow as he walks in the shop.  He had been starting to get a perfume scent…but once we puffs the air it mixes with the smell of incense.  Tapestry covered floor…very nice and plush. Tall, perfectly dressed drow looks him over and says nothing.  But another drow steps forward to help him.  Ains says “finally a place of worth in this town” and is rolling a gem in his finger.  He says a place with class, dignity, etc.  Ains name drops Endavo.  They then have a discussion about how velvet and purple are his colors.  He asks the other drow to help him decide what color that Ains is (he mentions the name of the guy that Coppereyes needs information from…and that guy walks up). Then Ky walks in.  She asks for a fitting room and she goes inside and changes into her fancy green dress.  Meanwhile, Ains discusses colors with him.  He asks him about his style…and asks how he normally dresses.  He is focusing on him and thinks something is familiar, but Ains cannot place him.  He says he is impeccable and not to compare himself.  He says he wants to visit **Ys’Nonterien** and wants to make a good impression and needs his help.  He then remembers him from the surface \- that someone close to Ains (**Tsulanan** \-maybe this is his uncle or father) was someone he dealt with.  Who is Tsulanan to you?  This drow says he adventured with Tsulanan.  When they were in **Ahnassa**, fanatics came and took Tsulanan in the night.  He then goes to get a journal and brings it to Ains.  He feels a ring around his neck burn.  This is a journal that I tried to read and cannot.  Ains then tries to mage hand the book from him and does.  When it is in his hand Ains’ hand is warm.  Ky comes out of the fitting room.  She asks about an accessory for the dress and the two drow confer and offer something to her to try.  While distracted, Ains tries to read the journal, but it is gibberish.  He feels like this is the biggest find he has come across.  They tell Ky that the scarf is 8 silver pieces and that she looks very nice.  She thinks it is strange that they are complimenting her but thinks this is a fair price.  Ains then tells the drow that the man is his grandfather, but that he hasn’t revealed that to many people because the fanatical cult group is after him.  He has been looking for pieces of information about his grandfather.  Ky picks up on this conversation, pays them the 8 sp for the scarf, and intimidates the drow into giving him the book.  The drow just asks that if Ains gets answers that he will come back and tell them.  Ky comes back out of the dressing room, picks up her scarf and is now wearing her fine blue cloak.  Ains tells him he is Anaron.  The drow tells her about the fine spider silk that the scarf is made of \- will offer special properties above ground when she’s on the surface (rain won’t penetrate, will keep her cool).  He says this is the finest silk YOU will ever have.  And she says, “Have you ever been to Dunduar?”  As he almost spits and says no, she says, “I didn’t think so…you have a nice day” and leaves.

### **The Trader’s Tankard**

Meanwhile, at the pub, which overlooks the Rothenloch (both peaceful and eerie), there is a hum of conversation. There is a bar in the middle and booths around the sides.  They see Durak members here.  “What can I get you?”  Beer.  Beer it is.  Would you rather have a mug each or a gallon?  They each get a mug and they look over and they see **She’nog** in here having a drink.  Well \- I didn’t think I’d see you here, but here you are.  Want to join us?  Sure \- if you are in the mood for a sad tale.  She says her fishing hole is not well.  She says she can’t get in because of **Stone Giants**.  They are just throwing rocks into the water…so she can’t get in.  They aren’t even fishing.  Aury offers to take care of the problem.
