She says she and her sisters at the **Fishery & Smokehouse** would be very pleased.  But she says “do you want to be crushed by boulders?”  She says she saw 2 of them…they are giants.  If you think you can handle them, I’ll take you there.  Hap asks how big are they again?  And they laugh.  Aury suggests that maybe they could be talked into moving to another fishing hole.  She says she knows of another one or two places that maybe we could move them to \- especially one that is one of her competitors' places.  They say that they will get their other members to come and help.  

The rest of the group meets Hap and Aury at the tavern and learns about the giant problem.  We spend the day in the town and hang out and then go back to the Draught to spend the night.  

We think Thallgrove is where we need to go to find a path of ascension up there to find the Sanctuary.

We know we could commission a boat to take us or ask She’nog to take us \- and perhaps help her along the way.

NEXT TIME \- we will start the next day and hear about Ains book study and make a decision about where we are going. 

– The End of the Session – 

# **17 \- Giant’s Silver**

**Session Date**:  January 28, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  26th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Nunglthil

### **25th of Talil in the Darkbottom Draught**

We all meet back at the Draught and go to our rooms (Ains, Og, and Ky’tha in one room; Hap and Aury in one; Zymve has her own room that she gets at a discount because of her 6-bead favor she’s earned). Ains is interested in the journal he got and starts to flip through it, but he can’t read it (strange, angular script).  But he studies it and stares at it and something starts to become clear:  this isn’t a language that is meant to be translated.  He starts to feel pressure behind his eyes and gets a mild headache…and Og and Ky notice that he is getting a soft glow around his neck (from his family necklace).  The script starts to reveal words now \- and he starts to read the title which says “Waiting Beneath the Name.”  He can’t make out words underneath…so he flips pages and the journal starts to feel heavy, weighing him down.  He then makes out another section “When Waking Fails.”  Og and Ky notice he looks very uncomfortable.  He then flips more and it says “The Key Ring.”  He is really hurting now.  The glow on the necklace fades and he can’t read anything…so he closes the journal and falls asleep promptly.  

A dream comes back to Ains \- it is of he and Og in Emberstran running.   As Ains sleeps, Og suggests that he and Ky pick up the book. Og can’t make anything out so he passes it to Ky and she thinks maybe it can be decoded, but nothing is obvious.  They both go to bed.

### **26th of Talil**

We all wake up the next day and go down to the dining room to eat breakfast.  The same person (Tikrin) asks “the usual” and we say yes \- but each picks the type of meal.  We talk about Aury’s finer tastes \- especially since he has given up so much (i.e., women \- “the only woman in my life is my goddess.”).   Ky comments on Zymve’s appearance and asks if she had a big day yesterday. She replies that it is too early for questions.  

The group discusses what we’d like to do today and talk about taking care of the stone giant problem for She’nog’s sister or going ahead to hire a boat to take the group to Thallgrove.  We discuss the warnings about Thallgrove and talk about possible ways to mitigate some of the danger (potion Og purchased, covering our noses and mouths). Zymve tells the group that the spores in Thallgrove would enter via the skin. More discussion about giants or Thallgrove.  Aury points out that we might not be able to come back to this area once we get to the sanctuary of Diverra \- assuming we find it.  

We decide to help She’nog’s sister with the stone giant problem.  Hap, Aury, Ky, and Og go to find She’nog.  Zymve goes to the Soothing Spores and Ains goes to see Copper Eyes. 

### **Gemcraft Store**

Ains enters the store.  He talks to Copper Eyes about what he should have made for the drow.  He finds out that he had made the previous item out of gold and a ruby.  Ains suggests that he uses platinum and amethyst/purple gems.  He is happy with the suggestion and says that if it works, he will give Ains some favor.   Ains also asks if he has another 300gp diamond.

### **Soothing Spores Shop**

Zymve steps into the Soothing Spores and talks to Nelka.  She asks her about fungi \- specifically about Thallgrove.  She tells her that she learned everything she knows from a little guy who is not often around \- his type is resistant to the fungi there.  He told her that very few people go in and come back out…that people go mad when they get there.  How do you protect yourself?  Don’t go in.  I have some extracts that might help \- Valecap extracts (10 potions).  I can sell them to you for 9gp/each.  I will sell you 2 for 17gp.  She agrees and purchases the extract.  

### **Fishery & Smokehouse**

The rest of the group makes their way to the docks near the Fishery & Smokehouse.  We don’t see She’nog, but are standing in front of the establishment and smell the aroma of fish.  We decide to go in and find She’nog’s sister.  This is a 2-story place \- the lower floor has fresh fish and upstairs is a loft area with preservation racks.  We see a fair-skinned, dark auburn hair half-orc female and another up top who is similar looking (maybe twins), both who favor She’nog.  We greet one of them and tell why we are there.  Soon after, She’nog arrives.  She is disappointed in her catch of the day so far…the quantity of the fish is lower than usual.  We tell her that we will help her and ask what we could get in exchange \- and they answer that the Kar’solar would be happy with our help (would give us favor).  Ky asks if there are some in that group that would be able to sail us to Thrallgrove.  She says she knows of a couple of ships, but maybe the only one big enough is Valor’s Steelwake…or the duergar Grendal might have the best one to take us.   About that time, Ains and Zymve come in and we talk about going today.

### **Giant Contraption**

We make our way out to She’nog’s boat and we board, after she makes is stable and safe for travel.  Og volunteers to help paddle.  Hap says he will, too.  We make our way across Rothenloch \- not the way we came initially, but to her fishing hole. Hap says he would like to send Fleur up as we start to get close. We travel about an hour and she says the hole is around the bend and Hap asks her to stop to allow Fleur to fly up.  As soon as Fleur goes around the bend, she sees that the fishing hole is right there. She’nog, meanwhile, ties off to the side of the lake where there is a slight path (not cliffs).  Fleur sees a wide ring of massive boulders and a giant standing there and reach into the hole and pull up a bucket and then turn and dump it out. And she sees a massive pickaxe where it looks like the giant has been working.  It looks like he’s created a well \- but a dry well.  Hap tells the group what Fleur saw.  The well is 40-50’ off shore in the water, and is about 25’ wide and deep enough for another giant to be standing inside, handing the bucket up to the giant on top. We discuss what the giants might be doing.  

Ains and Og decide to walk over on the path, conversing in giant “what a nice night.”  They hear one of the giants yell “we have company”.  Ky’tha (15) and Zymve (invisible) stealth behind them; with Hap and Aury coming afterwards (by 90’).  “You are not welcome here” they yell and they tell Ains and Og to leave.  Ains and Og try to play the “brotherhood” card, but it doesn’t seem to work well.  The giant tells them they do not belong here.  “Why” asks Ains.  They say they have taken claim of this area.  Ains says they are reps of the “Boulder Throwers” of the DIstancion Mountains and they are here to offer them aid, comfort, and money.  “How much money” the giant asks.  The giant says to come back in 3 days so I can bargain with you.  Og asks them what they are doing and the giant says that they should know if they are brothers.  Ains says they deal with matters of the sky and not water.  Ains tries to persuade them to tell him what they are doing.  The giant says he will tell them what they are doing if Ains and Og will leave.  Og says they will come back in 3 days.   The giant says they are mining here and it has taken them days to build this.  Ains asks what they are mining for.  They say “the stuff in the hole” and come back in 3 days and we won’t be here and you can do whatever you want.  Ains turns around and “pretends” like he is talking to the Chieftain who is around the corner.  The giants don’t buy this.  

IT IS INITIATIVE \- Combat vs. the Stone Giants

* ROUND ONE:  Aury moves up to Og, Ains, and Ky’tha and casts bless on each of them.  Zymve casts darkness on one of the giants.  We hear the giant moving rocks and he steps outside of the darkness and tosses a rock to Ains and Aury casts reaction of warding flare to make the giant disadvantaged and Ains shields himself and the giant misses and steps back into the darkness.  Ains touches Og and casts a spell and hastes him and then steps back further.  The other giant climbs out of the well and sets down the big bucket.  Og runs into the darkness and blindly swings at the giant and hits twice. Hap runs forward and has Fleur fly to the second giant we can see and delivers a chill touch spell and misses.  Ky’tha dashes forward and then takes a bonus action to hide.    
* ROUND TWO:  Aury moves up next to Hap and waits to see if there is a need for a reaction.  Zymve casts a spell on the giant she can see \- dissonant whispers \- and he takes damage and is forced to move to the other side of the well’s ring and is frightened of Zymve.  The first giant moves out of the darkness and swings his axe at Ky’tha.  He misses the first swing, but hits with the second attack and she dodges half of the damage.  Ains casts a spell on the giant using Aury to help aim and misses.  The second giant reaches down and grabs two boulders and throws them to Hap and Aury.  He misses Aury, fumbling.  And he misses Hap, too, and falls prone.  Og moves up and attacks the giant on the ground and misses (but grazes) and then attacks again and hits twice.  Hap has Fleur deliver a chill touch on the prone giant. Ky’tha steadies herself and has advantage and hits with her dagger…but has to stay put.  
* ROUND THREE:  Aury stays put and casts guiding bolt on the giant next to Ky’tha and misses.  Zymve casts a cloud of daggers on the giant nearest her.  That giant then moves out of the cloud and attacks Ky’tha again, missing first, then hitting her \- and she again, dodges some of the damage.  He then moves near Zymve.  Ains asks Aury for help and shoots a spell at the prone giant with disadvantage and hits.  That giant then stands up and tries to grab Og and does and then drops Og into the pit 50’ down, damaging him.  At the bottom, he sees the biggest silver ore chunk he has ever seen.  That giant moves around the ring of the well.  Og tries to climb out of the well and gets almost to the top, but can’t get all the way out, as he slips a little. He then uses an action to try to get up and slides a little further down and is now 10’ from the top of the well.  Hap moves up to the giant close by and hits with his new weapon and hits, then tries to hit with his dagger and hits.  He then attacks again with the new weapon and hits again.  Then attacks with the dagger again and hits and kills it.  Ky’tha steadies herself and shoots her hand crossbow and the remaining giant and hits.  
* ROUND FOUR:  Aury moves a little and stays close to Ains and casts light on himself so Ains can see close up.  Zymve moves up closer to the remaining giant and casts dancing lights on the giant to light him up and inspires Og.  Ains thinks the giant is bloodied and glowing purple energy flows around him and he lifts off the ground and he shoots a lightning bolt at the giant, who saves, but still takes damage.  The giant looks at Og and says “representatives my ass,” and dives into the water and starts swimming away.  Og climbs out of the well and jumps in the water to swim after him.  He attacks and misses all three efforts.  Hap tells Fleur to fly to him and he tries to chill touch and misses.  Ky’tha dashes forward to the end of the well, shouting to She’nog to take her boat out.  
* ROUND FIVE:  Aury cast a fireball into the water, trying to hit the giant.  He does, but the damage is lessened because of the water.  Zymve dashes forward 60’.  Ains steps to the shore and shoots a sorcerers’ burst and hits the giant.  The giant turns around and swims to Og and tries to grab him and gets him and swims down with him, taking his loin cloth off.  Og attacks underwater and hits with 3 attacks, hitting critically on the last hit, then hits with a fourth attack, killing him.  The group sees water pool up and then Og pops up and swims to shore.  

Ky’tha and Zymve peer down into the hole, as Zymve moves her dancing lights into the well and they see a mound of silver below the surface.  Og says he saw one chunk of silver down there.  She’nog moves her boat around the bend and sees the dead giants and the well.  Ky’tha takes two ropes and ties them together and affixes it to the bucket and ties it off to the boat.  We lower Hap down on the bucket as Og climbs down.  Once they are down, Ky’tha goes down.  Ky looks at the ore and can’t make out the total size and Hap tries to swim under the water to figure it out, too. Both aren’t sure how big it is.  They try to lower Ains into the hole and he takes damage, hitting his head as their grip slips.

Ains tries to shatter some of the rock around the ore with his spell.  He thinks it will be 100 pounds or more of silver.  He is able to loosen the ore from the rock.  We decide to get the people up out of the hole with ropes and then tie off the ore to have Og and Hap pull it up.  We get it to the top and Og carries it on to the boat.  We tie the bucket on to the boat and one of us travels in the bucket (Ky’tha).  Travel back to the town is slower, but we arrive back at the dock. 

She’nog speaks on our behalf to her sisters upon our arrival and we are each issued favor of Kar’Solar.  The group decides to take the ore to sell to the duergars.

### **Firmforge**

The group goes to the forge \- Firmforge \- to try to sell the ore.  As they walk in, there is a large forge and three anvils.  There are two gray skinned dwarves and we talk to them about it.  They agree that this is 200 pounds.  We tell them that we think it is worth 1000gp, and they counter with 800gp.  Ains counters with 850 plus favors for each, but Ky’tha is not convinced.  Zymve flaunts her favor and convinces them to give us 900gp, plus favor. 

### **A Ride on Grindle’s Chain**

After the sale, we go to try to find a captain to agree to take us the next day.  We see a cargo ship that looks captained by a duergar, that looks like it could take all of us.  Aury asks about the extract he asked Zymve to buy and she gives it to him (says she bought one for 12gp).  We approach the captain, Grindle Ashwake.  He initially tells us 36gp for the lot of us for 3 days, but Ky’tha mentions that we just took down a couple of stone giants, and Zymve intimidates him so we only have to pay 30gp.  He says to provision up and we will leave soon.

### **Message from Lady Rook**

We all grab our supplies from the Draught and Og goes to buy fishing supplies. We meet back at the docks and board the ship.  We pull out of the port.  Ky’tha hears a voice in her head “The Council has decided and Lady Rook is appreciative.  She has concerns about your locations with the marrowstone and she could use your help back in Emberstran.”  As we travel, we hear fishermen yelling at each other over the water \- arguing about their fishing hole.  Ains uses a sending spell to put words into one’s head to make them fight each other \- stirring up trouble.  We get a rest that night

### **27th of Talil \- next morning**

As we continue and are waking up, Zymve feels like the ceiling of the cavern is crashing and opening…and it does.  As the rocks shatter into the water, tentacles reach up out of the water and grab her.  She doesn’t see any of her friends.  Suddenly the vision ends and he gasps and is out of breath, waking up.  She thinks she was possessed by the Shadow of Rothenloch.  We all see that she is uncomfortable and gasping.  We discuss what happened to her.  We think it was a dream, but she thinks it was real.

### **28th of Talil \- next morning**

We see that Grindle is avoiding someone as we travel.  He points and Zymve and Ky’tha see smugglers in the distance, but he navigates us around them without incident.  We finally pass them.

### **29th of Talil \- next day**

We end up making it to a shore and see a massive cave mouth opening up ahead.  There are no torches lit, but we see light ahead in the cave.  We smell rich soil, sweet bark…we think we have made it to **Thallgrove**.  We part ways with the Captain.

### **Cave to enter Thallgrove**

We make our way to the cave and colors begin to emerge.  The air is cooler.  We see fungi of various colors.  Blues, rust colors…some are small, but others are the size of trees.  We notice tiny motes and spores that are filling the air.  Ky’tha and everyone wrap scarves around their faces, only leaving their eyes exposed. Time to make wisdom saving throws.  Ains has tourette’s syndrome telepathically and starts saying things in other party member’s heads.  Aury is fine.  Hap swears that the fungi is talking to him. Og also thinks the fungi are talking to him.  Zymve is okay for now. Ky’tha starts getting very emotional and crying for no reason.  Her tears almost glows.  The air is starting to make us a little feverish, like our skin is crawling.  

### **Dead People**

Up ahead we see a boot print that fungus has attached to and there is a coil of rope on ground.  There are uneven steps.  And then a body with a breastplate and sword sitting next to them.  Eyes stare ahead.  We see fungus growing up around the body.  We see another person up ahead in robes lying face down, grasping a tome, with fungus growing up around them.  Ains pulls the tome to him \- it is a spellbook.  He casts detect magic and sees none other than the tome.  Aury examines the bodies to see if he can tell what is going on.  He thinks lesser restoration might be the only thing that could help.

### **Makra the Genling**

We travel further.  Ky’tha notices movement ahead.  She sees a small creature ahead…small and wiry.  Unblinking eyes.  She asks \- are you a [genling](https://www.dndbeyond.com/monsters/6091146-genling-minion)?  A thought presses into Og’s mind:  “*Wow \- you are big.*”  Og replies out loud “yes, I am big.”  Zymve hears “*you understand, don’t you?*”  And she says in her head “yes, I do.”  But they do not understand her either.  Ky’tha tries to touch her and tries to talk to her, but she doesn’t understand.  Same with Ains.  Finally, Zymve speaks in deep speech and they can talk.  “*Why are you here?*” they ask in deep speech telepathically.  Zymve responds that we are looking for a temple.  He says **Makra**…introducing himself.  He says he will take us home to his village.  Ky’tha asks Zymve to ask if they are genlings.  She does and he responds that they are.  Zymve agrees that we will go to his village.  Aury casts Lesser Restoration on Ky’tha to make her stop crying and it works.  Zymve asks if he knows how we can avoid the effects of the land.  He doesn’t, but thinks maybe someone in his village will know.

The session ends as we are walking to Makra’s genling village. 

(Each character has gained enough XP to make it to level 7\)   
– The End of the Session – 

# **18 \- In Bimblefol**

**Session Date**:  February 17, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  29th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Thallgrove

### **Escort to Genling Village**

Walking in the cavern with violet and amber glow from fungi, we see spores in the air (land like snowflakes).  We are following Makra.  Ky’tha is no longer crying.  Makra guides the group through various tunnels.  He speaks telepathically to Zymve “the ground listens” telling the group to be quiet here and there (holding his finger up to his trunk). We move around and the fungi shift colors \- sometimes more blue, others orange, yellow, etc.  We continue and pause here and there…then pick the pace back up.

Whenever Makra sees/hears anyone in the group talk he says to Zymve “Bimblefol” and tilts his head to the side.  Then continues.  Every now and then Zymve notices that Makra is hitting a piece of fungus with his staff (Og and Hap hear “ouch”).  He throws those pieces in a sack.  He is collecting while we walk, but no one really notices this.  

We enter another area and the fungi is very thick here.  We see him “sniffing” the air.  He darts over to a wall…and barely visible he reaches and grabs and tiny violet pod and he sniffs it and it pulses and he runs over to Og and gives it to him.  Og tries to listen and thinks it is praying.  Makra tells Zymve that Og should eat it.  Og eats it.  Hap hears it say “oh gross” as Og chews it.  Og is then cured of his condition.   Makra says that is a rare fungi, but he will look for it.   Zymve asks if it cures everything and he says it does.  

We continue on. As Ains continues to twitch and yell things, Aury cures him (lesser restoration).  We continue forward.  

### **Thall Mound Chamber**

There is a waterfall in this new cavern and it is orange colored.  The far back wall suddenly looks like it shifts.  We all hear Makra make an audible noise and he hides.  We see an outline of a shadowy creature hold something up and start to eat it (it is one of Makra’s buddies).   

IT IS INITIATIVE:  Combat vs. Thall Mounds

* **ROUND ONE:** Ains casts haste on Og and shoves him forward 5’. Zymve casts a spell on the creature and it failed its save, taking psychic damage and disadvantage on his next attack then runs to hide behind a close fungi.  Makra hid by the back wall with his staff at the ready.  Og flies into a rage and moves forward and attacks him three times, hitting all times.  He then tries to restrain him with vines, but the giant succeeds.  Spores fall off of the creature onto Og and he fails his CON save and is stunned. He then reaches down and hits Og and is grappled and has the poisoned condition.  He then attacks again and fumbles, falls, then stands back up. And takes Og and moves forward.  Aury casts a spell on him and the creature fails his save and takes radiant damage. Aury then moves near Zymve.  Another creature suddenly emerges from another mound near Zymve and Aury and attacks Hap with spores, stunning him.  He then moves next to Zymve and grabs her, giving her temp hit points, as she takes damage, as she is grappled and has the poisoned condition.  He then moves forward to attack Aury with Zymve in hand and misses him.  Ky’tha moves up to hide from the one holding Og and tries to shoot the new one, but misses.  Hap is stunned, but frees himself at the end of his turn.  
* **ROUND TWO**: Ains casts a spell to make himself purple and then moves forward a little and casts a sorcerer’s burst at the one on Og and hits with thunder damage, then moves back.  Zymve casts mirror image on herself and then tries to charm the creature. Makra tries to use his blow gun and misses.  Og is stunned and continues to be stunned.  The creature with Og tries to shove him down his throat and does (he is still grappled, stunned, poisoned, restrained, and would take acid damage (but he’s resistant).  He then moves forward and tries to stun Ky’tha with spores.  He then burrows 10’ into the ground. Aury casts a spell on the one nearest him and hits as the creature takes radiant damage.  That creature then releases spores onto Aury, as he sets Zymve down.  Aury is stunned and he tries to grab him and does \- he is grappled, poisoned, and takes damage.   The creature then moves forward to attack Hap and fumbles, allowing Hap an attack of opportunity, but he misses.  Hap tries to run away toward the hole where the creature burrowed and the other creature misses with his attack of opportunity.  Hap makes it to the hole and attacks that creature and then frightens him.  He then misses with his second attack.  He tries a third time and hits. Ky’tha moves to the hole and attacks the creature.  Ky hits the creature that is holding Aury with a sneak attack and doesn’t move.  
* **ROUND THREE**: Ains moves away from creature two and tries to pull Aury from the creature, and he is released from the grapple. He then sorcerer’s burst that creature and inflicts thunder damage. Zymve dashes to the hole and gives an inspiration to Hap. Makra crits on the creature near Aury with his blow gun, making him have disadvantage on his next attack.  Og breaks from the stun, but he’s still in the hole and being compressed. He is no longer poisoned.  He has two points of exhaustion. The creature rolls around and grabs Hap, grappling him, poisoning him, and doing some damage.  He takes damage from a hellish rebuke (just 2 points), but it kills him anyway.  Hap is no longer grappled.  Aury is stunned and breaks it at the end of his turn.  He is no longer grappled, poisoned, or stunned.  The creature then tries to grab Aury again and Aury fires off a flare and the creature fumbles.  He tries to hit Aury again and does \- he is grappled and stunned.  He then moves toward Ky’tha and is no longer charmed.  Hap runs over to the other creature and misses with his first attack, then hits.  He then hits two more times.  He is frightened of Hap now and takes damage.  Ky’tha disengages and moves back, but gets to shoot her bow with advantage from vex (last turn) and hits with a sneak attack, then hides behind a rock outcropping.  
* **ROUND FOUR**:  Ains hits with a sorcerer’s burst and does more damage to the creature \- then moves to the hole and uses telekinetic shove to impose advantage for Og to try to get out.  Zymve uses mockery on the creature, who fails, and he takes psychic damage and is now disadvantaged.  Makra uses mind sliver to impose psychic damage on the creature.  Og tries to push the dead creature off him and crits \- pushing the guy out of the hole with ease.  He then crawls out of the hole, muddy and smelly, and moves to the other creature and hits twice, killing it.  

Makra runs over to Zymve and says “wow” and then sees the dead creatures in the hole \- 2 of his friends and says “Bimblefol” and points.  He is asking us to take the two with us.  Og runs over to grab them and picks them up.

Ains keeps his telepathic bond with Og.

### **Bimblefol**

We make our way to Bimblefol.  There are huge plant structures rising from a lake in a huge cavern with rope bridges stretched between them. The structure in the center is a little different \- more fungal covered.  Makra takes us to a small hut at the edge of the lake.  Another Makra is there \- a female. She checks us out and then nods at Makra and grabs a small decanter off of a shelf (top is glowing).  She pours tiny cups of this liquid and hands them to each of us.  We smell earthy sweetness and we all take the shot (except Ains who waits to see what happens).  We feel a slight headache and then we can now hear them.  She says “you can hear us” to each one of us.  Ky’tha says “can you hear us.” and she says “I’m Betuu.”  Makra tells her that we didn’t all fall to glow.  And she says “not yet.”  She says you may walk the bridges.  Ky’tha asks Betuu about the violet fungi.  Betuu asks Makra if he found any.  He says he only found one and Batuu says “maybe Tatti has it.”  Og says “I’m very sorry about this, but where shall I put them (referring to the dead bodies he carries)?”  And she says “I’ll get someone \- let them rest here.”  

Aury asks if there is a sanctuary around here.  Betuu says that we should go to the elder (Aniba) if we want to find information (and get distracted with images in the mind and touch and taste and smell)  Makra says he will take us.  Betuu points to various mushrooms and tells us who is where.  As we start to go, we head to the Watch tower (we see several gemlings with blowguns).  Zymve goes first across the first bridge.  It holds her fine and she crosses by herself.  Ains steps on it and crosses \- no issues.  Aury then goes across after Ains has finished his trek across.  We hear gemlings talk about how big we all are.  After he finishes, Ky’tha starts to cross as it sags and creaks.  After she finishes, Hap crosses, and it creaks again and sags, but he makes it.  Og decides to stay at the hut, but Makra insists that he comes along.  He grabs the chest to start dragging it across. Og starts to try to cross and the bridge gets really close to the water in the center, but he makes it across to the Watch Tower.  We hear other gemlings badmouth Makra for bringing the group to their village. They say “well, we are standing watch.”  They are looking for threatening creatures.  There are big creatures that float and have a bunch of eyeballs and others that fly and have leather wings.  We get the feeling that they are upset with us for disrupting their watch.  Aury tries to convince them that since we are not a danger don’t focus on us.

### **Bimblefol Sanctum**

We then proceed to the bridge to the Sanctum.   This was a large thick, hollowed out rock.  We step inside and it is cool with petrified mushroom that looks and feels like stone.  There is a big open area.  There is a big, plump fat gemling in a huge, oversized chair at the far side.  Behind her we see a shelf with strange objects \- bones, metal…we aren’t sure.   These don’t look like Underdark items \- she is a collector of other items.  “Ahhhh-large walkers.  Upright thunder shapes.  Ummm..Makra…good collectibles, I approve.”  She speaks “stronger.”  You stand before Aniba and we see a symbol on the ground \- Zymve looks at it.  I have survived, I say seated.  All important matters pass through me.  She looks at Og \- you are damp with service and it is fascinating.  Zymve makes out “wisdom’s final form” on the ground.  How can Aniba assist you?  The group looks at Aury.  “I have been sent by my goddess to find her Sanctuary.”  I have been told it may be in this area.  “Bimblefol has no such sanctuary, for I am the wisdom in its final form.”  Ains speaks up \- “my acquaintance only seeks guidance here because no other place has such reverence here as this place.  Truly his goddess sent us to you.”  Who might be his goddess?  The goddess of roses and love.  Ahhhh \- Diverra.  My friend here would like to know if there is any chance that your servants have looked around the area and might have found something \- a place touched by Diverra.  She says she might know of a place because she is wise.  She pats the side of her big chair \- the forgotten surface grove \- I know what you speak of.  Meanwhile, Zymve sees a dog skull, a pressed flower, a horseshoe.  Ains asks if she will grace us with directions to this place.  She says that she can’t take us.  Ains asks if Makra can take us and she says that she has a map.  She says maps are valuable, what do you have to offer.   Aury offers a tinderbox, Ky’tha offers a fancy blue cloak, Og offers a bottle of wine.  She seems interested in the wine and offers to swap the map for it.  She hands the map to Og…and also the dog skull because she thinks the wine was too much.  She loves it and samples it.  

We examine the map.  It is a fungal parchment that looks delicate.  Og can almost see through it.  She says it is special and take care of it.  We see Bimblefol on the map and as Og turns it, he thinks he sees the part she is talking about.  She has lots of maps under her chairs.  On the map there is an X…and lots of big wavy lines on it (we think it might be water).  She says “oh \- this might be a problem \- none of you breathe water.”  Ains thinks she is very confident in what she is saying.  She asks can you bend the weave to your favor?  If not, head to Tatti.  Anything else for the wise, she asks.  Zymve asks if they have music.  Not really.  Ains asks if they have arcane components (things to bend the weave).   She asks where we are from \- we say the surface, but came via Nunglthil.  She says, “you should visit the wisdom before you leave.”  Aury doesn’t think this is an arcane waypoint, but he feels like there is a sense of the weave here in this room.  Aury asks if there is anything she wants.  She says \- will you visit me from the surface.  He says “I don’t see why not.”  She longs for trinkets from the surface \- things that don’t belong in the Underdark.  You are friends of the wisdom and I shall call you “friends of the wisdom.”  You should feel blessed.  Aury casts comprehend languages to read the inscription on the floor. 

### **Bimblefol Soup Kitchen**

Og and Zymve take off to the next mushroom to find the soup.  Makra is yelling “there’s rules, there’s rules.”  But the two are ahead of everyone else.  As they walk into the Soup Kitchen, they see a long stone counter with large kettles that have deep speech above them.   There are several gemlings in line.  One cuts in line and there is a loud bang from another (Kasem) “line has order and order has line” and “no broth for you.”  They are in line. 

The rest of the group goes to the Soup Kitchen.  Zymve gets the Rootcoil Steep; Og gets Stoneflower Bisque; Ky’tha steps out to pour out her waterskin and then gets the violet soup in her skin to take with her (it will remove minor spore symptoms); Ains takes the Stoneflower Bisque; Aury takes Violet Pod Mash and pours it in Ky’tha’s waterskin; Hap takes the Violet Pod mash and eats it immediately.  Makra gives some ingredients to Kasem.

### **Bimblefol Commons**

As the group crosses over the bridge to the Commons, Aury and Ky’tha eat a ration. Bridges dip; we see gemlings making bets.  We make it over and see that everyone is just hanging out.  This is an open area with lofts \- people who have nowhere else to be, hang out here.  Zymve decides to try to play her instrument for the group.  She shares an original song and everyone looks up to watch and hear her.  She plays very well.  Some like this and start trying to pick up a beat, as they watch Ains try to dance.  Some come down from the lofts and gather around.  They ask who we are and what is happening.  This seems special since it is noise they aren’t used to.  Some get disturbing close, but she keeps them at bay.  We think we could rest here if needed.  She says “You are the most cultured people I have ever encountered.”  They are excited and clap for her and she feels amazing.  Zymve says she is going to stay here while we go find Tatti.  Ains creates a bond with her.

### **Bimblefol Alchemist**

We then leave to go find Tatti, the genling alchemist.  We get to the Chief mushroom and there are two ways to go.  We see a door that is closed (upper level) and then head over to the Mixologist.  Ains asks Makra about his opinion of the Chief and he says “I like Aniba.”  Ky’tha asks what he is doing behind the closed door and Makra replies that he is sleeping.  

The group goes to the next mushroom.  It is very bright inside.  There are many glowing bulbs hanging inside.  It is the closest we have been to the surface since we arrived in the Underdark.  Herbal smell, not neat.  Many bottles \- stuff everywhere.  Boxes overflowing with fungi.  Bundles of roots.  Various vials.  A bald gemling standing in the center wrapped in strips of fungus that are so layered that it seems like a robe (browns, rusts, grays).  She is pouring liquids.  She says in our heads “do not bump into that table.”  She says “oh \- large ones” and pauses.  You carry sunlight things.  She says we smell of sky.  We ask if she has ever been to the surface and she has not.  Aury looks around to read what she has. 

Ky’tha trades her candle for a Stillbreath Filter (waterbreathing for 1 hour).  Aury offers a Censer of Levitation for 3 potions of Stillbreath Filter.  Og offers some leaves in exchange for a Stillbreath Filter.  And offers the dog skull for a Violet Quiet Pod elixir. 

Hap pulls out a block of incense and lights it up.  He says you can have this for a Stillbreath Filter.   She says she needs more and Ky’tha intimidates her, asking Hap to light it again.  And she allows the trade. 

We head back to the Commons to get a rest.  We get a long rest. 

### **30th of Talil \- Leaving Bimblefol**

The next day, we leave the village and follow the path on the map that we believe will lead us to the Lost Sanctuary of Diverra.  The path leads through a shallow channel.  Water is clear looking, but we think it isn’t as clear as it looks (can’t see our feet, but it looks clear).  We make our way through the tunnels lined with fungi and water.  We have to crouch sometimes, tunnels vary by size.  We get several hours away and the stream deepens into a tunnel that tightens and we see a path up ahead that we are going to have to climb.  

Ky’tha climbs up and ties her rope off to something and throws the other end down.  Aury makes his way up.  They are on a natural shelf (dry air).  Hap goes next \- no issues (20).  Zymve goes next and she makes it up easily.  Og then goes up without issue.  Ains then ties off to the rope and pulls him up without issue.  All are now on the shelf.

We press on through another tunnel.  It is quiet. We start to feel that we are getting further from the fungi, from Thallgrove.  

### **Rest for the Night – Ky’tha’s Hallucination**

After another 3-4 hours, we find an opening where we can set up camp for the night.  We set up watch. Aury and Ky’tha take first watch.  Aury thinks about getting to the sanctuary \- he looks over at Ky’tha and she is zoning out, too.  Ky’tha is zoning out and gets lost in her thoughts, thinking am I here, not here as the stone dissolves around her.  A memory awakens, as if the time in the Thallgrove awakened something tucked away forever.  She is in the middle of Dunduar’s ancestral hall, lined with precious metals.  She feels a call/pull as the metals focus her on the symbol of Muerg in the halls of Dunduar (she had never noticed this before).  Her family stands before her \- “*we cannot leave it open.  It will be sealed forever.*”  Then she hears “*why*” (from someone she doesn’t know \- they must be Cragfury).  She sees her grandmother.  That Cragfury says “*the flow must remain.*”  Aury thinks Ky’tha is daydreaming, and doesn't see danger.  Aury thinks Ky’tha is excited about something.  Ky’tha sees Graddock slam his fist “*flow destroys, we bind it, we seal it…and laughs…we prosper*.”  Grandmother looks at her.  Then the memory shifts–she’s at the bottom of a mine shaft.  Further down the mineshaft is a beautifully carved circular stone ring around the tunnel, the stone accented by metal threads, like veins through the stone–enlightened with the weave.  She sees a recession on the side of the stone and her locket glows. Ky’tha’s heart beats faster.  Aury thinks something is up \- is concerned.  Ky’tha hears “*seal it forever*” and the other guy says “*nothing is forever beneath the stone*” and the mine collapses into water (something very out of place and more of a vision than a memory at this time).  The shocking change pulled her back to reality.  Aury and Ky discuss what happened…was this the past or a foretelling?  Was this the effects of fungi?  Who knows?  Ky’tha feels like she needs to go home…to make sure all is well.

– The End of the Session –

# **19 \- Lost Sanctuary**

**Session Date**:  March 2, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  30th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Underdark

Ky’tha and Aury wake up Og and Ains.  We brief them on the fact that the fungi might cause hallucinations, but otherwise, there was no danger.  Aury and Ky’tha get rest, while the other two are at watch. They talk about the water and what to expect, specifically about octopus/octopi…and do we talk to them or fight them, should we encounter them in the water.  Watch is uneventful and they wake up Zymve and Hap and get rest.  They briefly talk \- Hap says “it is pret’ner feels like home” as they talk about the swamp and gators \- he describes what they are to Zymve.  Night passes uneventfully.

### **The Crimson Lake — 31st of Talil, 1246**

We gather everything up and head out.  Ky’tha and Zymve lead \- about 20’ ahead of Og and Hap; followed by Ains and Aury.  After a couple of hours, we see a dim, constant red light ahead and the cavern opens up.  There is a massive underground lake in front of us.  The red light is from a crimson-colored fungus from the ceiling.  It looks like veins/strands going through the rock, and gives off a red glow.  The surface of the water is blood red.   As we look, we get the taste and smell of salt water…we remember the initial arcane waypoint when we were first under the sea in that bubble.  Aury also picks up a hint of rose in the air.  This is mentioned.  Zymve asks what a rose is. We see other caves/tunnels across the lake (would take a 10-15 minute swim across).  We talk about how to cross and decide to call Fleur (Hap’s familiar owl) out.

Fleur is going to do a low flyover to check to see if anything is moving below the surface.  She sees a few shadows in the water, fish, etc. She notices that the saltwater is being fed from an area.  Og studies the map and realizes that the “X” is in the middle of this lake.  We think we are going to have to drink the potions, but Og says he will scout first.  He jumps in to swim and look around. He gets to the center of the lake and goes under the surface to look.  He sees something 50’ down, but isn’t sure what it is.  Looks like a stone structure, but not a building.  He communicates this telepathically to Ains.  He dives down to check and sees this is an stone ring that is about 8 feet in diameter sitting on the bottom of the lake, standing upright.  This is exactly what Ky’tha saw in her vision last night.  She shares this with the group and they all decide to drink their potions and go under water. Aury and Hap walk in, while Ky’tha and Zymve swim down.  Ains waits for Og to surface and they both drink their potions and then both swim down.  

### **The Gate**

As the group descends, they can look up to see the red surface of the water.  We gather around the stone circle/ring that is mounted there; this is exactly with Ky’tha saw.  It is metallic with gold threads. Aury thinks this must be the gate he heard about related to the lost gate of the sanctuary.  He knows it was only created by or for a god or demi-god.  Aury starts to smell roses again.  He thinks he is at the right place.  He kneels in front of the gate and uses channel divinity to ask for guidance.  He puts his hands on the stone “is this where you need us to be?”  We see the metallic veins the rock glow and the water “trembles”  we see radiant light start to fill in the circle/ring.  Looks like surface within the water start to change into a portal.  He stands up and walks through with the group following.   As Ky’tha walks through she thinks that the ring she saw in her vision was likely a Muerg ring \- almost like this one, but a little different.

### **The Lost Sanctuary**

We all walk through and step into an area on the surface.  The gate now stands behind us.  There is a clearing with vines and wildflowers climbing over everything.  There are trees that create a hidden grove, there are roses everywhere, the light is muted…we would have expected it to be brighter.  Something is amiss.  There is a stone temple.  Aury smells roses, but all else smell earth and decay.  There is a large tree that is dying with peeling bark, the grass around it is barren.  The temple seems very old \- maybe an original inspiration to Diverra’s rise to power in the Awakening.  Aury is pulled toward the temple, while the rest of the group senses something is up.  Og is pulled to the tree.  His thornleaf is warm and itchy on his arm…he doesn’t know what to make of us, but he almost gets a feeling like he belongs \- like he is supposed to be here.  

Ains dries everyone off with prestidigitation.  Hap looks around for threats as Ains feels like this is similar to the grove for Diverra that we were in before. We all start to walk forward.  Dark shapes start to burst out of the earth…humanoid, then into treelike shapes.  The air fills with “defilers turn back.”  

IT IS INITIATIVE:  Combat vs. the Blight

* Round One:  We see creatures with black wispy-ness around them.  They are tree-like.  Vines (like roots) spring up around Zemve, Og, Hap, and Ains to grab them.  And branches pop off trees and land on the ground and are needle blights in front of the group.  Blight tries to Ains with needles and misses.  Another blight fumbles attacking Og.  The last one attacks Aury and misses.  The vine on Ains grapples him, inflicting damage.  The one on Og grapples him and inflicts damage.  Zymve is also grappled and takes damage.  Hap shrugs off his attack.  Og rages and swings at the vine on Ains and misses.   He then swings again and hits.  Ains casts a spell and purple tendrils circle the area and float in the area, creating magical darkness \- hitting the vines on Zymve and Ains and the bad tree next to him. Hap attacks the vine on him and misses, then attacks again and hits.  He is able to swing again and fumbles.  He then attacks one last time and hits.  The tree in the purple circle takes damage and moves out of the circle.  The other tree steps up and hits Aury twice doing big time damage, and roots burst up underneath him and he is grappled by them.  The piercing damage from the roots inflicts more damage.  Zymve breaks free of the root and moves away. She then gives temp hit points to herself, Ains, and Aury.  She moves to the edge of the glade. Ky’tha shoots the tree holding Aury and hits with her crossbow and she moves to the edge of the glade.  Aury casts a spell \- radiance of the dawn \- hitting all 4 vines, the tree on Aury, and the closest needle blight.  The tree takes half damage.  Needle blights take damage.  Vines take damage (two go own).   He also heals himself.  
* Round Two:  Mist rises up and forms in the area in front of one of the trees, forming a stinking cloud (the area is heavily obscured).  The vines take damage from the cloud. NB attacks Ains and hits; another attacks Og and hits; and another attacks Aury and misses.  Ains is poisoned from the cloud and he casts shatter and kills two needle blights and a tree takes some damage and he tries to shove the tree, but it saves.  Og has the poisoned condition.  He attacks a vine and hits, killing it.  He then runs outside of the cloud and hits the tree, dealing damage.  Hap saves against the poison and runs out of the cloud to attack the tree and misses, then tries again and crits.  Then misses again with his main weapon and hits again with the dagger.  The tree on Og attacks and hits then attacks and misses, then roots emerge from the ground and he is grappled and will take piercing damage on his next turn.  Zymve casts a spell on the tree on Og and it takes a lot of damage.  She then inspires Og. The tree on Aury gnaws on him, inflicting damage. He takes damage from the cloud. He then tries to bash Hap  and misses.  He also misses Aury.  A roots reaches out to grapple Hap, but he dodges the root.  Kytha moves over to shoot the tree on Og and hits.  Aury takes damage from the cloud and goes down.  
* Round Three:  A pit opens up on Aury, but he is grappled and limp \- the tree has him right now.  A vine moves out of the cloud next to Ains, but can’t take an action.  Og is grappled and tries to hit the tree and hits twice, dealing massive damage.  Ains screams at the tree and it takes damage, and dies \- and a body takes the place of the tree.  Ains shoves the vine, but it doesn’t move.  The seems to be the same infection that took over Diverra’s grove outside of Emberstran.  Hap attacks the tree and hits and also inflicts menace on it \- the tree is frightened and takes damage.  He then hits with a dagger and attacks with crits with his his shadowtrail and is knocked prone and then hits again with a dagger.  Zymve moves the daggers to the vine and it takes damage and dies.  The tree pulls Aury out of the cloud and munches on him (death fail).  He then stands up and attacks Hap w/disadvantage and misses and then fumbles and loses his reaction.  He then tries to grab Hap and misses.  Ky’tha moves back over and hits the tree with her crossbow.  Aury then rolls a death save (we don’t know if it is a save or a fail).  
* Round Four:  A new root pops up next to Og and Ains and Ky’tha and Hap.  The needle blight teleports next to Og and claws at him with a crit, double damage.  Hap, Ky’tha, and Og save against the vines; Ains is held.  Ains attacks the needle blight with a sorcerer’s burst and hits with fire damage.  Og attacks the needle blight and kills it.  He then moves over to attack the tree and hits twice, felling it.  Hap runs over to the priest and tries to heal him with his medicine kit.  He is stabilized.  Zymve dashes over, but can’t do anything.  Ky’tha runs over to rifle through Aury’s pack and finds nothing to help.  We decide that Aury needs to rest. 

We check the tree \- does it look better? It looks the same.  The two creatures on the ground are shadowy looking.  Hap thinks they are from some place not of this plane.  We loot the bodies.  They appear to be dark druids \- looks like they are creatures from Blackrend. They have sickles, leather armor.  They have studded leather that is black with black studs.  Looks very nice.  One wears a very nice cloak.  Ains checks to see if they are magical.  They are \- the mantle is abjuration magic, the leather has abjuration magic.  The building is also magical.  Zymve takes the studded leather, while Ky’tha takes the mantle.

The altar in the building is magical.  Og picks up Aury like a baby to carry him to the building.  Hap approaches the building first with Ky’tha by his side.  Hap sees through the open arch an altar inside that is covered with roses.  Og steps forward, but feels his thornleaf pinching him. He hands Aury off to Hap.  Hap, carrying Aury, walks in.  Ky’tha follows.  There are murals inside. Hap lays Aury on the altar.   Radiant light burns down and Aury’s hands cross on his chest and his book appears on his chest with a new cover.  Hap says “looka there \- what that.”

Ky’tha looks around and investigates the area \- checking out the carving.  She thinks this is great artwork, well built.  Aury heals for 1 point and he opens his eyes and feels warmth and more at home than he has ever felt.  He thinks this is the OG temple to Diverra.  

Ains follows Og to the tree. Zymve stands just outside the door of the Temple.  Og hears a voice in his head \- as if the thornleaf is speaking to him \- it speaks without words but says “Answer its call,” as clear as day.  He reaches for the tree with his hand.  The bark is cool and rough.  Og’s breathing slows and his eyes grow heavy.  He hears “rest now Ogman Crag, accept your destiny and fall into E’espran.”  He resists the pull.  Ains sees him and asks, “what is going on.”  He explains about resting \- and they talk and Ains tells him to rest.  Og puts his hand back on the tree. His skin starts to change.  His hand looks like it is one with the tree…changing into bark. He looks like he is about to collapse.  Ains helps set him down.  He sees his eyes close heavily \- he doesn’t think he is in danger \- but he’s never seen him this calm.  This might be bad \- he doesn’t know.  He tries to connect to Og’s thoughts.  “Are you good?”  Og says “ok” but is very tired.  He feels connected to him, but he thinks Og is talking to something else. He hears:

## *The great cycle of green and shadow, the living pulse that binds the world's roots and branches–The Alferumn. It is more than a tree, a connection of all that grows and withers in balance. When the balance frays, the Alferumn suffers, and guardians are called. You are E’erspan, thorn-bearer, the one who walks between bloom and rot to restore what has been taken.*

Og feels this thing’s pain.  They share the pain.  He has felt this before \- this is the second time he has felt the blight.  He feels that the tree’s roots are starved…it is poisoned.  This seems to be the single most important tree \- this is the Life Tree.  It says “you feel the pain.  Seek Dandelia of the Veil, for she has been seeking E’erspan’s aid.”  He is being called forth to be the guardian of this tree, of life.   He feels like he needs to find Dandelia (the druid of the Veil).  This would be to the east of the Baltwood. (Kahlbit’s Veil).  Ains thinks Og is going to be out for a long rest. Zymve sees this happening at the tree.  She has never seen trees and she and Ains talk about trees.  He explains that it looks like Og is okay, just resting.

Inside, Aury feels the weight of the tome on his chest \- heavier than the book he brought with him.  Radiant light shines on him.  The words have been replaced with the words “**Bloom Aeterna**” \- these are the original words of Diverra herself.  This is the complete, accurate, unabridged version of Diverra’s teachings.  Aury feels there must be answers in this book \- he has the urge to rest and study the book.  Ky’tha tells him what happened outside and that we don’t have the ability to heal him.  Hap’s mind is blown as he realizes this was his original vision \- why he was paired with Aury to begin with.  

We decide to rest.  Before resting, Zymve dons the armor (+1 studded leather); Ky’tha puts on the mantle (spell resistance).  We take a long rest \- Og and Ains by the tree; all else in the temple.  

As we sit there, the skies darken \- a new sensation for Zymve, but she’s in the temple. 

Aury \- he starts to read through the new tome.  It is celestial.  It reveals “I am Diverra the Ardent One.”  It opens into an area that lays out stories that encompass beliefs in values.  Passion, spark, beauty in its natural form.  All beings hold divine potential to be nurtured. Inner beauty is the most beautiful.  Then it goes into the tenants of Diverra.  These are similar to what he has already learned \- there are no differences, but it is more complete.  Then the next piece is about divine teachings \- how he projects this forward.  Other pieces that stand include:  Shattered Hearts \- she talks about broken love, and how while she is Beauty, she is also the goddess of love.

## 

## "*The Shattered Hearts, fragments of lost passion scattered across the world, must be sought and returned. When found, place each at the proper place of worship dedicated to me—shrines, temples, groves where my name is still honored. There, in sacred ground, their pieces may be reunited, their light restored, and the wounds of the heart healed. Only in this way shall the eternal bloom be made whole*."

These are material components of love (which is immaterial).  If someone comes across one they need to find out where it belongs.  Aury would need to put it in its rightful place (praying to Diverra to find out where it goes).  This is a sanctuary in its truest form.  As long as he holds the book and specific petals he can cast a ritual and bring us back to this place.  Anywhere in Myrdae…he can come back here.  But he has to have petals and the book.  This is the only copy of this book.

Ky’tha found on the altar a carving \- hands offering a chalice of receiving.  He needs to be looking for a chalice to place a shattered heart into.  This specific place is the actual chalice he will need to use. 

Zymve gets confused by Aury’s mumblings.  She is not used to the surface.  She is leaned up next to the archway.  She hears everyone relax and rest, she hears whispers of a melody.  No one else is responding to this.  Maybe she is the only one hearing it and doesn’t know why it is just her.  This melody is broken to everyone else, but to her it is complete and haunting and dark.  It seems to be coming from outside.  She looks outside and sees Og melded as one into the tree and a soft glow around Ains near his head and his eyes look pale in the starlight.  And she sees him studying a book \- he seems to be magically attached to the book.  The melody seems to be coming from Ains.  She sneaks over to him.  She feels the music coming together.  It reminds her of an early tune that she wrote.

Ains \- in his mind the journal’s chapters are starting to move around and converge.  Odd words are coming out.  There is something about a catastrophe that came from hunger.  Zymve sees that he is really intense.  She thinks he looks like he is in pain.  Words form as he is studying the book.  What emerges are lyrics and she feels a song build inside her.  She hears catastrophe and thinks this is like a title or chorus.  She then hears “run, escape, lineage, bloodline”  something along those lines (something innate is making you escape).  She doesn’t realize that she’s on top of her.  He closes the book, rubs his temples, and falls asleep.  She picks up the book and the pages seem blank to her.  A name pops into her mind:  **Szoradryn**.  This is the owner of the Silken Secrets.  He is from Ys’nonterien.  She thinks there is more to this book than Ains knows.  She wonders why this drow has given her friend a book…and why it is having an impact on her.  She is shaken up.  What does this have to do with Ains, because it feels personal to me?  She pockets the book, for now \- hiding it in her backpack.

– The End of the Session –

WE END HERE.  When we return, we will have had a long rest.  Options \- go back through the gate to where we just came from, take time to study the book to go through the portal to go back to the other Diverra grove.   Need to decide Surface or back to Underdark?

# **20 \- Coming Home**

**Session Date**:  March 2, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  32nd of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Bloomrest

### **In Bloomrest (The Lost Sanctuary of Diverra)**

We wake up. The tree that Og was attached to seems to have new life.  It seems that the blight was driven back.   Ky’tha tells the group that Lady Rook was elected…and is likely appreciative so a return to Emberstran would be in order. Need to check on Hap’s land; need for Aury to talk to his priestess. Hap and Aury pray and take a rose from the temple grounds using the incredibly powerful pruning dagger. It becomes a petrified rose that maintains its color.  He takes 10 petals \- pressed into the book.

### **Teleporting to Diverra’s Grove**

We talk a little and then go to an opening in the Sanctuary.  Aury starts the ritual using one of the petals, reading from the book.  The petal floats around as we are all wrapped in radiant light…then it fades and we find ourselves back in Diverra’s Grove in the Baltwood.  We follow the old hunting trail and look at the symbols carved into the trees to guide us back to Emberstran.  Zymve notices this.  

As we continue, we see three sweat-stained guys hefting logs onto a wagon.  They recognize us and offer us a ride to the southern gate of Emberstran.  These guys are **Jorm**, **Veed**, and **Sigur** (**Emberstran Timber Company**).  They say they don’t recognize one of us and point to Zymve \- Ains says she is dark and hides in the shadows and has always been with us.  We climb aboard and start the walk back.  Hap walks alongside with Fleur flying overhead.  We ask about the last stretch \- what news.  They say the port and vineyards seem to be flourishing.  

### **Brander & the Victors of the Vine**

The cart takes us to an area with real, unfiltered sunlight as we travel to Emberstran.  This is Zymve’s first view of a bright day.  Ains creates and hands Zymve a pair of “sunglasses” to help shield them from the bright light.  Every six seconds he recasts the spell to keep them up.  These guys are making small talk with us.  They say now that the blight is gone the lumber business is picking up.  Eventually a young rider comes into view riding a horse and singing.  His song has to do with the removal of the blight…

*“No more the blight shall claim our blood, no more the fear shall reign,*  
*For unseen hands have turned the tide—our thanks shall never wane\!”*

He then pulls next to us.  He is **Brander**, a “*wandering minstrel of no small repute*”.  That was the “**Victors of the Vine**” \- tales of our deeds.  He is shocked and pleased to have sung it to us. Zymve comments that it needs work.  He says, well maybe so.  He also comments how he thought there were 5 of us, but now sees that we are a group of 6\.  Ains tells the same tale…and in his head says “you are going along with this please.”  Aury flips him a gold piece and Ky’tha tells him that more words rhyme with six than five.  He then asks Zymve if she wants to be included in his next version and she tells him ‘to find another profession.’ And he leaves.

We are impressed by the speed and strength of the ox pulling the wagon.  Ky’tha asks his name and he says he can’t since he will likely eat him someday.  We start to come into view of the southern gate to the town. 

### **Southern Gate of Emberstran**

There are two guards there and they recognize us and are happy for our return.  They wave us through.  Ky’tha asks them to keep our return a secret \- we’d like to surprise a few people…and pays them each 1gp to keep quiet.  The cart pulls up to the Warm Bear Inn.  We pay the guys 2gp to buy a round for the tavern this evening. And then give them another 2 gp to stay longer.  Ains then messages her to tell her that he will be late. Zymve is overwhelmed, but stays quiet to observe everything.

We discuss who to visit.  Hap and Ky’tha want to visit Lady Rook; Aury to the Temple, Ains, Zymve, and Og to Raddie’s Rarities. Then all meet back at the Dyed Cane.  As we travel, we see that the town is busier and bustling.  And people point to us and look at us.  Zymve asks if there is a tailor \- we comment that there is, and an armorer.  She wants to buy a cloak with a hood to shield her identity and from the sun.  She finds some and buys a black cloak.  The others head to Raddic’s Rarities.  This shop is full of odds and ends, manned by a half orc and a sharply dressed half elf.  Ains asks him about a platinum vial that could hold a tentacle.  He says he can have one for him tonight.  Once they all make their purchases, they head to the Temple.

Ky’tha and Hap head to Imonorra’s Tower.  We learn from the doorman that Lady Rook is at the Suncrest Cellarium.  Normally the head would move to the Vintner’s Mansion, but she allowed Lady T (Tarathial Rulendor) to stay there.  We ask how the transition of power went and he said it went as expected.  He said he would send a message to Lady Rook to let us know we are coming. Im’s familiar appears and he gives him a message and he disappears.  They decide to go to the Cellarium.

### **The Temple to Diverra**

Meanwhile, the others head to the Temple.  They discuss what Ains is trying to buy, but he doesn’t reveal what it is for. They proceed. They enter the Temple and see the Priestess Meladra.  She is so happy to see them and grasps Aury’s hand and asks if he found the sanctuary/portal.   He says he did and starts to tell her about all that transpired.  He tells of trolls, an underground city…and the fact the portal takes you to the middle of the sea.  She comments that it predates the Awakening.  She sees a glow about Aury and comments as such.  Ains comments that Aury is beating around the bush.  Aury hesitates because he fears that it would be a difficult journey for others \- particularly non-believers.  He says it is in an isolated place that is likely to kill others.  Zymve says it almost killed him.  She says “you know you brought a drow back with you” and Zymve comments “you have a problem with that.”  She backpetals and says no \- we just don’t see many of you around here and apologizes, commenting that they typically don’t have the nicest of reputations.  But if you are hanging with my beloved Aurilius then you are fine in my eyes. Aury sees Arnol edging towards Ains. The priestess asks what else did you find?  Aury tells her about the blight we found and how Hap carried him to the Temple and was brought back by her. She asks what the experience was like.  He says it is hard to describe, but tries. He then tells her about the Tree of Life and she was surprised to find out that it exists next to Diverra’s Sanctuary.  He further describes the journey…start to finish.  And tells that the blight might be spreading more in Myrdae.  He then describes the Temple.  He then talks to Arnol, asking if he has been doing the daily workouts with the captain. He responds that he has not been doing it daily and asks Aury not to question his convictions.  He then bows and retires to his quarters…leaving quickly.  The priestess responds that Arnol has not been himself, but that Diverra smiles upon Aury. Aury questions this…asking what is going on and she comments that he hasn’t been around much. He says he goes to training, but then leaves for hours at a time.  Aury questions this, almost judging her.  She says she does not want to be judged…that she has an entire city to take care of.  Ains steps in to say that perhaps Arnol’s absence is more about puberty and learning.  Zymve comments that the strong guidance of a woman makes a young man.  Aury gets the sense that she is not covering for Arnol, but is really just excited to see Aury and for what he’s found.  He thinks that she is just accepting of change and is concerned about Arnol, but is distracted and captivated by what Aury has found.  He thinks she would be overwhelmed by the book he found \- it would be a miracle \- and decides not to share it at this time.  He thinks that he has passed her in terms of favor with the goddess…and doesn’t want to overwhelm her.  He inquires about his chambers and about additional rooms that might be available…and she says that space is always available for followers of Diverra.  Og says \- what about friends of followers and she says she could make an exception.  Ains just asks her to use discretion, and she agrees.

### **The Suncrest Cellarium**

Hap and Ky’tha head to the vineyards.  Walk through the streets, recognized by guards.  They are elusive about who and how many of the heroes are in town.  They continue on and make it to the Suncrest Cellarium.  Lady Rook is standing in a nook, set up like a tasting chamber.  Wine-colored gown.  She welcomes us and offers us a beverage.  She steps through a door and returns with a scroll case \- the deed to Hap’s property.  She tells him that the Digglepot’s have offered to buy the property.  He asks about his sister and she says that she never came.  She comments that \- looking at Ky’tha \- some relationships have grown stronger and others weaker.  She says your brother in law is a brother of the ruthless.  He skipped town choosing your sister over his family.  I have surrounded him with protection.  She asked about the **Marrowstone** and said she’d like to have it for Emberstran.  Ky’tha asks about the power of it and she comments that Imonorra has the conduit to bring it to its full potential.  She says that the stone could be used to take down the Cloak of Defiance \- she isn’t sure if it could take down Jerik, but thinks it could take most from the Crimson Swan.  She says the Digglepots have offered 2,200gp for the land.  She says that he shouldn’t bring back Phira from Trailpoint now, but with the stone, could make it safe to return her back.  She says that affixing Imonorra’s rod to the stone will release the necrotic energy and allow Og to use the stone again. She offers us food and drink.  Ky’tha asks about Lady T, the Lord, and other nobles and leaders.  She says the Lord is ok \- he knows where the power is \- though the Cloak tried to shake him down.  That Lady T wasn’t happy.  Hap tells her that he is happy that Lady T was taken care of.  Ky’tha agrees.  She asks if Ky’tha has checked in with Tessa yet and says she should.  Hap agrees to talk to Og about the stone.  They leave and head to the Dyed Cane.  Ky’tha notices a new sign that says **Our Exquisites**.  This is a new shop \- they head in to check it out.  It is an arts studio. Wide space, pedestals, sculptures, abstract carvings.  Smells new. Tessa bursts out and is happy to see Ky’tha.  They briefly talk and agree to catch up later \- that Ky’tha will stay there later. 

### **The Dyed Cane**

The rest of the group \- heads to the Dyed Cane and talks about Arnol on the way.  They arrive and Hap and Ky’tha arrive about 30 minutes later.  They have already had a round of drinks.  Ky’tha asks about how their morning fared \- on the docks and at the Temple. Ky’tha comments on the cloak that Zymve bought \- how she looks fetching in it.  Ains talks about how the drow in the Underdark made better clothes.  Bordi shows up and asks if we want the private room and we say yes.  He asks about the tablet we found in his cask and we tell him that we are still piecing it together and will let him know once we have all the answers.

We get to the room. Hap tells Og and the group about Lady Rook’s proposition.  Aury asks what Rook will do with the energy from the stone…and says he cannot condone those actions.  Ky’tha comments that Phira will always be in danger \- and probably Hap, too \- as long as the Cloak thrives.  Aury says that he doesn’t want any Diverra followers to be killed.  Hap continues to lobby for the emptying of the stone…”how she empties the stone…ah…do we really care about that.” We discuss how it would actually be to Ains and Og’s advantage to take the Cloak down.  Aury asks about Lady T’s fate and they share that she was allowed to stay.  Og asks Zymve for her perspective. She agrees that being in favor of the one in power would be to our benefit. Hap says to consider this “law enforcement” and Aury agrees that he will turn a blind eye, though we may be “kicking the pine cone down the street.”  The group agrees to give her the stone.  The group advises Aury that he should go to the Temple tomorrow.  We discuss that Ains will study the stone tonight and give Ky’tha the go ahead to contact Rook about meeting at the Tower. 

Zymve goes with Ains and Og to the Warm Bear Inn to get info about Mount Emberstran.  Hap and Aury plan to go downstairs at the Dyed Cane to eat and drink, while Ky’tha heads back to the new place to meet Tess.   All but Ky’tha will stay at the Temple \- she will stay with Tessa.

### **Our Exquisites**

Ky’tha goes back to Our Exquisites to see Tessa and is escorted upstairs.  There are others there \- some are familiar, and she realizes that this is the new base for the Etterset and Tessa is the new face.  She tells Ky’tha that she is welcome here and would like her to stay.  Ky’tha declines, but says that she is happy to help.  She responds that she’d like our help taking down the Cloak and Ky’tha tells her that they have plans with Lady Rook to do just that.  Ky’tha asks her about the transition and then she asks about Zymve.  Ky’tha tells her that she thinks she is running from something, but that she is to be trusted for now.  Tessa says that she will put out feelers, but Ky’tha asks to do this with discretion since she may not want the attention, nor would the group want it.

### **Raddic’s Rarities**

Ains, Zymve, and Og head to the drow….after Zymve disguises herself.  Ains introduces her as “Og’s lady of the night.”  Then they go to pick up the flask and here that it was purchased for 250gp.  Ains tries to negotiate.  He gets it down to 350gp, paying with 250gp and a 100gp ruby.  He gets an eyeball.  He then asks for a pickled tentacle.  He doesn’t have one, but he gives him a jar, salt, and vinegar to pickle his own tentacle.  They head to the docks to get one.  He finally has all his ingredients \- but doesn’t reveal for what.  They then head to the Warm Bear Inn.  

### **The Warm Bear Inn**

Og apologizes about the lady of the night comment.  At Warm Bear…the settle in.  They notice the lumbermen…pacing themselves. A few people recognize them:  “you are right, they are here.”  Zymve is introduced as Og’s half-sister.  They look around and Zymve and Ains notice a guy sitting in the corner with his head down, with a broken lute…several empty tankards–its Bander, the bard we met on the road to Emberstran.  Zymve \- disguised \- offers to remedy the situation.  Og gets chummy with some hunters, has a few drinks, and asks about Mount Emberstan \- paths, threats, etc.  A sturdy, strong woman sitting at the corner of the bar perks up and walks over and says “well I’ll be damned \- heroes of Emberstran are going to Mount Emberstran”  My name is **Agnett** and I’ve been traveling these parts for many years.  She tells them it will be 3-5 days to get there. They will have to go through the Mist Springs. They are hot springs that create mists and make the trail hard to see…  
“*\[The Mists are\] beautiful, but they’ll hide your footing and sometimes show things that aren’t really there. Stick to the high ridges when the mist rolls in.*” 

Meanwhile, Zymve still disguised as a human mosies up to the bard Bander we met along the path and begins talking to him.  She asks his deal, taking his drink away, he should be really experienced…(he had a bad day)... he says wrote a great song, but he didn’t get respect.  She says he needs to lock in while she uses the Mending spell to mend his lute.  He said he does well at every place he goes to, but those days are gone. She tries to convince him to play again.  She pushes the lute into his chest…showing him some tips (giving him Bardic Inspiration) and he tries again and really plays well, impressing her.  She offers to duet with him.  He mentions that Zymve (who is disguise) is much nicer than the “bitch” he met on the road (referring to Zymve).  They get up to play in the clearing and knock it out of the park (rolling a Natural 20 on Performance). Even Agnett pushes away to listen.  The entire tavern is enthralled with their performance.  They finish up and she asks his name.  It is Brander, he announced himself as a wandering menstrual of no ill repute. She introduces herself as **Lynda**.  

She wanders over to Ains and Og afterwards.  Agnett then comes up and says “that was fabulous” to Zymve.  Where were we?  She talks about the creatures they will find.  Sabers, black bears, boars, stags…in the lower areas.  She says there are ash-covered monsters to be careful of…they look like swirling, skeletal figures.  Ains asks about people.  You haven’t heard the tale of **Irragosa the Griever**.  She then tells them about it.  The story was that some harmons ago, old Brynn was tracking a bore near the ash groves when an orange dragon dropped out of the skies.  Scales were glistening with sea water.  Snatched Brynn clean up and no one ever saw him again.  Some say she is guarding something up there…others say she is looking for something.  Always keep one eye to the sky.  Og asked if anyone witnessed this.  She says no.  Ains tells Og in his head to stop asking questions.  And tells Zymve in her head good job on the song.  Agnett yells “Lynda, Lynda, Lynda…etc.” in admiration of the music.  Og asks if there is anything else.  She says to stay on the **Old Trader’s Trail** \- it will take you to the base.  But again, the springs will be a challenge, too.   Ains gives her 3sp and says to have another round on us. Ains then says that they are all going up to their rooms upstairs.  They say goodnight, as Agnett announces their departure.  Ains casts invisibility on Og so that he can sneak back to the temple.  Ains explains that he and Og are under attack from the Cloak which is why he is trying to fool everyone at the tavern.

Zymve replies that they need to talk.  Zymve pulls out the Journal of Tsulanan that she snuck from Ains the night before and asks “where the hell did he get it and why does he have it?”  He says that he got it from the guy at the Underdark town, but that it was one of his relatives' books.  That his blood is special \- that he is special \- that he gets his powers innately.  And that the book is answering questions about his power.  He says he is surprised she has the book. Zymve threatens him that he needs to inform her about the information he gets from the book.  He asks why she is interested in the book.  She says she heard it and she didn’t like what it said.  She says it is weird and came from a drow that came from my village.  She asks what he knows of the drow.  They talk about the evil goddess lady and spiders.  She said that she thought the evil goddess lady won’t let her go.  She ran away two years ago and no one has ever come for her, but now this book appears and she feels threatened by it.  She says that she couldn’t have come to the surface on her own as she was fleeing.  Ains asks her about their libraries where she could have gathered info.  She says she just needed to get out of the Underdark….and the group was her ticket up.  She feels like maybe she is ok.  But wants to know if the book tells him anything that might be valuable to her. He agrees to tell her.  He just says that his main concern here is about the Cloak and how they have bad favor with them, as well as other groups in the underbelly of the city.  He says he will make her invisible so she can travel to the temple.  She agrees.  She says she has never had friends before and she doesn’t know what that’s about but if “you help me, I’ll help you.”  He agrees and says that is what friends do…and how Og saved him before and how they became friends.  He then tells Zymve about the Thornleaf that is attached to Og (and a funny story about the bathhouse and how Og spends a lot of time there staring at his leaf).  She admits that she grew up in a cult where no one is a friend.  They discuss cults. Ains turns her invisible and she heads to the Temple.  

After she leaves, Ains studies the **Marrowstone** using the Scroll of Identify.  He realizes that it is a two-part magic item \- the stone and a rod.  He knows that he should not touch the stone when it releases the energy.  He thinks it would work like a Circle of Death; the radius and damage varies based on what is captured within. He knows he needs the rod to use it, but is trying to determine if there is a way to bypass the rod, using his own magical powers to release the energy.  He thinks it could be possible, but might require more studies and be extremely difficult. 

Zymve arrives at the temple…sees Og.  Eventually, Hap and Aury arrive back at the Temple.  Ains announces that he needs to use the restroom before going to bed at the Warm Bear Inn.  He then makes himself invisible and heads to the Temple.  He then appears 15 minutes later. A room has been prepared for everyone.  Ains asks Zymve if she can inspire him…and she does.  He then asks Aury if he can guide him in arcana.  He then studies the Marrowstone with Bardic Inspiration from Zymve, Guidance from Aury, and his own Luck feat after returning to his room in the temple.  Og and Zymve notice him studying it.  They hear something like a crackle and look over and see a flash of darkness in the room…and when the darkness clears, Ains is holding the stone and it is empty.  

– The End of the Session –

# **21 \- He Ate a Baby**

**Session Date**:  April 8, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  33rd of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Emberstran
