The night before, when Ainslie absorbed the necrotic energy, everyone was in the Temple of Diverra.  Ky’tha walked in right when Ainslie pulled the magical energy from the Marrowstone.  Overwhelmed by the day's events and shocked by what occurred between Ainslie and the Marrowstone, the party decides to wait until the next day to discuss it.

### **34th of Talil, 1246**

We all wake up in the Temple.   Ky’tha confronts the group, especially Ains \- Hap and I had promised the stone to Lady Rook to help take down the Cloak.  Ains feels different today \- like “something was scrubbed away from him” during the night (after the ritual with the marrowstone).  The group thinks he looks different \- he looks better and refreshed.  He is trying to figure out what is different \- why and how. He thinks this is something he cannot reverse.  It may keep him youthful.  Maybe that is why Lady Rook wanted it \- to keep her youthful.  Ky’tha wonders if consuming the marrowstone contents would help everyone or just Ainslee (or others like him). The group decides it may be something specific to Ainslee…and others like him (or who have a similar trait).  The group discusses keeping the **Marrowstone** to ourselves.  We know **Blackrend** is causing blight in the land…and we could use the marrowstone to resolve them as we encounter them. 

There was a discussion about Og bonded with the Tree of Life.  Is he able to sense where blight is in the world?  He doesn’t appear to be able to yet; maybe his powers are still growing?

The group splits up:

* Ains, Og, and Zymve are going to buy provisions \- rations, rope, and pitons for the adventure to Emberstran Mount and to find the Emberheart Vault.    
* Aury is going to stay at the Temple and talk to the Priestess.    
* Hap and Ky’tha are going to see Lady Rook.  

The plan is to meet up at the North Gate.  As we are about to leave, the Priestess walks over to Aury and hands her a piece of folded leather.  Aury then presents them with badges, it’s the Badges of Emberstran (the E.G.D.).  Aury took them from their packs the night before and had them blessed by the Priestess.

### **Buying Provisions**

Zymve goes to the leatherworking shop, introduces herself as Lynda and mentions her performance last night.  The proprietor said she has been the talk of the town.  She tries to sell the leather armor (he offers her 2gp) and tries to barter for a better price, intimidating the shopkeeper.  He offers her 5 gp and a pair of leather mittens.  

Og and Ains go to buy rations and rope.  Ains walks in with spectacles that he created and rips them off.  They are there on official business \- heading to the mountains. They ask for supplies \- the shopkeeper offers them at cost.  Ains then asks for additional discounts, using persuasion.  He is able to get a 50% discount (9gp instead of 17gp).  They buy 5 bedrolls, 3 ropes.  As they exit, a man walks by with a goat \- the two decide to flash their badges and investigate the man and the goat.  They don’t really find much.  He says he was bringing down some potatoes and just delivered them.  He says he is going back to his farm.  They offer to buy a goat and bags for 10gp.  He accepts.  They ask if he has ever been to Mount Emberstran…and says no, and that he has heard about the orange dragon and the hunters who never came back.

### **Visiting Lady Rook at the Cellarium**

Ky’tha and Hap go to see Lady Rook without the Marrowstone.  We spot a shadow sitting on the rooftop nearby.  Ky’tha catches a sign of red coloring and a red hooded cloak, with a glint of metal.  As soon as she looks, he is gone in a flash.  He was a Cloak of Defiance member, but no one we recognize specifically.  Hap calls Fleur, his horned owl familiar, to circle above to help keep watch.  She doesn’t see much.  They pass Tessa as they are headed to the Cellarium. She greets Ky’tha, who reintroduces Hap.  Another Etterset member shows up and more introductions.  Ky’tha tells them about the Cloak member who was spying on us…and they are not surprised since word got around about us coming back to town.  Tessa mentions that Hap came into something soon…and Ky’tha mentions that she hopes that no one else knows about that.  Hap inquires about the people who want to buy his land.  They say they are good people, potato farmers.  Hap says he needs to talk to his sister first.  They mention that Lady Rook threw a party last night \- a great party. And that Rook is likely hung over.  They knock on the door, no answer.  Are just about to pick the lock, when the door opens and they see one of the captains of Emberstran.  Ky and a Captain talk, he was at the party last night. He slept here, Ky wondered if he slept with Rook, but he seemed to be on the up and up. He reveals that Lady Rook was on the up and up. Hap tells the captain that Ainslie studied the stone and there was an accident and the stone is now empty, and we are going to refill the stone, and tells her to not be mad. The captain is confused, but low-key agrees to it without knowing better. Ky wants to try and join the next party if there is one when we return. They keep digging the hole deeper… Ky tells the captain that we are heading out of town, revealing we have a new companion… Hap says we will be back in a fortnite. 

Ky’tha and Hap discuss further on the way out to the gate, how maybe that went better than expected.  She can’t get mad about an accident, right?  And who knows what the captain will actually convey to Lady Rook \- he was very confused about the stone.

### **The Temple of Diverra**

Aury talks to the priestess.  He tells her the badges were great.  He tries to decide if she will be stable enough to handle hearing of the book and thinks that she might be jealous \- so he doesn’t show the book to her.  He instead shows her a writing of the mission that Diverra has given to the group (about the hearts, he quotes in the poem).  He asks her to write the poem down \- the shattered heart mission.  He takes out one of the petals from the sacred grove and gives it to her.  He doesn’t explain the use of the petal \- only that it came from there.  She is excited to have one. \[He does this to make sure that he always knows where at least one petal is\].  He also talks to her about **Arnol** \- he doesn’t think he is set for this profession.  Aury said he thinks he is following a different path.  She says she will study this further.  Aury tells her he is not sure when the group will return.  She asks if he has holy water for the trip \- he does.  She asks other questions about Arnol, about duties in the Temple.  Aury provides guidance.  She says that she thinks that Tarley is getting close to becoming a priestess and that she will look at bringing in another acolyte.   Then he leaves.

Og and Ains go to the Old Oak & Grapes.  As the head there, Og notices movement, but isn’t sure who it is (only that they are being sneaky).  They go inside. Ains puts in a special request for carafes of wine for their trip. They buy three at 10gp each.  

We all meet at the gate.  Ky’tha asks about the goat (it is Reggie).  It will help carry items..  Zymve shares her luck with the leatherworker.  Ky’tha and Hap tell about their visit to Lady Rook.  Some guards walk up and talk about when we are coming back.  We decline to tell when we are coming back or where we are going.

Fleur circles above and then flies back and lands on Hap’s shoulder and continues.   Ky’tha asks about if she poops.  Fleur looks at us like we are crazy…of course she doesn’t.  

### **Magic Goat Bags**

We step out of the gates.  When we do, we no longer have a goat, but instead we have a gertot (*an antlered sheep that is livestock in Mydrae raised for its wool and food*).  Ains detects magic on the saddlebags.  Ky’tha looks through the bag.  We don’t find anything on the bags that is out of the ordinary.  Aury checks the gertot out.  We deduce that maybe it had magic on it that protected it from being a gertot in town…since they are often on the menu.  We ask if it had a spell on it to protect it and it nods yes.  We ask about his name and find that it is **Ewing**.  He motions to Og to walk through the gates with the bags on him…and he does and turns into a goat.  He comes back out of the gates and turns back into himself.  Og puts the bags back on Ewing.  Aury asks if he minds carrying the bags \- he doesn’t.

### **The Red Rider**

A rider shows up on a horse…he is wearing a red cloak. He threatens the group \- says that we shouldn’t have come back.  That **Jerik** now has our scent and will not forget.  Ains shoots a lightning bolt, Og throws a hand ax and knocks him off the horse, and Ky’tha shoots a bolt that misses.  The lightning killed him.  The horse runs back to town. A lady runs into a cottage and slams her door.  She is scared of the group since she saw the killing.  Og and Ains pull out their Emberstran badges and say he was a wanted criminal and they needed to take care of him.  They are able to deceive her and her husband.  They ask what the guy did and Ains replies that “***he ate a baby***” and they are happy that he was killed.  Ains offers them 10sp for personal distress.  The townspeople are both horrified and elated at the news.  They convince them to keep this confidential \- for their safety.  Ferdinand (a neighbor) offers to dispose of the body for us (to bury them with others he has).  Instead we throw the body to the pig.  We take the sword and dagger off the body.  Ains messages the guard to take the riderless horse into custody \- official business.  We move along past the last cottages of Emberstran.

### **Into the Baltwood**

We move into the Baltwood. As we go, Ains feels like he is really in his prime. We travel for a couple of hours.  We see deer grazing.  And keep moving.  We find a clearing and set up camp. We see Mount Emberstran and think we are heading in the right direction.  Hap asks Ains to send a message to Phira:  “*Hap says looking forward to meeting you.  Jerik is angry.  Take precautions.  Also, inherited a vineyard \- interested in running it?*”  She replies:  “*It would be great to see you again.  Are you headed this way?  I don’t plan to run a vineyard \- I have an Inn in Trailpoint.*”  The camp watch goes in this order:

1. Fleur on alert in the trees all night.    
2. First watch – Aury reads the book from Diverra, and Ky’tha looks around.    
3. Second watch – Ains sees what is around the fire; Zymve can see 120’.  Ains talks to her about biscuits…and how he didn’t mean to offend her earlier talking about mushrooms.  He asks if she is homesick?  And takes out a carafe of wine and gives it to her.  He says he would not recommend touching his book again.  He asks her for inspiration as he casts Sending to Arnol (trying to be an evil wizard): “*He mentions looking into an artifact known as the marrowstone and keeping the information to himself.*”  Arnol replied confidentially (Ains said he will be safe but gone for some time).   
4. Third watch – Hap and Og talk, and bring up Hap’s sword. Hap talks to his sword, it warms up in his hands and it replies, “*acknowledged*”.  He’s a bit put off by the talking sword and doesn’t talk to it much. Hap can't bring himself to talk to the sword further. 

### **35th of Talil, 1246**

It's the next morning now, the crisp air lets us know the day will be cool again. An invisible hand wakes Ewing and Ainslie asks if he can graze. He also casts extended Mage Armor on it during the rest. We all got a long rest. The trails get harder to follow and Og and Aury take point with Zymve inspiring ahead of the survival check. We go all the way to a stream, find claw marks made by a bipedal creature, its not a gnoll, nor a bear, looks like it might have come down on all 4 feet.   We think this might be a creature as big or bigger than Og, based on the stance.   We all study the tracks and the only thing that comes to mind is they are similar to the creatures in Nungulthil at the bar (the quaggoths).   Ky’tha and Hap recall tales from the Underdark where beasts came up hundreds of years ago \- maybe this is a creature like that.  

We continue to move on the trail, traveling for a few hours.  It is close to twilight.  We knew it was going to take a while to get to the Mountain.  We see a trail open up ahead as we think we are approaching the foothills.  We see a motionless humanoid at the base of a cliff about 90’ ahead.   Ky’tha stealths ahead to check out the body; Og following behind.  She gets about 30’ from the body and sees he is wearing leather, but has a smashed head, likely from falling.  She also sees something following Og.  She pauses a moment to watch and no longer sees it.  She yells out “something is following you.”

IT IS INITIATIVE

* Round One:  A creature jumps out to attack Og.  This is a **quarath**, the surface version of a quaggoth \- that came up a long time ago and has evolved.  It hits him with a claw and then misses.  Another one jumps out at Aury from the shadows and misses with first claw and then Aury releases a warding flare and he still hits.  Og rages and attacks, hitting it well.  Ky’tha jumps in to attack the one on Og and kills it with her dagger. Hap attacks the one on Aury, with a flyby from Fleur, and misses.  The tries to hit with the pruning dagger and does and then he frightens him with a short sword, hitting him. Then bonus action uses the dagger and hits again.  Aury flashes his holy symbol and casts radiance of the dawn and will take full damage.  Ains hits him with a sorcerer’s burst and kills him. We hear leaves shudder in the distance and we see 4 similar creatures take off and scatter.   Out of combat.  

These creatures don’t have any gear \- they are just natural creatures.  Ky’tha takes a trophy of a claw from the one who attacked Og.  Hap reveals from his memory that these creatures supposedly created a town…so it is possible that the four who fled will come back with more.  

The figure at the bottom of the rocks is a half-elven male.  We think he was probably thrown from the top of the cliff by one of these creatures.  He has a silvered short sword, we see a finely crafted hand crossbow with bolts (which has an odd, oily sheen).  No markings to identify him. Ains detects that the oily sheen bolt is magical, hand crossbow is magical.  Pouch has 12gp, 14sp, 23 cp.  Sack has food (3 days of rations) and a piece of paper looks to be recently written:  “*orange shadow sited twice over the grove of ash, too clever for a beast, keeps to the heights near the old sealed way, warn the others.*”  We create a cairn as Aury blesses her.

The path leads over the cliff \- we follow it up.  The cliff is made out of lava stone (black and dark).  At the top, Aury tells us we should continue NE (the creature came from the north).  There is evidence of a struggle here of more than one of these creatures.  We still think we are another day or two away. We continue on the trail and press on.  We see a campsite that looks recently used by the half-elf.  We don’t think we have been followed.  We set up camp with the same watch order:  

* First watch – Ky’tha notices the blue moon in the sky; Aury does more reading to compare the differences in what he learned and the original text.  He learns that her followers come from all over.  She may be a goddess of many (many petals on a rose).   
* Second watch – Ains is reading and Zymve hears the chant (of her dreams).  This is inspirational to her \- it does not make her uneasy.  She feels inspired to create new songs, new words, particularly to create comfort.  This starts to put her angst at ease.  She is looking forward to hearing the songs Ains creates.  She realizes that no one else can hear what she hears. She peeks at the book as Ains is reading and the pages are still blank to her.  She thinks that maybe a drow was a companion of the one who wrote the book.  Thinks there are similarities between the words in the story and her lived experience with Ains. Bottom line \- **his studies bring her comfort**.  As Ains gets to the third part, the words don’t make sense, but the meaning of “flight” comes to mind \- related to honor (escape was in the second section).  He can tell that something shifted dramatically in the life of the writer.  As he is about to doze off, he goes back to the second part and he takes another look and he can't put his finger on this, but he thinks there is something to do with a calling.  Someone felt like they owed something to him \- they sought him out.  After his time of reading and reflection, he talks to Zymve.  She says she heard something.  He tells her what he read.  It is confusing.  He then asks what her deal is.  She asks about his deal.  He reveals that the dragon is supposed to give him information. She says that she is wanted by groups. He offers to send messages to any friends or family she has \- she says she has no one to contact.  Ains sends a message to Phira before going back to sleep: “*No need to come to Emberstran.  Enjoy your Inn.  Hap was just offering.  Sorry to wake you. Jerik’s a bad man. He is after Hap.  Night.*”  He gets a message back from Phira (he keeps it to himself). Then he casts another spell:  “*No need.  All good.  He is 100% safe.  If he dies you can blame Aurelius, a priest of Diverra.  Stay safe.*”  She replies to him. He then sends another message:  “*Don’t.  I’m sorry I sent anything.  Have a good night.  Don’t tell Lady Rook.  You don’t need to respond.*”  
* Third watch – Then Hap and Og wake up. Ains tells Hap that Phira is dangerous.  Asks if his brother-in-law is strong.  He tells him that Phira said that “*Aury’s name will be carved in blood on the very blade that takes him*” should anything happen to Hap.  Hap now thinks there is more to the story with Phira.  She may be as much or more of a badass than her husband.  They then go to sleep.

Og offers some suggestions of how to talk to his sword. Hap asks the sword if it has a name.  He hears “*you are not prepared*.” \[The name\] will come to you.  He asks “are you mad that we killed the dragon?”  No \- but I sense it.  Hap says “The dragon?”  It says:  “Goodnight, Hap.”

The fire starts to burn low and Og is looking up and he sees the moon.  Then suddenly, a silhouette goes across the moon \- it is a ship flying in the sky.  He points this out to Hap.  They can both see it flying in the sky.  Deep wing beats start to make noise above the adjacent tree tops.  A vast shadow sweeps over the campsite.  The wind sparks up the campfire and leaves blow off the trees.  They catch a brief glimpse of orange scales as they hear it make a screech that echoes through the forest as it heads for the ship.  Og, Hap, Fleur and the sword are the only ones who saw the dragon.

WE END HERE.  It is dawn, 36th of Talil, 1246

– The End of the Session –

# **22 \- The Tracker**

**Session Date**:  April 13, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  36th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Old Trader’s Trail en route to Mount Emberstran

We all comment about “what was that sound” and “what is that flying ship?” As we look, we see the dragon hit the ship with lightning as it closes on the ship.  Hap heard rumors of a ship that “years ago people crashed in Myrdae in a flying ship” and also that there was a flying cloud castle over **Basctdelm** \- but think these are rumors and tall tales.  We look at the dragon slam into the ship and wrap around it \- trying to collapse it.  We see a person fall from the ship as it tilts.  We see the dragon rip off the front of the ship.  We see both the ship and dragon tumble down ending in a loud crash in the distance.  This crash was to the west \- not in the direction we were heading. 

We discuss going to the crash site or continuing on to find the Emberheart Vault.   Hap sends Fleur up and can’t see anything. Aury and Og think it would take several hours off the trail to get to the crash site. Ains and Ky’tha think of dragons that shoot lightning \- bronze and blue dragons and that bronze dragons frequent water (the dragon they saw was dripping wet).  We think we didn’t get a good view of the dragon, but think this is a bronze dragon \- and likely the “orange” dragon that we are seeking.

Ains decides to try to use a Sending spell to contact the dragon.  Ky’tha climbs up a tall tree to keep watch \- looking for signs of the dragon taking flight, smoke, or anything in the distance.  She sees the dragon take flight and yells down to everyone and then it disappears in the direction of **Mount Emberstran** as she looks back up.  Meanwhile, Ains gets a message back and the dragon doesn’t think Ains is Ains \- but instead her master.  The group asks if he sounds like his parents \- he does.  We decide to head in the direction of Mount Emberstran. 

### **Being Followed**

Og leads the group as we follow the trail.  After 20-30 minutes, Fleur messages Hap and says “hoo, hoo (I think I caught a glimpse of the dragon flying low).”  Og leads, followed by Aury, Ky’tha, Zymve, Hap, Ains and Ewing.  Every now and then Hap closes his eyes to see through Fleur’s eyes \- and then when he opens his eyes he catches the sound of measured breathing next to him.   He feels like it is off the trail \- this gives him an odd sense.  He doesn’t say anything to the group, but decides to track it himself.  After a few minutes, the sound is gone.  Ky’tha decides to go over to see if there are any footprints.  She finds evidence that someone was on the trail \- someone was following and trying to be sneaky.  The group continues forward and hears the sound of water \- they approach a wide river after the round a bend.  Ky’tha continues to track the evidence of someone following the group.  She looks around and doesn’t see any animals \- nothing around. 

### **River Crossing** 

The group sees steam come off the water, feels heat coming from the water.  As they approach the water, Og sees a large log fallen across the river and almost makes it to the other bank.  Looking downstream, they see small rapids downstream.  As he gets closer, he sees scales darting around under the water \- orange and bronze colored. He comments that he isn’t sure what he sees \- a dragon, fish?  This river is big enough to support the dragon.  The scales are moving.   He comments on what he sees and Aury steps forward to look at the water.  Aury sees fish in the water with the orange/bronze scales.  He realizes that the color changes because of the water.  Aury reaches down to touch the water and takes fire damage \- the water is boiling \- as he yells out.  Og tries to spear a fish from the bank with his glaive.  He hits it but it isn’t a clean pull \- he is able to throw it onto the land.  It is a “fire-colored” scaled fish with a sucker mouth and fanged teeth.  

Ky’tha continues to the water and loses the trail \- no evidence here of anyone else.  She comes out of stealth and lets the group know of what she found. They tell her about the fish.  

Discuss the need to figure out how to cross.  We decide to cross with 2 ropes tied together and tie off…and Aury takes Ewing and flies across, sprouting wings and praying as he crosses over the boiling river.  He gets to the other side and ties the rope off.  Zymve decides to go first and grabs the rope and runs across the log and makes it.  While she crosses, Aury flies up to see above the canopy.  The Og gets large and takes Ains in his arms and tries to cross with both of them.  He slips but catches his footing…and falls to the other side safely.  Ains thinks that he heard Og laugh…and confronts him about it.  Aury sees a shadow disappearing behind the mountain \- and notices a dragon in the distance.  Hap decides to cross at the rocks and stumbles, but catches himself…then finishes gracefully.   Ky’tha unties the rope and crosses the log without advantage…rolling up the rope as she goes \- very gracefully.

### **The Tracker**

We continue forward on the trail \- same order as before.  As twilight starts to fall, we look for a campsite.  Og finds a clearing.  We think we could be as close as a day or two from the base of the mountain.  Ky’tha hears heavy breathing from ahead of her \- and realizes it is not Aury.  She points this out to the group.  Zymve starts to cast a spell and fairy fire erupts in front of Ky’tha…red lights up and catches a foot of someone fleeing.  Ky’tha yells for it to stop and it doesn’t.  Aury casts a spell and can see invisible creatures…and can see where the hooded figure is.  He whispers to Ky’tha where the figure is.  She diverts to go “in another direction” and then goes into stealth \- trying to approach the figure based on where Aury said.  As Ky’tha approaches the area, the figure moves.  Aury says out loud \- I can see you behind the fourth tree on the left.  He sees the figure step out from behind the tree, pull the hood down.  This is a blond elf \- she eyes Aury and then turns and walks off in the other direction.  Aury gives her a thumbs up and shows he can see her.  Ky’tha tries to track her and catches evidence here and there of her movement.  Finally at a spot 30’ away she sees a figure sitting next to a tree, reaching into a satchel \- doesn’t think she spots her.  It pulls out a flask and looks back at the direction of the camp.  She sees Ky’tha and disappears.  Ky’tha tries to convince her to stop and stay.  She throws two daggers at Ky’tha’s feet then comes to retrieve them (invisibly) and Ky’tha grabs her. They begin talking back and forth, and Ky tries to get her to reveal information… Ky tries to find out who “She” is when **Petal** (the blonde wood elf that we are talking to) mentions the Etterset and that “she wants us to succeed”.  Ky gets the impression that she works for Lady Rook.  Ky’tha tells her “tell the Lady that I said hello.”  Petal smiles and leaves.  As she goes away, Ky asks for and gets an invisibility potion from Petal. Ky returns to the group.

Ky’tha tells them what she learned \- that she thinks that Petal worked for Lady Rook.  The group discusses how and why the Lady Rook would want us to succeed.  Ains talks about how he had discussions with Rook about the vault \- and about his conversation with Master An’davo.  He also then tells the group that he has the ability to be invisible if needed.  The group talks about Lady Rook.  Zymve and Aury have major reservations about Lady Rook.  Ains and Zymve make small talk around the camp.  Ains shares that one of his ancestors rode Irragosa.  He shares that there were dragonriders of Myrdae \- that this wasn’t a tall tale.  Aury knows from history that occasionally the team of dragon and rider were formidable in war.  There was a dragonrider that lived on an island on an oversized lake that after the Awakening became the Glimmering Sea.  Riders were few and far between \- there are not many in this world.  Dragonriders were rare, and many considered them more legendary than a King.  No one has really spoken on dragonriders since the Awakening.  The group confronts Ains about his link to a dragonrider. He tries to explain that he shares the same blood as the dragonrider.  Aury thinks the story doesn’t add up.  He thinks Ains believes what he says, but doesn’t think that would link them. Ky’tha asks if it is possible that Ains was lied to his entire life.  He doesn’t think so. Ains decides to cast Sending to **Ehkel an’Davo**. He responds that Lady Rook wants what is in the vault and he doesn’t know what it is.  Also that the dragon is likely guarding the vault.  Ains knows that An’davo is in lock step with Rook, but that maybe a better offer might break his loyalty. So they keep watch and get rest.

* First watch – Hap and Aury. Aury sees a shadow far off in the distance, sitting next to a tree, watching the group. He knows it is Petal observing us.  He gives her a look to let her know that he sees her.  She pulls off her hood and gives him a “what do you want to do” look.  He notes that it is curious that a wood elf has blonde hair.  He shares this with Hap.  When he turns to look back at her, he sees that she is walking toward camp.  She stops about 10’ from camp.  “I have to watch you.  I know you can see me. But I have to watch you.  It is just a watch.  She knows where you are going, she just wants to make sure you get there.  I’m being paid 650gp to watch you.”  Aury talks to her and Hap walks up.  Aury tells her she is getting paid well and that he reminds her that the group acts on their own.  He tells her that he prefers that she watches with them knowing and she says okay and walks off.  Hap hears “Yo” from his sword. He says hello.  It says “I feel it…we are closer”  Hap says “to” and it responds “neverending.”  He shakes his head and scratches his head, confused.  He is also impatient.  “We should have it.”  Hap says “Look buddy, I don’t like riddles.  You are using pronouns without antecedents.”  It says “You’ll see…trust me.”  And he says “I’m sure you will tell me what you are going to tell me when you tell me.” And then goes back to the watch.  They wake up Ains and Zymve.    
* Second watch – Ains sends a message. He also reads his book. \- he fake reads it \- to see if Zymve is watching him.  He’s deep in thought and discombobulated. He seems very confused.  Then he reads the book for real for an hour. She hears the beats she likes.  She knows that when she feels that vibe she will sleep better.  She realizes that giving the journal back to him was a good thing \- it opens her mind and she starts to notice and connect with more around her and a zen moment of who she is.   Ains starts to get a vision of a strange, large construction effort \- digging, building, stones being removed. There was a calling of dwarves to the east to come forth and dig. They are building things.  As Ains tunes into the pages, they are about construction of something deep underground.  As he awakens, he is going into a mountain.  They then have their watch.  Ains shares that his ancestor is **Tsulanan**…he is the dragon rider.  He is the one who wrote the journal.  She asks him how the drow ended up with the journal.  All he knows is that his ancestor and the drow were best friends.  She encourages him to read more from the book.  He also shares that the contents of the vault belong to Ains (by inheritance).  They agree that Lady Rook and her spies should not have access to the contents of the vault.  He says that he will “kill anyone who stands in his way” of getting the vault contents.  He is trying to freak out the spy.  He tells Zymve in her head that he is trying to scare the spy.  Neither see the spy.  He responds in her head that he wishes he knew more about the spy.  She responds that she agrees.  He is casting detect thoughts in the area to see if he can pick up on the spy.  Spell allows thoughts in a 30’ area to be detected.  He picks up that the spy is close by.  He asks Zymve to inspire him…and she pulls out an instrument to play.  He then says out loud “*You know not who you are dealing with.  Reveal yourself to me or I will exterminate you from this land.*”  He tries to intimidate the spy. He sees leaves crumble in front of him and a hood pulled back.  She reveals herself.  “I don’t want to be in the middle of this \- I’m just doing a job.”  He detects thoughts \- “what is in this place.  What does she (Rook) want?”  She’s looking around.  “I’m just trying to do my job”  Zymve tells her that she should find a new job.  Ains probes deeper into her thoughts.  He sees a play-by-play session:  she and Rook at the Tower, eating, talking, these people are important to me (says Rook), they are going to go find what I need.  I’ve shown restraint and they are going to get what I need.  But I need you to follow them to make sure they are successful.  I need you to interrupt/intervene to help them if you can.  If you can’t, then bring me the marrowstone and the tablet.   She is given a purse and a sack of gold from Rook.  Do you best not to let them catch you \- they are talented.  If they find what I need, return it to me at once.” In her head, Zymve hears from Ains \- please intimidate her to leave now and never return to Emberstran.  Ains makes sparks appear in his hand to assist her. Zymve then grabs the front of her cloak \- “you are going to leave, you are not going back to Emberstran, you are going to appreciate the gold you have already been given, and go on with your life \- do you understand?”  She steps back as Zymve holds the cloak.  Her eyes are wide.  “If I see you again, I will kill you,”  She drops the purse on the floor, nods, turns and leaves.  Ains is trying to determine what she is thinking “It wasn’t supposed to be like this.”  She starts to leave.  Once out of site, Ains tells Zymve that he hopes that was the right call.  They each get a potion of invisibility from the dropped purse.  Zymve gives him grief that he didn’t kill her (implying that we are a group of do-gooders).  He ends up running after her and giving her one of the potions \- in a nice gesture to make sure she made it out of there safely.  She takes it, shows him she still had one, and leaves.  Ains feels okay with his actions \- he really didn’t mean her harm.  He casts a final detect thoughts \- he thinks she is not going to Emberstran, nor to talk to Lady Rook.  Instead to **Kalhbit’s Vale**.   He then shouts “one more thing.”  Do you know a Dandelia?  I’m not so sure (but her thoughts show that she does).  He says “we will be seeing you again, I assume.”  She says, “I hope not” and turns and leaves.  
* Third watch – They then wake up Ky’tha and Og. They share that they “ran off the spy” and will not likely see her again.  Ains then sends a final sending spell to the spy “ha ha ha” like she laughed at him when he almost fell in the water.   Og and Ky’tha talk about Rook \- maybe the spy was a decoy and Rook has more up her sleeve.  No other action that night.

### **37th of Talil, 1246**

The next day we are walking.  Aury swears that he heard a twig snap.  Then thinks he heard a leaf drop.  We continue moving along.  He thinks he felt something touch him, but isn’t sure.  He thinks that someone is there.  He casts see invisibility and looks around.  He spots the spy \- she is close.  We come to a fork in the trail: 

* To the **left** the path ascends, going up to the lava/rock/ashen area up the Mountain (where the dragon flew).    
* To the **right**, it is level, slightly descending, and more of a well-worn path.  

Aury pulls out the tablet and looks around.  He feels a warmth and vibration to the right.  He tells the group we need to go right. And then points out “our friend” and Zymve yells “you bitch.”   He sees her 45’ behind the group, behind a tree.  Aury casts fairie fire on top of her…and she saves.  She then takes off, Og takes off, and Aury casts it again (saving again).  He is pointing her out to the group as she runs.  Og is running where Aury is pointing.  Og sees the evidence of twigs and branches since she is dashing.  He swings to attack and rolls a crit.  She is knocked prone and he hits her hard.  She sits up bleeding, she begs for mercy.  We all see her now.  He puts weight on her to keep her down.  Ains mentions to her about “manners” which was part of their sending.  Zymve pulls out a dagger:  "What did I tell you, bitch.  I told you to run with the gold you already have.”    Ains looks through her bag \- there are no additional potions.  He says “we don’t have to kill her now” \- Zymve mentions that she needs no mercy, no additional chances.  She plays on the emotions of Zymve \- “*why are you here \- is it not strange \- this is the way they treated me in the Bathean Empire.*”  Ky’tha jumps in \- has Lady Rook communicated with you at all or anyone else?  She says no.  Ky’tha tells her that we have not promised Rook anything and that no one gets what is in the vault until we see it.  She says \- I just need to make sure you get to the vault.  I will just share your success.  I will just escort you and then leave.  Ky’tha intervenes for the spy \- “what’s the harm of her escorting us?”  Aury and Zymve both want her dead and/or gone.  She looks at Zymve “you don’t know what it is like to make promises (as a drow)”  She commands the spy to “sit down” and she does.  Og grabs her.   
IT IS INITIATIVE

* Round One:  Ky’tha tells her to drop her dagger (intimidation) and she does.  Ky’tha picks it up.  Ky’tha tells the group that this needs to end now.  That no one needs to die and she can come with us \- that she was only doing her job.  Aury says that he is not going any further if she goes, too. She then slips away and tries to run.  Og’s reaction is to try to punch her and she drops prone again.  She falls and is dazed.  Hap asks “what do y’all want to do with her” and ties her up with rope.  

The group has a discussion.  Aury feels like she would go to the vault and then betray us. Zymve wants to kill her (wants to pull a Hewson).  She pulls out a dagger and walks up to slit her throat.  Og is holding her and Zymve kills her and says “*I did this for all of you, since none of you could do it.  You can sit around and bicker all day, but she wasn’t going to stop*.”  Hap says “she makes a good point.”  Ains checks her pouch and there is 650gp.  A wood elf lies dead in front of us.   Ky’tha goes into stealth (29) and starts walking down the trail to the right.  Ains takes his spell to make it look like she was killed from a lightning bolt vs. a stab \- he does this with great surgical skill \- making it look like the dragon burned her.  Then he grabs the last potion of invisibility and turns to the left.  Hap asks for a shovel and wants to bury the dead girl. Og says a prayer and follows Ains.  They think Ky’tha is mad…is she?  Or maybe she just wanted to duck out from this situation and not be part of the burial or looting of her. Ains tries to feel an innate pull one direction or the other \- his blood tells him to go both ways.  Zymve states that talking to the dragon might be best.  Ains thinks that the dragon might not be reasonable \- we better be ready for the conversation when we get there.  

The group sits at the fork and decides to take the path to the right…

– The End of the Session –

# **23 \- Into the Volcano**

**Session Date**:  April 21, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  37th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Base of Mount Emberstran, fork in the path

### **Moving Foward**

The group stands at the fork, discussing the death of Petal.  Aury is concerned about Ky’tha \- where is she?  The group decides to go to the right \- marching order: Og, Hap, Zymvye, Erwin, Aury, and Ains.  As they walk they talk about if Petal had family and what they knew about her \- not much.  Zymve comments on the “artful scorching” that Ains accomplished on her body.  The group continues down the trail.  The terrain is starting to get darker \- more lava rock is visible now.  

Ains questions that habitat for the dragon \- seems a bit odd for a bronze dragon.  He mentions that those dragons like to live in water, not in a volcano.  Water is a few days away.  

They continue down the path through the afternoon and it seems strangely quiet.  There are no birds, squirrels, etc.  Fleur is scouting and doesn’t notice anything different. Og and Aury think things are abnormal.  Fleur sends a message to Hap "something is on the side of the mountain” \- Hap looks through her eyes and sees it is a large, winged reptile, but it is far away and up the mountain.  He tells everyone what he sees.  Then they hear loud wing beats as the dragon takes off from the mountain and flies off to the west.  The creature looks big \- if it is a dragon \- is it probably old.

### **Irragosa Visits Ky’tha**

The group keeps going \- they go around a bend.  Ky’tha pauses and hears something walking down the trail behind them.  She sees an elf, orange and white robes, long blond hair.  Ky’tha comes out of stealth.  Who are you?  Ky’tha introduces herself, asks who she is.  Ky’tha suspects/guesses this is Irragosa, in humanoid form.  She asks why we are there and if there is a dark haired, darker skinned young person in our party.  Ky’tha confirms.  She asks Ky’tha to keep her eye on Ains…and it would be worth her while.  She doesn’t say why.  She says that she likes Ky’tha.  Also says that if we are not supposed to be somewhere, we won’t be.  She is not interested in small talk.  She leaves and a few minutes later, the group hears wings beats again.

Ky’tha stealths (21) and catches back up to the group. The group continues and is talking about how fast a dragon flies vs. how fast they move and/or fly/teleport.  As they continue, they notice a lack of anything else in the forest or around the mountain \- no other signs of life.  The trail gets thicker as they go.  Og sees small footprints here and there.  They found a few items that might have belonged to hunters \- maybe this is as deep as they would have ever tracked.  Smell of earth \- maybe burning earth, molten.   This seems to be a good spot for a campsite as the night is drawing near.  

Ky’tha reveals herself.  Tells them about talking to Irragosa in her humanoid form. She tells them that she knew we were all here, and was a little choked up in asking/talking about the dark haired member of the group.   We set up camp and take watch.  Aury and Og, then Hap and Ky’tha, then Zymve and Ains.

* First watch \- Og asks his leaf if Petal was important.  No response.  Og thinks it was the wrong thing to do, but is glad that it wasn’t life altering.  Aury says that he agrees (but does he?).  He opens up Diverra’s book to find something that might be relevant to this scenario \- specifically about murder.   He doesn’t.  He admits that he said to kill her (in self defense at one point), but he decides that he needs to repent because her beauty was rare (a blond elf).   Aury notices Og’s leaf on his arm…he sees that one of the thorns is black (and he never noticed it was black before).  He points this out to Og.  Og thinks that maybe it turned black because Petal was killed.  Aury prays to Diverra to seek forgiveness on the group’s behalf. He doesn’t get a sense of direction \- he normally feels something in his heart of guilt, but doesn’t this time.  He tries to tell Og that the group might not have stepped over a line.  Og inspects his arm…sees no other alterations other than turning brown to black on one thorn.  
* Next watch \- Ky’tha and ol’ Hap. She asks Hap if he is going to keep or sell his land.  He thinks he might sell \- he is not a farmer.  Briefly talk of Lady Rook…and how she sent a spy \- maybe there is someone in our group she doesn’t trust.  
* Last watch \- Ains reads his book.  He is surrounded by warmth.  Zymve hears her song (it has something to do with protecting power:  this shouldn’t be found, we have to protect it) \-  the strange timing and hum. It is very pervasive, but she resists waking him up. Ains reads and realizes there is more breaking out.  He sees that this is divided into 4 sections.  He sees something about a relationship between Tsulanan and someone else. He has the sense that Tsulanan was a protector of someone.  He was a hired hand, a confidant, to someone special.  This is late in the first section of the book.  He gets to a part where he sees that he is now referring to a third party \- this person is Irragosa.  As he translates this, Zymve wakes him up.  She tries to replicate the song with her guitar and does.  She performs well.  Ains picks up on a little of it \- “guarded well” and “seal it” and “darkened stone” and “a heart” and “don’t leave a trail.” She is singing in a dialect that she doesn’t know completely, but is able to repeat.  This seems to her like a prelude about a party destined for great things that were stopped before they could achieve them.  The only thing they could do at that point is protect something. 

### **The Next Day – 38th of Talil, 1246**

Morning comes. Group eats rations from Og. Talk of the night. Aury shares his talk with Diverra with the group. Ky’tha talks to the group about compromising and making sure we make no ultimatums or act solely not on the group’s behalf.   Discussion about the vault \- Ains says “my vault” and group decides we will see.  Ains shares that he is learning more about himself as he reads the journal.  And Zymve is enjoying it.  We pack up and leave.  Ky’tha goes into stealth (21 \- she can because of her climb speed).  

The terrain is rough \- there is some climbing involved.  Trail moves between jagged lava rock outcrops, with ledges, there is an area where vines are thick next to a boulder and we see a skeleton.  Og goes to check it out.  He sees a jeweled knife.  A small bronze chain around the neck with a whistle.  There is a huge opening into the mountain on the other side of the boulder.   The body looks about a stretch old; thinks it is a halfling or gnome.  Ky’tha picks up the jeweled knife…then moves to the cave entrance.  She sees something made out of leather \- has a T on the top, made out of a shiny metal (platinum) and pockets it.  Zymve thinks it is a saddle horn.   Hap picks up the chain and whistle and dusts it off and gives it a blow.  Everyone hears a small noise from the whistle, except Zymve who hears a very high-pitched noise.  We then hear a growl.  

IT IS INITIATIVE – Combat vs. Wolves

* ROUND ONE:  Og rages and moves up and hits one of the wolves twice, killing it.  Then moves over to another wolf. Ky’tha shoots her crossbow and crits, killing one.  Aury moves forward and attacks the final wolf, hitting it.  Ains bursts the wolf and hits.  The wolf runs up to attack Aury and misses. He then looks at his fallen friends and starts to run.  Aury gets an opportunity attack and crits, killing it.  

We investigate the wolves.  They are large, dire wolves.  Aury and Zymve investigate them to see if they have been ridden or look magical. Nothing special \- looks like nature's creatures.   Ains thinks the “T” is for Tsulanan, who was a dragon rider (from riding Irragosa).  He tells the group about Irragosa.  

Ky’tha (21) and Zymve (invisible) scout ahead, as the group waits outside.  The tunnel looks burrowed.  Hard to find shadows, but they do.  They go around a bend and see orange light ahead.  Ky’tha hears the wall moving and sees rocks moving and starts to head back to the group.  Zymve sees this and watches the group.  Ky tells the group about the walls.  Tells everyone to alert Zymve if they are about to cast a spell. 

The group moves forward \- trying to be stealthy.  Ky (27), Hap (19), Og (10), Aury (17), and Ains (10).  Og rounds the corner and sees the light and starts forward.  Several bugs jump out.  Surprise attack on Og \- they both miss\!  

IT IS INITIATIVE \- Combat vs. Fire Beetles

* ROUND ONE: Og attacks one and hits and kills it.  Og attacks another and kills it.  He then attacks another and misses, but it still dies.  Zymve casts a spell in front of her and charms them.  Ky’tha runs forward and kills one with her dagger. Hap runs forward and attacks and fumbles (it says “you were not prepared”), losing his reaction.  He then attacks with the pruning dagger and hits, killing it. Ains bursts one and kills it. Aury moves up and tolls the dead on one, and it saves.  They can’t do anything on their turn.  
* ROUND TWO:  Og steps up to attack and crits, and kills one.  Then kills two more.  Zymve attacks one with mockery, but it saves.  Ky’tha kills one with a dagger.  Hap runs forward to kill the last one and misses (the sword is upset).  He then kills one with the pruning dagger.  

Ky’tha casts tremorsense and feels nothing. The group moves forward.  The path winds for some time.  After 5 minutes, Ky’tha feels movement on the ground.  The tunnel widens and we stand at the heart of Mount Emberstran.  We think the center could be a mile across \- it is too far, and too dark to see.  The air hits like a furnace \- it is very hot.  There are lightning arcs in the ceiling way above.  Lava below, but with rocks and paths through the middle. 

Ky’tha sees a shadowy giant form step out of her line of sight.  If we go right, it hugs the inside mountain wall.  If we go straight there is a path across.  If we go left, it would be a slanted path across.  Aury pulls out the tablet and it hums directing us to the path to the left.  Og leads the group ahead and to the left. He sees a lot of evidence of weefolk tracks.  To the right he sees a human and paw prints.  Straight ahead he sees sliding.  

With Og’s first step, he feels the place shake and we all hear a groan.  All of the things that pop, suddenly disappear into the lava.  He heads to the left.  He gets 30’ and sees in the distance a giant stand up \- he is wearing armor \- he is flaming.  He walks out of view.   Ains walks to the front of the party \- since he speaks giant. 

We continue forward.  We see a giant next to a lava pond and he hits something with his maul once it pops up.  And he laughs.  He then walks off in the distance.   We continue forward.  Where the giant is is 60 yards wide \- he is eying the lava pool.   We try to keep out the giant’s line of sight \- hugging the right side of the path (giant is on the left).  The giant is preoccupied with the lava pool \- which is keeping us safe at the moment.  We know there are critters of some kind in the lava, but keep moving.  As we move, a fire creature pops up out of a lava pool and has a surprise attack on Ains and misses, thanks to Aury’s warding flare.  

IT IS INITIATIVE – Combat vs. Salamander & Giant

* ROUND ONE: The fire creature goes after Og with a flame spear and hits. Then grabs Ains and hits.  Og gives Ains temp HP and then attacks and crits the creature and he is unconscious. He then hits again.  He then hits again with his third attack. The attack was so loud that the giant turns his attention our way.  He throws a hammer at Og and hits and he knocks him back 15’ into the lava.  Zymve casts mockery on the salamander and it succeeds.  She inspires Og.  Ains pulls Og 5’ closer.  He then speaks to the giant offering the salamander as a gift (speaking in giant) \- trying to persuade him.  He holds the maul and looks at Ains.  Not sure if this works, but Ains sees him chuckle at the salamander.  Ky’tha pulls out her immovable rod and ties a rope off and throws it to Og in the lava (they both make dex checks for the throw and catch).  Og catches it.  Hap has Fleur fly by the salamander and he attacks it with necrotic damage, and kills it.  The giant roars.  Aury steps to the rope and tries to pull Og and can’t.  He holds the rod.   
* ROUND TWO:  Og takes damage from the lava…45 points of fire. He then pulls himself out of the lava, burning up the rope.  The giant runs forward and asks Ains “is he dead?” Ains says “the bird did it” (in giant).  The giant takes no other actions.  Zymve inspires Ains and prepares a reaction of a cantrip.  Ains tells Hap that we need to sacrifice Fleur and then tells the giant “it is there in the sky” and points at the bird.  Ky takes her rod and stows it and says it common “you can have this” as she moves by and points to the salamander and tries to look respectful.  Hap tells Fleur to land on the salamander and she does.  Aury hides behind Hap and heals Og.   
* ROUND THREE:  Og gives Hap temp HP and puts his weapon up and gestures to the bird.  The giant walks up to the creature with the bird on top of it and he crushes Fleur and the dead creature.  He then shoves the creature into the lava.  He looks at Ky’tha and holds up his fingers, gesturing something small, and speaks in a language she cannot understand.  Zymve prepares a spell as a reaction.  Ains tells Ky’tha that the giant wants the rod. And then he tries to persuade the giant in giant “I am Moganna.” He looks at Ains.  Ky’tha moves forward 30’ and kneels down to look in her pack \- like she is trying to figure out what he wants. Hap moves more inland. Aury heals Og.  
* ROUND FOUR: Og maintains his rage and gives Zymve temp HP.  He then takes the dodge action should he need to move. The giant looks like he is about to say something and then stops.  He then moves to Ains, kneels and bows, and then moves away.  WE ARE FINALLY OUT OF COMBAT.

Ains tells us that he told the giant that this was his vault and the giant believed him.  He tells us that his giant name is Moganna.  Hap remembers that Moganna is attached to a legendary figure.

Group follows the giant…we go for 5 minutes and come to a fork to go left or right.  We pull out the tablet and go left.  We travel and find another place to pick a direction again.  We go right, following the pull of the tablet.  This passage goes a long way and starts to open up to the right (the lava is to the left).  The giant seems to be following the lava.  We hear commotion and see an embankment go up on the right hand side and we see figures moving and rushing toward us.  We see two little gnomes that are being chased by several strange looking fiery warriors on strange red ostrich-like creatures. 

– The End of the Session –

# **24 \- Let The Bodies Hit the Floor**
