IT IS INITIATIVE – Combat vs. Firenewts

* ROUND ONE:  The gnomes appear to be rock gnomes running toward us, daggers in hand.  Ains cast haste on Og.  The gnomes run forward and hide behind rocks.  Hap casts blade ward and moves up 30’.  One of the riders moves up to Og.  Another runs forward and fronts Hap.  The beast snaps at Hap with his beak and misses, then hits Hap with a claw taking piercing and fire damage.  Then the rider tries to hit Hap with a spear and misses, then hits Hap with a long sword, but he deflects the damage, only taking 1 point of fire damage.  Another creature jumps forward from the rocky hill and runs forward (not mounted).  And the final creature also runs down the hill and positions himself behind an outcropping (not mounted). Og hits the rider in front of him, but the guy is not prone.  When hit, the creature spewed lava onto Og.  Og attacks again, hitting and moving around the creature. Two additional creatures show up at the top of the rocky outcropping, holding fiery staffs. One moves up and sends rocks of lava to Hap magically, missing.  The second one hits Hap.  Ky’tha catches movement behind the rock on her left.   She attacks with a crossbow and hits the one on Og, hitting with sneak attack damage.  Zymve casts darkness in the middle of the cavern and moves over to a rock to take half cover. Aury casts channel divinity in 30’ radius around him, to catch the closest rider and mount, and they take radiant damage.  The rider falls off, dead.  Two other creatures with maces emerge at the top of the rocky outcrop, not mounted.  One steps forward and points at Hap, heating up his metal armor \- which grows red hot, inflicting fire damage. The other one runs forward and casts something, removing the darkness spell.  
* ROUND TWO:  Ains stands up and moves out from behind the rock, glowing purple and shoots a lightning bolt, attempting to hit a few creatures \- all 4 take damage.  One of the gnomes runs, the other throws a dagger at the mount, hitting. Then he backs up, but returns to his original spot.   Hap attacks the mount and hits, inflicting damage and tries to trip the mount and does, as the rider lands on his feet. He then attacks the prone mount and misses. Then attacks with his short sword, making a critical hit.  He then uses his dagger to hit the mount again.  The mount by Og jumps up and shoots lava from his mount at Ains and Og, both taking damage. He then disengages and runs and picks up an unmounted guy and moves forward. That rider has no additional movement.  The rider who was unmounted steps forward to hit Hap and the gnome with lava, killing it. The mount tries to bite and claw at Hap and misses, then stands up and scoops up his rider and then moves back.  Og grants Ains temp HP and charges forward to attack the mount in front of him, missing.  He then hits the mount, killing it.  The rider stands up.  Og attacks the rider and hits two more times.  Another creature steps forward from behind a rock and spits lava at Ains and Ky’tha.  Another creature sneaks up behind Hap and misses with an attack, then fumbles on his second attack.  One of the mace casters steps forward and  lava starts to fall from above on Aury, Ains, and Zymve.  Another one steps forward to do the same to Og and Hap.  Zymve stands up to cast a spell and one of the mace casters tries to counterspell it, but it succeeds, the mount and rider both took damage. Ky’tha critically hits the rider in front of Ains with a crossbow bolt, inflicting significant damage, but the rider is still up.  Aury steps forward and heals Og and tries to toll the dead and it misses. The blue caster steps forward and heals all of his allies then looks at Og and slings lava at him, hitting once.  The red caster in the back cheers and then steps up to the one with a cloak of daggers and heals him.   
* ROUND THREE:  Ains steps forward and tries to channel the power of the marrowstone to attack with the necrotic energy.  He feels that he has a choice \- max damage, which could hurt him with a consequence, or just release the energy.  He decides to just release the energy.  Four bad guys and a mount are all in range.  Black energy flows from Ains’ mouth to hit them all.  He kills the mount.  The others all take damage. As a bonus action he then tries to shove the rider in front of him and is pushed back 5’...and then he moves next to a rock.   The gnome takes a dagger and throws a dagger at a mace caster and hits him and hides next to a rock.  Hap turns to attack the creature next to him and misses.  He tries again with the dagger and hits, the creature saves his trip, but takes additional damage.  He then attacks again and misses.  He then misses with the dagger and swings around his bad guy.  One yells for everyone to “get the big one” and they run forward to attack Og.  One attacks and misses, then hits with a flaming blade. Another attacks and hits him twice. Another spins around and hits Og, taking him down. He then grins at the priest and hits Og again.  He then runs forward to Aury.  The guy on Hap disengages from him and moves behind a rock and hid.  Another one runs around a rock and hides. Another one runs forward and aims at Zymve and fumbles its spell, falling prone.  He then tries to hit her again and misses.  Another runs forward, get behind a rock and tries to hit Ains and misses twice.  Zymve tries to heal Og with a spell, but it doesn’t seem to work.  Zymve moves the cloud on top of the blue caster and it takes damage. She then moves next to Hap.  Ky’tha shoots her crossbow at the one in front of Aury, hitting it.  Aury grows wings and flies up, taking an attack of opportunity that hits, and he uses his channel divinity to cast radiance of the dead, hitting 4 characters., killing one of them.  Red caster casts a spell at Aury and heats metal on him.  Blue shoots lava at Aury and misses  
* ROUND FOUR: Ains casts a spell, purple tendrils shoot up from the ground and restrain the creatures. The gnome freaks out and dashes away behind a rock. Hap runs to the red spellcaster and attacks. The spellcaster spews lava on him and he takes damage. He then hits with the dagger and the heat metal spell drops from Aury.  He then hits with his sword. He then crits with his sword (action surge). Then hits again with the dagger and kills the red caster.  He then uses his bonus action to heal himself.  One runs over to Ky’tha and Ains and spits lava, hitting them both.  Another tries to break the restraint and doesn’t, taking damage.  The prone one casts a spell, raining lava on top of Ains and Ky’tha, taking Ains down.  Another moves closer to Aury and spits fire, but misses twice (fumbling).  One of the ones hiding steps out and attacks Zymve but misses twice.   The other one is hiding and pops out and tries to spit fire at Aury and hits.  Zymve moves up, taking an attack of opportunity that misses, and casts a spell that hits 3 people, killing the blue caster and the mace caster. As a bonus action she inspires herself, Aury and Hap, giving them temp HP.  Ky’tha attacks the one on her, hits, then disengages and runs behind the rock. Aury flies down 5’ and 10’ over then casts channel divinity and radiance of the dawn in 30’ radius.  One of the creatures dies.  The prone creature and standing rider both take damage.  He then moves back up 15’ up.  And then casts mass healing word on all living allies.    
* ROUND FIVE:  Ains feels the breath of life and stands up. He shoots lightning on two of the creatures, as both take damage, killing one.  The gnome asks Ky’tha who we are and she tells him “the heroes of Emberstran.”  The gnome holds its dagger and hides again.  Hap throws his pruning dagger and fumbles, losing his reaction.  He then throws a trident at the prone creature and misses.  A creature shows up behind Zymve and attacks her and hits, then crits on his second hit, taking her down.  The caster stands up and rains fire on Zymve and Hap then runs away.   Another runs over to Hap and attacks and misses twice.  Ky’tha drops her sword and shoots her crossbow at one and hits, sneak attack.  It shoots lava back at her, but misses.  Aury flies over and casts a spell on Zymve to heal her.   
* ROUND SIX:  Ains casts a sleep spell on the caster and it counters it, but it fails and is incapacitated. The gnome slips behind Ains and looks up.  Ains attacks the one on him with his sword and hits.  He then pulls another dagger and tries to hit and misses.  He tries his sword again and hits. Then tries the dagger again and hits, killing him.  The remaining one attack Hap and hits, as Hap parries some of the damage.  He then attacks again and hits, this disengages and steps around the corner.  The other one fails a save and falls asleep.  Ky’tha steps forward and shoots the creature and hits.  Zymve is no longer unconscious, but can’t do anything else on this turn.  Aury casts guiding bolt on one and hits, killing it.  Then moves 30’ toward Og.  
* ROUND SEVEN:  Ains tells Ky’tha that she should kill the sleeping one and then asks the gnome who he is.   He says his name is Zabeek and that he should go to his friend Fizzy.  Then Ains runs over to Og.  The gnome runs toward his friend.  Zymve walks over to one of the dead ones near her and kicks him and stomps his head with the heal of her boot.  Ky’tha runs over and crits the last one, killing him.

WE ARE OUT OF COMBAT.

Aury flies down, to address his companion Og, who isn’t moving.  Drawing on the divine power of Diverra, Aury restores Og to life with a Revivify spell (bringing him back up with 1 hit point).  Ains then heals Og, Ains and Zymve. 

The gnome comes over, very sad holding his dead friend.  He tells the group that he lives inside the mountain and had been making a run for it to try to find food. 

We loot the bodies.  There are 4 heavy iron spears, longswords and shortswords and staffs.  The mounts had saddlebags with a metallic lining to keep them from melting.  Og grabs scale mail from two of the creatures to take with him.  We ask Zabeek to take us to his hamlet in the volcano \- it is called [**Sparkwhisper**](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1qY82tYJ_K6VMwEhfBZ-NvuX6WMKdSOpga3E40UzFUdE/edit?tab=t.se4iu1vn9fr8#heading=h.xtkm7fj3vxgs).  He leads the group.

We work our way to his hamlet.  It is at an edge of the mountain, inside.  There are little gnomes with a portcullis that have the area blocked off.  They question the group, but Zabeek vouches for the group, citing that 3 of the party went down, too, at various points.

The area has homes built into the rocky caves.  They are round homes, pieced together with odd pieces of metal, vent pipes, etc.  As soon as the portcullis goes up, it gets dead quiet.  We hear whistling from inside the homes as we walk by \- this seems to be a signal among the gnomes.  We see the Sparkwatch Tower where a couple of gnomes keep a lookout.  A couple of gnomes come out to see us walk by.  We don’t see an inn or tavern, but see a community place where people hang out.  We see a small market when trading occurs.  We see a nearby forge, where wind veins and whistles are made.  We feel like we can get a long rest here.

That night, Zymve uses the forge (Whisper Forge) and a few hours to fashion something for Og.  She talks to Lyra at the forge.  Emberbogs and Fibbergoans seem to be the names of the gnomes here.  We see that the gnomes eat mushrooms and fungi.  They tell us they haven’t been here all that long \- maybe 15+ years.   Aury asks if they have a temple or a place to pray and are told they don’t.  Ains talks to Lyra and finds that she is the Elder \- he mentions Moganna and she has never heard that name.  We find out that the creatures we fought are called “fire newts” and come from “down below” (where Zymve is from).   She also tells us that the dragon flies around all the time.  We ask her about the giants and she says there are many around on the other side of the mountain.  

Hap brings back Fleur and makes amends.   Aury studies his book.  Ains reads his book and has a hard time concentrating because of the whistling in the town. 

We get a long rest. 

### **The Next Day, the 39th of Talil, 1246**

The next morning, Zymve plays a farewell song that is well received.  Many come out with whistles and try to whistle along.  The whistle looks familiar.  We find out that Hap’s whistle (from outside the Mountain) is the same as those in town.  We tell them we found a dead gnome outside and recovered his whistle \- this was Ryland FIbbergoan.  

We eat rations.  We think this hamlet likes us. 

We decide to leave and head towards where the tablet is pulling us.  Aury pulls it out.

We leave Sparkwhisper and go back to the battlefield and find that half of the creatures have been scavenged.  We can tell that critters from the lava pools have probably taken/moved some of them.  We work our way to the right, following the pull of the tablet.

We see a lava pool to the right, but don’t get near the edge.  We decide we need to go direct north.  We notice that the lightning at the top of the cavern we saw yesterday seems to be more active.  Ky’tha looks up and sees 100-150’ up a ledge and notices a shadow of Irrogosa looking around. She points this out to the group.  We continue forward and the lava pond to the right gets smaller.  We see a stairway passage start to head up.  It looks like it winds around up to the ledge. 

We decide to go up:  Ky’tha, Hap/OG, Aury/Ains, Zymve at the back.  Ky’tha notices that these steps predate the last time this chamber was full of lava.  They are made of hardened obsidian that will not melt from lava.  Architecture is dwarven.  We climb up 100+’ up to be level to the ledge.  As we get to the top, it narrows into a massive perfectly smooth dark stone, crafted by master dwarves.  A large depression in the center of the slab is rectangular and there are runes.  Ky’tha tries to read them.  Seems to be a ward of arcane rune format.  She tells Hap they are arcane…and he tries to read them.  He is having trouble as well.  He thinks it is purposefully meant to not say anything.  Ky’tha says the words out loud and the words pulse and glow briefly, but then fade.  She reads it again, once Aury gets the tablet out.  And the tablet glows.  Og suggests that Aury puts the tablet into the depression.  He walks up and places it in the depression. As he pushes, it pushes back.  He turns it around and it still doesn’t work.  He then reads the message inscribed on it and nothing happens \- it pushes back a little harder.  He hands the tablet to Ains who tries to press the tablet in.  A golden light flares along the runes and the mountain rumbles and a door opens, revealing a massive dark corridor ahead.

– The End of the Session –

# **25 \- Grief Stricken**

**Session Date**:  May 14, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  39th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Mount Emberstran

### **Inside the Emberheart Vault \- First Test**

The inside of the vault is clearly of dwarven construction, representing construction that rivals even the most notable dwarven kingdoms.  Through the doorway, there is a tall lengthy tunnel, standing 15-foot high and 10-foot wide, that winds to the left.  Ains pulls out his bullseye lantern and lights the way.  Ky’tha and Ains lead the pack, followed by Hap and Og, then Zymve and Aury.  Ky’tha looks around for traps, sees that everything is very clean.  We start to see a glow from the left.  Ains turns the lantern off.  Ky’tha starts around the bend alone to see where the light is from.  She sees an arch that appears to be raining “fire” \- she goes back to the group to tell them.  The group advances and as we get closer the flame glows brighter and crackles \- everyone needs to make wisdom saves \- we are all feared.  Ky’tha sees runes above the arch “Flames of Fervor.”  Ky’tha feels that the flames almost flare up in line with her heartbeat.  The fear keeps us from proceeding.  Aury argues to turn back.  Ains considers the situation and casts Calm Emotions on the group \- appearing as a warm embrace from Ains in each character’s mind.  And the flames start to flicker and fade away and the passage starts to come into view.  

### **Inside the Emberheart Vault \- SecondTest**

We continue down the passage; same dwarven make.  It levels off and winds back to the right.  We see a familiar glow.  We see another archway with a ghostly figure and a golden cloud/fog.  The figure is holding a massive bronze lance, almost too large for a rider to use that is pointed down the hall toward the party. Ky’tha pauses and notices that it looks very similar to Ains holding the lance.  The figure is 30’ out.  Ains tries to form a telepathic connection with the figure, but it doesn’t work.  We continue forward.  As we get 10’ out, Ains says hello, but there is no response.  Ky’tha and Ains both keep going forward.  “It is Within” is on the runes above the arch.  Ains tries to hug the figure and his hands go through the figure, but the lance is real and not moving.  Og tries to move the lance, it doesn’t budge.   Hap offers to help Og and they try together, but it doesn’t budge.  Ains decides to walk through the arch and into the golden cloud…but it is like a wall. Zymve suggests to Ains to prick himself on the lance and draw blood.  When he does, the cloud fades and the passage continues.  

### **Inside the Emberheart Vault \- The Vault Room**

This passage continues to two double doors made of obsidian.  As we walk to the doors, they start to open.  It opens into a vast, vaulted cavern made out of black obsidian with golden veins that dimly light the area.  There is a black metal pedestal that looks like a dragon talon.  There is a heart-shaped gemstone, a fist sized crystal that pulses.  The other two sockets stand empty. There are draconic runes that pulse slightly.  We hear someone approach from behind. Aury sees a regal elven lady, long blond hair.  Ky’tha notices her and says, ah, hello.  **Irragosa** responds:  You have same smell as **Orah Nelcrath** (stone sister, she calls Ky’tha).  And same Glint in your eye as **Vhurkir Inni** (she says to Zymve, calling her shadow child).  And you have his fire, she says to Ains.  Ains moves closer to the talon and the glowing runes pulse a little faster.  Aury starts to cast a spell to see invisibility and the elf spins to look and listen \- and then turns back to Ains.  Ains says:  you knew Tsulanon and Moganna?  And she responds that she did \- and is waiting for the burden to be lifted.  In her head she then hears:  so I told them that I’m **Moganna** and this is Moganna’s vault.  She says that Moganna designed it.  She says I am here to guard this vault.  Ains moves forward and feels that it is drawing his energy, almost like the marrowstone. He then touches the gem and needs to make a constitution saving throw.  His head falls back and he is caught by the elven lady (as he takes exhaustion).  She then looks at Aury and says “When Moganna first claimed the heart, it nearly consumed him.  Diverra is what helped him. You should take him to the temple of Diverra and get him some rest.  Only then will the fire cease to consume him.”  Aury mentions that he might have a better temple.  She says that Moganna stayed at the Temple in Wimbalt \- this is where I know Diverra’s divine light will help.  He asks if that would be better than taking him to the Tree of Life.  She says she knows not of that location.   Ky’tha asks if Ains is intended to have the gem.  She says yes \- this is one of three items.  She was intended to guard this one.  The other two items are called **Soulroot** and **Starvein** \- and she does not know where they are \- this was knowledge she wasn’t given.  Aury mentions Bloomrest and she says she does not know of that location, but that he needs to take him to someplace where Diverra’s guiding light can help him.  He wants to take everyone to Bloomrest.  Ky’tha asks her if this ends her purpose here in the mountain \- and she says yes \- that she will be returning back to the sea (Glimmering Sea).  Zymve asks if there is anything of value in the mountain \- she laughs and says that she is cut from the same cloth as Vhurkir.  Ky’tha asks of Orah Nelcrath \- she is a shorter dragonborn, from the halls of Dunduar.  Nelcrath is a clan of dragonbornes that lived with Umberfists in that city.  She tells Ky’tha that she carries the same aura as her old circle.  That Orah was a dragonrider (paladin). Vhurkir was a drow that was a dragonrider (rogue).  Orah rode **Karalafene** who was known as **Whiteflare** (silver dragon).  Vhurkir rode **Kaelykk the Daring (Copper)**.  Tsul rode Irragosa (Bronze).  The relic Moganna was attuned to realized that it would break the cycle and chose to break it up and gave to each dragon/rider to hide and guard (not telling each other where they hid the others).  **Ahn’tseul** was a timeless abhorrent being that passes on to a new soul every 50 years.  This relic \- if the 3 parts were combined by the same person \- could extend life and break the cycle.  

Irragosa has not seen her friends in a long time.  When Moganna died, Ainslee was born.  No one has seen White Flare since before the Awakening. Aury starts to tell the elf/dragon about Lady Rook.  Ky’tha jumps in to ask who and how others would have known about this secret.  She tells the group that Ains grandfather was taken by a cult who knew of this \- this was a cult who Moganna was running from.

Zymve asks Iragossa of the fallen flying ship.  She registers guilt on her face and almost breaks down, very vulnerable.  She realizes that she killed innocents and says “I’ll have to find a way to right that wrong.”  She says that she takes down everything around the volcano that doesn’t belong here.  I probably should not have taken them down \- they were just flying off course.  

Discussion about taking Ains to Diverra’s temple.  Ky’tha asks for safe passage out of the mountain and she says she will help.  Og lifts Ains up and puts him on his shoulder.  Ains starts to stir, but he’s exhausted.  Ky’tha confirms that Ains was intended to find this vault \- and she says that he was the only one who could enter and take the gem.  She also confirms that if Ains combines all 3 parts, it will have a different effect than if we find them and reopen the circle, making sure to keep the other parts out of the wrong hands.

### **Leaving the Vault**

As we leave, the elf starts to transition to a dragon.  She provides safe passage for the group by the giants and out of the mountain.   Ky’tha asks if she can touch her and she lets her do it (as the rest of the group freaks out at the suggestion).  No one else wants to touch her.

We wind our way out and start to leave the mountain.  The gertot is where we left it.  Perched atop his saddle is the gnome we rescued, **Zabeek**.  “I kept watch the entire time \- no one touched your beast.  I’m going to go with you because it is time for me to be a proper adventurer.  I won’t slow you down much, don’t you worry.”  Og tells him to ride Ewing. Og asks him what he can do \- he says he can tinker and pulls out a whistle, but the group keeps him from blowing it.  

Group starts to head back, Og carrying Ains.  We travel and eventually find a place to make camp.  Zabeek is helpful with wood gathering.  Ains is too exhausted to study his journal.  Aury studies his book, trying to learn how to use the petals.  Watch order \- Zymve and Ains; Og and Aury, Hap and Ky’tha.  Og gets the sense that we are being watched by the dragon, thinks it would be nice if she were to observe for a while.  Aury tries to read and finds references to the petals:  they state that he can take up to 3 with him and it will consume 3 petals (4 total people, including Aury).  If he goes alone or takes 1 with him, it will consume 1 petal.  Ritual will take 10 minutes to cast.  Zabeek is on duty with the first watch \- asks about rules of the camp.  Og tries to tell him \-see something, say it, sort it. They wake up Ains and Zymve.  Fleur flies around during the night \- when Hap sees through Fleur, he knows that the dragon is near by.  Ains and Zymve talk about his exhaustion.  He speaks to her about how their ancestors know each other.  Ains asks if he is still alive, but Zymve knows:  she remembers the name of her ancestor as the person who founded jewelcrafting.  She thinks he was quickwitted, how he was secretive and conniving, had a knack for designing things.  She thinks maybe he designed the layout of the vault in the mountain, and might know of other vaults.  She doesn’t know what happened to him \- he was long gone from the Underdark.  Ains talks about what the plans are and Zymve talks about needing to take him to Diverra’s temple.  He asks her to play him a song and she agrees.  As she does this, Ains is going to try to mimic Aury and send a message to the priestess of Diverra in Emberstran.   “This is Aury.  Urgent matter.  My drow companion and handsome human companion need care.  Prepare room for them, fine silks, as prepared by Diverra herself.”  He rolls a 30 for deception.  He then continues “I’m always excited to see you.  Your beauty shines as bright as Diverra herself. Explain further why excited?  Two need new clothes.  Blessed be.” Gets a response then he sends “Don’t mean to be forward. Filled with so much love.  The world looks beautiful now. Wish you the best forever.  I still celibate babe, bye.”

Hap and Ky’tha then speak about the day.  Touching the dragon.  Hap comments that she is about and Ky’tha speaks to the air to her.  Hap then talks to his sword and it tells him in his head that “we could take her,” referring to the dragon.  He tells Ky’tha they are speaking.  She encourages them to speak.  Hap asks for a name \- “Shadowtrail.”  Ky then asks if it has told him about others who have wielded him.  It hasn’t. Night passes uneventfully.  All, but Ains get a long rest. 

### **It’s the morning of the 40th of Talil, 1246**

We continue on.  We eventually come to some dead quaraths on the side of the road, with lightning slashes on them (clearly the dragon has been helping).  We decide to investigate the fallen flying ship. We make camp that night. Over dinner, we talk to Aury about finding a companion and love.  He says that he is not open to that.  That he is 100% devoted to his goddess. Aury says he loves everyone in our group.  Ky’tha speaks that she’s open to a companion and that we all should be \- that someone who gives you the tingling feeling in your parts.  The gnome speaks up about avenging his friend who died \- that he is going to adventure because of him.  We all get a long rest.

### **It’s the morning of the 41st of Talil, 1246**

Next day, we head to the ship.  We no longer feel the presence of the dragon \- we start to hear nature again.  Og tries to find his way to the wreckage. After a half day’s travel we find the wreckage \- first by smell, and then by sight. We find ripped and torn clothing, bodies not in good shape.  We find 112gp, another 275sp, a couple of misc. rings and necklaces (100gp), destroyed bedding.  Ky’tha finds something odd:  metallic casing that is shaped like an orb that looks like it was set on top of a pedestal that may have been mounted to the deck of the ship.  Inside are floating pieces of stone.  She asks Ains about magic with the orb, which he does.  And it is transmutation.  We think it might have been related to how the ship flew and decide to take the orb with us.  Hap thinks about the orb and remembers that Iannan Rambibur was a gnome in Tarnsport who was studying magic that would make things levitate and fly.  He thinks that this might have something to do with that.  Is thinking about lodestone.  Ky’tha walks around the ship, looking for a name, but finds it was destroyed by lightning.

Aury looks in his book for a prayer that would help forgive the dragon, while blessing the innocent lives lost. He sets up a pyre to burn the bodies and bless them.  Hap assists him.  He feels like his prayers were heard and feels like there is an understanding that he could convey to the dragon, should their paths cross again.  

We take a few hours at the ship and then leave. As we walk, we talk about preparations at the temple \- how Ains will need to be on an altar once we get back, in order to bathe in Diverra’s light. 

We make camp.  During the night, Ains asks Zymve to sing to him again.  He casts sending to the priestess.  “Preparations for the handsome human will need to be at the altar, as deemed by a dragon met on the road.  The dragon was in favor of Diverra.  Still celibate.  Blessed be.”  

### **It’s the morning of the 42nd of Talil, 1246**

Travel, camp.

### **It’s the morning of the 43rd of Talil, 1246**

Travel, camp.

### **It’s the morning of the 44th of Talil, 1246**

We start traveling into Emberstran, with the gnome on the back of Ewing.  Ky’tha instructs the gnome to be seen and not heard.  The guards say “welcome home heroes.” Zabeek leans in and says “is that one of those things we are not supposed to talk about.”  Zymve notices and raises her eyebrow at Ky’tha.  Ky replies \- let us make our way to the temple and all will be revealed.  The streets have ears.

### **The Temple of Diverra**

As we get to the temple, we see the priestess and Lady Rook.  There is a new garden under construction \- a new stone fountain is half built.  “You have returned at the right moment, Aury” and Ains messages Aury to “go with it.”  Lady Rook and her guild have funded and designed a new Temple of Diverra, says the Priestess.  Then she looks at Ains and says \- this is the person you spoke of \- you look terrible.  Come, you must go and rest under the guiding light of Diverra.  She says she will go get the acolytes to get everything ready.  Lady Rook stands with us. Aury is disappointed. Zymve glares at Rook.  Rook says I won’t insult your intelligence.  Yes, I had you followed.  I knew that the tablet was tied to ancient bloodlines and I feared what would happen if it fell into the wrong hand.  I offer my knowledge freely and offer it to you.  She pulls out a silver dragon scale and offers it to us.  I will help if you’ll let me.  Ky asks her about the dragon scale. It was found on the peak of Tibul (she got it from Endavo).  He’s a bit preoccupied.  He’s found a spitespirit key.  She knows of nothing else about him.  Do you know when he found the scale, asks Ky’tha.  She replies that she has had this for a while.  Ky’tha asks why she is just now telling our group about all of this \- why now?  Because she was found out or because she views us as allies?  She takes the scale and puts it in her pouch and was offended and walks away.  Hap runs after her and says I’ve been thinking about it and think I want to sell that land \- I don’t want it to go to waste.  Let me know what i need to do.  She advises him to talk to one of his colleagues. 

The priestess comes back:  Aury can I have a moment of your time.  She takes him into the Temple.   When they go, Ky’tha tells Hap that his sister is on the way and shares the contents of the note that she was given at the gate.  She is likely 3 days out \- coming from Ulgrey.  Ky’tha asks Hap if he needs anything to prepare for her visit \- accommodations as well as making sure the Cloak of Defiance is sufficiently weakened. 

When Aury and the Priestess come back, she looks at all of us suspiciously.  Then she says that she will go find the boy and leaves.  Arnol, two nights ago, was ambushed and barely alive.  The priestess didn’t press him to find out answers as to who ambushed him.  Zymve offers to press him, and Aury mentions that he is safe for now so we don’t need to do anything.  Aury comments that the preparations have been made. 

We decide to break up for the night \- Aury and Ains at the Temple, Og and Zymve will take Zabeek to the Warm Bear and take Ains possessions, Hap will stay at the Temple, Ky’tha will go find Tessa and then make arrangements to stay at the Hydra for herself and for Hap and his family for tomorrow. 

### **The Warm Bear Inn**

It looks like there is a bard who is a regular there.  Zymve goes in as herself to drink.  The three of them sit down to eat and drink.  The bard is doing a good job, but then notices Zymve and stops his song in the middle and pauses and then says “something has come up \- I’ll have to…see you tomorrow night.”  And starts to gather his belongings.  As he is packing up, Zymve flips him 1gp and winks at him.  He is unsure of what to do and is intimidated and walks away.  Zabeek asks what that was about.  Zymve says don’t worry about it.  Og says it is a long story.  Gnome says it sounds like there is a lot to learn with this adventuring.  He asks about fighting with a dagger.  Og and Zymve try to talk to him about balance…and she catches him trying to snag something off her pouch.  She yells for more drinks (at half price for the heroes).  

Ains goes into the altar room and there is a tapestry of roses draped around the altar surrounded by candles in stands.  Aury says for him to lay upon the altar.  He agrees and lays down. The second he does, he feels at peace.  He sees Aury standing over him…and the Priestess as well.  Aury kneels down and opens his new book and starts reading passages from the book in front of the Priestess…hoping she will notice the changes in the material.  He feels inspired and confident in what he is saying.  He feels like she will likely ask him about it later.  Aury plans to spend most of the evening with Ains.  She says for Aury to let her know if he needs a break, and leaves.  Ains decides to submit to the sleep to try to get the full benefit.  But before going to sleep, he tries to cast a sending spell to Iragossa \- “sleeping now in Diverra’s church in Emberstran.   If you don’t hear from me soon, avenge me.  You were my grandfather’s favorite.” Then falls asleep.  As he sleeps, Aury is praying with sincerity.  Purely for Diverra’s blessing to fully heal him.

### **Our Exquisites**

Ky’tha finds Tessa at Our Exquisites, the new Etterset HQ.  Tessa runs up to her and says “I’m afraid that message got to you too late.”  Why do you say that?  “I’m afraid we have had misfortunes in Ulgrey.”

– The End of the Session –

| 8 | *Character level at end of the session* |
| :---: | :---- |
| **4** | *Sessions played at that level* |

# **26 \- Bad News & A Wild Goose Chase**

**Session Date**:  May 25, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  44th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  City of Emberstran

### **In Emberstran**

At “**Our Exquisites**”, the headquarters for the **Etterset**, Tessa pulls a paper from her pouch and slides it to Ky’tha.  This came from **Ulgrey**.  

## 

## “Four of them pulled up in a carriage. Some wore the crimson cloaks. Not all. One pointed a finger at her husband… black fog wrapped around him and he dropped. Another cast something on Phira. She started groveling on the ground. They chained her and took her with them.”

She finds out that they left **Ulgrey**.  There is a quick discussion between Tessa and Ky’tha about how word of Phira’s travels got to the Cloak, but no conclusions are drawn.

Ky’tha heads to the Temple to Diverra and looks for Hap.  She tells him the news and he replies “confound it; bad news and a wild goose chase.”  They discuss needing to go to Ulgrey to investigate, but also maybe trying to confront the carriage on the road if we assume that they are heading to Emberstran from Ulgrey.    They discuss Ky’tha heading to the Warm Bear Inn to tell Og and Zymve; while Hap will catch Aury and Ains up to speed.

Ky’tha gets to the Inn and there is still frivolity in the air.  Og is drinking with the hunters, Zymve, and Zabeek.  Ky’tha briefs them.  Discussion about how Rook might not be the best to tell, but Ky’tha explains that she and Hap will visit her, while the rest provision up.  Ky’tha advises rest and heads back to the Hydra to rest as well.

### **It’s the 45th of Talil, 1246**

The group wakes up.  At the Warm Bear Inn, Zymve goes down and sees the owner (Gustav) cleaning up.  She notices a platinum band on his hand with a ruby \- and a distinctive style that looks drow. She is on edge and tries to make small talk to look closer.  He notices and holds up his hand to show her.  He says that someone left it here, settling his tab with it vs. coin.  It was a patron who had been there a few days.   This is a shadow thread gem \- from Zymve’s family.  She inquires about the man and is told he was a nicely dressed drow, black leather and midnight blue silks, very polite.  Points out where he sat….like he was expecting something. Zabeek then comes down and asks where they are going, then remembers.  Og then comes down, yawning.  Zymve thanks him for the information and is on edge.  Og notices that Zymve is off today as they start heading to the temple.

Ains feels better today than he has in a long time.  He overhears a conversation in the room next to him.  He hears the Priestess talking to Arnol.  Arnol said he got a message saying he needed to get information and was going to Argent Halls when he was jumped.  He tells her he is not going to have anything to do with the cult anymore and that he should have listened to Aury sooner.  She then comes into the room and places a hand on Aury’s shoulder and tells him that Arnol will be ok; that he is leaving the cult.  She asks Aury to look into that cult and make sure that they are not a threat to Emberstran.  About this time, Ains wakes up and asks how he got here.  Aury tells him that Og carried him and that he prayed over him all night.   Ains says he feels “open” (but not open to Diverra when Aury asks him).  Aury says it might be best to send Arnol to another temple in another city to give him a fresh start and away from here.  They discuss where to send him.  

### **A Stranger Visits the Temple**

Hap then comes into the room and notices an acolyte letting in a tall elf with ice blue eyes who smiles at Hap as he approaches him.  He hands Hap a small wrapped box wrapped in crimson silk with a bow. 

## 

## “Some gifts carry things beyond their size and this gift has waited long enough.”  

Hap says we have not met and asks him who he is:  “probably a story for another day,” as he accepts the box and the elf walks away and out of the Temple.  

Aury and the Priestess discuss sending Arnol to **Stoneshore**.  The Priestess leaves as Aury gives her 20 gold pieces for Arnol’s travel.  Hap approaches and briefs Aury on his sister and also carries this box.  Ains hears as well.  They discuss strategies.  He tells them he wants to go to Ulgrey.  Ains asks about the package.  No one recognized the elf who delivered it. They discuss the plan \- needing to reprovision, maybe getting mounts.  Aury questions if Zymve would know how to ride. Ains mentions that he knows that drow have associations with spiders so maybe so.  Hap starts to open the package.   It is nice crimson silk with a black bow.  There is a very fine box inside \- elven make, with inlay of silver in the wood.  He sees a severed finger wearing a signet ring.  This is definitely his father’s signet ring and the finger is fresh.  There is a note attached to the lid. 

## 

## “Hap \- unfortunately the brothers Varmond had to be dealt with.  I’ve asked your sister to be sent to me for a discussion.  Maybe in time we can earn the respect of you and your companions.” 

## – signed the ***Shadow Sovereign***.  

Ky’tha is able to piece together the following:

* The Shadow Sovereign is the leader of the Cloak of Defiance and is likely in the Bellows in Basctdelm (*successful History check*)  
* This finger was likely **Jerik**’s finger \- he must have taken the ring from Hap’s father  
* The elf that delivered the box is likely the new leader of the Cloak of Defiance in Emberstran  
* This new leader likely is the one that took out Jerik Varmond

So Phira is being taken there to talk to the Shadow Sovereign…and we no longer need to go to Ulgrey.  Discussion about finding a quicker way to get there \- maybe through Imonorra?  

Ains asks the group to sit around him for 10 minutes.  We do and it feels like we have webs in our brain that allows the group to talk telepathically to each other (this is the Telepathic Bond spell).  Aury chose to not take part….but Ains can still talk to him, he just isn’t part of the group chat.  Hap and Ky’tha head to talk to Lady Rook, while Ains goes to Imonorra’s.  Zymve, Zabeek, and Og go shopping. Aury stays back to heal Arnol and take him to a ship. 

### **At the Suncrest Cellarium**

Hap and Ky’tha head to the Suncrest Cellarium out of the town to meet with Lady Rook.  They knock on the door and are met with someone who says that Lady Rook probably doesn’t want to talk to us.  But we insist and the guy goes to get her and we go in.  Ky’tha and Hap apologize and brief her on the situation.  Ask for the dragon’s scale.  Offers to give her information.  Tells her about the brothers being taken out and discussion about why someone would do that.  Hap shows her the finger and the note.  Discussion about new leadership in the Cloak.  Discussion about mutual trust.  She hands Ky’tha the silver dragon scale.  Asks for the funds returned from the spy (600 gold pieces).  Ky’tha gives her 50 gold pieces as a show of good faith.   Discussion about Ains asking for help with passage to Basctdelm from Imonorra and finds out that the wizard is out of town, returning tomorrow.  And they leave.

### **At the Silver Spaulder**

Zymve, Zabeek, and Og go to the Silver Spaulder.  They see the owner, **Vyaanis Aeldorien**, opening up the shop.  Zymve disguises herself as Lynda again and asks Og and Zabeek to wait outside.  She goes inside and is welcomed.  She mentions that she ran into a drow like him the other day and asks him if he knew another drow \- she mentions the ring he was wearing and that she knows that he came from the Underdark.  She asks if he has Underdark contacts.  She asks him if he has seen a drow in the last few days \- trying to find out who that guy might have been.  He says his memory is fading and she pays him 1gp.  He then mentions that the guy was asking about fine armor.  She then pays him another 1gp and asks for his name.  He tells her he was from a big lake in the Underdark (**Ys’nonterien**).  He didn’t give him his name….said he was from a certain house, but can’t remember. She says that she will give him a 20% cut of anything she buys from the drow.  He is from **House Maerth**.  

## 

## Vyaanis leans in … “*This guy seems very dangerous, you should be careful.*”

She says if you ever run across him again, I’d be obliged if you would tell the Priestess to tell Aury and will pay you for information.  She then pays him another 1gp for his information. 

She walks outside and is more anxious than before.  Og asks if she is ok.  He hands her a bug and she squashes it.  He knows that she is grumpy.  They decide to go buy rations for Og and ink for Ains. Zabeek pulls up to Zymve and asks her what is wrong \-”you don’t look fine.”  She says that you have to hide your feelings when you become a hero: “never let them know your weaknesses.”  Og then brings up his dead friend and Zabeek gets sad and says “this is really hard, Zymve” and tries to push the emotions down.  She tells him that is the healthy thing to do, as Og comments that drinking will help, too.  They then stock up. 

### **Aurelius to Escort Arnol**

Aury heals Arnol and talks with him about moving him to another city to get a fresh start.  He is excited about this and is a willing participant.  He gathers his belongings and the two head to the docks to secure passage for Arnol on the ship known as *The Wandering Lantern*.

### **At Imonorra’s Tower**

Ains goes to Imonorra’s Tower and the assistant comes to the door.  He asks to see the wizard and finds out that she is not there.  The butler goes to get tea for Ains and a small fire elemental emberling appears.  Ains mentions that the butler and wizard are close.  They talk about her powers to help transport people and Ains asks directly if she has a book of sigils.  He mentions that she probably has her books on her.  Says that she will be back tomorrow.  He questions if she has an extra set of books and asks to borrow it.  He says that he can’t loan out her books.  Ains offers a trade to him and he says to share what he has to offer.  Ains says he has important political information that the wizard would like and then asks for the **Basctdelm Arcane Waypoint Sigil**.  He says it takes it to the House of Myr and if you show up unannounced that they will kill you.  Ains asks what the man looks like who runs sigil in the **House of Myr**.  The butler describes the contacts at the House of Myr to reach out to before arriving:

* Primary contact:  **Igdan O’bald** – male human, mid-forties, short salt-and-pepper hair, trimmed beard, weathered features  
* Secondary contact:  **Gelora Fornak** – female half-elf, long auburn hair, keen emerald eyes  
* Alternate contact:  **Abaddonn** – May not always be there; male tiefling, tall and muscular, tattoos  

Ains then asks to get the sigil in return for the info.  Butler says that he needs to give up the information first.  Ains says that he has info about the Cloak of Defiance and offers it up.  The butler gets a book, goes to the opposite end of the table, turns to a particular page, shows Ains the Basctdelm sigil.  He tells Ains not to touch the book as he studies the image.   He sees the name of the person that he should contact before using the circle.  Ains thanks him for his help, drinks his tea, and Ains tells him about Jerik being killed and about the tall, blue-eyed elf that appears to now be running the Cloak of Defiance in Emberstran. Ains then tries to send a message to the blue-eyed elf from “the heroes of Emberstran.”  He receives a message back and tells the butler that the guy is in town and seems to be powerful and that the wizard should be “on her guard.”  Butler agrees to relay the warning to her and hopes she will find the information an equitable trade.  Ains then asks to see the wizard’s sigil and says he would never show up unannounced.  He agrees to show him.  They walk upstairs to the third floor where there is a circle.  Ainslie makes note of the **Tower of Imonarra’s Arcane Waypoint Sigil**.  Keep in mind that if you show up the arcane wards will, well ... and points to a display on the wall where there are gemstones set out.  One of them looks like a film inside…they hold the people who showed up unannounced.  Ains asks for her titles.  He says he isn’t sure, but maybe Imonorra the Magnificent.   And Ains leaves.  He tells us that the wizard was not at the tower, but “I got what we needed.”  Ky’tha says they will meet at the temple.  Hap asks Ains to reach out to Phira and see how she is \- is she safe, well, where is she, that we know about her husband and Jerik is dead, too; Hap is worried about her; and that Ains will save her. 

Ainslie reaches out to Phira with a Sending spell.  Phira responds that she is not well, but safe. Says she doesn’t know where she is at.  Knows she will be questioned.  Is unable to be saved. Ains responds back and finds out that the group listened to his message to Phira. She says do not go to Basctdelm or she might die.  Ainslie knows these must be powerful people, reading her thoughts and having a way of cutting off any ability to locate her.   

Meanwhile, Aury puts Arnol on the ship \- makes sure he is safe.  They discuss how Arnol doesn’t know how Aury adventures.  He then shares that the people who beat him up weren’t cultists, but seemed to be thugs.  He also says I don’t know why the master (of the cult) would have sent me out alone.  He is evil.  He says he hopes the cultists don’t hurt anyone else in the town.  He then offers to take Aury to the cultist hideout, but Aury convinces him that they can’t go, but asks Arnol for directions on how to get there.  Arnol admits that he thought Ains was smarter than he was.  Arnol tells Aury that Ains asked Arnol to spy on the cultists for them.  And tells Aury that Ains and Og also visited the cult group, too \- that Arnol saw him there.  Aury asks if there is anything else Ains said.  Arnol just says he went to be a spy and got pulled in and that “those people are weird.”  Aury gives him instructions on what to do when he gets to Stoneshore:  start small, don’t be too ambitious.  Arnol hugs Aury and then says “stay celibate” as he turns to board the ship.

### **At the Dyed Cane**

The group meets at the Temple and walks to the Dyed Cane.  There we ask to go up to the private room upstairs \- Aury pays the owner 1gp.  He asks about the tablet that we were working on. Aury shows it to him and they talk about it a little bit.  Aury shares that the group met a dragon.  He knows of Iragossa.  He is surprised that we are still alive.  He says that we need to tell this story to the town.  That Zymve would be the best one to tell it.  She is not excited to jump in.  Discussion of changing our name.  Ky’tha brings up wanting to talk to Zymve at some point about her drow connections.  She says she doesn’t really have any.  Ky’tha asks her if she feels like the drow armorer is an honest broker. Zymve thinks so.  Aury brings up needing to make sure that the cultist group is not a threat to the town.  Zymve asks about the cult group \- the **Eradic of Ghone** \- how they rob graves.  

We discuss approaching the cult.  Hap puts the family signet ring on after Ains cleans it up.  Hap needs to thank the elf for the ring.  He wants to find out what the guy wants.  He has his sister and yet also wants to make peace with the Heroes of Emberstran.  “This is not congruent.”  As we talk, Aury starts to pray to Diverra.   Seems like we have multiple paths \- take care of the cultist group, approach OR wait to talk to the new Cloak of Defiance leader(s), take the dragon scale and try to find the next gem. Aury lifts his head and says that “I got an actual answer from Diverra:  we are not meant to go after the Cloak (that is not Aury’s calling), the cult (the cult is no more), and about Phira (and she says that could hurt Phira).  

Aury asks the group to check out the cultist group.  We agree to go. It is mid-afternoon. We walk to the Church of Crael to gather for 10 minutes \- all except Aury \- including Zabeek. Zabeek goes to Warm Bear Inn to be a lookout.  He gets lost and goes to the hideout.  The rest of the group catches up.  There are people outside who look like hired Emberstran militia.  The entrance is heavily boarded over.  The area has been sealed off by the Lord. Ains and Og present their EGD badges and ask for entrance.  The guard tells us that he has heard noises inside \- he thinks the cultist have all become zombies.  The guard pulls boards off to open up the entrance.  As the final board is pulled off and the door is opened \- there is a nasty stench of rotting flesh, decay and sewage.  Many of the walls are busted down and broken.  We see a hole in the floor that leads to the sewers.

We go inside. We hear splintering boards above and dust and debris fall down. Haps asks Aury to light up Og’s glaive. We see something huge drop and stop and turn and look at us.  This is an eye with a lot of tentacles pointing to us.  

IT IS INITIATIVE – Combat vs. Zombie Beholder and Vampire

* ROUND ONE:  Og rages and moves up to the side of the creature and attacks and hits.  He tries to knock it prone, but can’t.  He then attacks again and hits again and stays put. Ains shoots an orb of lightning at it and misses.  He then runs into another room and pushes Hap 5’ to the side.  Ky’tha moves over to Og and attacks and misses then disengages.  Zymve casts a spell and it hits, inflicting psychic damage. Then moves to the corner of the adjacent room.  The creature casts a pulse of energy at Aury (wisdom) and sends another pulse of energy to Hap (constitution).  Both save. Aury casts radiance of the dawn on it and it saves, taking half damage. Hap runs up to the other side of him, Fleur providing a distraction, and hits with his sword.  Then he hits with the pruning dagger. He then attacks with his short sword again and fumbles, falling prone. From the ground he hits with the pruning dagger again.    
* ROUND TWO:  Zymve sees a mist come down and wrap around Ains and then appears behind him.  She is scantily clad with pale skin, long dark hair, eyes glowing red.  She whispers to Ains:  **Countess Nas Verdidra** will not be pleased with this (Hap overhears).  Both Ains and Hap know that the Countess rules a territory on the east side of Myrdae:  a place called **Khasvadia in Var Shala.**  She attacks Ains and misses him, as he throws up a shield.  She then hits him with her second attack and knocks him prone and tries to bite his neck as he is grappled.  She bites him and he takes additional damage, which also reduces his maximum hit points.Og hears something and sees a woman biting Ains on the neck beyond the beholder. He attacks the creature and hits. He then hits again and kills him \- as he falls into the sewer below, where Og sees zombies below.  He then moves to the vampire and attacks her, hitting and knocking her prone.  Ains’ eyes flare purple with gold and reaches out his hands to cast a spell on her, imposing disadvantage on her strength save, but she chooses to save.  Ky’tha dashes over and attacks, hitting her with a sneak attack.  Zymve attacks with a spell, hitting with psychic damage.  She would be frightened of Zymve, but chooses to save.  She then gives inspiration to Ky’tha, Ains, and Og, giving them 8 temp hit points.  Aury casts daylight around the vampire and group.  Hap runs over to the vampire and hits her with his shortsword.  Then hits with the pruning dagger. Then hits with the short sword again and tries the dagger again and hits.   
* ROUND THREE:  Og tries something to move the vampire, but can’t.  She maintains her bite on Ains, inflicting more damage and reducing his max hit points further. She looks up at Aury and tries something on him that fails. She then uses her claws on Ains and misses then fumbles, staying prone. She stands up and moves and Hap hits with his opportunity attack.  Og gives Ains 9 temp hit points then moves to attack the vampire and hits twice, dealing huge damage and reducing her speed by 25’. She looks in the pits at a zombie and “misty steps” into the pit next to the zombie.  She claws a zombie’s neck.  Ains stands up and moves to the edge of the pit and tries to do something, but she chooses to save.  He then misty steps away from the hole.  She looks at Og and he has to make a saving throw and says “halt” to him.  Ky’tha moves next to the hole and looks down, she is 15’ down.  Ky’tha jumps into the pit on her and attacks and hits, then disengages.  Zymve runs to the hole and casts a spell and she saves.  She then gives bardic inspiration to Ky’tha.  Aury casts daylight in the hole.  Hap jumps into the hole and lands on her.  He then attacks her with his shortsword and hits and then hits with the pruning dagger.  She yells “avenge me countess” and the sunlight starts to fry her body.  

Hap and Ky’tha then kill the zombies in the hole.  They set fire to the zombies/vampire.

Then the group hears in their heads from Zabeek (except Aury) …

## 

## *“Hey \- this guy is really cool, he just gave me a ruby ring.”* 

– The End of the Session –

# **27 \- A Bitter Day**

**Session Date**:  June 9, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  45th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  City of Emberstran, Ghone cultist hideout

### **In Emberstran**

We leave the hideout and stand next to two guards in an alleyway.  They gush. We tell them that we took care of everything.  They ask us to tell the Captain on our way back into the city.  Aury says he needs to tell the Priestess about what just happened.  We talk to Zabeek in the group thread and tell him to meet us at the Market.  Ains mentions that he is not feeling well and Aury offers to heal him as we walk to the Market.  Zymve appears anxious and Ky’tha tells her that she can calm down \- we are out of the fight.  

### **Zabeek Meets Phryzn**

We see the Market in the distance as we start walking and we see Zabeek walking to us.  He walks up and says “you made it out.  I think I did something \- I was heading back \- and this fella stopped me and said his name was ***Bitter*** or something weird like that and we just started to talk.” Zymve interrupts:  “what did you tell him?”  And he said that he was hanging out with adventurers and named us all, including Lynda \- but don’t worry they are going to be leaving town soon and heading down the road.  He offered to give me a ring for the “goat.” And he said the ring looked too valuable and tried to decline the trade, but the guy was insistent.  He asked about if Lynda played musical instruments and how you played and I told him that you had the instruments with you.  Zymve remembers that she knew someone called Bitter \- he is a distant cousin (**Phyrzn Inni** is his real name \- “**Bitter**” was the bullying nickname they gave him).

Ky’tha asks which way he went \- maybe we could catch up to him.  He points to the Market and Og takes off after him.  Ky’tha asks what he looks like \- silver hair, tied back, pale violet eyes, dark skin, dark traveling clothes, hooded cloak.  The group takes off to follow him and Ky’tha kindly admonishes Zabeek for making the trade.  I shouldn’t have done it, he asks?  She says simply “No.”

### **The Halfling Rudi** 

As Og enters the market he sees a family of halflings with Ewing.  They are trying to stuff his packs with their gear.  Og looms up to them “excuse me, I hate to be a burden, but that is my goat.  It was traded without my permission.”  He asks Ewing to paw into the dirt to prove he is our goat.  He does.  And the halfling says the same thing \- and he does.  We all approach and the halfling recognizes us and asks Hap to have a meal with him.  He says his name is **Rudi Digglepot**.  He even offers to throw the goat in.  But if Hap doesn’t want to sell the land \- he will pay to rent it and look the land over.  Hap tells him that his proposal sounds sweet. “You seem like a right fine fella \- you can go tend to the land and we will come to an arrangement later.”  They share a spit handshake.  The guy says we can meet at the Crimson Swan…as Ains says I’d rather not eat there.   The guy says he could meet when or wherever…and suggests other places.  But Hap says he will follow up \- he will find you on his land at some point.  And the halflings say they will just move in…and seem pleased.

Meanwhile, Ky’tha points out that the stuff in the packs they are trying to overload is ours.  And the man says that the guy \- that looked like Zymve \- just offered to give it to them.  Zymve asks which way he went and takes off in that direction.  Aury yells for her to stop and Ky’tha follows her.  Aury follows, too.  They go through the center of the Market and it is dinnertime and busy \- and we can’t find him.  Ky’tha thanks her for her zeal to find the guy who took our goat, but it’s ok.  Aury expresses concern about the ring \- thinks maybe it is going to be used for spying.  Zymve recognizes the gem \- this is from her family.  They wait and eventually the halfling family walks by, followed by Ains, Og, and Hap.

Zymve asks the halfling what was traded for a goat \- and nothing was traded.  He was just offered the goat because they looked like they could use it.  She gives the ring to Ains and asks him to detect magic.  He uses his wand.  No magic is revealed on the ring \- he sees magic on other people (various weapons).  Ains tells the group that he senses divination magic on the ring and she throws it to the ground and starts to break it with a hammer (this is worth at least 400 gold pieces).  She doesn’t care about the value and shatters the gem and ring.  Ains and Aury gather up the ruby dust.  Zymve and Aury reach for the pewter ring \- but she grabs it first and chucks it into the water.  Aury does not like that she destroyed the ring and says that Zymve has anger issues.  

Ky’tha asks Zymve why she’s so upset.  She mentions that she thinks she was the target here.  Aury pipes up to disperse the crowd as a priest \- telling everyone to move along.  Og and Ains flash their EGD badges.  We head to the Dyed Cane for some food and privacy.  As we walk, Ains expresses his desire to lay down and be alone \- says he will catch up with Og at the Warm Bear Inn later.   He makes his way to the Inn and casts a spell on himself as he gets close to make sure he isn’t seen.  He tries to go and find a room.

### **Ainslie Goes to the Warm Bear Inn**

When he gets there it is lively and he asks Gustavo how much a keg would be to purchase \- or at least keep it open for a few hours.  He says for 5gp you can keep it flowing for the room.  He just asks to not be disturbed as he puts his head on the bar.  He says “free drinks for everyone as long as I’m not touched.”  And he takes a short rest while the bar drinks. Once he wakes up he gets his usual room.  He feels a little better and pays another 5gp to have another keg tapped and he goes to his room.   He props a chair against the door and sits on the bed and starts reading.  

### **At the Dyed Cane**

We get to the Cane and find Bordi and Aury pays him 2gp.  He says we can’t take Ewing in, but we convince him that we will go straight to the private room and Og runs him up the stairs.  Zabeek expresses his disappointment in our activities, but we tell him this isn’t normal.  We then go back to the conversation.  Zymve says that she has seen thousands of them \- that she’s cut them herself.  This is a family occupation.  Her family cuts gems for the drow nobility.  So a man with these rings is a sign that they are trying to get her back.   She says that “the spider queen” doesn’t just let people go.   She says that she didn’t want to do that for the rest of her life and snuck away in the night.  Ky’tha wonders why the man didn’t approach her personally and she doesn’t know.   Zymve says this is why we need to get out of town. Ky’tha posits that maybe we make her bait and put her in the open to draw him out and find out what he wants \- that the group has her back. Aury agrees \- thinks we need to find out what the problem is.  She says that she has been running for at least three years so maybe it is time she faces it.  

About that time, Bordi drops off a note \- says a guy dropped it off for Zymve.  A black hair, pale skinned elf asked to pass this along. She says it says “my grandmother will forgive me if I can get “the relic” for my house’s matron **Inidra Maerth**.” It is signed Bitter.    We aren’t sure what relic this is referring to \- the *Marrowstone*, the *Emberheart*, or maybe one of the other two pieces of the puzzle \- one that Zymve knows is connected to her family.

### **Ky’tha Goes to Our Exquisites**

Ky’tha goes downstairs to ask Bordi if Zymve can perform \- he says yes and she thanks him.  Meanwhile, Zymve talks to the group about what she should sing about (subtly talking about rubies and relics).  Ky’tha then leaves to find Tessa, spreading rumors about the performance that will happen later tonight.  She goes to **Our Exquisites** \- as she is walking she notices an elf and she follows him into the Hydra. She walks up to him at the front desk and asks if he is a courier of letters.  His name is **Nindrol**.  They agree to meet at the Cane later \- after he professes to know who Ky’tha (full name, makes fun of her being a dwarf with en elvish name).  She then finds Tessa and briefs her on all the happenings \- Ghone hideout, drow looking for Zymve, the pale elf who knows Ky’tha, etc.  She asks her to be on the lookout for the drow.  She says that the elf matching the description of Nindrol has been asking about the Etterset.  

### **Back at the Dyed Cane**

Ky’tha then heads back over to the Dyed Cane and to the room to tell Zymve that she’s on and everyone is buzzing.  The group (sans Ewing) heads to the bar and Aury pays him 10gp to give us a great table and asks one of his children to feed the goat. He does. The female tiefling is our server. Sets us up with food and spirits.  The guy playing the harp steps off the stage \- he was just okay.  People give a polite clap.  Zymve (as Lynda) steps up to the stage (she is average…brown hair and eyes f).  She performs a special song made for the occasion (nat 20).  The crowd goes wild…as she is performing the door opens and a few candles snuff out.  The tiefling starts trying to relight them.  Aury and Hap notice a hooded figure walk in.  As she is wrapping up he reaches into a pouch and throws 1pp at her feet and sits down at the bar.  He pulls his hood off and this is a silver-haired drow. Aury points him out to Og.  Zymve performs again with her yarding.

Ky’tha eases over to the bar, followed by Og. She asks him what he is having.  They agree to have the same thing and he orders his finest wine. Bordi says that will be 10gp and she says to him \- you have this right.  And he pays 11gp.  He admits to being Nindrol.  He asks about “his” goat.  He asks her if this is how dwarves flirt.  She says that they should listen…and they do.  Ky’tha then talks more about why he’s here. She insists that he talk to Lynda.  He says that she knows what she wants.  He asks Og to get the item and bring it to his home.  He’d like it there “with spiders, in the dark, etc.”  

He comes over to the table to meet the group.  He knows everyone by name.  Aury says he is going to ask a question and does.  He says that Zymve has become a friend of ours.  He asks if Zymve is in danger?  He asks if we know much about Zymve…and if we do then we should know if she is in danger or not.  Aury asks is she in danger from him?  He says not tonight, but maybe tomorrow it could be different.  He asks Aury “which one we are going for next.”  And Aury says that we aren’t going for any of them.  He tells us we should reconsider.  Aury tells him that if he makes a threat to her then he makes a threat to all of us.  He thanks him for being direct.  Zymve casts Suggestion on him, but it doesn’t work.  

Meanwhile, Ains casts Sending to Og:  he let’s them know that he has a room, asks if Zymve is sleeping there tonight, tells him about the free drinks, asks for a response.  Zymve says she will come later.   Og tells her they need to go soon.

Ky’tha catches him and asks what his angle is.  He says he was asked to complete a task…which isn’t much different than taking tasks off the bulletin board (and points there).  She asks if he is being paid by the queen and he says he is being paid by the Matron.  But tells Ky’tha to ask Zymve how the drow pay \- he is not being paid with coin.  As he leaves Zymve yells “I will not go back” across the bar.

Ky’tha walks back to the table and asks Zymve what the drow pay with. She says power and station and Hap pipes up “and the threat of death.” She says she wants to kill him, but they talk about how if she does, the Matron will send another in his place and she agrees.  She just says that as we get closer to finding the relic, more will come for the group.  Group splits up:  Ky’tha to the Hydra, Hap and Aury to the Temple, and Og and Zymve take Zabeek and Ewing to the Warm Bear Inn to find Ains.

Hap and Aury make it to the Temple.  Aury meets with the Priestess \- tells her that Arnol got put on a ship.  Then he tells her about the cult \- how they were zombies and a vampire. And how we killed them all \- tells this very matter of fact-like.  Tells her how the daylight spell is effective.  Recounts the name of the vampire.  He then talks about the tome he has from Diverra…and how it is an original copy.  He then tells her about the rose petal that he gave her…and how to use it.  He asks her to keep the petal safe so that someday he may take her.  He then tells her they are traveling in the morning.  He tells her about the shattered heart mission and how this will take him all over the continent. She mentions telling Arnol but he counsels her to leave Arnol alone for now.  He just asks her to notify the acolytes and others in the practice to keep their eyes open for this mission.  Then goes to bed.

Ky’tha goes to the Hydra and gets the room number for the elf.  Goes up to the room and keys in.  Sees nothing, but sees a shadow, and checks it out but nothing is there. Sees something at the window and opens it and there is a human.  She closes the window and makes some comments “we could have had fun” then shuts the door, goes to her room, and goes to bed.

Og, Zymve, and Zabeek get to the Inn and can’t get in \- there is line out the door.  They try to flash the EGD badge and also try to intimidate the crowd to let them pass.  Zymve is able to intimidate them and Og clears a path.  The crowd comments that the EGD and HOEs are a bunch of assholes…but let’s them by.  Og stables Ewing, but carries Zabeek on his shoulders and they work their way in.  Og and Zabeek go to the room, as Zymve grabs 4 mugs and takes them upstairs.

Og gets to the room and they discuss how Ains caused that riot because he “wanted peace and quiet.”  Zymve kicks open the door with the mugs, hands two to Og.  They drink two each.  Zabeek feels left out.  Og tells Ains that she gave a great performance. She then tells Ains about how her family wants the dragon-related relics.   She says “we might have to kill some people.”  Ains expresses surprise that the Cloak of Defiance nor Lady Rook have not made an appearance.  He casts a spell to make sure no one else is there.  He expresses some paranoia.  He says he doesn’t trust Ky’tha or Aury with the marrowstone.  But says he trusts Zymve with it and throws it to her.  Zymve agrees to sing for Ains and then casts a sending spell to Arnol privately, twice (the second one full of extended laughs). He gets back a confused response.   He then reads his book, as Zymve watches. He goes into a zone.  A message starts to convey to Zymve.  Ains picks up that it makes him think of dry land, desert land.  She gets uncomfortable heat as the melody comes from him.  It is dry and suffocating and begins to feel like brittle bones.  It gets very sad near the end of what he is reading.  He sees the word “loss” and it is bones and desert and feels like there is something deeper in the text that someone has lost something very important to them.  Zymve feels very vulnerable, but doesn’t show this.  They all agree to go to bed. 

### **It’s the Next Morning, the 46th of Talil, 1246**

The next morning Ky’tha wakes up, grabs bread and cheese and walks to the gate.  Hap and Aury grab breakfast, Hap donates 10gp to the Temple, and they walk to the gate.  Og, Ains, and Zymve wake up and talk to Zabeek about the danger that is coming.  He says this may be too much for him and expresses his fear.  Ains try to persuade him to join in, as Zymve tries to intimidate him.  Og picks up Ewing.  Ky’tha makes it there and asks if everyone is ready.  Zabeek is nervous \- Ky’tha gives him a dagger.  He says he wants to be a powerful wizard…but will have to wait.  Hap and Aury show up.  Everyone is ready…The group heads out. 

### **Weftirin at the Barebase Camp**

We travel for a day.  Zabeek cozies up to Hap and tries to learn tips from as the party walks.  The day passes without incident.  We pass the hunter’s camp where we went into the grove.  We continue and the sun starts to set.  We come to the area we are going to make camp.  Ky’tha has been this way, but it has been a while.  This is **Barebase**. It is a traveler’s camp.  We see 4 covered wagons sitting around a campfire.  The wagons are decorated and painted…bright colors and beads and gems and trinkets.  We are greeted by a man who says “come over \- the murder welcomes you.”  These are the **Weftirins**.  They are gypsies/hippies of Myrdae.  They ask us to join them \- they have stew.  Ains asks why they referred to themselves as murderers.  Aury knows that this group travels in groups.  Each group takes the name of a group of animals.  This group took Murders (like crows).  We all make introductions.  There is a supply wagon (the greeter is named Marek).  There is a trinket wagon/dreamcatchers.  There is an instrument wagon.  And finally a fabric wagon.  We ask where they are going or coming from.  They say they are aiming to be at Stonewood by next Spring.  The skirted Emberstran and are headed east.  We share we are heading to Ulgrey.

Ains asks them about spitespirit…has he heard anything about him.  A few tales are shared, but nothing of importance.  He specifically asks about the keys but they don’t know much about it.  Aury asks him if they have a map of the lands. He says he will have to paint one and Aury offers him some parchment to draw one for him.  He asks where we want to go and draws Ulgrey, Kahlbit’s Veil, Trailpoint, etc.  Aury helps them put it down.  Ky’tha starts to talk and ask if they have noticed signs of blight or disease in their travels.  As Ky’tha speaks up…the trinket lady looks at Ky’tha curiously.  The group the Skulk says they saw some dying land near Halesworth.   They got their info from a Seer.  They think she’s traveling with the School group.  Myra (the tinkerer) walks up to Ky’tha and says “you know I’ve been around these parts, and it looks to me like you are carrying something heavy in your sleep.”  You are fighting those dreams aren’t you and she gives her a dreamcatcher and asks her to sleep with it tonight.  She says she’s good at reading people. Ains asks what everyone does for fun around here.  And a couple of players come out to play for the group.  

### **Dreams Caught**

We take watch per usual.  Zymve and Ains; Og and Aury; Hap and Ky’tha.  Watch is shared by the members of the Murder.  During his watch, Ains lifts a wagon to impress the others.  He also lifts Zymve up and spins her then sets her down gently.  She then casts a spell on him and he takes some psychic damage.  During Aury and Og’s watch, Aury reads from his book. When Ky’tha rests, she sleeps with her dreamcatcher. When she wakes from her watch, she sees Myra and she walks over and asks for the dreamcatcher since Ky’tha responds that she slept the same.  

Myra says the catcher caught something old that carries weight \- she says that she sees two groups standing across from one another.  

## 

## “*You gave it a lot to hold last night,*” Mira says, “*It caught something old. Something that still carries weight.*”

## “*I saw two groups standing across from one another. One bore the markings of your clan. The other… the Cragfury. There was anger. Fear. Words were spoken that could not be taken back. And then…*” Mira’s brow furrows slightly. “T*here was a tearing. Not of flesh, but of the world itself. **The Cragfury were pulled away — not by choice, but by force**. Thrown through the spaces between places. I felt the land itself resist it.*”

## “*There was something else at the end of it all. A steady, deep rhythm. Like the beating of a great heart. It was calm, suppressing everything that was torn apart into peace. **A guardian’s heart**, I think.*”

You may want to keep this with you and hands her back the dreamcatcher.  She shows her how to work with the dreamcatcher.  Says that she should work with one of great wisdom in her “family.”

### **It’s the Next Morning, the 47th of Talil, 1246**

Pack up and head out, thanking them for the hospitality.  We move along the road and we smell the salt and water from the Sea as the road gets closer to the water.  We find another campsite known as **Featherfall**.  We see this is at a cliff area.  There are many types of birds flying around.  It is noisy since this is still during the day.  Seems like an obvious place to camp.  There is no one else here.  We stop and listen and can hear the wagons not far behind. Zymve expresses an interest in moving ahead to get away from the noise and as far as we can before the sun sets.  As we do, Og looks for another camp site.  He is able to find something, but it is not as good as the Featherfall.  We set up camp.  We set up watch again \- same pairs, opposite order.  Birds quiet down, but then get very quiet.  It is very peaceful and we hear the surf below.  Zymve is in a trance and she hears something, a song on the wind \- this is faint in the distance.  Probably not far off from the Featherfall campsite.  Ky’tha and Hap don’t hear it.  Hap sends Fleur over to check it out and takes off.   Ky’tha pulls out her Silver Raven messenger (wonderous magic item) and sends a message to Myra “we are down the road if needed.”  Fleur observes things and makes it back to Hap as the raven tries to find Myra.

Fleur tells Hap “it is chaotic and looks like someone is about to fall and there are people trying to hold people.”  Hap tells the group that there is danger.  We wake the group and dash that way. 

We see in the distance the campfire as high as it can be, we see people trying to tie others off with rope, and we see others constantly moving.  We see someone climbing down the 60’ cliffs and we hear a song of Sirens (evil creatures) off to the left.

– The End of the Session –
