# **28 \- Those Effers Ruined My Boots**

**Session Date**:  June 23, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  47th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  near Featherfall campsite on the Glimmercoast Way

### **At the Featherfall campsite**

“Old Hap ain't skeert of no musical beguilements.”  
The session begins picking up at the campsite with the party attempting to rescue the Weftirins that have been lured off the cliff by harpies.

* ROUND ONE:  Og appears in the camp and sees the campers trying to go down the cliff with a rope and chaos at the top.  He asks “what’s happening?”  The song has called our friends and two have fallen \- he gets in reply.  He moves to the edge of the cliff and helps one of the campers with the rope \- tying it off to a wagon.  A winged creature in the water pops up and starts to sing \- Og makes a CON saving throw to avoid hearing it. The camper at the top of the cliff hears the song and falls down.  Three more winged creatures fly in.  One screeches and targets a camper who avoids it.  Ains gets to the edge of the campsite and interacts with Og who says “bird ladies singing and hurting others.”  Ains dashes forward and offers help, but can’t do anything this turn.  Hap enters the campsite and dashes forward and uses his action surge to hit one of the winged creatures with a firebolt.  Aury enters the campsite and prays and sends a spark out to winged creatures in the distance, casting fireball at two of them (one saves and one doesn’t).  One of the campers looks over the edge and says “they don’t look good at all.”  Another tries to climb down with the rope and slips and falls onto a ledge \- hurting his leg.  Another is scared and climbs into a wagon.  Another grabs his child and climbs into a wagon yelling “save our family.”  Zymve dashes forward in the direction of Hap and hides behind a tree.  Kytha gets to the edge of the cliff and shoots her crossbow at the one Hap attacked and hits.  Two creatures emerge from rocks in the water and move forward \- they sound like lightning as they crack when they move forward. 

* ROUND TWO:  Og finishes tying off the rope, rages, and then moves to the cliffside to help one of the fallen campers, trying to stabilize him and does so then moves him from the cliff’s edge. One of the winged creatures start to sing against Ky’tha and charms her.  Another one focuses on Ains and charms him. Another flies closer to the group as another flies behind a tree, seeking cover.  One flies to Hap and attacks \- first squealing at him (and he has to make a saving throw and fails)...inflicting psychic damage and imposing disadvantage.  Then tries to claw him and hits, but he parries some of the damage.  One looks up at a camper on the cliffside and he is dropped unconscious.  Ains starts to move toward the creature who charmed it.  He continues to be charmed. Hap has Fleur negative disadvantage and then attacks with his sword and hits. Then hits with the pruning dagger. Then crits with his sword, damaging the creature’s wing/hand.  Then crits again with the dagger.  Aury moves forward 5’ to see past the wagon and casts another fireball at two in the trees (both fail their saves). One dies and a tree goes up in flames.  The campers who are out make death saves. Zymve casts a spell on the winged creature nearest to her and takes her down.  She then casts inspiration on Ky’tha, Ains, and Hap \- giving 8 temp hp.  Ky’tha fails a save and walks off the cliff, hitting the shallow ground taking damage and swimming 5’ forward.  The strange creatures in the water move forward \- one moves up to Ky’tha and sparks fly around \- attacking her with lightning bolts, hitting once, then missing, but she takes additional damage because she’s in range of the creature.  The charm falls off.  The other one swirls up and there is a vortex and it rises in the water and flies next to Aury and bolts shoot out at him, missing twice.  But he takes damage because he’s standing next to it.  And two more similar creatures appear in the water.

* ROUND THREE: Og makes his way to the bottom of the cliff using the rope and asks Ky’tha if she needs help (she says she can take care of herself).  The winged creatures go \- one flies behind some rocks, another tries to charm Zymve and she fails and tries again and saves, and another flies over the water and tries to charm Hap and doesn’t. Ains is no longer charmed.  He looks over the edge and levitates one of the camper children up in the air. Hap runs over to the strange creature in front of Aury and is able to determine his resistances and immunities \- and yells some of this to the group (poison, weapons, paralysis, grappled).. Then attacks with his sword and hits.  He then tries to menace him and he takes additional damage. Then attacks with the dagger and hits. Then attacks with a short sword, making a critical hit.  Aury sprouts his wings, and the creature misses his opportunity attack, then he casts fireball at some of the creatures in the water (2 save and 1 fails). The campers make death saves. Zymve casts shatter at two of the creatures in the water (1 saves and 1 fails).  And she inspires Ky’tha.  Og’s reaction pulls Ky’tha  to him. She then cunning action dash-climbs up the cliff, shoots a crossbow at a harpy and hits, then moves another 10’ to get behind a wagon.  One of the creatures flies up next to Zymve and misses, then crits her with lightning, limiting her to half movement by injuring her foot (and ruining one of her boots), then she takes additional damage at the end of the turn.  Another one attacks Hap and hits, then misses.  And he takes additional damage, but uses a hellish rebuke and the creature takes a point of damage.  The other two move forward in the water, and Og takes some lightning damage at the end of its turn.

* ROUND FOUR: Og grabs a camper and realizes he is dead and then moves back to stab one of the elemental creatures and hits twice, pushing one 15’ away and slowing it.  The harpies go-one flies next to Ains and attacks, missing twice. Another flies up and looks at and screeches at Ains and he fails and takes damage. It then flies behind a tree.  Ains looks at the kid he is levitating and the man at his feet and decides to climb down to the child and he falls to a ledge below, he also takes damage from an opportunity attack from the harpy next to him.  He then stands up and gets under the child and releases the telekinesis.  He catches the child and stabilizes him.  Hap attacks the elemental creature and hits with his sword, then hits with the dagger. Then hits again with the sword and dagger.   Aury casts healing word at Ky’tha then casts sacred flame on the harpy on the cliff and it fails.  The campers make death saves. Zymve moves the cloud of daggers to the elemental next to her and it takes damage. Ky’tha turns and shoots a bolt at the elemental on Zymve and crits with a sneak attack, killing it.  An elemental tries to attack Hap and misses, but he takes a little lightning damage.  Another moves up on the ledge next to Og and attacks and hits twice and he takes additional damage from the bolts at the end of his turn. And a harpy moves back to the rocks.

* ROUND FIVE:  Og interacts with Ains and holds his reaction to try to catch a camper who is about to fall off the cliff.  One of the harpies flies to Ains and screeches and he fails, taking damage and imposing disadvantage.  Then misses her next attack.  Another harpy flies down and attacks Ains with her claws and hits with both. Ains telekinetically shoves a camper into Og’s hands, who is levitating \- he catches him and stabilizes him.  Hap gets some help from Fleur and hits the elemental twice, with sword and dagger.  Then misses with his sword, but hits with the dagger again.  Aury looks around and casts mass healing word on the party and one of the campers. Then tolls the dead on one of the harpies and she saves. One of the campers stands up and runs next to Ky’tha and asks what he should do?  She replies “get in the wagon.”  Zymve moves on the cliff edge 15’ closer to the harpies and casts ray of sickness on the flying harpy and hits. Ky’tha shoots at the elemental on Hap and gets sneak attack damage.  That guy then hits Hap with one of his attacks and he takes additional lightning damage.  One flies up and hits Zymve with lightning damage and then additional damage from being in range, and she loses her concentration on her spell. Another moves across and Ains takes damage from being in range and the child drops down again from that damage.

* ROUND SIX:  Og holds his action to dash with the camper to put him to safety. Harpies:  one fumbles and another attacks Ains and misses twice.  The last one flies up on top of the cliff.  Ains moves Og to the wall and then stabilizes the child and moves him from the lightning, giving the harpy a chance to opportunity attack him, but she misses (shield).  He then drops the kid off and then moves back to put himself between the harpy and child.  Telekinesis drops and Og’s reaction sets in.  He tries to climb up the wall and makes it.  He drops off the camper at the top of the cliff and finishes his movement. Hap attacks the elemental and hits with his sword, then hits with the dagger.  Then hits with the sword again, then hits with the dagger again, killing it.  Then he runs up next to the elemental next to Zymve.  Aury uses radiance of the dawn to attack the harpy and elemental.  Then heals Zymve. The camper jumps in the wagon. Zymve attacks the harpy with a spell (it fails).  It takes psychic damage and disadvantage is imposed. Ky’tha shoots a bolt at the elemental on Zymve and hits with a sneak attack.  The elementals attack:  one attacks Ains and misses twice, but he takes damage from being in range, as does the child.  The other attacks Hap and Zymve, missing Hap and then hitting Zymve.  She takes damage, and then both take more damage being in range, and both lose concentration on their spells.

* ROUND SEVEN:  Og runs up to the elemental on the cliffside and attacks twice and hits both times. Harpies attack:  the one in the air attacks Aury and hits with her claw.  The other attacks Ains and misses twice. Ains moves the child to safety.  Hap attacks the elemental and then pushes it off the cliff and it dies.  And then he heals himself.  Aury casts mass healing word on the party and the child.  Then he flashes his channel divinity at the harpy (she fails save) and kills it.  The child stands up and starts to run to the wagon.  Zymve attacks the harpy at the bottom of the cliff with a dissonant whisper spell and it saves, but takes damage and is frightened.  Ky’tha runs to the edge of the cliff and looks down, climbs down and misses with her crossbow.  The last harpy screeches at Ains and he takes psychic damage and flies off.  The other elemental attacks Ains and hits then misses and he takes damage, plus damage in range.  Once the harpy flew off, the elemental disappears.

* ROUND EIGHT:  Og drops down and takes damage and jumps on the harpy, attacking her and killing her. Ains taps Og and they dimension door to the location of the harpy and try to fall on her and grab her as they do so.  Hap gets to the bottom to help the campers.  Aury mass heals the party near him and the camper with the broken leg.  The camper stands up and asks what is going on \- and Aury tells him to get in the wagon.  Zymve is upset about the hole in her foot, exclaiming, “Those f\*\*\*ers ruined my boots, do you know how much they cost?\!”.  She proceeds to cast mending on them. Ky’tha climbs down to help the guy with the broken leg.  The harpy has to make a strength save and fails and it is wrapped in the branches in Og’s hands. It then charms Og.  

* ROUND NINE:  Og is charmed.  Ains looks into the water and lightning bolts it and it is immune.  He then selects Og to be able to talk in their heads. Hap goes down the rope to help at the bottom.  Aury flies out over the water, is now 80’ away (as Og and the harpy are 10’ underwater and Ains is bobbing on the surface).   Zymve helps with rescue efforts, as does Ky’tha.  The harpy swims to the surface and flies 30’ then dashes away.  

* ROUND TEN:  Og swims up and after her.  Ains looks in the air and misty steps to her and tries to grab her and restrains her and pulls her into the water.  Aury flies back to the cliff.  Harpy is restrained and charms Ains, incapacitating him.  Og is no longer charmed. {The rest of the party tends to the campers the remainder of the fight}

* ROUND ELEVEN:  Og swims to the harpy and rages, and is 10’ away from her.  Ains swims to the harpy. 

The harpy is under Og and underwater and restrained.

– The End of the Session –

| 9 | *Character level at end of the session* |
| :---: | :---- |
| **3** | *Sessions played at that level* |

# **29 \- Made it to Ulgrey**

**Session Date**:  June 23, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  47th of Talil, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  near Featherfall campsite on the Glimmercoast Way

### **At the Featherfall campsite**

Og and Ains successfully defeat the harpy in the sea and swim back to shore, regrouping with the party at the campsite as Ains casts a spell and puts them there.   The Weftirins are dealing with their dead and we offer to stay the night, after getting our gertot, Zabeek, and our gear.  Og and Hap collect them.

We talk to everyone about healing, keeping watch \- discussion about what we could have done better. Finn is the one who passed \- he is the son of the clothiers Bram and Sable.  Ky’tha goes over to talk to Mira and overhears her talking to Marek \- she mentions the name “Veyra Deamonne” (this is the Seer).  Ky’tha interrupts and asks about her and why they are seeking her out.  They reply that it is to determine what to do with Finn.  Ky’tha mentions that maybe Aury could help, but they decline to help because of wanting Cembus’ blessing and not Diverra’s.  We then discuss if we want to escort them further.   After a difference of opinion, we decide to wait until the morning to make the decision. 

Aury wanders from camp, but no one notices.  Ains and Og take the first watch with the Weftirins, and Zymve, Hap, and Ky’tha will rest.   During watch, they discuss how rough the night was, as they bandage themselves. The feel guilt for not making camp with them to begin with. Og steps away from the camp \- he sees a shadow of a small humanoid form laying down looking over the edge of the bay.  He says hello and the head turns and puts his finger up to hush Og…he steps forward and sees this is one of the guys who takes care of the instrument wagon.  He feels bad about Finn.  Is questioning if there are more out there…asks where they came from. They briefly discuss this \- Og says “they came from the sky.”  They are debating about what the creatures were and where they came from \- as Ains joins the conversation.  Og apologizes that he wasn’t able to help.  Ains encourages him to sleep.  Ains tells him that he and Og are Heroes of Emberstran and promises to help by escorting them to Ulgrey.  He tells them that they are not going to stay in Ulgrey, but is thankful for the offer to assist until then.

### **Og’s Oath**

Og then steps forward to perform a rite as part of his following of **Leyeth**. He vows that the food and coin and weapons will be in service to others.  He then rises to go back to camp and notices Aury kneeling in the woods.  He leaves Aury be.  Og feels warmth in his heart, knowing he is doing the right thing by helping this group and he is reassured that he did help, even though a life was lost.

### **Aury’s Commune**

Meanwhile, Aury sits in the woods.  He feels a cold feeling \- he is not getting responses that he is hoping for.  Last time he cast this ritual, he got a response.  As he looks up, he starts to have doubts.  He then feels warmth in the book he is holding and he sees sprouting from the sides of the binder a prick of a thorn hit him and a petal falls off.  He then sees another petal slip up to a page and he opens the book to that page \- this is a part of the book he has never read.  This is a tale:  sometimes the direct can’t be answered (this is the message he gets).  As he does this, blood drips from his hand and the first drop that falls goes into the book on the letter Y.  It then blends into the pages, creating a rose watermark.  The letter Y is legible in this watermark.  He is now unsure of what this means.  This is new to him. The second drop of blood misses the book and lands on the ground.  He feels like he isn’t going to bleed anymore.  He closes the book and sees on the title, the blood is smeared into the book (on the N in the word ‘Aeterna’).  He thinks his goddess is trying to communicate in a new way.  These were answers to three questions that he asked privately. Aury picks up the fallen petal and stays there until Aury and Og complete their watch, contemplating what he learned and didn’t.

Ains sits watching \- sees nothing new. Og returns.  They convince the boy to return to the instrument carriage.  Og asks his name-it is Root. Og wishes him to sleep well and asks for an introduction to Finn’s parents tomorrow.  The watch ends and they rise to wake up the others for the next watch.  As they stand, Og sees something sparkle on the water, but he can’t focus on what it was.  It was a brief glimpse. Og mentions this to Ains.  They decide to go and check this out, with Og climbing down the side of the cliff.  He makes it down without issue.  He looks out to the rocks and notices there is some type of cave entrance on the side of the rocky outcrop.   He swims out to check this out.  The cave mouth almost seems like a “wound” to the rock, but opens into a cavern. There is foul stink of rot inside.  He looks in and sees above the waterline \- some filthy nests and smooth stone.  He sees bones, broken weapons, etc. but sees a 1’ tall statue.  It almost looks like a carving of a goliath.  He sees markings on its heads and arms, but it's hard to tell from afar. He sees a spark of static electricity on it.  He and Ains communicate to each other and Ains offers to go and help see what the object is.  Ains loses his footing and falls as he heads that way, but doesn’t scream out. He swims out and finally makes it to the cave and sees what Og sees.  He uses his wand to detect magic.  He picks up conjuration magic on the idol and also sees enchantment in another nest. They walk into the cave to check out the nests.  They go carefully, looking for traps and find none.  Og finds a bloodstained, large canvas pack and open it.  There are coins in it in a small purse inside the pack.  Tied around the purse is a small silver chain and a locket (25gp). There are 63cp, 18sp, 42gp in the purse. Ains finds a tattered backpack.  He finds two daggers, a torn cloak, with a silver bird clasp (15gp).  They continue on and find a leather satchel (spyglass, set of thieves tools, 2 vials of liquid, a journal where all the ink is smeared, the last entry says (beautiful women singing on the rocks), bottle of ink, quill, 12gp, carved bone whistle) and worn belt pouch (set of bone dice (Og), single pearl earring (25gp), 15gp), as well as various crap.  They also find a reinforced sack (3 days of stale rations, grappling hook (Hap), silver box (5gp)) and drawstring pouch (magic sensed \- several coiled strings with enchantment magic; look like musical instrument strings).  They continue forward to the last nest and find an oil skinned sack (it would protect items from getting wet,  27gp, 41sp, small silver mirror (15gp), 2 amberstones) and then Og moves forward to grab the statue.  They think it is a version of Vo’egurn.  Og wraps a rope around the statue and lifts it up as Ains casts shocking grasp on it (he feels the lightning on the head).  They make the way back to the camp. This is an Idol of Vo’egurn with all magic depleted. Ains sits on the cliffside, but refuses to sleep.  He sits with the Idol.  Og goes to sleep.  Ains tells the Weftirins that they don’t need to wake anyone else up.  The leader says he will keep Ains company \- this is Marek. 

He thanks Ains for the group’s help.  He says he noticed that he and Og came back from the caves.  He asks what all they found.  Ains shows him the Idol.  He says he knows exactly what that Idol is.  They then have a discussion about who or what might worship Vo’egurn \- Ains questions if a follower of a gold dragon might worship him.  Aury walks up to the two sitting on the cliffside.  Aury has been crying.  He kneels in front of Marek and Ains.  Ains gives Marek the bird pendant as he excuses himself.  Aury holds up the petal to Ains and asks for forgiveness, exclaiming that he failed the group and vowing that it will never happen again.  Ains has to make a wisdom saving throw. As Aury does this, before Ains can answer, he looks and sees a red-headed woman giving him a rose. He says out loud \- is this a disguise?  And he blinks and now sees Aury.  Ains says that he can stand up.  Aury says to please take my offering.  When Ains does, his wounds heal and Ains feels like he has had a long rest.  He looks spooked.  Aury gets up and walks away.  

Aury then walks back to the group to sleep and feels like the acceptance was more than forgiveness. He thinks there was something more to this.  He then gets a rest.

### **Ainslie Reaches Out to Irragosa**

Ains then casts Sending to Irragosa, holding the Idol.  

## 

## “*Hey \- its Ains \- do you worship **Vo’egurn**.  Trying to figure out god things.  He is lightning and you are lightning and I shoot lightning.  Coincidence.  Good morning*.”  

He hears back:  

## 

## “*Do you make a habit of waking others in the middle of the night?  Yes \- I draw energy from Vo’egurn, but I let Ol’Farium guide me.  Good night.*”  

He then spends time studying the Idol. He doesn’t think he can attune to it, but thinks it is good he saved this Idol.

### **The next morning – It’s the 48th of Talil, 1246**

All get a long rest. Mira comes over to Ky’tha and says we are going back to Emberstran.  We have responses back and we think going that way will be better.  She tells her that the group was planning to escort them and Mira thanks her for the group’s help.  They start to pack up.  Ky’tha thanks Mira for the dreamcatcher and says she looks forward to sleeping with it soon.  Aury finds Finn’s parents and hands them a sack with 25gp.  They are thankful and sad.  Og tries to give much of the loot from the cave to Finn’s parents.  They accept the items, but present him with the Shademoor Lute in return \- it is solid black.

Zymve rolls her eyes as she sees them proffer items to Finn’s family. The group splits the gold and silver that Ains and Og found.  Aury studies the Idol \- thinks this is something that belongs in a temple.  Doesn’t think the magic will return.  He thinks about Ulgrey and remembers there is a hill or area there devoted to Torec (god of elements) who Vo’egurn was a demigod for \- thinks he heard something about Cembus.  Thinks maybe a group there went from worshipping Phoe to Torec to Cembus.  Hap takes the grappling hook, Ky’tha takes the earring (with some coaxing from the group) and asks Zymve to pierce her ear at the next camp, Og offers Zymve the Shademoor Lute and she takes it. Ains says he is planning to give the Idol to the gold dragon (Aury tells him it might be worth several hundred gold pieces).

As the group walks, Zymve talks to the group about why everyone feels guilty and why no one woke her up.  Ains says he thought everyone could use more rest and says he wanted to be alone.  We discuss having a group earring.  Ky’tha knows that we are heading to the Old Lodge \- and there will be a ferry on the other side of the river.

### **At the River Ring Rest**

We make it to the dock and stand on the edge of the river.  We see a bell that we are supposed to ring.  It is small and on a pole.  Og rings it, but hears no sound.  We see the bell tower in the distance ring and see the ferryman start to pole over from the other side.  This ferry is large and flat.  It can bring over the entire group, including Ewing. This is the Harbok River.  He comments on the gertot and Og says his name is Ewing.  The ferryman is Tomas (discussion over the pronunciation of the name).  We introduce ourselves as the Heroes of Emberstran.  He welcomes us to River Ring Rest \- encourages us to get drinks, unloads us, etc.  Unfortunately, Ewing cannot go inside.  There is a stable.  Tomas says that “Ol’Fen” will take care of Ewing and we comment how odd it is that the stableman’s name is Finn.  

There is an older lady walking around the lodge \- catch as catch can.  We can all stay and it won’t cost much.  Aury offers her a gp, as does Ains, as well as Ky’tha and Hap.  4gp for the group.  They take good care of Ewing and offer us a good place to rest.  

### **A Shattered Heart Clue**

There is a large fireplace, a warm place to rest.  We walk through and Zymve looks above the fireplace at the massive stone hearth and sees a tapestry fragment that isn’t quite complete and it looks foreign to her, but here it would look like a stitching from a grandma.  It is two hands intertwined together \- one side looks pulled away on purpose.  As she studies it, she finds a need to tap Aury and points this out to him \- this looks like you, Aury \- she says.  Aury likes it. He thinks this is a love \- torn piece \- this is where something went wrong in someone’s life.  This is a shattered heart piece.  He recognizes that this is part of the tenants that he was supposed to find in the lands.  The group questions Aury \- who is very excited \- and tells him to pull out his book and figure out what he is supposed to do. 

He finds the passage he needs.  Whatever shattered heart symbol he finds, the only way for Diverra to intervene is for him to take it to a place of worship for Diverra.  The group decides that Ains will talk to the woman. The group gets a table.  Ains gets up with his stew and stands in front of the hearth, catching the lady as she walks by.  “Excuse me madam (lovely lady) \- she is flattered.  She apologizes for the dull stew.  He spices his food up with magic and she tastes it.  He then brings up the tapestry and asks if she stitched it.  She says she made it for her husband.  He was caught in a storm and tried to save them in the river and he passed away.  Every year, she pulls a thread off of it in remembrance of him.  She balls it up and puts it under her pillow once a year on their anniversary.  She says they never found his body and she never got a chance to say goodbye as it was a chaotic night during the storm.  She asks if Ains can keep her company that night and he says he knows of someone who could stay with her, but she laughs it off and says she doesn’t need that care.  She says it has been 2 decades since he was killed.  He tells her that he is from Emberstran and shows her his badge, telling her he is an investigator.  He offers her closure to try to find his body.  She is freaked out, but says that he can help her if he wants.  This seems to offer her hope, but she is trying not to be hopeful.  He tells her about Aury. He says it is our responsibility to help her since she clearly loved her husband.  He persuades her and she says to make ourselves at home. 

Ains walks to the group and shares the story.  Og cries.  Zymve says it has been “two fucking decades.” Aury isn’t sure how to help.  Hap and Aury know that the Awakening occurred 2 decades ago…and that the river changed as the landscape changed.  We discuss that we can help her, but it may take time.  Aury offers to pray to Diverra to see if he can get answers.  Zymve, Zabeek, Ky’tha and Hap will go to get a piercing.  Zymve pierces Ky’tha’s right ear and then makes and pierces Zabeek’s left ear.  Turns out that ol’Hap already has cuffs on both ears. 

Ains and Og walk up to the lady (named Maren Bellright).  Tell her that Aury is reaching out to Diverra.  She says she has prayed to Diverra for 20 years.  Og produces the Idol for her to touch. They tell her they are taking her case, but it may take some time and it may take some extra connection.  They then ask for the tapestry and tell her that they need to study it then take it to a Diverra shrine or temple. She asks when will it come back…and they say that it will be back before her anniversary in 2 harmons.  She asks Og to lift her up and she pulls off a thread and rolls it up and gives it to Ains.  They say they will avoid lava-based situations (and then have to improvise about the lava situation).  Then they go to the room for the night.

Meanwhile, Aury prays and enacts his ritual spell.  He lights up  the candle and incense, uses his book, etc.  He gets a quick answer to his first question \- a coolness that is a “no.”  The second question (about being close by) and gets no response.  Then for the third question the candle flickers and he hears a clinking noise and the ring where he held the candlestick fell off and rolled to his feet.  It has no damage.  This is a sign.  He needs to figure out a sign of what. He then gets up and thanks Diverra and impresses upon her that he is trying not to be a bother.  He feels a warmth.  He then goes back down the stairs to find Ains and Og and notices the tapestry is off the wall.  

He says that he got answers.  He says that the body is not recoverable and that it is therefore not close by. But then tells them about the ring on the candlestick. Ains looks at the object and knows it is a ring from a candlestick. They discuss that maybe they need to find the husband’s ring. They discuss if they have arcane skills to find the ring.  Aury says that he could ask his goddess questions like that once a day. He says he could sleep on this topic and might have an answer tomorrow.

Hap has a chat with his sword at bedtime.  It says “more, stronger” to him.  He thinks that it is waiting for him to fight something strong and powerful before it will bond with him further.

We go to sleep.

### **It’s the next day – the 1st of Paramor, 1246**

In the morning, Aury feels like he could use magic to locate the ring.  We smell muffins and bacon.  There is fresh gertot milk.  We sit and eat. Aury shares his new found power and says he will try it.  He studies Maren’s ring then casts the spell, walking to the docks.  He picks up a signal downstream.  The group walks along the shore on the bank and he casts the spell again \- and thinks it is in the center of the river down below.  Ky’tha offers up the immovable rod to help locate the precise point and keep him from moving with the current.  Og wades in \- it is cold.  He stays on the surface following Aury’s command then dives down.  He has some issues.  Ains asks if he is doing ok.  He surfaces.  Zymve offers to inspire him and sings him a song. Ains levitates Aury over the water and asks him to tell him when he is directly over the spot.  Ky’tha offers to try and, inspired by Zymve, is able to find it.  She levitates up with Ains help and he dries her off.  She hands Aury the ring and Ains cleans it \- he sees a worn design pattern. Zymve tries to mend it with her jewelcrafting skills.  With Aury’s guidance (it was a bell design), she casts her spell and is able to mend it and bring it back to its origin.  She gives it back to Ains.  Ains then takes it to Maren. 

We find Maren.  “That bacon was the shit” says Zymve.  Ains tells her to come outside and he lowers the ring into the water and then Zymve and Hap with spells make the wind blow and the earth shake and Ains touches Aury’s head.  Zymve plays her oboe.  Ky’tha’s raven flies around.  Og is having cool mist rise up from the river.  Ains asks her to describe the ring and makes a theatrical production of raising it from the water.  She holds her hands out and he levitates it to her and she holds out her hands and grabs it.  She starts crying, exclaiming “my precious.”  Ains tells her that her love protected its form.  She is very grateful.  She will tell everyone what the EGD did to help.  She says this is more than she had for 20 years…and is glad to know where he died.  She knows that divine magic was in use.  

We pack up and head out.  We travel all day and arrive at a plain campsite that night.  When we arrive there seem to be hunters hanging out.  We are welcomed by the hunters.  We take turns with the watch.  Ains and Zymve, Og and Ky’tha, Aury and Hap last.  

First watch \- Ains apologizes for not reading the book lately.  She says she was mad that he didn’t wake her up the other night.  He says he thought she was tired.  They joke about her dark circles under her eyes.  They talk about reading, but he asks her to play music with her new instrument.  He makes a string break.  Wow \- this lute is “a piece of shit.”  He says “if only you had a new set of strings” and pulls out the new strings.  She wonders if he caused the string to pop, but thinks he didn’t.  He pulls out the magical string and gives it to her to replace the broken strings.  Once they are in tune….they shrink to the size of the instrument.  Once she plays they never get out of tune.  Ains sees the enchantment magic coming off of the strings as if they are empowering Zymve.  He knows that any enchantment spell that she casts while playing, her difficulty level for the saving throw will increase by \+1.  She doesn’t know that yet.  He says that she should play more often now that she sounds better now.  She casts vicious mockery on him in response to the teasing, but it has no impact on him.  

Ky’tha and Og take their watch \- briefly talk about the cold water.  They wake up Aury and Hap.  Aury confesses that he should have saved the child and how he still feels bad.  Hap tries to make him feel better and he eases Aury’s woes.  

Night passes.

### **It’s the next day – the 2nd of Paramor, 1246**

We see that the hunters are gone.  We pack up.  Zabeek says his piercing is itchy and asks if it is okay.  We spend the day traveling to Ulgrey.  We arrive by crossing a bridge that leads to the town.  Most buildings are constructed of wood.  We work our way around the town to the stables to drop off Ewing and then make it to the Riverdog Inn. 

### **The Gilded Vulture Marking**

As we approach the Inn and are passing the tower to the left of the Inn.  Something grabs Og’s attention.  He sees a little stylized emblem at the base of the tall tower.  It looks like a bird in flight.  It is similar to the bird on the cloak pin that he gave to Finn’s parents.  He thinks this is a vulture.  He thinks that this is a sigil of the Gilded Vulture.  A note is stuck up under the emblem.  A silver-haired gabük steps out, pulls off the letter and walks back in.  A black cat sitting in a window in the tower looks down at us and jumps down and disappears.  Og doesn’t say anything but he looks distressed (mentally says to Ains the Gilded Vulture is here…not sure if the gabük recognized us).  He comments that we need to get inside the Inn \- that he is starving.  

### **At the Riverdog Inn**

We go into the Inn, noting that there are boats on the docks outside.  It is busy, but not bustling.  We smell meats, ale, the fire in the hearth, hear laughter.  A female looks our way and says “I’ll be right there (motioning with her hands).”  Ky’tha sees her using thieves’ cant to identify herself as Etterset...no one else notices this.  She walks away and we take a seat and the barmaid sets down drinks “the first round is on us, but the next round will cost you.”  Ky’tha asks about the specialty.  She says her name is Shanna.  The specialty is kahlbit \- but that they don’t tell most people.  We all introduce ourselves \- Zymve says “hmmmm” and Og says “stone” and Ains says “Mossy.”  Ky’tha signs back and says “how about bread and cheese and ale”, Aury says “meat” and Zymve says “the special sounds good.”  Shanna whispers in her “are you here about Phira?” As Ains overhears this.  Ky’tha responds that “yes \- any cheese sounds great to me” as she nods.  

As Zymve asks about kahlbit, Ky’tha tells the group that she is already working on why they are there \- that she has made contact.  The group is surprised by this, but trust Ky’tha.  Og is looking around and on guard about his surroundings. He thinks to himself \- why is that group over here \- the Vultures?  Ky’tha \- reacting to his unease \- tells him not to worry that the waitress is to be trusted.  Og tells the group that is not why is he is upset.  He tells the group that he used to work for a mercenary group called the Gilded Vultures.  Ains says that they rescued him a long time ago.  And Og says that the two of them ran from this group.  They say that their leader \- a blue dragonborn \- Vilmuss \- is a bad person and wanted Ains for his magical powers.  Ky’tha asks how Og knows they are here in Ulgrey.  He tells the group that he saw the sigil on the tower \- that it must be a place that messages are exchanged by this group. He says he did not recognize the gabük, nor does he think they she recognized him.  Ky’tha offers to ask her new contact about this group’s presence in the city. 

As we sit here, the door from the dock opens up and two patrons walk in.  The place goes quiet.  A female soldier in half plate wearing a Meridian tabard steps in, carrying herself with pride.  As much as she looks important, the person she is with is much more important \- draws more attention.  This is a wizard in a regal blue robe with gold trim and an ornate staff.  He has a wiry, white beard.  He commands attention.  He comments “That was nice to travel that way once in a while.”  Shanna says “Welcome to Ulgrey \- to what do we owe this visit?”  The wizard says “I have heard of some strange events in these parts and thought I would check them out for myself.”  

– The End of the Session –

| 9 | *Character level at end of the session* |
| :---: | :---- |
| **4** | *Sessions played at that level* |

# **30 \- We Don’t Trust Wizards**

**Session Date**:  July 14, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  2nd of Paramor, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Ulgrey

### **At Ulgrey’s Riverdog Inn** 

Inn is warm, feels like home.  Smell of ale and stew. We observe the lady and man who have walked in.  Shanna takes them to a table where the female soldier could watch the door (as observed by Og and Aury).  She then takes orders \- the wizard orders one of the special porters.  Ky’tha asks Og if he recognizes the wizard \- we all recognize the Meridian tabard, except Zymve.  He does not.  Ky’tha tells Zymve about the Meridian in whisper.  Hap has heard tales of some notable wizards in the lands \- specifically of the Tegenwald Phet \- and thinks this wizard is not a member of the Phet.  He has heard of a powerful mage that has roamed the lands who goes by Thalex \- but isn’t sure this is him.  He mentions this to the group and Ains has heard of him.  

Ains starts a ritual so that the group can talk in their heads (except Aury) \- but it is denied by the soldier.  She asks him to stop \- prefers to keep the weave to a minimum.  Ains stops.  Aury says while I respectfully hear what you are asking \- your table is over there.  Ains freezes (he is paralyzed).  Ky’tha immediately jumps up and walks over to the wizard and tells him there has been a misunderstanding \- tells him about the specific spell. She asks him if he is Thalex and he says yes \- and she introduces herself. She asks him to join the group, but he asks Ky’tha to join them.  She says she will, but will need to let her group know first.  She does and he yells over that “the pierced one” can join, too.  And she does.  

Thalex immediately asks Zymve “what brings you to the surface?”  Says she has a sparkle. She responds that she is here for adventure.  Ky’tha tells him that we are the Heroes of Emberstran. He knows of us \- he is a friend of Immonara.  Ky’tha inquires if this was planned, but he says no \- so it must be serendipity.  He then asks if “that is Phira’s brother over there?” as he points to Hap. They nod.  He would like to include Hap. Ky’tha responds that she doesn’t want the other three to feel left out and he offers to buy them a pitcher of the porter (as he throws down 1pp).  As this happens, Paeris (another wizard) walks in and takes “her” seat in the corner.

Hap then comes over and expresses that he is shy and Ky’tha conveys this.  Ky’tha tells Hap that he knows who he is.  They make introductions and Hap says he has heard of him.  Zymve says she has not.  

Thalex says he is here to figure out what happened that send Phira to Basctdelm.  Hap then starts trying to tell him about the Cloak of Defiance, the connection to his family, etc.  Zymve mentions the finger.  Ky’tha adds that this is why we are here \- to investigate Phira’s disappearance.  Shanna joins the group.  Ky’tha cants to her…then tells her we are here about a recent murder and disappearance.  Shanna asks Ky’tha to join her for a moment in another room \- she agrees to go.  Ky’tha then tells the party (in their heads) that she will be back in a few minutes \- that she trusts this person.

They leave.  Og replies to Ky’tha \- please ask about the Gilded Vultures. And she says yes.

Hap asks Thalex if he knows Phira.  He tells him that he heard of a land grab that had to do with his family and his sister…and then heard that Phira was in Basctdelm…which gave him cause for concern because he doesn’t want anything going on in “my city.”  My colleague has a tower here that was a part of the Knights of Myr \- was the Tower of Zibeaus and is now the Tower of Myr \- so he can travel back and forth with ease through waypoints.  There may be concern that the Cloak is trying to operate in Basctdelm. Hap says he doesn’t know other than his brother in law was running the Cloak in Emberstran, but he was killed.  He says he doesn’t know, though.  Thalex says if they are merely questioning her then this may be said and done easily.  Hap says he would like to talk to her, but Zymve says they threatened us that we cannot talk to her.  He offers to aid the group in helping get us to Basctdelm.  Hap says he doesn’t want to endanger her.  

Meanwhile, Ains asks everyone who the lady in the corner is. Ky’tha responds that she is a colleague of Thalex.  Ains responds that this is the tower where the vulture symbol was.  Og and Aury start to drink the porter.  She walks over to the table and asks why Ains is not drinking the beverage “does this offend you?”  He replies that he just doesn’t imbibe.  She asks them what brings you three to Ulgrey?  He says that he wanted his priest friend to see the world since he is getting up there in age.  She looks at Aury and says “Oh dear.”  Aury then replies that he is okay. And watches them go back and forth teasing each other.  She says that there must be a mistake \- you fit the description of someone I have heard of \- but not sure if you are they.  Ains asks what have you heard \- she says that she’s heard of people who match your descriptions.  Aury says he needs a drink and she says that she hopes the porter if up to their standards since she helped the River Dog craft it.  Ains questions what she heard and she calls him a curious fellow.  Then glances at the other table and asks if the group carries on when they are all together and Aury says “no \- it is worse” then she goes to sit back down at her spot.

Meanwhile, Ky’tha goes up with Shanna.  They walk into an unmarked room and Shanna locks the door behind them.   Ky’tha questions why the lock and she says I think you should have this \- and pulls out a box with a pack “mostly clothes” but pulls out a stuffed cat toy (looks like a toy, not real) and it has a necklace with a symbol of Villari on it and a small vial of perfume.  Ky’tha knows that Villari is the goddess of life and that this is a kid’s toy.  This is what was left behind by Phira \- she had a room here.  They took her husband’s belongings…and they did this before they took them. They had just checked in that day.  They took his body…it occurred around the bend up past the stables.  They were together.  Rumors are they strapped the dead body strapped to the back. Ky’tha asks about what the couple had when they checked in (packs for each, traveling lightly). She says that the stablemaster said she saw the carriage, interaction, etc.  Ky’tha asks if others have come asking questions and she says no \- only you and Thalex.

She then asks about the Gilded Vulture \- and she doesn’t know.  But she responds that Hilda is the assistant of Paeris, and Tatters is the cat. Paeris is often gone, but Tatters and Hilda are always present.  Ky’tha then thanks Shanna for her help, keeps the pack on her person, and walks back down \- telling the group in their heads what she learned.

Ains asks if Paeris is a powerful wizard, but Ky’tha doesn’t know.  Og doesn’t recognize the names Hilda or Tatters, who is a lykoi cat.  All of the party thinks about how ugly the cat is. We are planning.  Hap tells the group \- in head \- that Thalex could transport the group to Basctdelm.  Ains shares that he can also do that.  He asks if we can help get the sigil and Ky’tha says that we can use his service and get the sigil. Ky’tha hands Hap the pack and says “this is yours” and in his head “don’t open this here.”  

Aury says out loud “how good the porter is” and Paeris motions him over to her.  She says “you really don’t do you?”  and he says “what” and she says “you have no mental issues” and he says “no” \- they just have fun.  She asks about his trust level with them \- he says “I would trust them with my life, but not my money.” They talk about how long the party has been together, but he says he doesn’t know much about either’s past.  She asks if Aury has heard of **Ahn’tsuel**?  And he has. He is considered almost a fable \- is he real or not.  This is a spiritual power that goes into a new vessel every 50 years…and that person rises in power.  If that person combines with others they can be more powerful or not.  She says I have no stake in this game, but there are people who want your friends.  One for his power and the other for betrayal.  Aury says he understands \- Lowkey betraying the party. She tells him that she is not “taking the job” but that he needs to watch his back and the backs of his friends.  Someone knows they are here.  She gets up and exits the tavern.  Ains gets up to go the restroom complaining of needing to pee. Aury notices that Ains goes out the back door (and she went out the front door).  Aury walks back to the table and shares the information with Og that he just heard. Og questions Paeris’ motives and the two talk about this.  Aury asks why Og is wanted this way and he tells him a little of the story.  Og says he had hoped that he would never have run into this group.  Aury asks Og to get Ains to come back…and he won’t right now.  Og tells him that Ains is going to follow her.  They need to figure out if we need to leave now. 

Ains walks out back and sees people at the docks.  He speaks in a female human’s head trying to make her think she is thinking certain thoughts about the wizard…and did she ever use powers on me (and the person thinks that she flew one time).  She then sees Ains and says hello.  Ains walks away and sees Paeris walking into her tower with a short person.  The short person says “oh I know that guy” and she says “yes \- we are going to talk about him” as they walk in and shut the door.  Ains disguises himself and goes to the other side of the tower. 

Meanwhile, Ky’tha tells the group that we should talk to the stablemaster and why.  Tells Thalex that answers probably can be found from witnesses.  He agrees.  He then asks Zymve to “grab a breath of fresh air” with him alone outside.  

They go for a walk and as he leaves says he wishes Hap the best and hopes it all works out with his sister.  He and Zymve then walk and are also.  He says I guess that you are probably the newest members of the party (the freshman).  He says I find it interesting that you are walking around with this group.  And that you walk around with multiple instruments.  She responds yes \- is curt.  He then asks if she will play something soft for him, while they talk.  He says he’d like to hear music from the Underdark.  And she says that it is her thing (she rolls a nat 20 on performance).  He is pleased and says it is taking him a moment to grasp it…he says it paints a picture.  He can almost see her homeland \- and says that he would like to visit there.  She says no \- you don’t want that \- the people are not friendly (but it is pretty).  He says that for the past several years, more and more people have risen from the Underdark.  He says music like this you just don’t hear everyday.  He says I hope that if you retire, I hope you come to Basctdelm and play for the Queen.  She is flattered.  He is trying to make her feel like he is trustworthy. She feels like he tries to figure out what about a person he can \- like his role in life is to try to figure out who people are and how they feel.  He’s trying to figure out why she is on the surface.  He pauses at the end of the song and says he is going to retire. He says that he thinks there is a bit more than meets the eye to her.  He says he trusts Immonara and pulls out a stone that he hands to Zymve.  Says “if you ever need me, pull this out and you can get to me.”  I bid you good evening.  This is a sending stone connected to Thalex.  She asks what this is and he tells her it can be used once/day, but don’t bother me.  Use it when you need me, not for trivial stuff.  She gets the sense that this is unique and special and that he is interested in the group. 

Ky’tha asks Ains where he is.  He says bathroom and tower. She says that she doesn’t know what’s up but the group should visit the stablemaster in the morning.  Group decides to retire to rooms for the night.

Meanwhile, Ains is now disguised at as a 13 year old, short child, covered in flour with a tunic and apron and no front teeth.  He walks up to the tower door and knocks uncontrollably.  The top of the frame opens and asks “what do you want.”  Hello sir, I’m looking for the lady of the Tower.  Do you realize the time of night?  Yes \- I do sir.  Then “Hilda \- there is someone here.”  Door opens and it is Hilda.  Hello \- I am a cousin of Filbert up the street.  I’m visiting and working.  I wanted to play in the river and lost track of time.  I talked to some of the townsfolk and was told that Paeris is a godmother of the town and I was wondering if I could spend the night.  And Hilda reaches around and finds some coin and hands it to him saying “The River Dog will take care of you.”  And he begs to see her…I heard she is powerful.  Maybe she can make me fly.  Can I just please talk to her?  Hilda says, one second and she closes the door. Ains messages everyone that this is going well.  Then Zabeek walks out of the tower.  The door aboves Ains opens and he starts to fly up.  “I have never seen such a black gnome before” as he looks down on Zabeek.  Then he begs to talk to the wizard.  He flies over to the window and sees the cat through the window.  He knocks on the window.  The curtains part and he is told that he should go home.  He is told to “go back to the mill.” Who are you?  Go back downstairs (as Ains persuades her).  Hilda opens the door and cleans the kid up with a spell and tells him to wait. He is sitting on a rug and he looks across the room and sees himself in a mirror (as Ains).  She walks away and says I’ll go get the lady.  Ains sees a little table with a drawer next to him.  He doesn’t think he has been spotted, but isn’t sure. Ains lifts up the table to see how heavy it is (it is not too heavy).  There are doors to the left and right.  He checks and both doors are locked.  He looks under the cracks at the bottom of the door.  One is a storage area (he sees shoes). He tries to unlock the door that Hilda went through.  He can’t.  He hears someone coming and he sees a cat walking as he spies under the door.  He misty steps on the other side.  He is in a dining area and there is a cat.  The cat looks up at him and dispels the magic and Ains is Ains. He then walks up and nuzzles him.  He tries to animal handle the cat, but doesn’t.  About that time, Hilda walks in with a tray and says “she was expecting you tonight” and sets the tray down with dessert.  He tries to talk to the group, but no one hears anything.  

Meanwhile, Hap and Ky’tha go up to their rooms (Hap/Aury, Ky’tha/Zymve, Og/Ains).  Zymve stays downstairs with Og and Hap to drink a little more.  In the room, Ky’tha locks the door and pulls the items out of the bag.  Hap thinks that maybe Phira wanted to have a child and was carrying it around hoping to get pregnant. The perfume reminds him of something \-but isn’t sure what (maybe his mom?). He is sad. He doesn’t want to bring danger to her so agrees we should wait to visit Basctdelm.  Ky’tha then leaves and goes next door to her room.  Hap puts the items in his bags.

Meanwhile, the three are getting drunk and Zymve shares the stone she got from Thalex.  They laugh and have fun. At some point, Og tries to communicate with Ains but gets no response.  Og communicates to the group “Ains might be in trouble.”  Ky’tha asks why you think that…and Og says his personal connection might be severed.  Hap asks why he went to the tower and Og says to check it out and get information.  Ky’tha offers to help check things out.  As she is walking out, she runs into Zabeek.  She questions Zabeek and he tells all about his night \- meeting with the wizard and giving her their history. Zymve and Og ask why he shared so much.  Ky’tha tells Og \-in his head \- that the wizard could have killed he and Ains in the Inn \- that we don’t really know who she is or what her connection is to the Vultures.  She then tells Zabeek that he overshared.  Then asks Zabeek about what he saw inside the tower.  And he also discloses about the “living forever” quest that the party is on, and asks if this is a mercenary group or a good group.  He also talks about the party going to visit a druid.   She tells him that the group shouldn’t go visit the kahlbits, but won’t say why \- they will figure that out themselves.  Ky’tha then confirms that Zymve will have Zabeek for the night \- she will \- and excuses herself to go out for a walk (telling the others in their heads that she will check things out). 

Ky’tha then goes outside and walks to the Tower.  Hap goes back downstairs to the taproom and goes to drink on the dock.

Ky’tha notices a patrol \- one person \- walking around the town in general.  

Meanwhile, Ains sees a plate and Hilda encourages him to sit down.  Would you like tea with your crumbcake?  You aren’t the one I want to talk to…and she says that she will be down soon.  Paeris comes down.  Ains realizes the cat is magical, too.  She eats cake and tea.  So \- are you having a good evening?  Ains says he has had better \- I am not going to lie.  He then name drops Lady Immonarra and Thalex (and comments about his robe).  She said she would kill for that robe \- it is just an expression.  I’m going to cut to the point.  What’s up with the Gilded Vulture logo out front?  She says that I already explained that to your friend.  She says ‘now I have to answer to you \- I don’t think you understand this is my place.’  He then defends his position.  They argue. Tell me what you know about the Vultures. She says “do you not understand the drop off.”  She then mentions a couple of hypothetical requests “like finding (Og) and (Ains).”  But please tell me why you broke into my house.  He then talks about his power and about being captured by the Vultures.  Hilda and the cat watch.  If you had been captured and tortured by a group, wouldn’t you want to find out why they are now in a new location?  She says I heard you were captured by a cult…and he says yes, captured by the cult then taken by the Vultures.  Hilda takes his cake away.  She said I thought with this passion you have we might be able to work something out, but now I’m not sure.  I told your friend that I wasn’t accepting the job (she was offered 2005 gold).  What else would you like from me?  He says I just needed to know about the Gilded Vulture situation.  Just so you are aware \- when they ask what is going on \- I will tell them.  And just so you know \- I’m familiar with the druid, the captured sister, and the wanting to live forever quests.  You think there are no consequences for your attitude.  Shall we change this and slides the cake to him?  He then takes a bite and falls asleep.  

Ky’tha walks around the tower and is greeted by the Tower.  “I thinks she is done with him \- would you like to take him back?”  Yes.  Come in.  And she walks into the foyer.  She catches the mirror and sees her grandmother’s face in the mirror.  She looks to the left and sees Hilda carrying Ains.  She looks at Ky’tha and she says “I’ll take him.”  Ky’tha asks what happened to him and she says “crumb cake.”  Where is the wizard?  She is exhausted for the night, but she might want to see you.”  She tells the cat to get the wizard.   She motions Ky’tha to the chair and she leaves to take Ains outside.  She says “keep in mind the (town leader) relies on Paeris.”  

Og walks outside and sees Hilda carrying Ains outside (he knows this is the gabük he saw earlier, only larger).  Og sees her going to the Inn.  He sees her go inside and steps up to claim him.  By all means \- and hands him over.  She says “no harm, no foul” and he says “if you say nothing, no harm.”  And she says “you don’t know Paeris.”  Og then slaps Ains in the face.

Meanwhile, the cat jumps on the table and eats the cake as the wizard walks in.  Ky’tha tells her we are there looking into the murder and disappearance that happened earlier.  She says she doesn’t want anything to befall her town.  She asks how Ky’tha can get along with Ains and she tells her it is because Ky’tha appreciates that he has had an interesting life.  Tell Ains that she means no hard feelings.  Ky’tha asks her if there is something about Ains that would help him.  She says that she would be happy to assist and help him. She wants me to take tea back for Og, Ains and myself. Understand that friends try and protect you and not try to put their burden on their back…and if you are going to truly trust each other you need to carry his burden, too. She then expresses Og’s concerns. And she says that she feels our group’s pursuits are more noble than those of the Vultures and will not help them.  But at some point the reward may be substantial and hard to say no to. And Ky’tha encourages her to mislead them \- or at least tell them Inverland (where we may be going at some point).  Ky’tha thanks her and leaves.

Outside, she runs into Og and Ains and tells them what she learned.  Conversation about how Ains and Og don’t want to stay in town \-group has to convince them.  The group decides to go upstairs to adjacent rooms.  Aury and Zymve drink the tea from Paeris.  Zabeek rooms with Ky’tha and Zymve.  Og and Ains share a poor room, Ky’tha, Zymve and Zabeek share a poor room, Hap and Aury share a modest room.  Aury fights dreams.  Zymve goes to sleep and sleeps well \- then starts to dream.  She sees as if a third person \- a recognizable drow walk up to a man and start having a conversation.  The man is unknown.  The drow is someone who was in Emberstran (the guy she knows).  They are talking.  They then argue.  The drow then slashes him down and as the man falls, he sees the sigil that Og was talking about fall from the man.   Ky’tha falls asleep and dreams.  She is walking down an underground path in the caves under the mountain.  She looks up and sees veins of ore that are unique to this place (pewter) \- notable only to this place (Dunduar).  She looks and feels a hand on her shoulder.  She turns and sees a man \- she has never seen.  Before she gets a full glimpse, she feels a hood cover her face and everything goes dark.  She wrestles to pull off the hood and then turns and hears a struggle next to her \- she turns and sees a dead drow.  Walking off is a shadow with drops of blood. She rolls the drow over and it looks similar to Zymve, but it is not her (but it is a female).  Doesn’t have the instruments or piercings.  But resembles her.  She thinks back and as she starts to place it thinks it is someone kin to the bookkeeper in Nungulthil. The shadow as it walked away walks into the light and disappears…and as he disappears he turns and nods at me.  Her gut says this was some type of angelic guardian.

The rest of the group gets a good night's rest \- Og, Hap, and Aury.  Ains does not go to sleep.  He stays awake, paranoid.  

### **It’s the 3rd of Paramor, 1246**

Og wakes up with something that feels like a vibration next to him.  There is a cat there.  It wakes up with Og and meows. It is Tatters.  Ains tells Og he has powers.  Og grapples it and picks it up and studies the collar. It is bejeweled on leather.  As Og holds the cat it disappears and shows up on Ains’ shoulders.  Ains mage hands the collar to place it over the eyes of the cat.  The second he tries to move the collar he feels it dip its head and he feels a pop.  And it shows up next to the door.  It then becomes a spider and crawls away under the door.  He is still holding the collar.  Ains sees that the collar is magical with abjuration magic. Ains feels bad and Og suggests that they return it. Og punches Ainsley and tells him to never force him to fall asleep again.

Aury and Hap open their door and there is a note outside.  The note says “Look, it was an interesting evening and quite honestly I like some of you more than I should and others less than I oughta.  We don’t see eye to eye, but I hope Hap gets what he is looking for. And that last gift is from Paeris.”  This is from Thalex.  We all meet downstairs.  

Ky’tha, Aury, and Zymve have advantage on wisdom saving throws this full day. 

The group heads outside.  Aury says he needs to pick up a pipe from the market \- so group goes to the market area then stables.  They visit Solly’s Supplies.  Ains and Og look for rare ink and they find it.  Aury looks for a pipe and Ky’tha helps him.  Ky’tha finds one for him made of white wood, long and ornate with rosy red around the top of the bowl.  Zymve is looking for something that catches her eye and she notices on the wall a ton of keys (and she thinks \- oh crap, there was a special key \- and she finds a Spitespirit Cache key).  Solly looks at Zymve and says that is going to cost you a whole gold.  She tries to talk her down. And Solly says it is a gold. And Zymve pays it.  Ains ink is 50 gp worth that she is charging 10 gp.  Aury’s pipe is worth 5 sp.  Ains starts to try to talk her down by telling her he is going to write a story about a shop.  He offers 1gp for the ink, but then ups it to 5 gp and she agrees.  He then says “read about Zymve’s magnificent story.”  She comments what an odd name it is.   Ains pulls out his wand and looks for magical items, as Ky’tha decides to find something for herself. Only 1 thing shows up.  He finds a small wand made from strange wood.  He approaches her about the wooden stick. He offers her a copper piece for the “back scratcher.”  He trades her dice for the wand.  This is a magical [Xorian Wenge Focus](https://www.dndbeyond.com/magic-items/215739-xorian-wenge-focus). Ky’tha sees a chunk of Dunduar ore set on the side under crap.  She thinks it is valuable.  She trades her 4 candles and 1gp for the chunk of ore.  

As the group leaves the store they see the Ulgrey mansion. They see someone knocking on the door  yelling “hey, come on open the doors.”    The person is lean and mud spattered in leathers.   Ains yells “why are you knocking so loud.”  “I need the Bergermeister.  I can’t go hunting into Kahlbit’s Vale and I need to hunt and I can’t.”  What is it?  There is a mist \- a sticky, nasty mist.  And scary shit out there in those woods.  Like I used to hunt in Kahlbit’s Veil, but you can’t hunt there now.  You don’t go to Kahlbit’s Veil now.  So we tried to hunt between the two rivers.  But it was a half a day away and he points…and it gets yucky and it didn’t used to be yucky.  And there was a mist and a pool and I saw a red form in the pool (bullywug?) and I turned to run and I tripped over a body and I saw red worms eating on the body and I ran all the way back to the town to tell them.  KV is bad because there are no more kahlbits…because it is big, thick and full and they can’t run there anymore.  It has changed (from the Awakening).”  Ains thinks this is a psychic frog-like creature.  We agree to help.  It is morning. We will split and talk to the stablemaster and Burgermeister.

– The End of the Session –
