# **31 \- Oh No\!  Zabeek\!**

**Session Date**: July 23, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  3rd of Paramor, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Ulgrey

### **In the town of Ulgrey**

We talk to Erland about the threat he saw outside of town. We see a wiry man approaching in leathers, limping (Qaddie). Ky’tha notices Erland making a “hmmmm” face at Qaddie. He asks if anyone has seen the Bergermeister \- he says he needs to talk to her, too.  Ky’tha says get in line.  About that time a door to the Seeker’s Church (oratory) opens up.  A lady comes out and says hello to Qaddie and Erland \- I need to speak with you both.  Ainslie thinks something seems off. Ky’tha interrupts them leaving to ask who she is \- this is the Bergermeister Elsie.  She welcomes the group.  We question if our help is going to be required or not \- and she inquires what about.  Erland shares a little and Ky’tha and Ainslie fill in a few blanks.   She bids us to come inside the church (Ky’tha questions why the church and not her mansion \- which leads to Elsie questioning why Ky’tha would tell her what to do), but the group goes.

The enter the church.  It was initially a building dedicated to Neera.  There are recent paintings of maps on the walls.  The church is welcoming and bright.  There is an elderly robed lady there \- she appears to be over this church.  Aury introduces himself out of respect to the sanctuary, as the group is motioned over to a sitting area in an enclave.  They notice the paintings are giving off Cembus vibes.  The priestess seems to be a follower of Muerg or Cembus.  This is Mother Velna. She welcomes everyone to “the Oratory of Ulgrey.”  She looks everyone up and down. Elsie reaches out to comfort Ky’tha \- proud that she elicited some emotion \- and says she hopes Ky’tha took no offense.  Ky’tha responds no more than you being upset that I suggested to you that it seems odd that you’d work out of the church vs. your home or office.

Ainslie introduces themselves as the EGD and says that the party is there investigating the murder and kidnapping. She asks for details.  Ainslie explains that we know there are ties to criminal organizations that are associated with the murder.  Ky’tha overhears Elsie say to Velna, “see I told you.”  Ky’tha then shares more of the details given what she overheard.  Elsie then asks Qaddie to share what he learned.  He says that he is traveled and it took him a while before he found clues.  He said he nearly got to Harbuck before he found their trail.  He found off the path the carriage \- empty and no trace of anyone.  And he returned to town just now. Ky’tha comments that Harbok is the opposite direction of Basctdelm and Qaddie/Elsie agree.  But they question why Basctdelm \- and Ainslie tells them that we had evidence that they were going to Basctdelm. Aury comments that our sources about Basctdelm were confirmed.  We discuss the situation as well as Erland’s issue.  Ky’tha mentions that maybe the Cloak has an operation in Harbok.

We notice an elven female escorting a shorter figure to a door across on the other side of the church. Ainslie flips Velna a donation as he casts a spell to read the thoughts of Elsie and Velna (maybe they are worthy, maybe they are the ones; I’m curious about the dark-skinned one) and Erland (I need to find a place, I’ve been on the road a long time and it is time) and Qaddie (I hope this is helpful, I’m trying to impress the Bergermeister).  He also picks up on the thoughts of one of the other figures (oh, they are the ones \- but has no idea who that is referring to in the group).  Aury is trying to figure out who the short figure is.  He sees shadows.  Ky’tha wonders if they may have overheard the conversation and thinks they were likely preoccupied with their own affairs.

Ainslie tells the Bergermeister that he would like to read her mind \- something he can do with the EGD.  But he can’t read her thoughts.  Elsie asks Ainslie what she is thinking and he tells her, then tells Velna what she was thinking.  When she hears this, Zymve speaks up expressing frustration about why they would be concerned about her.  Velna mentions that they are with the Advents of Harmony and asks Zymve if she knows of them \- she doesn’t \- and says that this would be a conversation for another time since there are more pressing issues, but that they meant no harm to Zymve \- just found it curious to see her on the surface since there aren’t many like her around here.

Elsie asks Erland to give her more info about the pond and body and worms.  He says it is probably a half a day down the road on the way to Harbok.  Qaddie starts to speak up that it is close to the carriage and Ky’tha comments that the dead body could be Phira’s husband. Erland excuses himself to go get rest. 

Ainslie tells Elsie that there town is a little gray and Ky’tha tells him that Elsie doesn’t like to be told what to do.  And then she tells the group that the party is going to leave now to investigate the dead body \- that Qaddie is welcome to join us, but we are going because it is important to our investigation. 

Zabeek shows up and asks if he can take Ewing on a stroll and Zymve tells him to escort us.  Velna asks if she can have a moment to finish her conversation.  Aury encourages her. Velna asks if Zymve is interested in learning more about the Advents of Harmony.  She says she is a retired Savant.  She says her services are now to Cembus, but she acts as a safe place for those seeking information or help. Aury tells Zymve that is a great place for her to be.  Zymve asks what the benefits are for joining the group.  Velna tells her that it would be networking and contacts…and access to information from the Argent Halls.  Zymve is not terribly trusting of surface dwellers and declines the offer.   Ainslie starts to ask questions to Velna and Zymve tells him “they didn’t invite you.”  Ky’tha then asks Velna if anyone would be welcome and she says yes and extends the invitation to all. No one responds immediately, but Ainslie tells her he has a journal of history and lore…and starts to share a little about what he is reading (dragon riders, etc.).  Zymve asks him to quiet down.  She asks if Ainslie is always this sensitive and Ky’tha and Zymve both say yes \- that she needs to extend to him a personal invitation.  But she would like to know more and talk to him more about the contents of the journal. 

Bottom line:  we deduce that this church is where the Advents of Harmony meet up.  The group heads outside, but Aury stays back to talk to Velna.  He takes her hand and kisses it formally and gets down on one knee and says he is showing his respect and offers her a donation.  He then opens up his book \- and she asks what it is \- and he tells her it is the original text from Diverra.  She gasps and is taken aback that he is walking around with something that valuable.  He says he is not worried \- that his goddess will take care of it.  She tells him of the Uktali Monastery and how they copy vital records and preserve the history of items.  She encourages him to protect the tome \- with more than his life.  Aury thanks her for the information and reaches into the book and gives her a rose petal and explains the significance of it.  She walks over to the map, closes her eyes and touches a point on the map that “moved” her.  She pointed to a place in the middle of Shademeadow \- this would be the surface location of the shrine and the life tree.  He memorizes the location.  She says that she will keep the petal safe and puts it away.  He tells her the name of the book.   Then he goes outside.

Meanwhile, Zymve and Ainslie discuss the book and how she is surprised that he told of some of the contents.  Ky’tha asks Hap if he is ready to investigate the dead body that might be his brother-in-law.  He is ready.  Ainslie the offers a trade to Zymve \- he offers her the wand in exchange for the key.  She declines. When Aury comes outside, the group heads out. Zabeek is a little frightened by the prospect of seeing a dead body.  

The group heads to the stables to get Ewing and question the stablemaster. Discussion on the walk about what Zabeek would like to be \- a bard, a mage, etc.  He needs training.  Ainslie offers him a wand.  Zymve offers him a drum. They try to each persuade him to be their own apprentice…but decide to share the duty

The group arrives at the Stables. An elven woman is brushing a horse. She asks if we are there to pick up our gertot.  We say yes.  Ky’tha asks if she is the one who works here and the lady gives her a smart ass answer.  Ky’tha rephrases to say the SOLE proprietor of this establishment, swearing as she asks, and the lady says no \- it is a family business.  Ky’tha then asks Hap to tell her why we are there.  Hap asks her about the murder.  She tells him there were four people.  She overheard the name “Ni-Ro” mentioned (Neirroh).  Hap has not heard of that.  She tells the group that they were headed in the direction of Harbok.  Hap tells her that the carriage was just found.  She tells him that it was a full black carriage and stood out that way.  Hap asks if she is aware of anything in that area where the carriage would be abandoned.  She says not exactly in that direction \- but the Tower of Myr is roughly in that direction.  Ky’tha then asks her about Kahlbits Vale. She says that no one goes back there anymore.  We ask why not?  She says that it used to be a nice forest with a pleasant grove and animals aplenty.  But that is no longer that way.  This happened a while ago \- maybe even a year ago.  There used to be a Druid of the Vale and she interacted with the hunters with kindness.  But then the forest got too thick.  Rumors are that the druid may have started the problems.  She was a secluded wood elf, but has been hard to find.  Aury mentions that he saw a wood elf at the Sanctuary \- but the group decides to head out of town \- can follow up about who that might have been when we return.

Ainslie wants to return the collar at the Tower so the group walks by there on the way out.  He looks to see if the window is open.  Someone bumps into Ainslie and apologizes and hands him a brown journal.  Og picks up that the guy may have slight of handed a book to Ainslie.   The guys walks away.  Ainslie looks at the empty book and it has an owl’s head on it.  Ky’tha tells him that it is a journal for the Advents of Harmony and that must be his personal invitation. Ainslie sees someone sweeping at the front door and sees the cat in the window.  He realizes that the gabuk was the one in the church earlier.  He tells her that the cat left something in his room.  She replies that this is gift from the lady and are you sure you want to return it?  He says yes and she takes it and calls the cat over.  She thanks him and says no hard feelings \- and he says “neutral.”  And Ky’tha interrupts and says ‘let’s go.’  Aury pulls out his map and she asks to see it, as he tells her that we are “mapping the lands of Myrdae.”  He then tells her about the Wefterians.  She tells him that the Oratory has maps and that he should use the walls to help fill in his map.  She responds that “oh \- you were the ones I saw there earlier.”  She offers muffins, but the group declines.

The group starts to head out of town, headed toward Harbok.  As they go, Zymve and Ainslie trade off telling/teaching him things.  We know it will be a half a day’s travel to try to find the carriage.  Qaddie said we will come to a stream and then come to a road…if we go there, we have gone too far.  We need to turn around and go back 50 paces…and then go on the right side.  We think we will get there mid-afternoon.

We have some rations around lunchtime and continue.  We notice Og starts to pull back a little.  Og feels his wrist growing warmer where the thornleaf is.  There is heat and a “pumping of the life force” through his body.  His vision starts to go \- eyes are milky.  A weight settles in to his mind about the blight.  He feels the corrupted roots choking life, but it feels disturbingly unnatural.  He feels the connection to the world tree…and feels a headache coming on.  He looks at the thornleaf and is expecting to see black thorns…and sees three of them. He thinks this is tied to the blight.  The thornleaf starts to hum and get warm and feels like it may be a stronger bond to him.  The haze then lifts and his vision returns and he sees the party looking at him intently.  \[His bond has gone from Acquainted to Aware \- level 1 to level 2\].   Og talks of how he feels.  Ky’tha comments that Ewing broke wind.  More random talk- which leads to Zymve say that “she doesn’t need anyone.”

We continue.  We notice the grass is flattened off where a campsite was. Ky’tha walks over to investigate.  She is thrown off.  She sees where there were 3 horses.  The rocks are still warm from a fire.  There was only one bedroll used.  She sees a piece of burned paper in the ashes “Found 2 strange horses walking the road alone…(smudge)...message delivered.  Returning.  Bacna.” She shares this with the group.  No one has heard of them, except Og.  \[Bacna was a messenger in the Gilded Vultures\].  Discussion about what this means.  Zymve thinks this means that this was a courier that must have been delivering a note and trying to decide what to write down.  Like someone just wrote something down, but it wasn’t going to anyone.  Which is why it was burned.  We decide that the person probably has a half day’s jump on us and is on horseback.  

We continue.  Make it to the bridge and then follow Qaddie’s instructions to march back 50 paces and go to the right.  Og takes a look around and notices an area that looks like someone tried to cover tracks of a carriage.  He is able to find evidence of broken limbs and finds a trail to follow.  We take the trail and it leaves the road.  We go for a while…further than we expect to go. Fleur goes up to scout, but it is thick.  After a mile, Fleur catches site of the black carriage off to the side.  It sits at a strange angle (like a wheel is stuck).  We find things amiss.  Hard to tell what happened exactly.  Hap and Ky’tha investigate the scene and tell where they headed off, which direction the horses headed off, but can’t quite tell which direction the body is.  They find evidence on the carriage where a body was tied to it \- dried blood and rope.  We see tracks, but aren’t sure where to look.  Hap says he wants to follow the footsteps.  Ainslie creates the mind link for the group, Zabeek, and Ewing \- except Aury.   Og uses a spell to speak with plants.  We spread out and start to follow the tracks. He tells a tree he is looking for a body.  The tree tells him that he saw a horse.  And that the horse was eating.  He speaks to other grasses and plants.  They exclaim that they say a rabbit.  Og tells the group they have seen nothing other than horses and rabbits.  Nothing spoken about a dead body.  He then asks about a red creature.  And gets no response. Things just happened too long ago for the plants to remember.  

We continue deeper into the woods.  The ground gets softer.  Og is bothered \- he doesn’t see blight, but he sees lack of growth.  Like something has stunted the ground/vegetation. Og asks a plant what is wrong \- it says “I can’t breathe anymore \- something has changed.  This just started recently.”  Og notices a smell of marshland.   The group is having issues trying to find clues. The swamp/marsh-like lands have obscured clues.  Og notices some stone areas up ahead and he sees a low haze around the bottom of a hill we are approaching.  The group reflects on the words from Erland about a mist.  Og again questions plants about a red figure.  We continue forward. Ainslie and Og seem to go up hill; the others find more swampland.  Ainslie tops the hill and sees footprints.  Og tops the hill and sees a door on the stone wall that is opened up with vines that have grown up the wall on the hillside.  He sees blackened evergreen junipers that have covered the walls and consumed the area. Above the door is the top of the hill.  He can’t tell if the door is part of the hill or not.  Doesn’t think it was constructed this way and is old, but sees recent footprints.   He asks the juniper tree why it looks the way it does.  It says Goo-igs-ian \- he has returned (Guigzien).  Hap hears this and knows that Guigzien was aspiring to be one of Torec’s hands (Torec \- god of elements).  Guigzien embraced earth and water (swamp and mud and muck).  He always heard that he was not a good guy.   He tells this to the group.  Og calls for the group to come up the hill \- and everyone does.  Og yells for Aury to come up the hill, too.  Ky’tha asks Og if the plant saw this person and asks the plant.  It says that it just assumes it was the return of that demi-god now. Everyone gathers.  Og asks if someone has been here recently and they reply that no one has been here within the past day and that they are fading.  

Ky’tha offers to stealth forward (23).  She sees that the door is about to fall off the hinges.  That it looks like the roof is breaking down.  A fire pit near a place where the roof is more stable.  Discarded fabric in the area.  She sees scratches on the floor, purposefully made. She takes a moment to fill the group in and study the scratches further. It appears to be in a pattern that she doesn’t recognize.  There is coloration in the scratches.  She sees other papers in the items/gear.  Handwriting is not familiar.  Ainslie looks over her shoulder “filthy hole but it keeps the rain off and no one is looking.”  Ky’tha shows Hap her note “Phira secured.  Scroll used.  Basctdelm.  Clean the circle if you return.”   She points out the scratches and asks Ainslie to take a look.  Ainslie knows this is a teleportation circle.  Ainslie looks at the other papers.  “Lower stairs stay sealed…something moves the muck and it doesn’t like the light.  Not our problem.”  And another “mage practiced…something went sideways.  Let the town deal with it.  Distraction works.”   Og tells Aury about Guigzien and he thinks this place was destroyed 20 years ago during the Awakening.  He thinks this was a temple to the “muck god.”    Aury says we need to make sure to take care of the threat.  Ainslie tells the group that he isn’t full strength, but we decide to move forward.

Zabeek is scared.  Og tells him to stay in back.  Og rages and removes the boards.  As he does he notices a wider hall on the other side of the barrier.  IT IS INITIATIVE.

* Round One:  The board shatters and what leaps through is a big blue creature.  Zabeek gets squished by the creature as everyone else dodges the creature.  Zabeek dies.  We see a red creature ahead.  Og attacks the Blue behind him with a critical hit (20 on second roll) and damages him with slashing (26) and acid (7) and knocks him prone (giving temp HP to Zymve). And then moves up to the red guy and takes a critical opportunity attack against Og in his stomach (imposing disadvantage on Og’s next attack).  Og takes 15 slashing damage (rounded down to 7\) and 4 points of poison damage.  He then hits the red creature (14 slashing and 2 acid).  He then takes another attack and red and hits (20 slashing and 1 acid) and reduces his speed by 25’ for this turn.  Zymve casts a spell on the blue one (phantasmal killer) and he saves and takes half damage (10 psychic) and he is frightened.  She then uses inspiration to give Hap, Ky’tha, and Og temp hit points.   Ky’tha attacks Blue with a sneak attack (32 points of piercing damage) and then disengages and moves closer to Og (out of range of both red and blue).   Blue closes some wounds a bit then hits Ainslie for some damage and he is also cursed and cannot regain HP and his HP maximum is decreased by 4\.  He then turns to Aury and (warding flare) misses Aury.  He then hits Hap who reduces the damage (parry), but still takes damage but avoids the curse.  Ainslie blackens his hand and hits the Blue (he takes necrotic damage and cannot heal).  He then moves over and tells Ewing to disengage and move to the door (and he does).  Hap has Fleur buzz the blue and he attacks and hits him (10 piercing).  Then uses the dagger and hits (6 piercing).  Then tries the sword again and misses.  And finally another try with the dagger and fumbles (loses reaction until end of next turn).   The Red heals a little bit.  He then reaches out to attack Og with advantage and hits (10 piercing and Og is cursed).  He then attacks him again and hits again (9 piercing). Then hits Og again (8 piercing).  Aury attacks with burning hands on Blue and he saves and takes fire damage (5 \- but he doesn’t think it did that much).    
* Round Two:  Og attacks Red and gives Ky’tha temp HP (13).  He hits twice and does damage (43 slashing and 3 acid damage \- speed reduced by 25’).  Zymve uses cloud of daggers on the Blue and it hits and inflicts damage (13 slashing).  Ky’tha attacks Red with a sneak attack (28 piercing) and it falls…and she moves back.  Blue is still frightened by Zymve.  He attacks Ainslie and warding flare (gives Ainslie 11 temp hit points) and hits (16 slashing and 3 poison damage).  Then attacks Hap and misses twice. He is still frightened. He takes slashing damage from the cloud of daggers (20 slashing).  Ainslie attacks and misses.  Hap attacks Blue with a chill touch and misses.   Red does not get up.  Aury casts toll of the dead on Blue and he saves (20).    
* Round Three:  Og moves to the Blue character and attacks and hits twice and inflicts damage (46 slashing and 5 acid) and drops him.  

Ky’tha notices that the chamber descends down into a chamber where it looks like there is a big opening and she sees a splash.  She yells out to the group.

WE END HERE

Og is cursed (Red) and Ainslie is cursed (Blue).  Ky’tha is in the front by 10’…the rest of the group is in the circular chamber around the dead Blue.  \[Chip has picture on phone\]  Zabeek is dead.  Ewing is alive next to the door in the original cave chamber. 

– The End of the Session –

# **32 \- Claim your Slaad\!**

**Session Date**: July 30, 2026  
**In-game Date**:  4th of Paramor, 1246  
**Starting Location**:  Lost Shrine to Guigzien

We start by recognizing Zabeek.  Aury prays for him and asks Diverra to take his soul.   Zymve looks at the drums that she gave Zabeek and mends them, as Ains picks up the wand he gave him.   Ky’tha stealths (31) and goes to check out the chamber, heading down stairs. She gets to the edge of the water to see if she sees anything.   She just sees rubble on the other side of the hallway \- this entire hallway is under water.    She thinks this isn’t the highest quality stone, but thinks this happened during the Awakening.  

The group decides to pick up a rock and go down as a group.  We get 10’ from the edge of the water and hear coughing from above and hear “guys, help” and Aury, Zymve and Ains look and see Zabeek.  He is only wearing his shirt…no pants or shoes.  Aury starts to touch him to heal him….ROLL FOR INITIATIVE

ROUND ONE:  As Aury gets ready to cast the spell, the “Zabeek” character shifts into a toad-like creature and gets ready to attack.  Aury casts burning hands on the creature and it saves, but still takes damage.  It then needs to make a strength saving throw, as Og teleports him a few feet in front of him, reducing his speed to 0\.   It attacks Ky’tha (Aury imposes disadvantage and give Ky’tha temp hit points) and Zymve, and it damages and frightens both, but also poison damage to Zymve.  Ky’tha uses uncanny dodge to take only half the damage. Og attacks recklessly attacks and misses, then hits, dealing damage, and he gives Zymve temp hit points.  Ains attacks and hits and deals thunder damage, but it absorbs some of the damage.   He then backs up and pulls Zymve back 15’.  Ky’tha attacks with her dagger, but misses.  Zymve attacks with vicious mockery and hits \- unleashing a string of swear words at him \- and he takes 3 psychic points of damage. Hap, using Fleur to fly by, misses his first attack, then tries with his pruning dagger and misses.  Then tries the sword again and fumbles, losing his movement.  Then tries the dagger again and misses.

ROUND TWO:  Aury looks around and, seeing no one is hurt, decides to bless Ky’tha, Hap, and Og (Diverra blesses you and makes you celibate).   The creature attacks Ky’tha and fumbles (loses his reaction), then hits her and frightens her (she uses uncanny dodge to lessen the damage).   Og attacks and hits once and then misses.  He inflicts acid and slashing damage on the hit. Ains attacks and hits using the wand. Ky’tha fumbles and hurts herself as she catches the dagger from falling down.  Zymve casts cloud of daggers on the creature, inflicting slashing damage.  Hap doesn’t use the fly by of Fleur and attacks with his sword and hits and tries to trip him up.  He takes damage and stays upright.  He then uses the dagger and hits.  Then tries the sword again and hits and tries to trip him again, and he stays upright. Then tries the dagger again and hits again.

ROUND THREE:  Aury casts toll the dead on the creature and it fails its saving throw, taking necrotic damage.  The creature laughs and farts and a poisonous cloud covers the group, heavily obscuring the group giving the group the blinded condition. Og attacks and hits twice, dealing slashing and acid damage, killing it.  The fart cloud disappears.

Ains goes up to check Zebeek and he is still there, but is shirtless.  The creature was able to take on the disguise of Zabeek.  Aury casts “death ward” on Ky’tha (if she goes to 0 she will go to 1 hit point). Hap realizes that Shadowtrail is sucking this in \- he’s enjoying this fight (in his head, Hap taunts the sword “nice of you to finally show up”).

Og proceeds to throw the rock into the wet hallway ahead about 60’ ahead.  Ky’tha asks Aury to light up something and he lights up Ains’ shirt.  Ky’tha asks him to light up a crossbow bolt and she shoots it and embeds it across the hallway.  She shoots a bolt (at disadvantage) and successfully embeds it at the far end of the hallway.   Zymve moves her cloud of daggers through the water ahead of the group. She thinks the water is 3’ deep.  The party moves forward into the water. Og steps in and feels like it is slick and murky and tells everyone to proceed with caution.  Ky’tha moves forward stealthily (24) to check out the rubble at the far end of the hall.  She is thigh deep moving forward.  She telepathically tells Ains when she is halfway down the hall.  She makes it to the corner and sees the rubble and sees it turns and there is a door hanging on hinges 20’ down.  Checking out the rubble, she thinks something was waiting here and is no longer there.  She sees there is a dim light in the room beyond the door hanging off hinges.  She communicates to the group and then proceeds to the room.  Aury wants Ains to look for diamonds as they proceed.  

Ky’tha moves forward to the door and touches the wall and uses tremorsense to see if there is movement in the room beyond.  She senses at least 4 creatures in the room (35’, 40’, 50’, right and left and further back).   Zymve casts mirror image and Hap casts blade ward.

Ky’tha moves into the room, still deep and still slick.  It opens out to the right and left and she can see on the far side of the room that light is shining down on an altar. She moves in to the left and sees a shadow dip into the water.  She looks at the altar and sees it  has vines growing on it. She tells the others to come now\!

There is a raised dais on the far in with a body wrapped in vines on it.  Several shapes move in the water and IT IS INITITATIVE.

ROUND ONE:  The guy in the back takes a step and disappears.  Ains moves into the room and surveys the situation.  He lights up his body and glows purple and holds his action to cast a spell before Hap.  One of the creatures raises his staff and casts a spell \- fireball \- on the party in the door (all except Ky’tha).  The group makes saving throws…all saved, but still take damage.  He then moves forward toward the party. Aury casts a fireball at the right side, aiming for the red and blue. One saves and one doesn’t.  He then takes a step back 30’.  Zymve casts hypnotic patterns on the red and blue on the right side.  The blue guy is charmed and incapacitated. All else save.  She thinks the person on the altar is a victim. Og rages and recklessly attacks the green guy and hits and then fumbles and falls prone, then stands up.  He inflicts slashing and acid damage.  The red guy advances to Og and attacks him and hits three times, dealing 34 piercing damage, but he only takes half damage.  The other red guy advances forward to Hap and misses twice, but then crits on the final hit, also hitting Og.  Ains pops off his reaction and they must make wisdom shaking throws.  This spell slows the invisible and one of the red creatures and the blue creature, makes them have disadvantage, makes them lose \-2 to AC, speed halved, \-2 to dex saves, one attack per turn, 25% chance of spell failing.  Hap attacks the red creature in front of him and hits with his sword.  Then attacks with the dagger and misses.  Tries the sword again and hits and frightens him (imposing disadvantage on attack and ability rolls).  Then uses a second wind to heal himself.  Ky’tha uses her crossbow to attack the red creature.  She hits with sneak attack and then cunning action hides. 

ROUND TWO:  The invisible creature tried to attack with a spell and failed (because of the slow) and is now visible.  Ains casts dissonant whispers on the non-slowed red creature and it fails its save.  It must run away. Hap and Og get opportunity attacks.  Hap hits and damages him.  Og hits and damages him.  Ains pushes Hap forward and yells \- “I can only hold them for so long \- take them out.”  The green one disappears.  Aury walks up to Og and heals him (33 points).  Zymve gives temp HP to Ains, Hap, and Og (2/each).  She and Ains teleport to the back corner of the room near the wall.  Og gives Aury temp HP (11) and he attacks slowed red and hits twice (the guy is further slowed \- cannot move).  The red one who Hap frightened dashes back to the corner where Zymve and Ains are.  The slowed red creature attacks Hap and misses. Red and blue creatures both stay slowed.  Hap attacks the red creature and fumbles, but it is okay.  Then hits with the dagger.  Then attacks with the sword and hits.  Then attacks with the dagger and hits.  He uses second wind and hits again with his sword then hits again twice.  Ky’tha attacks with her crossbow with sneak attack and kills him.   Then uses cunning action to try to hide (23).

ROUND THREE:  The gray one goes invisible. Ains casts lightning bolt trying to hit the red, blue, and altar.  The red one saves, but still takes some damage (7 points).  The altar fails and takes 30 points of damage.  The blue one saves and takes damage (7 points).  He uses a bonus action to shove the red guy away and he succeeds so he doesn’t move. The green one pops into view next to Aury and Hap and attacks them.  He misses Aury twice.  Then attacks Hap once and Aury fires off a warding flare (giving Hap 11 temp HP) and hits Hap, who takes force damage (parrying some) and is incapacitated for a turn. Aury disengages and moves back 15’ and casts healing word on Hap. Zymve casts shatter on the altar, the person on the altar, and the red creature…who need to make CON saves.  The altar cracks and Zymve sees a symbol on the altar, but can’t make it out.  The Red creature and person on the altar take thunder damage (7).  Og hits the creature once and misses once, inflicting slashing and acid damage.  The blue creature looks fully healed.  The red creature looks at Ains and attacks twice and misses, but hits with the third attack.  He saves the curse.  He then turns and faces the altar and gets in a defensive stance.  Hap is incapacitated.  The group notices the person on the altar standing up and looks down at Zymve and Ains “who hit me with that?  I will help you dispense of these minions and then I will feast upon you.  I sought the power of the sludge god.  \[Guigzien has been released\].  After I slay these minions, I will seek him out.”    This is Blackrend.  

Ky’tha steps forward and shoots the red one, killing it with a sneak attack.  She looks at Blackrend.  She knows he caused the blight in Myrdae.  He came through the Shadowfell.  He wants the power of the god \- he wants his power to create more sludge. Ky’tha thinks he is undead. 

Who summoned Guigzien?  We have to deal with him, the person who summoned him, and Blackrend.  Guigzien is somewhere in Myrdae. 

– The End of the Session –

Aury picks up Hap from the water and takes him to the upper level area.  We collect our dropped weapons (glaive, the sword, the trident, the pruning dagger, other daggers). Aury drops the wall of fire that was blocking the path.  We notice beyond the altar the cultists had a tomb.   

Zymve and Ky’tha search beyond the alter room into the flooded tomb.  They several sarcophagus, some with lids open just barely above the waterline.  The right side of the tomb is collapsed, light filtering in from outside.  The search the sarcophagi and find:  

1. First Sarcophagus – A heavy torque of blackened silver set with three cloudy green stones (75 gp) and a pair of matching bracers (40 gp).  
2. Second Sarcophagus – A thick finger ring carved from a single piece of dark jade in the shape of a spiral (90 gp) and a small pouch of 12 tarnished gold coins.  
3. Third Sarcophagus – A ceremonial dagger with a blade of green-tinged bronze and a hilt wrapped in what was once living vine (now desiccated); the dagger is non-magical but finely made (60 gp).  
4. Fourth Sarcophagus – A necklace of alternating black iron links and polished river stones (55 gp) and a single large, cloudy emerald (120 gp).  
5. Fifth Sarcophagus – A set of three matching arm-rings of corroded silver, each etched with the Clotted Spiral (80 gp total).  
6. Sixth Sarcophagus – A heavy pendant of black stone carved into a clenched fist dripping spiral lines (100 gp) and a small sealed clay vial containing a thick, still-liquid sludge that smells of the tomb (worthless but disturbing).

The tunnel leads to the outdoors, where there is a pool.  This must be where the hunter saw the red slaad, the threat that led us here.

Sitting above in the upper level where Zabeek died, Aury heals Og and Zymve returns to him the Thornleaf.  Putting the pieces together, the party surmises:

* Blackrend was no longer a druid, but a corrupt undead, perhaps from his loyalty to Nigrum.  
* The aboleth must have a history here in the shrine, feeding off the memories of the cultist, and using those memories to call forth Guigzien.  
* Blackrend was used as part of the ritual to return Guigzien … who may be roaming the land today.

On the upper level the party plans is to return to Ulgrey to find a way to bring Hap back … if possible.  Aury plans on reaching out to his goddess Diverra after some time to rest and gather himself.  Zymve will also reach back out to Thalex to see if he can help.

# Campaign Prep

# DM Brief

## Session 00 \- 03/19/25

I plan on running a campaign that is driven by you.  To do this, Session 0 will be about working together on a few things, and here’s what you’ll need to be prepared for:

1. Character Type \- Have an idea of what type of character you want to play.  Many of you are way ahead on this and have already created the character.  
2. Goal(s) \- Make sure that you have thought of at least one, if not more, goals for your character.  It is a good idea to have 3 to 4 goals for your character at any given time.  There is a caveat … make sure you are flexible to adjusting that goal during Session 0\.  We will ensure that the goals align to party cohesion.

### Examples

Goals can be as complex or as simple as you would like.  Here are some examples:

| Goal | Reason |
| :---- | :---- |
| Win the heart of a certain person | A deep seated desire to be loved |
| Discover the true identity of an important person | The need to be appreciated |
| Slay a powerful monster | A promise to a dying family member |
| Discover the location of a fabled feature | A hunger for power |
| Obtain a magical artifact | The search for spiritual enlightenment |
| Prove the existence of a certain phenomenon | A prophecy |
| Interrupt a ritual of negative effect | Prove themselves worthy of a love interest |

# Player Prep

## Character Creation

Here are some things that I hope the players consider when making their characters:

1. vision – have a vision for your character to be renown throughout Myrdae when the campaign is over  
2. cooperation – play a character that wants to work with other players in the party  
   don’t get hung up in “*but that’s what my character would do*” if it goes against the party  
3. role – consider one of the roles below that you want your character to play and take initiative during gameplay to fill that role  
   * defender \- quick to stand between the biggest monsters and the party members that die easily  
   * face \- someone that helps in npc interaction that relies on skills like deception, persuasion and intimidation  
   * healer \- able to help restore hit points and maybe assist with debuffs (curses, poisons, etc.)  
   * scholar \- can handle the knowledge skills, like arcana, nature and religion  
   * scout \- interesting in moving forward to check the path is clear (stealth, perception, investigation, survival)

   think of your character as playing a primary role, but also having a secondary role if possible

4. progression – when your character accomplishes something, think about what else would be good to work towards  
   make sure the dungeon master knows this and it goes with the flow of the campaign

## The Notebook

During Session 0, we will roll up characters and start filling out the character’s Notebook.    
It can be found in a link on the [Google Site](https://sites.google.com/view/suwanee-gamers/campaigns/another-whirlwind-adventure).    
Here is what you should know about the Notebook:

1. It Helps Your DM – The DM will use the Notebook throughout the campaign and when planning game sessions.    
2. Be Concise – Make sure that what you have planned in your vision of your character can be understood by the DM.  
3. The Backstory – Your backstory is used to help you roleplay your character and to anchor the character’s goals.  
   It is entirely optional, but will help you flush out the Religion, Relations and Goals/Motivations section.  
4. Religion – While religion is optional, it will also help with how you play your character and how the DM may react.  
5. Relations – Another optional section, but helps if you have important NPCs that would impact your Goals/Motivations.  
6. Goals and Motivations – This is the most important part for the DM and the player for this campaign.  
   It will be a living portion of the document throughout the campaign, acting almost as a “to-do” list for your character.  
   We will walk through how to fill this out during Session 0, but I wanted to provide an example …

### What Others Should Play

I wanted to do a brainstorming session to help players think about what they would like to play.    
Take a minute to consider what you would like to see the other players choose as their character.    
You could come up with a class, a role, a species … anything that you think would be neat to see the other player play.    
When coming up with the type of character you want to see the other player play …

* do NOT prank them \- come up with something you would really like to see  
* try not to pick something that they do not want to play as outlined above

# Session 0

# Session 00 \- 3/16/25

We make a party…

* Sean – Ainslie Anaerin, Sorcerer (mind) \- Face Backup Scout, Scholar?  Att: Range, some AoE  
* Larry – Aurelius Valeheart, Cleric (Light) \- Healer  Att:  Range, some AoE  
* Josh – Ogmund “Og” Crag, Barbarian (world tree) – Tank (Melee)  Backup Scout  
* Lesley – Ky’tha Fawnborn, Rogue (sub) \- Scout  Backup Face  Att: Range, melee  
* Ty – Hap Garemon, Fighter (Battle Master) \- Defender   Backup Scholar, aerial scout  Att:Melee, Range

The gathering:  Fill, edit, and elaborate\!  Words in *italics* should be replaced when known.

Hap Garemon is a Tiefling from the swamps near *Emberstran*.  He had a vision from Diverra the Ardent One (Goddess of Love, Beauty), which included the party, and a simple message:  “Join them in *Emberstran*”.  Considering this was the only message he ever got from his deity, and the fact that he was rather lonely and aimless at this point in his life, he picked up and left.  He visited the temple when he got there, and immediately recognized Aurelius Valeheart, where he told him of his vision.  After describing the people in the vision, *Aurelius* thought he knew at least one of the others: Ainslie.  They met Ainslie and Hap immediately recognized him.

*Og and Ky’tha met a few harmons ago when he did some work for hire for her father.  They became friends and he told her to “look him up in Emberstran sometime \- I hang out at the Dyed Cane.”  She decided it would be fun to connect with him and meet some of his friends (he was a nice guy, seemed trustworthy).  A harmon has passed since she last saw him, but she ventured there to see if she could find him.  After a stretch in town, the two connected at the tavern \- where she was introduced to Ainslie.  Eventually, Hap and Aurelius were introduced to her as well \- the group hanging out in the tavern almost nightly.*

Everyone was curious about what the vision could mean, so they met at the pub over the next few nights and shared stories to try to determine what they should do.  Here are their stories:

### **Hap**

About Hap:  In general, as a 6’4” tiefling, Hap doesn't lean into being "scary", and bleaches his hair/beard white as a symbol of "Purity" and "Goodness". Although young, his white beard and habit of referring to himself as "ole' Hap" in the third person with a strong rural accent, makes him seem much older. His happy and young demeanor vs his older tiefling appearance leaves people not knowing what to make of him.

Hap’s story:  Hap described how he grew up with his father Benoit in the swamp, who was a Tiefling of some renown, who taught him a little “gris gris” (magic).  However, when he was 15, some thieves broke in and stole a valuable family ring from Benoit, killing him in a huge fight.  Benoit never told Hap anything about the ring, except that it was precious to him and he would give it to Hap one day.  Hap believes the thugs were from the Cloak of Defiance, since he remembered seeing a couple of them bear a mark he later connected with the group.  He confesses that he has really given up finding them or his father’s ring, but the scars remain, and he keeps a wary eye out for the Cloak’s mark, and a hopeful eye out for the ring.  After the murder, Hap joined the army for a few years, where he learned to fight.  He had just finished a tour of duty, when he had the vision.

### **Aurelius** 

As Hap entered the temple for the first time, being a Tiefling, his presence stirred a curiosity among my temple’s regular attendees, myself included. I, an Aasimar of radiant golden skin and glowing emerald-green eyes, stood out amidst the worshippers of Diverra, the Ardent One—a goddess whose youthful presence had only recently begun to echo through the hearts of her followers.

Clad in elegant white and gold robes adorned with intricate floral patterns, I exuded an air of reverence and purpose. My demeanor, a blend of quiet intensity and gentle warmth, reflected a devotion uncommon among Aasimar, especially towards a deity still in the bloom of her divine influence.

In the temple, I spend my days meticulously caring for the sacred fires and tending to the offerings left in homage to Diverra’s ideals of love, beauty, and pleasure. Just before Hap shared his vision, I was deeply engrossed in studying an old tome believed to contain celestial prophecies and the secrets of divine magic—a pursuit that aligned with my quest to unravel the mysteries of Diverra’s newly found influence.

Hap’s arrival marked a pivotal moment. His tale of loss and resilience struck a chord within me, resonating with my own quest for understanding and purpose. His vision hinted at forces beyond my understanding, urging action and exploration beyond the confines of the temple.

Moved by Diverra’s guidance and Hap’s steadfast spirit, I decided to depart from the sanctuary of the temple. It was a journey not just for answers but also to stand beside Hap and others touched by the divine, seeking to uncover truths and forge alliances that would illuminate our path forward.

This led me to seek out Ainslie, a Sorcerer whose path has intertwined with mine before, hoping to forge alliances and gather knowledge that would aid us in our shared quest.
